

================ FILE: artifacts/lager/src/pages/inventering/exportInventeringsomgangXlsx.ts ================

import * as XLSX from "xlsx";
import type { OmgangRapportSvar } from "./types";
import type { Branding } from "@/components/branding/BrandingContext";

export function encodeField(name: string, value: string | null | undefined): string {
  if (!value) return `${name}=∅`;
  const escaped = value.trim().toUpperCase().replace(/\\/g, "\\\\").replace(/\|/g, "\\|").replace(/=/g, "\\=");
  return `${name}=${escaped}`;
}

export function generateProductKey(
  traslag: string | null,
  profil: string | null,
  kvalitet: string | null,
  nomT: string | null,
  nomB: string | null,
  actT: string | null,
  actB: string | null
): string {
  return [
    encodeField("TRASLAG", traslag),
    encodeField("PROFIL", profil),
    encodeField("KVALITET", kvalitet),
    encodeField("NOM_T", nomT),
    encodeField("NOM_B", nomB),
    encodeField("ACT_T", actT),
    encodeField("ACT_B", actB),
  ].join("|");
}

export function formatProductDesc(
  traslag: string | null,
  profil: string | null,
  kvalitet: string | null,
  nomT: string | null,
  nomB: string | null,
  actT: string | null,
  actB: string | null
): string {
  const name = [traslag, profil, kvalitet].filter(Boolean).join(" ");
  const nomDims = [nomT, nomB].filter(Boolean).join("×");
  const actDims = [actT, actB].filter(Boolean).join("×");

  const dims = [];
  if (nomDims) dims.push(nomDims);
  if (actDims) dims.push(`(ACT ${actDims})`);
  const dimStr = dims.join(" ");

  if (name && dimStr) return `${name} ${dimStr}`;
  if (name) return name;
  if (dimStr) return dimStr;
  return "Okänd produkt";
}

export function calculateActM3(
  actT: string | null,
  actB: string | null,
  langdDm: number | null,
  antal: number
): number | null {
  if (!actT || !actB || !langdDm || antal == null) return null;
  const t = parseFloat(actT.replace(",", "."));
  const b = parseFloat(actB.replace(",", "."));
  if (isNaN(t) || isNaN(b)) return null;
  return (t * b * (langdDm / 10) * antal) / 1000000;
}

export function calculateLpm(langdDm: number | null, antal: number): number {
  if (!langdDm || !antal) return 0;
  return (langdDm / 10) * antal;
}

export function createInventeringsomgangWorkbook(report: OmgangRapportSvar, branding: Branding): XLSX.WorkBook {
  const wb = XLSX.utils.book_new();
  wb.Workbook = {
    ...wb.Workbook,
    CalcPr: {
      calcMode: "auto",
      fullCalcOnLoad: true,
      forceFullCalc: true,
    },
  };

  const productMap = new Map<string, any>();
  const lagerRows: any[] = [];
  let rowIdx = 2;

  report.slutlager.forEach((pkt) => {
    pkt.frystaLangdrader.forEach((rad) => {
      const key = generateProductKey(rad.traslag, rad.profil, rad.kvalitet, rad.nomTjocklek, rad.nomBredd, rad.actTjocklek, rad.actBredd);

      if (!productMap.has(key)) {
        productMap.set(key, {
          "Prisnyckel": key,
          "Beskrivning": formatProductDesc(rad.traslag, rad.profil, rad.kvalitet, rad.nomTjocklek, rad.nomBredd, rad.actTjocklek, rad.actBredd),
          "Träslag": rad.traslag || "",
          "Profil": rad.profil || "",
          "Kvalitet": rad.kvalitet || "",
          "NOM tjocklek": rad.nomTjocklek || "",
          "NOM bredd": rad.nomBredd || "",
          "ACT tjocklek": rad.actTjocklek || "",
          "ACT bredd": rad.actBredd || "",
          "Pris": null,
          "Prisenhet": "kr/NOM m³",
          "Kommentar": "",
        });
      }

      const actM3 = calculateActM3(rad.actTjocklek, rad.actBredd, rad.langdDm, rad.antal);
      const lpm = calculateLpm(rad.langdDm, rad.antal);

      const prisFormula = `IFERROR(IF(INDEX(Prislista!J:J, MATCH(A${rowIdx}, Prislista!A:A, 0))="", "", INDEX(Prislista!J:J, MATCH(A${rowIdx}, Prislista!A:A, 0))), "")`;
      const enhetFormula = `IFERROR(INDEX(Prislista!K:K, MATCH(A${rowIdx}, Prislista!A:A, 0)), "")`;
      const lagervardeFormula = `IF(U${rowIdx}="", "", IF(V${rowIdx}="kr/NOM m³", U${rowIdx}*O${rowIdx}, IF(V${rowIdx}="kr/lpm", U${rowIdx}*N${rowIdx}, "")))`;

      lagerRows.push({
        "Prisnyckel": key,
        "Paketnr": pkt.pktNr,
        "Paketstatus": pkt.status || "",
        "Beslut": pkt.beslut || "",
        "Träslag": rad.traslag || "",
        "Profil": rad.profil || "",
        "Kvalitet": rad.kvalitet || "",
        "NOM tjocklek": rad.nomTjocklek || "",
        "NOM bredd": rad.nomBredd || "",
        "ACT tjocklek": rad.actTjocklek || "",
        "ACT bredd": rad.actBredd || "",
        "Längd": rad.langdDm ? rad.langdDm / 10 : null,
        "Antal st": rad.antal,
        "Löpmeter": lpm || null,
        "NOM m³": rad.nomM3,
        "ACT m³": actM3 !== null ? actM3 : null,
        "Område": pkt.placering.omradeNamn || "",
        "Fack": pkt.placering.fackKod || "",
        "Lagerplatsstatus": pkt.korrigerad ? "Korrigerad" : "",
        "Inventerad/skannad tid": rad.inventeradSkannadAt
          ? new Date(rad.inventeradSkannadAt)
          : null,
        "Pris": { t: "str", v: "", f: prisFormula },
        "Prisenhet": { t: "s", v: "kr/NOM m³", f: enhetFormula },
        "Lagervärde": { t: "str", v: "", f: lagervardeFormula },
        "Datakvalitetsvarning": rad.historiskaLuckor?.join(", ") || ""
      });
      rowIdx++;
    });
  });

  const wsLager = XLSX.utils.json_to_sheet(lagerRows);
  wsLager["!freeze"] = { xSplit: 0, ySplit: 1, topLeftCell: "A2", activePane: "bottomLeft", state: "frozen" };
  if (lagerRows.length > 0) {
    wsLager["!autofilter"] = { ref: `A1:X${lagerRows.length + 1}` };
  }

  const prisRows = Array.from(productMap.values());
  const wsPris = XLSX.utils.json_to_sheet(prisRows);

  const sammRows = [
    ["Inventeringsrapport"],
    ["Namn", report.metadata.namn],
    ["Typ", report.metadata.typ],
    ["År", report.metadata.ar || ""],
    ["Startad", report.metadata.startadAt ? new Date(report.metadata.startadAt).toLocaleString("sv-SE") : ""],
    ["Avslutad", report.metadata.avslutadAt ? new Date(report.metadata.avslutadAt).toLocaleString("sv-SE") : ""],
    ["Företag", branding.companyName || ""],
    [],
    ["Inventeringsdata"],
    ["Fack klara", `${report.metadata.antalFackKlara} / ${report.metadata.antalFackTotalt}`],
    ["Saknade paket utan beslut", report.totals.saknadePaketUtanBeslut],
    ["Ofullständig ACT-data (paket)", report.totals.actM3SaknadePaket],
    [],
    ["Totaler Slutlager"],
    ["Antal Unika Paket", report.totals.antalUnikaPaket],
    ["Totalt Styck", report.totals.totaltStyck],
    ["Totalt Lpm", report.totals.totaltLpm],
    ["Totalt NOM m³", report.totals.totalNomM3],
    ["Totalt ACT m³", report.totals.totalActM3 !== null ? report.totals.totalActM3 : "Ofullständig"],
    ["Totalt Lagervärde (kr)", { t: "n", v: 0, f: `SUM(Lagerlista!W:W)` }],
    [],
    ["Värde per Produktnyckel"],
    ["Prisnyckel", "Beskrivning", "NOM m³", "Värde (kr)"]
  ];

  prisRows.forEach((p, index) => {
    const key = p.Prisnyckel;
    const desc = p.Beskrivning;
    const priceRow = index + 2;
    const formulaM3 = `SUMIF(Lagerlista!A:A, Prislista!A${priceRow}, Lagerlista!O:O)`;
    const formulaVal = `SUMIF(Lagerlista!A:A, Prislista!A${priceRow}, Lagerlista!W:W)`;
    sammRows.push([
      key,
      desc,
      { t: "n", v: 0, f: formulaM3 },
      { t: "n", v: 0, f: formulaVal },
    ]);
  });

  const wsSamm = XLSX.utils.aoa_to_sheet(sammRows);
  const boldCells = ["A1", "A9", "A14", "A22", "A23", "B23", "C23", "D23"];
  boldCells.forEach(cell => {
    if (wsSamm[cell]) wsSamm[cell].s = { font: { bold: true } };
  });

  XLSX.utils.book_append_sheet(wb, wsLager, "Lagerlista");
  XLSX.utils.book_append_sheet(wb, wsPris, "Prislista");
  XLSX.utils.book_append_sheet(wb, wsSamm, "Sammanställning");

  return wb;
}

export function exportInventeringsomgangXlsx(report: OmgangRapportSvar, branding: Branding) {
  const wb = createInventeringsomgangWorkbook(report, branding);
  const safeName = report.metadata.namn.replace(/\s+/g, "_");
  XLSX.writeFile(wb, `Inventeringsrapport_${safeName}.xlsx`);
}

// Delade typer för inventeringsflödet (Task #13). Speglar svarsformen från
// routes/inventering.ts på API-servern.

export type Klassificering = "forvantad_hittad" | "felplacerad" | "okand" | "otydlig_match";
export type SessionStatus = "pagaende" | "avslutad" | "avbruten";

export interface ForvantatPaket {
  pktNr: string;
  antalRader: number;
  totalAntal: number;
  totalM3: number;
  // Fryst produktdata VID INVENTERINGSSTART (Task #20). Null för äldre
  // rader från innan frysningen infördes (live-fallback på servern).
  traslag: string | null;
  profil: string | null;
  kvalitet: string | null;
  nomTjocklek: string | null;
  nomBredd: string | null;
  langder: number[];
  omradeNamn: string | null;
  fackKod: string | null;
}

export interface LangdBrytning {
  langdDm: number;
  styck: number;
}

export interface SkanningDetalj {
  id: number;
  pktNr: string;
  klassificeringVidSkanning: Klassificering;
  klassificering: Klassificering;
  metod: "skanner" | "kamera" | "manuell";
  skannadAv: string;
  skannadAt: string;
  antalRader: number;
  totalAntal: number;
  totalM3: number;
  // Produktdata för identifiering (t.ex. i "Felplacerade paket"-listan) —
  // kommer i första hand från den frysta inventeringssnapshoten. ACT-mått
  // är endast för visning, aldrig för volymberäkning (totalM3 är alltid NOM).
  traslag: string | null;
  profil: string | null;
  kvalitet: string | null;
  nomTjocklek: string | null;
  nomBredd: string | null;
  actTjocklek: string | null;
  actBredd: string | null;
  langdBrytning: LangdBrytning[];
  fortfarandeAvvikande: boolean;
}

export interface Raknare {
  forvantadeAntalPaket: number;
  hittade: number;
  felplacerade: number;
  okanda: number;
  otydliga: number;
  saknade: number;
}

export interface SessionOversikt {
  id: number;
  status: SessionStatus;
  omradeId: number;
  omradeNamn: string | null;
  fackId: number;
  fackKod: string | null;
  omgangId: number | null;
  omgangNamn: string | null;
  startadAv: string;
  startadAt: string;
  avslutadAv: string | null;
  avslutadAt: string | null;
  forvantadePaket: ForvantatPaket[];
  skanningar: SkanningDetalj[];
  saknadePaket: ForvantatPaket[];
  raknare: Raknare;
}

export interface SessionListItem {
  id: number;
  status: SessionStatus;
  omradeId: number;
  omradeNamn: string | null;
  fackId: number;
  fackKod: string | null;
  startadAv: string;
  startadAt: string;
  avslutadAv: string | null;
  avslutadAt: string | null;
}

// --- Inventeringsomgångar (Task #20) --------------------------------------

export type OmgangTyp = "arsinventering" | "kontrollinventering";
export type OmgangStatus = "pagaende" | "avslutad" | "avbruten";

export interface OmgangListItem {
  id: number;
  namn: string;
  typ: OmgangTyp;
  status: OmgangStatus;
  ar: number | null;
  startadAv: string;
  startadAt: string;
  avslutadAv: string | null;
  avslutadAt: string | null;
  antalFackTotalt: number;
  antalFackKlara: number;
}

export interface OmgangFackRad {
  fackId: number;
  fackKod: string | null;
  fackAktivKod: string | null;
  omradeIdVidStart: number;
  omradeNamnVidStart: string | null;
  sessionId: number | null;
  sessionStatus: SessionStatus | "ej_startad";
}

export interface OmgangSammanfattningRad {
  id: number;
  omgangId: number;
  omradeId: number | null;
  antalInventeradePaket: number;
  antalLagerRader: number;
  // Fysiskt FUNNA paket (hittade/felplacerade/okända/otydliga) — saknade
  // paket bidrar aldrig hit, se styckSaknat/nomM3Saknat nedan.
  styck: number;
  totaltNomM3: number;
  prissattNomM3: number;
  ejPrissattNomM3: number;
  totaltLagervarde: number;
  antalSaknade: number;
  // Separata avvikelsesiffror för paket som aldrig hittades i omgången.
  styckSaknat: number;
  nomM3Saknat: number;
  antalFelplacerade: number;
  antalOkanda: number;
  antalOtydliga: number;
  // Antal pktNr i omgången som förekom i flera fack-sessioner på ett sätt
  // som krävde ett auktoritativt val (dubbelskannat, eller saknat i en
  // session men hittat i en annan) — se OmgangKonflikt för detaljer.
  antalKonflikter: number;
  antalFackKlara: number;
  antalFackTotalt: number;
  // Global saknas-avstämning (endast satt på totalraden för en
  // 'arsinventering' med en global startsnapshot — annars null). Se
  // OmgangSaknadeSvar/OmgangTillkommitSvar: antalSaknade/styckSaknat/
  // nomM3Saknat ovan är i detta fall härledda från SAMMA globala
  // avstämning som den frysta historiska saknas-listan, inte per
  // fack-session — se .agents/memory/lager-inventeringsomgangar.md.
  antalGlobalSnapshot: number | null;
  nomM3GlobalSnapshot: number | null;
  antalTillkommitEfterStart: number | null;
  styckTillkommitEfterStart: number | null;
  nomM3TillkommitEfterStart: number | null;
  berakndAt: string;
}

export type OmgangKonfliktTyp = "dubbelskannad" | "saknad_sedan_hittad" | "saknad_flera_sessioner";

export interface OmgangKonflikt {
  pktNr: string;
  typ: OmgangKonfliktTyp;
  sessionIdAnvand: number;
  ovrigaSessionIder: number[];
  beskrivning: string;
  // Krävde denna konflikt ett manuellt beslut innan omgången fick avslutas?
  // (Endast 'dubbelskannad' — se KONFLIKT_LABEL/OmgangDubbelskanning.)
  blockerarAvslut: boolean;
  losningSessionId: number | null;
  hanteradAv: string | null;
  hanteradAt: string | null;
}

export const KONFLIKT_LABEL: Record<OmgangKonfliktTyp, string> = {
  dubbelskannad: "Dubbelskannat i flera fack",
  saknad_sedan_hittad: "Saknat i ett fack, hittat i ett annat",
  saknad_flera_sessioner: "Saknat i flera sessioner",
};

export interface OmgangDetalj extends OmgangListItem {
  fack: OmgangFackRad[];
  sammanfattning: {
    totalt: OmgangSammanfattningRad | null;
    perOmrade: OmgangSammanfattningRad[];

  } | null;
  slutsammanstallning: OmgangSlutsammanstallning | null;
  felplacerade: OmgangFelplaceratPaket[];
}

  hittat: { antalPaket: number; nomM3: number };
  saknas: { antalPaket: number; nomM3: number };
  tillkommitEfterStart: { antalPaket: number; styck: number; nomM3: number };

}

}

// --- Dubbelskanningskonflikter (kräver manuell hantering) -----------------
//
// En "dubbelskannad" konflikt (samma pktNr fysiskt funnet i flera olika
// fack-sessioner inom samma omgång) blockerar avslut tills en människa
// antingen pekar ut vilket fynd som är korrekt, eller uttryckligen
// bekräftar konflikten som hanterad. Se POST /:id/dubbelskanningar/:pktNr/los.

export interface OmgangDubbelskanningKandidat {

  fackId: number;
  fackKod: string | null;
  omradeNamn: string | null;
  skannadAt: string;
  styck: number;
  nomM3: number;
  klassificering: Klassificering;
}

export interface OmgangDubbelskanningLosning {
  valdSessionId: number | null;
  hanteradAv: string;
  hanteradAt: string;
}

export interface OmgangDubbelskanning {

  olost: boolean;
  losning: OmgangDubbelskanningLosning | null;
  kandidater: OmgangDubbelskanningKandidat[];
}

export interface OmgangDubbelskanningarSvar {

  dubbelskanningar: OmgangDubbelskanning[];
}

}

// --- Ej inventerade / Saknas (global startsnapshot, Task #20 uppföljning) --

// Skiljer sig från den frysta sammanfattningens antalSaknade/styckSaknat/
// nomM3Saknat (som fortfarande härleds per fack-session, se

// lagrets globala startsnapshot och fångar därför även paket utan
// registrerat område/fack. Ett pktNr som skannats i NÅGOT fack i omgången
// kan aldrig hamna här.

export type OmgangSaknatBeslutTyp = "behall_i_lager" | "avrapporterad";

export interface OmgangSaknatBeslut {

  kommentar: string | null;
  beslutadAv: string;
  beslutadAt: string;
}

}

export interface OmgangSaknadeSvar {

  antalSkannat: number;
  antalSaknas: number;
  paket: OmgangSaknatPaket[];
}

// --- Tillkommit efter start (global startsnapshot, samma avstämning som
// ovan) ------------------------------------------------------------------
//
// Ett pktNr som skannades NÅGONSTANS under omgången men som INTE fanns med
// i den globala startsnapshoten alls (t.ex. levererat in efter att
// snapshoten togs). Räknas medvetet INTE som "hittat" mot startsnapshoten
// — annars skulle Hittade + Saknade kunna överstiga antalGlobalSnapshot.
// Visas separat i rapporten, med egna antal/styck/NOM m³.

export interface OmgangTillkommitPaket {
  pktNr: string;
  lagerId: number;
  traslag: string | null;
  profil: string | null;
  kvalitet: string | null;
  nomTjocklek: string | null;
  nomBredd: string | null;
  actTjocklek: string | null;
  actBredd: string | null;
  langder: number[];
  langdBrytning: LangdBrytning[];
  styck: number;
  nomM3: number;
  omradeIdVidSkanning: number | null;
  omradeNamnVidSkanning: string | null;
  fackIdVidSkanning: number | null;
  fackKodVidSkanning: string | null;
  blandadPlacering: boolean;
}

export interface OmgangTillkommitSvar {
  harGlobalSnapshot: boolean;
  antal: number;
  styck: number;
  nomM3: number;
  paket: OmgangTillkommitPaket[];
}

================ FILE: artifacts/lager/src/pages/inventory/InventoryDetail.tsx ================

import { useState } from "react";
import { useParams, useLocation } from "wouter";
import { format } from "date-fns";
import { renderToStaticMarkup } from "react-dom/server";
import QRCode from "qrcode";
import { 
  useGetLagerPost,
  getGetLagerPostQueryKey,
  useDeleteLagerPost,
  useUpdateLagerPost,
  customFetch,
} from "@workspace/api-client-react";
import { useQueryClient, useQuery, useMutation } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import { Card, CardContent, CardHeader, CardTitle, CardDescription } from "@/components/ui/card";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { Badge } from "@/components/ui/badge";
import { Skeleton } from "@/components/ui/skeleton";
import { Separator } from "@/components/ui/separator";
import { useToast } from "@/hooks/use-toast";
import { 
  ArrowLeft, 
  Pencil, 
  Printer,
  Trash2, 
  Box, 
  Calendar, 
  MapPin, 
  FileText,
  AlertTriangle,
  CheckCircle2,
  Truck,
  History,
  ClipboardList,
  Copy,
  Wrench,
  Coins,
} from "lucide-react";
import PaketlappPrint from "./PaketlappPrint";
import { skapaSakerDupliceringsPrefill } from "./duplicatePrefill";
import { getStructuredLagerplatsDetails } from "@/lib/structuredLagerplats";
import { Link } from "wouter";
import {
  AlertDialog,
  AlertDialogAction,
  AlertDialogCancel,
  AlertDialogContent,
  AlertDialogDescription,
  AlertDialogFooter,
  AlertDialogHeader,
  AlertDialogTitle,
  AlertDialogTrigger,
} from "@/components/ui/alert-dialog";
import {
  Sheet,
  SheetContent,
  SheetHeader,
  SheetTitle,
  SheetDescription,
} from "@/components/ui/sheet";
import { Plus, ExternalLink } from "lucide-react";

interface SpecSummary {
  id: number;
  typ?: string;
  kund: string | null;
  trailerNr: string | null;
  orderNr: string | null;
  status: string;
  antalPaket: number;
}

export default function InventoryDetail() {
  const params = useParams();
  const id = parseInt(params.id as string);
  const [, navigate] = useLocation();
  const { toast } = useToast();
  const queryClient = useQueryClient();
  const [specSheetOpen, setSpecSheetOpen] = useState(false);
  const [hyvlingSheetOpen, setHyvlingSheetOpen] = useState(false);

  const { data: post, isLoading, error } = useGetLagerPost(id, {
    query: {
      enabled: !!id,
      queryKey: getGetLagerPostQueryKey(id)
    }
  });

  const lagerplatsDetails = post ? getStructuredLagerplatsDetails(post) : null;

  type SpecRef = { id: number; typ: string; kund: string | null; trailerNr: string | null; orderNr: string | null; status: string; bekraftadAt: string | null; skapadAt: string };
  const { data: specRefs = [] } = useQuery<SpecRef[]>({
    queryKey: [`/api/spec/by-lager/${id}`],
    queryFn: () => customFetch<SpecRef[]>(`/api/spec/by-lager/${id}`),
    enabled: !!id,
  });

  const { data: historik = [] } = useQuery<{ id: number; action: string; andringar: Record<string, unknown> | null; changedBy: string | null; skapadAt: string }[]>({
    queryKey: [`/api/lager/${id}/historik`],
    queryFn: () => customFetch<{ id: number; action: string; andringar: Record<string, unknown> | null; changedBy: string | null; skapadAt: string }[]>(`/api/lager/${id}/historik`),
    enabled: !!id,
  });

  const deleteMutation = useDeleteLagerPost({
    mutation: {
      onSuccess: () => {
        toast({
          title: "Lagerpost raderad",
          description: "Posten har tagits bort från systemet."
        });
        queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["/api/lager"] });
        queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["/api/lager/statistik"] });
        navigate("/lager");
      },
      onError: () => {
        toast({
          title: "Fel vid radering",
          description: "Kunde inte radera lagerposten.",
          variant: "destructive"
        });
      }
    }
  });

  const updateMutation = useUpdateLagerPost({
    mutation: {
      onSuccess: () => {
        toast({
          title: "Status uppdaterad",
          description: "Lagerpostens status har ändrats."
        });
        queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: getGetLagerPostQueryKey(id) });
        queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["/api/lager"] });
        queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["/api/lager/statistik"] });
      }
    }
  });

  // Specs som innehåller denna post
  const { data: postensSpecar = [], refetch: refetchPostensSpecar } = useQuery<SpecSummary[]>({
    queryKey: [`/api/spec/by-lager/${id}`],
    queryFn: () => customFetch<SpecSummary[]>(`/api/spec/by-lager/${id}`),
    enabled: !!id,
  });

  // Alla öppna specs (för att välja bland)
  const { data: allaSpecar = [] } = useQuery<SpecSummary[]>({
    queryKey: ["/api/spec"],
    queryFn: () => customFetch<SpecSummary[]>("/api/spec"),
    enabled: specSheetOpen,
  });

  const laggTillISpecMutation = useMutation({
    mutationFn: async (specId: number) => {
      return customFetch<{ already: boolean }>(`/api/spec/${specId}/paket`, {
        method: "POST",
        headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
        body: JSON.stringify({ lagerId: id }),
      });
    },
    onSuccess: (data, specId) => {
      queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: [`/api/spec/by-lager/${id}`] });
      queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["/api/spec"] });
      if (data.already) {
        toast({ title: "Redan tillagd", description: "Posten finns redan i den specifikationen." });
      } else {
        toast({ title: "Tillagd i specifikation" });
      }
      refetchPostensSpecar();
    },
    onError: () => toast({ title: "Fel", description: "Kunde inte lägga till i specifikation.", variant: "destructive" }),
  });

  const skapaNySpecMutation = useMutation({
    mutationFn: async () => {
      return customFetch<{ id: number }>("/api/spec", {
        method: "POST",
        headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
        body: JSON.stringify({ paketIds: [id] }),
      });
    },
    onSuccess: (spec) => {
      setSpecSheetOpen(false);
      navigate(`/spec/${spec.id}`);
    },
    onError: () => toast({ title: "Fel", description: "Kunde inte skapa specifikation.", variant: "destructive" }),
  });

  const { data: allaHyvlingar = [] } = useQuery<SpecSummary[]>({
    queryKey: ["/api/spec", "hyvling"],
    queryFn: () => customFetch<SpecSummary[]>("/api/spec?typ=hyvling"),
    enabled: hyvlingSheetOpen,
  });

  const laggTillIHyvlingMutation = useMutation({
    mutationFn: async (hyvlingId: number) => {
      return customFetch<{ already: boolean }>(`/api/spec/${hyvlingId}/paket`, {
        method: "POST",
        headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
        body: JSON.stringify({ lagerId: id }),
      });
    },
    onSuccess: (data, hyvlingId) => {
      queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: [`/api/spec/by-lager/${id}`] });
      queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["/api/spec", "hyvling"] });
      if (data.already) {
        toast({ title: "Redan tillagd", description: "Posten finns redan i den hyvlingsordern." });
      } else {
        toast({ title: "Tillagd i hyvlingsorder" });
      }
      refetchPostensSpecar();
    },
    onError: () => toast({ title: "Fel", description: "Kunde inte lägga till i hyvlingsorder.", variant: "destructive" }),
  });

  const skapaNyHyvlingMutation = useMutation({
    mutationFn: async () => {
      return customFetch<{ id: number }>("/api/spec", {
        method: "POST",
        headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
        body: JSON.stringify({ typ: "hyvling", paketIds: [id] }),
      });
    },
    onSuccess: (spec) => {
      setHyvlingSheetOpen(false);
      navigate(`/hyvling/${spec.id}`);
    },
    onError: () => toast({ title: "Fel", description: "Kunde inte skapa hyvlingsorder.", variant: "destructive" }),
  });

  const toggleStatus = () => {
    if (!post) return;
    
    // Clean up post data for update payload
    const updateData = { ...post };
    delete (updateData as any).id;
    delete (updateData as any).skapadAt;
    delete (updateData as any).uppdateradAt;
    
    if (post.datumUt) {
      // Revert to in stock
      updateData.datumUt = null;
    } else {
      // Mark as delivered out today
      updateData.datumUt = format(new Date(), "yyyy-MM-dd");
    }
    
    updateMutation.mutate({ id, data: updateData });
  };

  const printPaketlapp = async () => {
    if (!post) return;
    let qrDataUrl: string | undefined;
    try {
      const url = `${window.location.origin}/lager/${post.id}`;
      qrDataUrl = await QRCode.toDataURL(url, { width: 160, margin: 1, color: { dark: "#000", light: "#fff" } });
    } catch { /* silently skip if QR fails */ }

    const html = renderToStaticMarkup(<PaketlappPrint post={post} qrDataUrl={qrDataUrl} />);
    const win = window.open("", "_blank");
    if (!win) return;
    win.document.write(`<!DOCTYPE html>
<html lang="sv">
<head>
  <meta charset="utf-8" />
  <title>Paketlapp – Pkt-Nr ${post.pktNr || post.id}</title>
  <style>
    * { box-sizing: border-box; margin: 0; padding: 0; }
    body { background: #fff; }
    @media print {
      html, body { width: 210mm; height: 297mm; }
      @page { size: A4 portrait; margin: 0; }
    }
  </style>
</head>
<body>${html}</body>
</html>`);
    win.document.close();
    win.focus();
    setTimeout(() => win.print(), 400);
  };

  if (isLoading) {
    return (
      <div className="space-y-6 max-w-5xl mx-auto">
        <Skeleton className="h-10 w-32" />
        <Card>
          <CardHeader>
            <Skeleton className="h-8 w-64 mb-2" />
            <Skeleton className="h-4 w-32" />
          </CardHeader>
          <CardContent className="grid gap-6 md:grid-cols-2">
            <Skeleton className="h-48 w-full" />
            <Skeleton className="h-48 w-full" />
          </CardContent>
        </Card>
      </div>
    );
  }

  if (error || !post) {
    return (
      <div className="max-w-xl mx-auto text-center py-12">
        <AlertTriangle className="h-12 w-12 text-destructive mx-auto mb-4" />
        <h2 className="text-2xl font-bold mb-2">Posten hittades inte</h2>
        <p className="text-muted-foreground mb-6">
          Kunde inte ladda detaljer för lagerpost #{id}.
        </p>
        <Button onClick={() => navigate("/lager")}>Tillbaka till listan</Button>
      </div>
    );
  }

  const isActive = !post.datumUt;

  const InfoItem = ({ label, value, icon: Icon }: { label: string, value: React.ReactNode, icon?: any }) => (
    <div className="flex flex-col gap-1.5 p-3 rounded-lg border bg-card/50">
      <span className="text-xs font-medium text-muted-foreground flex items-center gap-1.5 uppercase tracking-wider">
        {Icon && <Icon className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />}
        {label}
      </span>
      <span className="text-sm font-semibold">{value || "-"}</span>
    </div>
  );

  return (
    <div className="space-y-6 max-w-5xl mx-auto pb-12">
      <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row items-start sm:items-center justify-between gap-4">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-4">
          <Button variant="outline" size="icon" asChild>
            <Link href="/lager">
              <ArrowLeft className="h-4 w-4" />
            </Link>
          </Button>
          <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
            <h1 className="text-3xl font-bold tracking-tight">Post {post.pktNr ? `#${post.pktNr}` : `ID:${post.id}`}</h1>
            {isActive ? (
              <Badge className="bg-primary/20 text-primary hover:bg-primary/30 border-0 flex items-center gap-1">
                <CheckCircle2 className="h-3 w-3" /> I Lager
              </Badge>
            ) : (
              <Badge variant="secondary" className="border-0 flex items-center gap-1">
                <Truck className="h-3 w-3" /> Utlevererad
              </Badge>
            )}
          </div>
        </div>
        
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2 w-full sm:w-auto">
          <Button 
            variant={isActive ? "outline" : "default"} 
            onClick={toggleStatus}
            disabled={updateMutation.isPending}
            className="flex-1 sm:flex-none"
          >
            {isActive ? "Markera utlevererad" : "Återför till lager"}
          </Button>
          
          <Button variant="outline" asChild>
            <Link href={`/lager/${id}/redigera`}>
              <Pencil className="h-4 w-4 mr-2" /> Redigera
            </Link>
          </Button>

          <Button
            variant="outline"
            title="Duplicera — öppnar ny post med samma uppgifter"
            onClick={() => {
              const prefill = skapaSakerDupliceringsPrefill(
                post as unknown as Record<string, unknown>,
              );
              sessionStorage.setItem("lager_duplicate", JSON.stringify(prefill));
              navigate("/lager/ny");
            }}
          >
            <Copy className="h-4 w-4 mr-2" /> Duplicera
          </Button>

          <Button
            variant="outline"
            title="Skriv ut paketlapp"
            onClick={printPaketlapp}
          >
            <Printer className="h-4 w-4 mr-2" /> Skriv ut
          </Button>

          <Button
            variant="outline"
            onClick={() => setSpecSheetOpen(true)}
            title="Lägg till i specifikation"
          >
            <ClipboardList className="h-4 w-4 mr-2" />
            Lägg till i spec
            {postensSpecar.filter(s => (s as SpecSummary & { typ?: string }).typ !== "hyvling").length > 0 && (
              <span className="ml-1.5 bg-primary text-primary-foreground text-xs rounded-full px-1.5 py-0.5 leading-none">
                {postensSpecar.filter(s => (s as SpecSummary & { typ?: string }).typ !== "hyvling").length}
              </span>
            )}
          </Button>

          <Button
            variant="outline"
            className="text-orange-700 border-orange-300 hover:bg-orange-50"
            onClick={() => setHyvlingSheetOpen(true)}
            title="Lägg till i hyvlingsorder"
          >
            <Wrench className="h-4 w-4 mr-2" />
            Lägg till i hyvlingsorder
            {postensSpecar.filter(s => (s as SpecSummary & { typ?: string }).typ === "hyvling").length > 0 && (
              <span className="ml-1.5 bg-orange-600 text-white text-xs rounded-full px-1.5 py-0.5 leading-none">
                {postensSpecar.filter(s => (s as SpecSummary & { typ?: string }).typ === "hyvling").length}
              </span>
            )}
          </Button>
          
          <AlertDialog>
            <AlertDialogTrigger asChild>
              <Button variant="destructive" size="icon" title="Radera post">
                <Trash2 className="h-4 w-4" />
              </Button>
            </AlertDialogTrigger>
            <AlertDialogContent>
              <AlertDialogHeader>
                <AlertDialogTitle>Flytta till papperskorgen?</AlertDialogTitle>
                <AlertDialogDescription>
                  Lagerposten flyttas till papperskorgen. En admin kan återställa den därifrån om det behövs.
                </AlertDialogDescription>
              </AlertDialogHeader>
              <AlertDialogFooter>
                <AlertDialogCancel>Avbryt</AlertDialogCancel>
                <AlertDialogAction 
                  onClick={() => deleteMutation.mutate({ id })}
                  className="bg-destructive text-destructive-foreground hover:bg-destructive/90"
                >
                  Flytta till papperskorgen
                </AlertDialogAction>
              </AlertDialogFooter>
            </AlertDialogContent>
          </AlertDialog>
        </div>
      </div>

      <div className="grid gap-6 md:grid-cols-3">
        <Card className="md:col-span-2">
          <CardHeader>
            <CardTitle className="text-lg flex items-center gap-2">
              <Box className="h-5 w-5 text-primary" />
              Specifikation
            </CardTitle>
            <CardDescription>Träslag, profil, kvalitet, behandling och utförande</CardDescription>
          </CardHeader>
          <CardContent>
            <div className="grid grid-cols-2 sm:grid-cols-4 gap-4 mb-6">
              <InfoItem label="Varuslag" value={post.varuslag} />
              <InfoItem label="Träslag" value={post.traslag} />
              <InfoItem label="Kvalitet" value={post.kvalitet} />
              <InfoItem label="Profil" value={post.profil} />
              <InfoItem label="Behandling" value={post.behandling} />
              <InfoItem label="Utförande" value={post.utforande} />
            </div>

            <Separator className="my-6" />
            
            <h3 className="text-sm font-semibold mb-4 text-muted-foreground uppercase tracking-wider">Dimensioner & Mängd</h3>
            <div className="grid grid-cols-2 sm:grid-cols-4 gap-4">
              <InfoItem 
                label="Nom. Dimension" 
                value={post.nomTjocklek && post.nomBredd ? `${post.nomTjocklek} × ${post.nomBredd} mm` : "-"} 
              />
              <InfoItem 
                label="Akt. Dimension" 
                value={post.actTjocklek && post.actBredd ? `${post.actTjocklek} × ${post.actBredd} mm` : "-"} 
              />
              <InfoItem label="Längd" value={post.langdDm ? `${post.langdDm} dm` : "-"} />
              <InfoItem label="Antal st" value={post.antal} />
              <InfoItem label="Volym (m³)" value={post.m3 ? `${post.m3.toFixed(3)} m³` : "-"} />
              <InfoItem label="Akt. Volym (m³)" value={post.actM3 ? `${post.actM3.toFixed(3)} m³` : "-"} />
              <InfoItem label="Löpmeter" value={post.lpm ? `${post.lpm} m` : "-"} />
            </div>

            {((post as any).m3Pris || (post as any).m3Sum) && (
              <>
                <Separator className="my-6" />
                <h3 className="text-sm font-semibold mb-4 text-muted-foreground uppercase tracking-wider flex items-center gap-2">
                  <Coins className="h-4 w-4" />
                  Prissättning
                </h3>
                <div className="grid grid-cols-2 sm:grid-cols-4 gap-4">
                  <InfoItem
                    label="Inköpspris"
                    value={(post as any).m3Pris ? `${Number((post as any).m3Pris).toLocaleString("sv-SE", { minimumFractionDigits: 0, maximumFractionDigits: 2 })} kr/m³` : "-"}
                  />
                  <InfoItem
                    label="Totalt värde"
                    value={(post as any).m3Sum ? `${Number((post as any).m3Sum).toLocaleString("sv-SE", { minimumFractionDigits: 0, maximumFractionDigits: 2 })} kr` : "-"}
                  />
                </div>
              </>
            )}
          </CardContent>
        </Card>

        <div className="space-y-6">
          <Card>
            <CardHeader>
              <CardTitle className="text-lg flex items-center gap-2">
                <MapPin className="h-5 w-5 text-primary" />
                Spårbarhet
              </CardTitle>
            </CardHeader>
            <CardContent className="space-y-4">
              <InfoItem label="Lagerplats/område" value={lagerplatsDetails?.omrade} icon={MapPin} />
              <InfoItem label="Fack" value={lagerplatsDetails?.fack} />
              <InfoItem label="Mottaget från" value={post.mottaget} />
              <InfoItem label="Produktion" value={post.produktion} />
              <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3">
                <InfoItem label="Datum in" value={post.datumIn ? format(new Date(post.datumIn), "yyyy-MM-dd") : "-"} icon={Calendar} />
                <InfoItem label="Datum ut" value={post.datumUt ? format(new Date(post.datumUt), "yyyy-MM-dd") : "-"} icon={Calendar} />
              </div>

              {specRefs.length > 0 && (
                <div className="pt-1">
                  <span className="text-xs font-medium text-muted-foreground flex items-center gap-1.5 uppercase tracking-wider mb-2">
                    <ClipboardList className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
                    Tillhör följesedel
                  </span>
                  <div className="space-y-1.5">
                    {specRefs.map(s => {
                      const label = [s.kund, s.trailerNr && `Trailer ${s.trailerNr}`, s.orderNr && `Order ${s.orderNr}`].filter(Boolean).join(" · ") || `Följesedel #${s.id}`;
                      const statusLabel = s.status === "bekraftad" ? "Bekräftad" : "Utkast";
                      const statusClass = s.status === "bekraftad" ? "bg-green-100 text-green-800" : "bg-yellow-100 text-yellow-800";
                      return (
                        <Link key={s.id} href={`/spec/${s.id}`} className="flex items-center justify-between gap-2 p-2.5 rounded-lg border bg-card/50 hover:bg-muted/50 transition-colors">
                          <span className="text-sm font-medium truncate">{label}</span>
                          <span className={`text-xs font-medium px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full shrink-0 ${statusClass}`}>{statusLabel}</span>
                        </Link>
                      );
                    })}
                  </div>
                </div>
              )}
            </CardContent>
          </Card>

          <Card>
            <CardHeader>
              <CardTitle className="text-lg flex items-center gap-2">
                <FileText className="h-5 w-5 text-primary" />
                Övrigt
              </CardTitle>
            </CardHeader>
            <CardContent className="space-y-4">
              <InfoItem label="Avrapportering" value={post.avrapp} />
              {(post as any).avrappTyp && (
                <InfoItem
                  label="Typ"
                  value={(post as any).avrappTyp === "hyvlat_till_lager" ? "Hyvlas till lager" : "Utlevererat till kund"}
                />
              )}
              <InfoItem label="Avräknad Volym" value={post.avrappM3 ? `${post.avrappM3.toFixed(3)} m³` : "-"} />
              
              <div className="pt-2">
                <span className="text-xs font-medium text-muted-foreground block mb-1 uppercase tracking-wider">Artikel/Anmärkning</span>
                <p className="text-sm p-3 bg-muted/50 rounded-md border whitespace-pre-wrap">
                  {post.anmarkNing || "Ingen artikelanmärkning"}
                </p>
              </div>
              
              <div className="pt-2">
                <span className="text-xs font-medium text-muted-foreground block mb-1 uppercase tracking-wider">Anteckning</span>
                <p className="text-sm p-3 bg-muted/50 rounded-md border whitespace-pre-wrap">
                  {post.anteckning || "Ingen fritextanteckning"}
                </p>
              </div>
            </CardContent>
          </Card>

          {historik.length > 0 && (
            <Card>
              <CardHeader>
                <CardTitle className="text-lg flex items-center gap-2">
                  <History className="h-5 w-5 text-primary" />
                  Ändringshistorik
                </CardTitle>
              </CardHeader>
              <CardContent>
                <div className="space-y-2">
                  {[...historik].reverse().map((h) => {
                    const actionMap: Record<string, string> = {
                      create: "Skapad",
                      update: "Uppdaterad",
                      delete: "Raderad",
                      batch_update: "Batch-uppdaterad",
                      avrapportera: "Avrapporterad",
                      ateroppen: "Återöppnad",
                    };
                    const label = actionMap[h.action] ?? h.action;
                    return (
                      <div key={h.id} className="flex items-start gap-3 text-sm border-b last:border-0 pb-2 last:pb-0">
                        <div className="flex-1">
                          <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                            <span className="font-medium">{label}</span>
                            {h.changedBy && (
                              <span className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">av {h.changedBy}</span>
                            )}
                          </div>
                          {h.andringar && Object.keys(h.andringar).length > 0 && (
                            <div className="text-xs text-muted-foreground mt-0.5">
                              {Object.entries(h.andringar)
                                .filter(([k]) => !["uppdateradAt"].includes(k))
                                .map(([k, v]) => `${k}: ${v}`)
                                .join(", ")}
                            </div>
                          )}
                        </div>
                        <span className="text-xs text-muted-foreground whitespace-nowrap">
                          {format(new Date(h.skapadAt), "yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm")}
                        </span>
                      </div>
                    );
                  })}
                </div>
              </CardContent>
            </Card>
          )}
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Sheet: Lägg till i specifikation */}
      <Sheet open={specSheetOpen} onOpenChange={setSpecSheetOpen}>
        <SheetContent side="right" className="w-full sm:max-w-md overflow-y-auto">
          <SheetHeader className="mb-4">
            <SheetTitle>Lägg till i specifikation</SheetTitle>
            <SheetDescription>
              Välj en befintlig spec eller skapa en ny med denna post.
            </SheetDescription>
          </SheetHeader>

          {/* Redan tillagd i leveransspecs */}
          {postensSpecar.filter(s => (s as SpecSummary & { typ?: string }).typ !== "hyvling").length > 0 && (
            <div className="mb-6">
              <p className="text-xs font-semibold text-muted-foreground uppercase tracking-wider mb-2">
                Finns redan i
              </p>
              <div className="space-y-2">
                {postensSpecar.filter(s => (s as SpecSummary & { typ?: string }).typ !== "hyvling").map(spec => (
                  <Link key={spec.id} href={`/spec/${spec.id}`} onClick={() => setSpecSheetOpen(false)}>
                    <div className="flex items-center justify-between p-3 rounded-lg border bg-primary/5 hover:bg-primary/10 cursor-pointer">
                      <div>
                        <p className="font-medium text-sm">{spec.kund || `Spec #${(spec as any).specNr ?? spec.id}`}</p>
                        <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">
                          {[spec.trailerNr && `Trailer: ${spec.trailerNr}`, spec.orderNr && `Order: ${spec.orderNr}`].filter(Boolean).join(" · ") || "Inga detaljer"}
                        </p>
                      </div>
                      <ExternalLink className="h-4 w-4 text-muted-foreground shrink-0" />
                    </div>
                  </Link>
                ))}
              </div>
            </div>
          )}

          {/* Öppna specs att välja bland */}
          {(() => {
            const alreadyIds = new Set(postensSpecar.map(s => s.id));
            const oppna = allaSpecar.filter(s => s.status !== "bekraftad" && !alreadyIds.has(s.id) && s.typ !== "hyvling");
            return oppna.length > 0 ? (
              <div className="mb-6">
                <p className="text-xs font-semibold text-muted-foreground uppercase tracking-wider mb-2">
                  Öppna specifikationer
                </p>
                <div className="space-y-2">
                  {oppna.map(spec => (
                    <button
                      key={spec.id}
                      className="w-full flex items-center justify-between p-3 rounded-lg border hover:bg-muted/50 cursor-pointer text-left transition-colors"
                      disabled={laggTillISpecMutation.isPending}
                      onClick={() => laggTillISpecMutation.mutate(spec.id)}
                    >
                      <div>
                        <p className="font-medium text-sm">{spec.kund || `Spec #${(spec as any).specNr ?? spec.id}`}</p>
                        <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">
                          {spec.antalPaket} paket
                          {spec.trailerNr ? ` · Trailer: ${spec.trailerNr}` : ""}
                          {spec.orderNr ? ` · Order: ${spec.orderNr}` : ""}
                        </p>
                      </div>
                      <Plus className="h-4 w-4 text-muted-foreground shrink-0" />
                    </button>
                  ))}
                </div>
              </div>
            ) : null;
          })()}

          {/* Skapa ny spec */}
          <div className="border-t pt-4">
            <p className="text-xs font-semibold text-muted-foreground uppercase tracking-wider mb-3">
              Skapa ny specifikation
            </p>
            <Button
              className="w-full"
              onClick={() => skapaNySpecMutation.mutate()}
              disabled={skapaNySpecMutation.isPending}
            >
              <Plus className="h-4 w-4 mr-2" />
              Ny spec med denna post
            </Button>
            <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground mt-2">
              Öppnar den nya specifikationen direkt.
            </p>
          </div>
        </SheetContent>
      </Sheet>

      {/* Sheet: Lägg till i hyvlingsorder */}
      <Sheet open={hyvlingSheetOpen} onOpenChange={setHyvlingSheetOpen}>
        <SheetContent side="right" className="w-full sm:max-w-md overflow-y-auto">
          <SheetHeader className="mb-4">
            <SheetTitle className="text-orange-700">Lägg till i hyvlingsorder</SheetTitle>
            <SheetDescription>
              Välj en befintlig hyvlingsorder eller skapa en ny med denna post.
            </SheetDescription>
          </SheetHeader>

          {/* Redan tillagd i hyvlingsorder */}
          {postensSpecar.filter(s => (s as SpecSummary & { typ?: string }).typ === "hyvling").length > 0 && (
            <div className="mb-6">
              <p className="text-xs font-semibold text-muted-foreground uppercase tracking-wider mb-2">
                Finns redan i
              </p>
              <div className="space-y-2">
                {postensSpecar.filter(s => (s as SpecSummary & { typ?: string }).typ === "hyvling").map(h => (
                  <Link key={h.id} href={`/hyvling/${h.id}`} onClick={() => setHyvlingSheetOpen(false)}>
                    <div className="flex items-center justify-between p-3 rounded-lg border bg-orange-50 hover:bg-orange-100 cursor-pointer">
                      <div>
                        <p className="font-medium text-sm">{h.kund || `Hyvlingsorder #${h.id}`}</p>
                        <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">
                          {h.status === "bekraftad" ? "Bekräftad" : "Utkast"}
                        </p>
                      </div>
                      <ExternalLink className="h-4 w-4 text-muted-foreground shrink-0" />
                    </div>
                  </Link>
                ))}
              </div>
            </div>
          )}

          {/* Öppna hyvlingsordrar att välja bland */}
          {(() => {
            const alreadyIds = new Set(postensSpecar.filter(s => (s as SpecSummary & { typ?: string }).typ === "hyvling").map(s => s.id));
            const oppna = allaHyvlingar.filter(h => h.status !== "bekraftad" && !alreadyIds.has(h.id));
            return oppna.length > 0 ? (
              <div className="mb-6">
                <p className="text-xs font-semibold text-muted-foreground uppercase tracking-wider mb-2">
                  Öppna hyvlingsordrar
                </p>
                <div className="space-y-2">
                  {oppna.map(h => (
                    <button
                      key={h.id}
                      className="w-full flex items-center justify-between p-3 rounded-lg border hover:bg-orange-50 cursor-pointer text-left transition-colors"
                      disabled={laggTillIHyvlingMutation.isPending}
                      onClick={() => laggTillIHyvlingMutation.mutate(h.id)}
                    >
                      <div>
                        <p className="font-medium text-sm">{h.kund || `Hyvlingsorder #${h.id}`}</p>
                        <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">{h.antalPaket} paket</p>
                      </div>
                      <Plus className="h-4 w-4 text-muted-foreground shrink-0" />
                    </button>
                  ))}
                </div>
              </div>
            ) : null;
          })()}

          {/* Skapa ny hyvlingsorder */}
          <div className="border-t pt-4">
            <p className="text-xs font-semibold text-muted-foreground uppercase tracking-wider mb-3">
              Skapa ny hyvlingsorder
            </p>
            <Button
              className="w-full bg-orange-600 hover:bg-orange-700 text-white"
              onClick={() => skapaNyHyvlingMutation.mutate()}
              disabled={skapaNyHyvlingMutation.isPending}
            >
              <Plus className="h-4 w-4 mr-2" />
              Ny hyvlingsorder med denna post
            </Button>
            <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground mt-2">
              Öppnar den nya hyvlingsordern direkt.
            </p>
          </div>
        </SheetContent>
      </Sheet>
    </div>
  );
}


================ FILE: artifacts/lager/src/pages/inventory/InventoryForm.tsx ================

import { useEffect, useState, useRef } from "react";
import { useLocation, useParams } from "wouter";
import { useForm, useWatch } from "react-hook-form";
import { zodResolver } from "@hookform/resolvers/zod";
import { z } from "zod";
import { format } from "date-fns";
import { 
  useCreateLagerPost,
  useGetLagerPost,
  getGetLagerPostQueryKey,
  useUpdateLagerPost,
  customFetch,
  useSearchVilmabas,
  useListLagerOmraden,
  useGetLagerOmrade,
  getGetLagerOmradeQueryKey,
  type VilmabasArtikel,
  ApiError,
} from "@workspace/api-client-react";
import { useQueryClient, useQuery } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import {
  Form,
  FormControl,
  FormField,
  FormItem,
  FormLabel,
  FormMessage,
  FormDescription,
} from "@/components/ui/form";
import { Input } from "@/components/ui/input";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { Card, CardContent, CardHeader, CardTitle } from "@/components/ui/card";
import { Separator } from "@/components/ui/separator";
import { useToast } from "@/hooks/use-toast";
import { ArrowLeft, Save, Loader2, AlertTriangle, Copy, X, Search, Plus, Trash2, Layers } from "lucide-react";
import { Link } from "wouter";
import { Textarea } from "@/components/ui/textarea";
import { RadioGroup, RadioGroupItem } from "@/components/ui/radio-group";
import { Skeleton } from "@/components/ui/skeleton";
import { SuggestInput } from "@/components/SuggestInput";
import { Select, SelectContent, SelectItem, SelectTrigger, SelectValue } from "@/components/ui/select";
import {
  farSkapaLagerpost,
  foreslaDupliceratPaketnummer,
  type PaketnummerkontrollStatus,
} from "./duplicatePrefill";

function useSuggestions(endpoint: string) {
  const { data = [] } = useQuery<string[]>({
    queryKey: [endpoint],
    queryFn: () => customFetch<string[]>(endpoint),
    staleTime: 5 * 60 * 1000,
  });
  return data;
}

// Custom schema based on CreateLagerPostBody with types mapped to what react-hook-form inputs can easily produce
const formSchema = z.object({
  vilmabasId: z.string().optional().nullable(),
  datumIn: z.string().optional().nullable(),
  datumUt: z.string().optional().nullable(),
  mottaget: z.string().optional().nullable(),
  plats: z.string().optional().nullable(),
  omradeId: z.coerce.number().optional().nullable(),
  fackId: z.coerce.number().optional().nullable(),
  anmarkNing: z.string().optional().nullable(),
  pktNr: z.string().optional().nullable(),
  produktion: z.string().optional().nullable(),
  profil: z.string().optional().nullable(),
  varuslag: z.string().optional().nullable(),
  traslag: z.string().optional().nullable(),
  behandling: z.string().optional().nullable(),
  utforande: z.string().optional().nullable(),
  kvalitet: z.string().optional().nullable(),
  avrapp: z.string().optional().nullable(),
  avrappTyp: z.enum(["hyvlat_till_lager", "utlevererat"]).optional().nullable(),
  nomTjocklek: z.coerce.number().optional().nullable(),
  nomBredd: z.coerce.number().optional().nullable(),
  actTjocklek: z.coerce.number().optional().nullable(),
  actBredd: z.coerce.number().optional().nullable(),
  antal: z.coerce.number().optional().nullable(),
  langdDm: z.coerce.number().optional().nullable(),
  m3: z.coerce.number().optional().nullable(),
  m3Pris: z.coerce.number().optional().nullable(),
  m3Sum: z.coerce.number().optional().nullable(),
  avrappM3: z.coerce.number().optional().nullable(),
  lpm: z.coerce.number().optional().nullable(),
  actM3: z.coerce.number().optional().nullable(),
  anteckning: z.string().optional().nullable(),
  leverantor: z.string().optional().nullable(),
});

type FormValues = z.infer<typeof formSchema>;

export default function InventoryForm() {
  const [, navigate] = useLocation();
  const params = useParams();
  const isEditing = !!params.id && params.id !== "ny";
  const id = isEditing ? parseInt(params.id as string) : null;
  const { toast } = useToast();
  const queryClient = useQueryClient();

  const varuslagSugg = useSuggestions("/api/lager/varuslag");
  const traslagSugg = useSuggestions("/api/lager/traslag");
  const profilSugg = useSuggestions("/api/lager/profiler");
  const platsSugg = useSuggestions("/api/lager/platser");
  const kvalitetSugg = useSuggestions("/api/lager/kvaliteter");
  const leverantorSugg = useSuggestions("/api/lager/leverantorer");
  const tidigareBehandlingar = useSuggestions("/api/lager/behandlingar");
  const behandlingSugg = Array.from(
    new Set(["Impregnerad", "Grundmålad", ...tidigareBehandlingar]),
  );
  const tidigareUtforanden = useSuggestions("/api/lager/utforanden");
  const utforandeSugg = Array.from(
    new Set(["Buntat", "Planhyvlat", "4RH", ...tidigareUtforanden]),
  );
  const { data: omradenList = [] } = useListLagerOmraden();

  const [vbInput, setVbInput] = useState("");
  const [vbOpen, setVbOpen] = useState(false);
  const vbRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
  const { data: vbResults = [] } = useSearchVilmabas(
    { q: vbInput },
    { query: { enabled: vbInput.length >= 2, staleTime: 30_000, queryKey: [`/api/vilmabas`, { q: vbInput }] } }
  );
  useEffect(() => {
    function onClickOutside(e: MouseEvent) {
      if (vbRef.current && !vbRef.current.contains(e.target as Node)) setVbOpen(false);
    }
    document.addEventListener("mousedown", onClickOutside);
    return () => document.removeEventListener("mousedown", onClickOutside);
  }, []);
  function selectVbArtikel(a: VilmabasArtikel) {
    form.setValue("vilmabasId", a.vilmabasId, { shouldDirty: true });
    if (a.traslag) form.setValue("traslag", a.traslag, { shouldDirty: true });
    if (a.tjocklek) form.setValue("nomTjocklek", parseFloat(a.tjocklek as string), { shouldDirty: true });
    if (a.totalBradd) form.setValue("nomBredd", parseFloat(a.totalBradd as string), { shouldDirty: true });
    setVbInput("");
    setVbOpen(false);
  }

  const prefill = (() => {
    try {
      const raw = sessionStorage.getItem("lager_duplicate");
      if (raw) { sessionStorage.removeItem("lager_duplicate"); return JSON.parse(raw); }
    } catch {}
    return null;
  })();

  const form = useForm<FormValues>({
    resolver: zodResolver(formSchema),
    defaultValues: prefill ? {
      ...prefill,
      pktNr: foreslaDupliceratPaketnummer(prefill.pktNr),
      datumIn: format(new Date(), "yyyy-MM-dd"),
      datumUt: null,
    } : {
      datumIn: format(new Date(), "yyyy-MM-dd"),
    }
  });

  const [pktNrWarning, setPktNrWarning] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [pktNrKontrollStatus, setPktNrKontrollStatus] =
    useState<PaketnummerkontrollStatus>("idle");
  const watchedPktNr = useWatch({ control: form.control, name: "pktNr" });
  useEffect(() => {
    const val = (watchedPktNr ?? "").trim();
    if (!val) {
      setPktNrWarning(null);
      setPktNrKontrollStatus("idle");
      return;
    }
    const controller = new AbortController();
    setPktNrWarning(null);
    setPktNrKontrollStatus("checking");
    const timer = setTimeout(async () => {
      try {
        const json = await customFetch<{ data?: { pktNr?: string | null; id?: number }[] }>(
          `/api/lager?search=${encodeURIComponent(val)}&limit=50`,
          { signal: controller.signal },
        );
        const found = (json.data ?? []).find(
          (p: { pktNr?: string | null; id?: number }) =>
            (p.pktNr ?? "").toLowerCase() === val.toLowerCase() && p.id !== id
        );
        setPktNrWarning(found ? `Paketnummer "${val}" finns redan i lagret.` : null);
        setPktNrKontrollStatus(found ? "duplicate" : "available");
      } catch (error) {
        if (controller.signal.aborted) return;
        setPktNrWarning("Paketnumret kunde inte kontrolleras. Försök igen.");
        setPktNrKontrollStatus("error");
      }
    }, 500);
    return () => {
      clearTimeout(timer);
      controller.abort();
    };
  }, [watchedPktNr, id]);

  const { data: post, isLoading: isLoadingPost } = useGetLagerPost(id as number, {
    query: {
      enabled: isEditing && !!id,
      queryKey: getGetLagerPostQueryKey(id as number)
    }
  });

  const watchedOmradeId = useWatch({ control: form.control, name: "omradeId" });
  const selectedOmrade = omradenList.find((omrade) => omrade.id === Number(watchedOmradeId));
  const { data: selectedOmradeDetail, isLoading: isLoadingFack } = useGetLagerOmrade(
    selectedOmrade?.slug ?? "",
    {
      query: {
        enabled: isEditing && !!selectedOmrade?.slug,
        queryKey: getGetLagerOmradeQueryKey(selectedOmrade?.slug ?? ""),
      },
    },
  );

  useEffect(() => {
    if (post && isEditing) {
      form.reset({
        vilmabasId: (post as any).vilmabasId || null,
        datumIn: post.datumIn ? format(new Date(post.datumIn), "yyyy-MM-dd") : "",
        datumUt: post.datumUt ? format(new Date(post.datumUt), "yyyy-MM-dd") : "",
        mottaget: post.mottaget || "",
        plats: post.plats || "",
        omradeId: post.omradeId ?? null,
        fackId: post.fackId ?? null,
        anmarkNing: post.anmarkNing || "",
        pktNr: post.pktNr || "",
        produktion: post.produktion || "",
        profil: post.profil || "",
        varuslag: post.varuslag || "",
        traslag: post.traslag || "",
        behandling: post.behandling || "",
        utforande: post.utforande || "",
        kvalitet: post.kvalitet || "",
        avrapp: post.avrapp || "",
        avrappTyp: (post as any).avrappTyp || null,
        nomTjocklek: post.nomTjocklek,
        nomBredd: post.nomBredd,
        actTjocklek: post.actTjocklek,
        actBredd: post.actBredd,
        antal: post.antal,
        langdDm: post.langdDm,
        m3: post.m3,
        m3Pris: post.m3Pris,
        m3Sum: post.m3Sum,
        avrappM3: post.avrappM3,
        lpm: post.lpm,
        actM3: post.actM3,
        anteckning: post.anteckning || "",
        leverantor: post.leverantor || "",
      });
    }
  }, [post, isEditing, form]);

  const watchedFields = useWatch({
    control: form.control,
    name: ["nomTjocklek", "nomBredd", "actTjocklek", "actBredd", "langdDm", "antal", "m3", "m3Pris"],
  });
  const isHyvlad = !!(watchedFields[2] || watchedFields[3]);

  useEffect(() => {
    const toN = (v: unknown) =>
      v !== undefined && v !== null && v !== "" ? parseFloat(String(v)) : NaN;
    const [nomTjocklek, nomBredd, actTjocklek, actBredd, langdDm, antal, m3, m3Pris] =
      watchedFields.map(toN);

    const hasLen = !isNaN(langdDm) && !isNaN(antal);

    if (hasLen) {
      const calculatedLpm = parseFloat(((langdDm / 10) * antal).toFixed(2));
      if (form.getValues("lpm") !== calculatedLpm)
        form.setValue("lpm", calculatedLpm, { shouldDirty: true });
    }

    if (hasLen && !isNaN(nomTjocklek) && !isNaN(nomBredd)) {
      const calculatedM3 = parseFloat(
        ((nomTjocklek / 1000) * (nomBredd / 1000) * (langdDm / 10) * antal).toFixed(4)
      );
      if (form.getValues("m3") !== calculatedM3)
        form.setValue("m3", calculatedM3, { shouldDirty: true });

      if (!isNaN(m3Pris)) {
        const calculatedSum = parseFloat((calculatedM3 * m3Pris).toFixed(2));
        if (form.getValues("m3Sum") !== calculatedSum)
          form.setValue("m3Sum", calculatedSum, { shouldDirty: true });
      }
    } else if (!isNaN(m3) && !isNaN(m3Pris)) {
      const calculatedSum = parseFloat((m3 * m3Pris).toFixed(2));
      if (form.getValues("m3Sum") !== calculatedSum)
        form.setValue("m3Sum", calculatedSum, { shouldDirty: true });
    }

    if (hasLen && !isNaN(actTjocklek) && !isNaN(actBredd)) {
      const calculatedActM3 = parseFloat(
        ((actTjocklek / 1000) * (actBredd / 1000) * (langdDm / 10) * antal).toFixed(5)
      );
      if (form.getValues("actM3") !== calculatedActM3)
        form.setValue("actM3", calculatedActM3, { shouldDirty: true });
    }
  }, [watchedFields[0], watchedFields[1], watchedFields[2], watchedFields[3], watchedFields[4], watchedFields[5], watchedFields[6], watchedFields[7]]);

  const createMutation = useCreateLagerPost({
    mutation: {
      onSuccess: (data) => {
        toast({
          title: "Lagerpost skapad",
          description: `Post med pkt nr ${data.pktNr || data.id} har skapats.`,
        });
        queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["/api/lager"] });
        queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["/api/lager/statistik"] });
        navigate(`/lager/${data.id}`);
      },
      onError: (error) => {
        toast({
          title: error instanceof ApiError && error.status === 409
            ? "Paketnumret används redan"
            : "Ett fel uppstod",
          description: error instanceof ApiError && error.status === 409
            ? error.message.replace(/^HTTP 409 Conflict:\s*/, "")
            : "Kunde inte skapa lagerposten.",
          variant: "destructive"
        });
      }
    }
  });

  const updateMutation = useUpdateLagerPost({
    mutation: {
      onSuccess: (data) => {
        toast({
          title: "Lagerpost uppdaterad",
          description: `Posten har sparats.`,
        });
        queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: getGetLagerPostQueryKey(id as number) });
        queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["/api/lager"] });
        queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["/api/lager/statistik"] });
        navigate(`/lager/${data.id}`);
      },
      onError: () => {
        toast({
          title: "Ett fel uppstod",
          description: "Kunde inte uppdatera lagerposten.",
          variant: "destructive"
        });
      }
    }
  });

  const onSubmit = (data: FormValues) => {
    if (!isEditing && !farSkapaLagerpost(pktNrKontrollStatus, !!data.pktNr?.trim())) {
      toast({
        title: "Paketnumret måste kontrolleras",
        description: pktNrWarning ?? "Vänta tills kontrollen är färdig.",
        variant: "destructive",
      });
      return;
    }
    // Clean up empty strings to null for numbers and optional fields
    const cleanedData = Object.entries(data).reduce((acc, [key, value]) => {
      acc[key] = value === "" ? null : value;
      return acc;
    }, {} as Record<string, any>);

    if (isEditing && id) {
      updateMutation.mutate({ id, data: cleanedData });
    } else {
      createMutation.mutate({ data: cleanedData });
    }
  };

  const isPending = createMutation.isPending || updateMutation.isPending;

  const [blandadeLangder, setBlandadeLangder] = useState(false);
  const [langdRader, setLangdRader] = useState<{ antal: string; langdDm: string }[]>([
    { antal: "", langdDm: "" },
    { antal: "", langdDm: "" },
  ]);
  const [batchPending, setBatchPending] = useState(false);

  function addLangdRad() {
    setLangdRader(r => [...r, { antal: "", langdDm: "" }]);
  }
  function removeLangdRad(i: number) {
    setLangdRader(r => r.filter((_, idx) => idx !== i));
  }
  function updateLangdRad(i: number, field: "antal" | "langdDm", val: string) {
    setLangdRader(r => r.map((row, idx) => idx === i ? { ...row, [field]: val } : row));
  }

  function calcRowValues(langdDm: number, antal: number, nomTjocklek: number | null | undefined, nomBredd: number | null | undefined, actTjocklek: number | null | undefined, actBredd: number | null | undefined) {
    const lpm = parseFloat(((langdDm / 10) * antal).toFixed(2));
    const m3 = (nomTjocklek && nomBredd)
      ? parseFloat(((nomTjocklek / 1000) * (nomBredd / 1000) * (langdDm / 10) * antal).toFixed(4))
      : null;
    const actM3 = (actTjocklek && actBredd)
      ? parseFloat(((actTjocklek / 1000) * (actBredd / 1000) * (langdDm / 10) * antal).toFixed(5))
      : null;
    return { lpm, m3, actM3 };
  }

  async function onSubmitBatch(data: FormValues) {
    if (!farSkapaLagerpost(pktNrKontrollStatus, !!data.pktNr?.trim())) {
      toast({
        title: "Paketnumret måste kontrolleras",
        description: pktNrWarning ?? "Vänta tills kontrollen är färdig.",
        variant: "destructive",
      });
      return;
    }
    const fylldaRader = langdRader.filter(r => r.antal.trim() && r.langdDm.trim());
    if (fylldaRader.length === 0) {
      toast({ title: "Inga längdrader ifyllda", description: "Fyll i minst en rad med antal och längd.", variant: "destructive" });
      return;
    }
    const cleaned = Object.entries(data).reduce((acc, [key, value]) => {
      acc[key] = value === "" ? null : value;
      return acc;
    }, {} as Record<string, unknown>);

    setBatchPending(true);
    const created: number[] = [];
    try {
      const poster = fylldaRader.map((rad) => {
        const antal = parseFloat(rad.antal);
        const langdDm = parseFloat(rad.langdDm.replace(",", "."));
        const rowCalc = calcRowValues(langdDm, antal, data.nomTjocklek, data.nomBredd, data.actTjocklek, data.actBredd);
        return { ...cleaned, antal, langdDm, lpm: rowCalc.lpm, m3: rowCalc.m3, actM3: rowCalc.actM3 };
      });
      const result = await customFetch<{ id: number }[]>("/api/lager/manual-batch", {
        method: "POST",
        body: JSON.stringify({ poster }),
      });
      created.push(...result.map((row) => row.id));
      queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["/api/lager"] });
      queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["/api/lager/statistik"] });
      toast({ title: `${created.length} lagerposter skapade`, description: `Paketnummer ${data.pktNr || created[0]}` });
      navigate(created.length === 1 ? `/lager/${created[0]}` : "/lager");
    } catch (error) {
      toast({
        title: error instanceof ApiError && error.status === 409 ? "Paketnumret används redan" : "Fel vid skapande",
        description: error instanceof ApiError && error.status === 409
          ? error.message.replace(/^HTTP 409 Conflict:\s*/, "")
          : "Längdraderna kunde inte skapas.",
        variant: "destructive",
      });
    } finally {
      setBatchPending(false);
    }
  }

  if (isEditing && isLoadingPost) {
    return (
      <div className="space-y-6 max-w-4xl mx-auto">
        <Skeleton className="h-10 w-48" />
        <Card>
          <CardContent className="p-6">
            <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-4">
              <Skeleton className="h-12 w-full" />
              <Skeleton className="h-12 w-full" />
              <Skeleton className="h-12 w-full" />
              <Skeleton className="h-12 w-full" />
            </div>
          </CardContent>
        </Card>
      </div>
    );
  }

  return (
    <div className="space-y-6 max-w-4xl mx-auto pb-12">
      <div className="flex items-center gap-4">
        <Button variant="outline" size="icon" asChild>
          <Link href={isEditing ? `/lager/${id}` : "/lager"}>
            <ArrowLeft className="h-4 w-4" />
          </Link>
        </Button>
        <div>
          <h1 className="text-3xl font-bold tracking-tight">
            {isEditing ? `Redigera post: ${post?.pktNr || id}` : "Ny Lagerpost"}
          </h1>
          <p className="text-muted-foreground">
            Fyll i uppgifter för virkesposten nedan.
          </p>
        </div>
      </div>

      <Form {...form}>
        <form onSubmit={form.handleSubmit(onSubmit)} className="space-y-6">
          <Card>
            <CardHeader>
              <CardTitle className="text-lg">Identifikation & Datum</CardTitle>
            </CardHeader>
            <CardContent className="grid gap-4 md:grid-cols-2">
              <FormField
                control={form.control}
                name="pktNr"
                render={({ field }) => (
                  <FormItem>
                    <FormLabel>Paketnummer</FormLabel>
                    <FormControl>
                      <Input {...field} value={field.value || ""} />
                    </FormControl>
                    {pktNrWarning && (
                      <p className={`flex items-center gap-1.5 text-xs font-medium mt-1 ${pktNrKontrollStatus === "error" ? "text-destructive" : "text-amber-600"}`}>
                        <AlertTriangle className="h-3.5 w-3.5 shrink-0" />
                        {pktNrWarning}
                      </p>
                    )}
                    {pktNrKontrollStatus === "checking" && (
                      <p className="flex items-center gap-1.5 text-xs text-muted-foreground mt-1">
                        <Loader2 className="h-3.5 w-3.5 shrink-0 animate-spin" />
                        Kontrollerar paketnumret…
                      </p>
                    )}
                    <FormMessage />
                  </FormItem>
                )}
              />
              <FormField
                control={form.control}
                name="plats"
                render={({ field }) => (
                  <FormItem>
                    <FormLabel>{isEditing && watchedOmradeId != null ? "Lagerplats (strukturerad)" : "Lagerplats"}</FormLabel>
                    <FormControl>
                      <SuggestInput
                        {...field}
                        value={field.value || ""}
                        suggestions={platsSugg}
                        disabled={isEditing && watchedOmradeId != null}
                      />
                    </FormControl>
                    {isEditing && watchedOmradeId != null && (
                      <FormDescription>Fylls automatiskt från valt område.</FormDescription>
                    )}
                    <FormMessage />
                  </FormItem>
                )}
              />
              <FormField
                control={form.control}
                name="datumIn"
                render={({ field }) => (
                  <FormItem>
                    <FormLabel>Datum Inkommit</FormLabel>
                    <FormControl>
                      <Input type="date" {...field} value={field.value || ""} />
                    </FormControl>
                    <FormMessage />
                  </FormItem>
                )}
              />
              <FormField
                control={form.control}
                name="datumUt"
                render={({ field }) => (
                  <FormItem>
                    <FormLabel>Datum Utlevererat (Lämna tomt för aktiv i lager)</FormLabel>
                    <FormControl>
                      <Input type="date" {...field} value={field.value || ""} />
                    </FormControl>
                    <FormMessage />
                  </FormItem>
                )}
              />
              <FormField
                control={form.control}
                name="mottaget"
                render={({ field }) => (
                  <FormItem>
                    <FormLabel>Mottaget från / Avsändare</FormLabel>
                    <FormControl>
                      <Input {...field} value={field.value || ""} />
                    </FormControl>
                    <FormMessage />
                  </FormItem>
                )}
              />
              <FormField
                control={form.control}
                name="leverantor"
                render={({ field }) => (
                  <FormItem>
                    <FormLabel>Leverantör / Sågverk</FormLabel>
                    <FormControl>
                      <SuggestInput {...field} value={field.value || ""} suggestions={leverantorSugg} placeholder="t.ex. Södra, Setra, ..." />
                    </FormControl>
                    <FormMessage />
                  </FormItem>
                )}
              />
              <FormField
                control={form.control}
                name="produktion"
                render={({ field }) => (
                  <FormItem>
                    <FormLabel>Produktion</FormLabel>
                    <FormControl>
                      <Input {...field} value={field.value || ""} />
                    </FormControl>
                    <FormMessage />
                  </FormItem>
                )}
              />
            </CardContent>
          </Card>

          {isEditing && (
            <Card>
              <CardHeader>
                <CardTitle className="text-lg">Strukturerad lagerplats</CardTitle>
              </CardHeader>
              <CardContent className="grid gap-4 md:grid-cols-2">
                <FormField
                  control={form.control}
                  name="omradeId"
                  render={({ field }) => (
                    <FormItem>
                      <FormLabel>Område</FormLabel>
                      <Select
                        value={field.value == null ? "none" : String(field.value)}
                        onValueChange={(value) => {
                          field.onChange(value === "none" ? null : Number(value));
                          form.setValue("fackId", null, { shouldDirty: true });
                        }}
                      >
                        <FormControl>
                          <SelectTrigger>
                            <SelectValue placeholder="Välj område" />
                          </SelectTrigger>
                        </FormControl>
                        <SelectContent className="inventory-location-select-content">
                          <SelectItem value="none">Ingen strukturerad placering</SelectItem>
                          {omradenList.map((omrade) => (
                            <SelectItem key={omrade.id} value={String(omrade.id)}>
                              {omrade.namn}
                            </SelectItem>
                          ))}
                        </SelectContent>
                      </Select>
                      <FormDescription>Välj område innan du väljer fack.</FormDescription>
                      <FormMessage />
                    </FormItem>
                  )}
                />
                <FormField
                  control={form.control}
                  name="fackId"
                  render={({ field }) => (
                    <FormItem>
                      <FormLabel>Fack</FormLabel>
                      <Select
                        value={field.value == null ? "none" : String(field.value)}
                        onValueChange={(value) => field.onChange(value === "none" ? null : Number(value))}
                        disabled={!selectedOmrade || isLoadingFack}
                      >
                        <FormControl>
                          <SelectTrigger>
                            <SelectValue placeholder={isLoadingFack ? "Laddar fack…" : "Välj fack"} />
                          </SelectTrigger>
                        </FormControl>
                        <SelectContent className="inventory-location-select-content">
                          <SelectItem value="none">Inget fack</SelectItem>
                          {(selectedOmradeDetail?.fack ?? []).map((fack) => (
                            <SelectItem key={fack.id} value={String(fack.id)}>
                              {fack.kod}
                            </SelectItem>
                          ))}
                        </SelectContent>
                      </Select>
                      <FormDescription>
                        {selectedOmrade ? `Visar endast fack i ${selectedOmrade.namn}.` : "Välj ett område först."}
                      </FormDescription>
                      <FormMessage />
                    </FormItem>
                  )}
                />
              </CardContent>
            </Card>
          )}

          <Card>
            <CardHeader>
              <CardTitle className="text-lg">Specifikation</CardTitle>
            </CardHeader>
            <CardContent className="grid gap-4 md:grid-cols-3">
              {/* VilmaBas VB-nummer field — visas bara för hyvlade paket (act-dim ifyllda) */}
              {isHyvlad && <div className="md:col-span-3">
                <FormField
                  control={form.control}
                  name="vilmabasId"
                  render={({ field }) => (
                    <FormItem>
                      <FormLabel>VB-nummer <span className="text-xs font-normal text-muted-foreground">(frivilligt — kopplar till VilmaBas-artikel)</span></FormLabel>
                      <FormControl>
                        <div ref={vbRef} className="relative">
                          {field.value ? (
                            <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                              <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 rounded-md border bg-muted px-3 py-1.5 text-sm font-mono">
                                <span className="text-primary font-semibold">{field.value}</span>
                                <button
                                  type="button"
                                  onClick={() => field.onChange(null)}
                                  className="text-muted-foreground hover:text-foreground ml-1"
                                >
                                  <X className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
                                </button>
                              </div>
                              <span className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">Klicka på ✕ för att byta artikel</span>
                            </div>
                          ) : (
                            <>
                              <div className="relative">
                                <Search className="absolute left-2.5 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 h-4 w-4 text-muted-foreground pointer-events-none" />
                                <Input
