                                  placeholder="Sök VB-nr, namn, dimension eller träslag…"
                                  value={vbInput}
                                  onChange={e => { setVbInput(e.target.value); setVbOpen(true); }}
                                  onFocus={() => { if (vbInput.length >= 2) setVbOpen(true); }}
                                  className="pl-8"
                                />
                              </div>
                              {vbOpen && vbResults.length > 0 && (
                                <div className="absolute z-50 left-0 right-0 mt-1 rounded-md border bg-popover shadow-md max-h-60 overflow-y-auto">
                                  {vbResults.map(a => (
                                    <button
                                      key={a.id}
                                      type="button"
                                      onMouseDown={e => { e.preventDefault(); selectVbArtikel(a); }}
                                      className="w-full flex items-start gap-3 px-3 py-2 text-sm hover:bg-accent text-left"
                                    >
                                      <span className="font-mono font-semibold text-primary shrink-0 w-16">{a.vilmabasId}</span>
                                      <span className="flex-1 text-foreground">{a.namn}</span>
                                      <span className="text-muted-foreground shrink-0">
                                        {a.tjocklek && a.totalBradd ? `${a.tjocklek}×${a.totalBradd}` : ""}
                                        {a.traslag ? ` · ${a.traslag}` : ""}
                                      </span>
                                    </button>
                                  ))}
                                </div>
                              )}
                            </>
                          )}
                        </div>
                      </FormControl>
                      <FormMessage />
                    </FormItem>
                  )}
                />
              </div>}
              <FormField
                control={form.control}
                name="varuslag"
                render={({ field }) => (
                  <FormItem>
                    <FormLabel>Varuslag</FormLabel>
                    <FormControl>
                      <SuggestInput {...field} value={field.value || ""} suggestions={varuslagSugg} placeholder="t.ex. regel, klädsel" />
                    </FormControl>
                    <FormMessage />
                  </FormItem>
                )}
              />
              <FormField
                control={form.control}
                name="traslag"
                render={({ field }) => (
                  <FormItem>
                    <FormLabel>Träslag</FormLabel>
                    <FormControl>
                      <SuggestInput {...field} value={field.value || ""} suggestions={traslagSugg} placeholder="t.ex. Gran, Furu" />
                    </FormControl>
                    <FormMessage />
                  </FormItem>
                )}
              />
              <FormField
                control={form.control}
                name="behandling"
                render={({ field }) => (
                  <FormItem>
                    <FormLabel>Behandling</FormLabel>
                    <FormControl>
                      <SuggestInput
                        {...field}
                        value={field.value || ""}
                        suggestions={behandlingSugg}
                        placeholder="t.ex. Impregnerad, Grundmålad"
                      />
                    </FormControl>
                    <FormDescription>Frivilligt. Lämna tomt om ingen behandling är angiven.</FormDescription>
                    <FormMessage />
                  </FormItem>
                )}
              />
              <FormField
                control={form.control}
                name="utforande"
                render={({ field }) => (
                  <FormItem>
                    <FormLabel>Utförande</FormLabel>
                    <FormControl>
                      <SuggestInput
                        {...field}
                        value={field.value || ""}
                        suggestions={utforandeSugg}
                        placeholder="t.ex. Buntat, Planhyvlat, 4RH"
                      />
                    </FormControl>
                    <FormDescription>Frivilligt. Lämna tomt om inget utförande är angivet.</FormDescription>
                    <FormMessage />
                  </FormItem>
                )}
              />
              <FormField
                control={form.control}
                name="kvalitet"
                render={({ field }) => (
                  <FormItem>
                    <FormLabel>Kvalitet</FormLabel>
                    <FormControl>
                      <SuggestInput {...field} value={field.value || ""} suggestions={kvalitetSugg} />
                    </FormControl>
                    <FormMessage />
                  </FormItem>
                )}
              />
              <FormField
                control={form.control}
                name="profil"
                render={({ field }) => (
                  <FormItem>
                    <FormLabel>Profil</FormLabel>
                    <FormControl>
                      <SuggestInput {...field} value={field.value || ""} suggestions={profilSugg} />
                    </FormControl>
                    <FormMessage />
                  </FormItem>
                )}
              />
              <FormField
                control={form.control}
                name="anmarkNing"
                render={({ field }) => (
                  <FormItem className="col-span-1 md:col-span-2">
                    <FormLabel>Artikel/Anmärkning</FormLabel>
                    <FormControl>
                      <Input {...field} value={field.value || ""} />
                    </FormControl>
                    <FormMessage />
                  </FormItem>
                )}
              />
            </CardContent>
          </Card>

          <Card>
            <CardHeader>
              <CardTitle className="text-lg">Dimensioner & Volym</CardTitle>
            </CardHeader>
            <CardContent>
              <div className="grid gap-4 md:grid-cols-4 mb-4">
                <FormField
                  control={form.control}
                  name="nomTjocklek"
                  render={({ field }) => (
                    <FormItem>
                      <FormLabel>Nom. Tjocklek (mm)</FormLabel>
                      <FormControl>
                        <Input type="number" step="0.1" {...field} value={field.value || ""} />
                      </FormControl>
                      <FormMessage />
                    </FormItem>
                  )}
                />
                <FormField
                  control={form.control}
                  name="nomBredd"
                  render={({ field }) => (
                    <FormItem>
                      <FormLabel>Nom. Bredd (mm)</FormLabel>
                      <FormControl>
                        <Input type="number" step="0.1" {...field} value={field.value || ""} />
                      </FormControl>
                      <FormMessage />
                    </FormItem>
                  )}
                />
                <FormField
                  control={form.control}
                  name="actTjocklek"
                  render={({ field }) => (
                    <FormItem>
                      <FormLabel>Akt. Tjocklek (mm)</FormLabel>
                      <FormControl>
                        <Input type="number" step="0.1" {...field} value={field.value || ""} />
                      </FormControl>
                      <FormMessage />
                    </FormItem>
                  )}
                />
                <FormField
                  control={form.control}
                  name="actBredd"
                  render={({ field }) => (
                    <FormItem>
                      <FormLabel>Akt. Bredd (mm)</FormLabel>
                      <FormControl>
                        <Input type="number" step="0.1" {...field} value={field.value || ""} />
                      </FormControl>
                      <FormMessage />
                    </FormItem>
                  )}
                />
              </div>

              <Separator className="my-4" />

              {!isEditing && (
                <div className="flex items-center gap-3 mb-2">
                  <button
                    type="button"
                    onClick={() => setBlandadeLangder(v => !v)}
                    className={`inline-flex items-center gap-2 rounded-md border px-3 py-1.5 text-sm font-medium transition-colors ${blandadeLangder ? "bg-green-700 text-white border-green-700" : "bg-background text-foreground border-border hover:bg-muted"}`}
                  >
                    <Layers className="h-4 w-4" />
                    Blandade längder
                  </button>
                  {blandadeLangder && <span className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">Fyll i antal och längd per rad — skapar en lagerpost per rad med samma paketnummer.</span>}
                </div>
              )}

              {blandadeLangder && !isEditing ? (
                <div className="space-y-2">
                  <div className="grid grid-cols-[1fr_1fr_auto] gap-2 text-xs font-medium text-muted-foreground px-1">
                    <span>Antal (st)</span>
                    <span>Längd (dm)</span>
                    <span />
                  </div>
                  {langdRader.map((rad, i) => (
                    <div key={i} className="grid grid-cols-[1fr_1fr_auto] gap-2 items-center">
                      <Input
                        type="number"
                        placeholder="t.ex. 34"
                        value={rad.antal}
                        onChange={e => updateLangdRad(i, "antal", e.target.value)}
                      />
                      <Input
                        type="number"
                        step="0.1"
                        placeholder="t.ex. 42"
                        value={rad.langdDm}
                        onChange={e => updateLangdRad(i, "langdDm", e.target.value)}
                      />
                      <Button type="button" variant="ghost" size="icon" className="h-9 w-9 text-muted-foreground hover:text-destructive" onClick={() => removeLangdRad(i)} disabled={langdRader.length <= 1}>
                        <Trash2 className="h-4 w-4" />
                      </Button>
                    </div>
                  ))}
                  <Button type="button" variant="outline" size="sm" className="gap-1.5 mt-1" onClick={addLangdRad}>
                    <Plus className="h-3.5 w-3.5" /> Lägg till rad
                  </Button>
                </div>
              ) : (
              <div className="grid gap-4 md:grid-cols-4">
                <FormField
                  control={form.control}
                  name="antal"
                  render={({ field }) => (
                    <FormItem>
                      <FormLabel>Antal st</FormLabel>
                      <FormControl>
                        <Input type="number" {...field} value={field.value || ""} />
                      </FormControl>
                      <FormMessage />
                    </FormItem>
                  )}
                />
                <FormField
                  control={form.control}
                  name="langdDm"
                  render={({ field }) => (
                    <FormItem>
                      <FormLabel>Längd (dm)</FormLabel>
                      <FormControl>
                        <Input type="number" step="0.1" {...field} value={field.value || ""} />
                      </FormControl>
                      <FormMessage />
                    </FormItem>
                  )}
                />
                <FormField
                  control={form.control}
                  name="lpm"
                  render={({ field }) => (
                    <FormItem>
                      <FormLabel>Löpmeter <span className="text-xs text-muted-foreground font-normal">(auto)</span></FormLabel>
                      <FormControl>
                        <Input type="number" step="0.01" {...field} value={field.value ?? ""} className="bg-muted/40" />
                      </FormControl>
                      <FormMessage />
                    </FormItem>
                  )}
                />
                <FormField
                  control={form.control}
                  name="m3"
                  render={({ field }) => (
                    <FormItem>
                      <FormLabel>Volym (m³) <span className="text-xs text-muted-foreground font-normal">(auto)</span></FormLabel>
                      <FormControl>
                        <Input type="number" step="0.0001" {...field} value={field.value ?? ""} className="bg-muted/40" />
                      </FormControl>
                      <FormMessage />
                    </FormItem>
                  )}
                />
                <FormField
                  control={form.control}
                  name="actM3"
                  render={({ field }) => (
                    <FormItem>
                      <FormLabel>Akt. Volym (m³) <span className="text-xs text-muted-foreground font-normal">(auto)</span></FormLabel>
                      <FormControl>
                        <Input type="number" step="0.00001" {...field} value={field.value ?? ""} className="bg-muted/40" />
                      </FormControl>
                      <FormMessage />
                    </FormItem>
                  )}
                />
                <FormField
                  control={form.control}
                  name="m3Pris"
                  render={({ field }) => (
                    <FormItem>
                      <FormLabel>Pris per m³</FormLabel>
                      <FormControl>
                        <Input type="number" step="0.01" {...field} value={field.value || ""} />
                      </FormControl>
                      <FormMessage />
                    </FormItem>
                  )}
                />
                <FormField
                  control={form.control}
                  name="m3Sum"
                  render={({ field }) => (
                    <FormItem>
                      <FormLabel>Total summa <span className="text-xs text-muted-foreground font-normal">(auto)</span></FormLabel>
                      <FormControl>
                        <Input type="number" step="0.01" {...field} value={field.value ?? ""} className="bg-muted/40" />
                      </FormControl>
                      <FormMessage />
                    </FormItem>
                  )}
                />
              </div>
              )}
            </CardContent>
          </Card>

          <Card>
            <CardHeader>
              <CardTitle className="text-lg">Avrapportering & Övrigt</CardTitle>
            </CardHeader>
            <CardContent className="grid gap-4">
              <FormField
                control={form.control}
                name="avrappTyp"
                render={({ field }) => (
                  <FormItem>
                    <FormLabel>Typ av avrapportering</FormLabel>
                    <FormControl>
                      <RadioGroup
                        value={field.value ?? ""}
                        onValueChange={v => field.onChange(v || null)}
                        className="flex gap-6 pt-1"
                      >
                        <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                          <RadioGroupItem value="hyvlat_till_lager" id="avrapp-hyvlat" />
                          <label htmlFor="avrapp-hyvlat" className="text-sm cursor-pointer">Hyvlas till lager</label>
                        </div>
                        <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                          <RadioGroupItem value="utlevererat" id="avrapp-utlev" />
                          <label htmlFor="avrapp-utlev" className="text-sm cursor-pointer">Utlevererat till kund</label>
                        </div>
                      </RadioGroup>
                    </FormControl>
                    <FormMessage />
                  </FormItem>
                )}
              />
              <div className="grid gap-4 md:grid-cols-2">
                <FormField
                  control={form.control}
                  name="avrapp"
                  render={({ field }) => (
                    <FormItem>
                      <FormLabel>Avrapportering / Projekt</FormLabel>
                      <FormControl>
                        <Input {...field} value={field.value || ""} />
                      </FormControl>
                      <FormMessage />
                    </FormItem>
                  )}
                />
                <FormField
                  control={form.control}
                  name="avrappM3"
                  render={({ field }) => (
                    <FormItem>
                      <FormLabel>Avräknad Volym (m³)</FormLabel>
                      <FormControl>
                        <Input type="number" step="0.001" {...field} value={field.value || ""} />
                      </FormControl>
                      <FormMessage />
                    </FormItem>
                  )}
                />
              </div>
              <FormField
                control={form.control}
                name="anteckning"
                render={({ field }) => (
                  <FormItem>
                    <FormLabel>Fritext Anteckning</FormLabel>
                    <FormControl>
                      <Textarea {...field} value={field.value || ""} className="min-h-[100px]" />
                    </FormControl>
                    <FormMessage />
                  </FormItem>
                )}
              />
            </CardContent>
          </Card>

          <div className="flex justify-end gap-4 pt-4">
            <Button variant="outline" type="button" onClick={() => navigate(isEditing ? `/lager/${id}` : "/lager")}>
              Avbryt
            </Button>
            {blandadeLangder && !isEditing ? (
              <Button
                type="button"
                disabled={batchPending || !farSkapaLagerpost(pktNrKontrollStatus, !!watchedPktNr?.trim())}
                onClick={form.handleSubmit(onSubmitBatch)}
              >
                {batchPending ? <Loader2 className="mr-2 h-4 w-4 animate-spin" /> : <Save className="mr-2 h-4 w-4" />}
                Skapa {langdRader.filter(r => r.antal && r.langdDm).length || ""} lagerposter
              </Button>
            ) : (
              <Button
                type="submit"
                disabled={
                  isPending ||
                  (!isEditing && !farSkapaLagerpost(pktNrKontrollStatus, !!watchedPktNr?.trim()))
                }
              >
                {isPending ? <Loader2 className="mr-2 h-4 w-4 animate-spin" /> : <Save className="mr-2 h-4 w-4" />}
                {isEditing ? "Spara ändringar" : "Skapa lagerpost"}
              </Button>
            )}
          </div>
        </form>
      </Form>
    </div>
  );
}
