type SortKey = "pktNr" | "datumIn" | "profil" | "traslag" | "behandling" | "utforande" | "kvalitet" | "nomTjocklek" | "actTjocklek" | "langdDm" | "antal" | "m3" | "plats";
type SortDir = "asc" | "desc";
type SpecRef = { specId: number; kund: string | null; status: string };

function SortableHead({ label, col, sortKey, sortDir, sortKey2, onSort, className }: {
  label: string;
  col: SortKey;
  sortKey: SortKey | null;
  sortDir: SortDir;
  sortKey2?: SortKey | null;
  onSort: (col: SortKey) => void;
  className?: string;
}) {
  const active = sortKey === col;
  const isSecondary = !active && sortKey2 === col;
  const isRight = className?.includes("text-right");
  const icon = active
    ? (sortDir === "asc"
      ? <ArrowUp className="h-3 w-3 text-primary shrink-0" />
      : <ArrowDown className="h-3 w-3 text-primary shrink-0" />)
    : isSecondary
      ? <span className="text-[10px] font-bold text-muted-foreground shrink-0 leading-none">2</span>
      : <ArrowUpDown className="h-3 w-3 opacity-0 group-hover:opacity-40 transition-opacity shrink-0" />;
  return (
    <TableHead className={className}>
      <button
        onClick={() => onSort(col)}
        className={`flex items-center gap-1 hover:text-foreground transition-colors group w-full${isRight ? " justify-end" : ""}${active ? " text-primary font-medium" : ""}${isSecondary ? " text-muted-foreground" : ""}`}
      >
        {isRight ? <>{icon}<span className="text-right">{label}</span></> : <><span>{label}</span>{icon}</>}
      </button>
    </TableHead>
  );
}

function compareVal(a: unknown, b: unknown, dir: SortDir): number {
  if (a === null || a === undefined || a === "") return 1;
  if (b === null || b === undefined || b === "") return -1;
  const aNum = Number(a);
  const bNum = Number(b);
  const cmp = !isNaN(aNum) && !isNaN(bNum)
    ? aNum - bNum
    : String(a).localeCompare(String(b), "sv", { sensitivity: "base" });
  return dir === "asc" ? cmp : -cmp;
}

function kortBehandling(behandling: string | null | undefined): string | null {
  const value = behandling?.trim();
  if (!value) return null;
  return value.toLocaleLowerCase("sv-SE") === "impregnerad" ? "Imp" : value;
}

export default function InventoryList() {
  const branding = useBranding();
  const rawSearch = useSearch();
  const urlParams = new URLSearchParams(rawSearch);
  const initPlats = urlParams.get("plats") ?? "";
  // Strukturerad filtrering (FK) från områdesdetaljkartan — additivt till plats-filtret ovan.
  // omradeNamn/fackKod är bara för visning (skickas med i länken, sparar en extra API-anrop).
  const omradeIdParam = urlParams.get("omradeId");
  const fackIdParam = urlParams.get("fackId");
  const omradeNamnParam = urlParams.get("omradeNamn");
  const fackKodParam = urlParams.get("fackKod");
  const structuredOmradeId = omradeIdParam ? Number(omradeIdParam) : undefined;
  const structuredFackId = fackIdParam ?? undefined;

  const STORAGE_KEY = "inventoryList_filters";
  const savedFilters = (() => {
    try { return JSON.parse(sessionStorage.getItem(STORAGE_KEY) ?? "{}"); } catch { return {}; }
  })();

  const [search, setSearch] = useState<string>(savedFilters.search ?? "");
  const debouncedSearch = useDebounce(search, 300);
  const [status, setStatus] = useState<"i_lager" | "utlevererat" | "alla">(savedFilters.status ?? "i_lager");
  const [plats, setPlats] = useState<string>(initPlats || savedFilters.plats || "");
  const [traslag, setTraslag] = useState<string>(savedFilters.traslag ?? "");
  const [scanOpen, setScanOpen] = useState(false);
  const [sortKey, setSortKey] = useState<SortKey | null>(savedFilters.sortKey ?? null);
  const [sortDir, setSortDir] = useState<SortDir>(savedFilters.sortDir ?? "asc");
  const [sortKey2, setSortKey2] = useState<SortKey | null>(savedFilters.sortKey2 ?? null);

  useEffect(() => {
    sessionStorage.setItem(STORAGE_KEY, JSON.stringify({ search, status, plats, traslag, sortKey, sortDir, sortKey2 }));
  }, [search, status, plats, traslag, sortKey, sortDir, sortKey2]);
  const [syncing, setSyncing] = useState(false);
  const { toast } = useToast();
  const queryClient = useQueryClient();
  const [, navigate] = useLocation();

  const [selectedIds, setSelectedIds] = useState<Set<number>>(new Set());
  const [batchPlatsDialog, setBatchPlatsDialog] = useState(false);
  const [batchPlatsValue, setBatchPlatsValue] = useState("");
  // Ändra plats: "fritext" = klassiskt fritextfält, annars ett omradeId =
  // strukturerad Område → Fack-placering (t.ex. Röda Magasin → V01).
  const [batchPlatsMode, setBatchPlatsMode] = useState<"fritext" | number>("fritext");
  const [batchFackId, setBatchFackId] = useState<string>("none");
  const [laggTillISpecDialog, setLaggTillISpecDialog] = useState(false);
  const [selectedSpecId, setSelectedSpecId] = useState<string>("");
  const [laggTillIHyvlingDialog, setLaggTillIHyvlingDialog] = useState(false);
  const [selectedHyvlingId, setSelectedHyvlingId] = useState<string>("");
  const [batchLoading, setBatchLoading] = useState(false);
  const [confirmUtleverera, setConfirmUtleverera] = useState(false);
  const [confirmDelete, setConfirmDelete] = useState(false);

  const createSpec = async () => {
    setBatchLoading(true);
    try {
      const spec = await customFetch<{ id: number }>("/api/spec", {
        method: "POST",
        headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
        body: JSON.stringify({ paketIds: Array.from(selectedIds) }),
      });
      navigate(`/spec/${spec.id}`);
    } catch {
      toast({ title: "Kunde inte skapa spec", variant: "destructive" });
    } finally {
      setBatchLoading(false);
    }
  };

  const handleLaggTillISpec = async () => {
    if (!selectedSpecId) return;
    setBatchLoading(true);
    try {
      const data = await customFetch<{ tillagda: number; duplicates: number }>(`/api/spec/${selectedSpecId}/paket/bulk`, {
        method: "POST",
        headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
        body: JSON.stringify({ lagerIds: Array.from(selectedIds) }),
      });
      setLaggTillISpecDialog(false);
      setSelectedSpecId("");
      clearSelection();
      toast({ title: `${data.tillagda} paket tillagda i spec${data.duplicates > 0 ? ` (${data.duplicates} redan inlagda)` : ""}` });
    } catch {
      toast({ title: "Kunde inte lägga till i spec", variant: "destructive" });
    } finally {
      setBatchLoading(false);
    }
  };

  const createHyvling = async () => {
    setBatchLoading(true);
    try {
      const spec = await customFetch<{ id: number }>("/api/spec", {
        method: "POST",
        body: JSON.stringify({ typ: "hyvling", paketIds: Array.from(selectedIds) }),
        headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
      });
      if (!spec?.id) throw new Error("Ogiltigt svar från servern");
      navigate(`/hyvling/${spec.id}`);
    } catch {
      toast({ title: "Kunde inte skapa hyvlingsorder", variant: "destructive" });
    } finally {
      setBatchLoading(false);
    }
  };

  const handleLaggTillIHyvling = async () => {
    if (!selectedHyvlingId) return;
    setBatchLoading(true);
    try {
      const data = await customFetch<{ tillagda: number; duplicates: number }>(`/api/spec/${selectedHyvlingId}/paket/bulk`, {
        method: "POST",
        headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
        body: JSON.stringify({ lagerIds: Array.from(selectedIds) }),
      });
      setLaggTillIHyvlingDialog(false);
      setSelectedHyvlingId("");
      clearSelection();
      toast({ title: `${data.tillagda} paket tillagda i hyvlingsorder${data.duplicates > 0 ? ` (${data.duplicates} redan inlagda)` : ""}` });
    } catch {
      toast({ title: "Kunde inte lägga till i hyvlingsorder", variant: "destructive" });
    } finally {
      setBatchLoading(false);
    }
  };

  const activePlats = plats && plats !== "alla_platser" ? plats : undefined;

  const listQueryParams = {
    search: debouncedSearch || undefined,
    status,
    plats: activePlats,
    omradeId: structuredOmradeId,
    fackId: structuredFackId,
    limit: 3000,
  };

  const { data: listResponse, isLoading } = useListLagerPoster(listQueryParams, {
    query: { queryKey: getListLagerPosterQueryKey(listQueryParams) },
  });

  const { data: platser = [] } = useGetLagerPlatser({ query: { queryKey: getGetLagerPlatserQueryKey() } });

  // Data för "Ändra plats"-dialogens strukturerade läge (Område → Fack).
  const { data: omradenList = [] } = useListLagerOmraden();
  const selectedOmrade = typeof batchPlatsMode === "number"
    ? omradenList.find(o => o.id === batchPlatsMode)
    : undefined;
  const { data: selectedOmradeDetail } = useGetLagerOmrade(selectedOmrade?.slug ?? "");

  const { data: specList = [] } = useQuery<{ id: number; kund: string | null; orderNr: string | null; trailerNr: string | null; antalPaket: number; status: string }[]>({
    queryKey: ["/api/spec", "leverans"],
    queryFn: () => customFetch<{ id: number; kund: string | null; orderNr: string | null; trailerNr: string | null; antalPaket: number; status: string }[]>("/api/spec?typ=leverans"),
    enabled: laggTillISpecDialog,
  });

  const { data: hyvlingList = [] } = useQuery<{ id: number; kund: string | null; antalPaket: number; status: string }[]>({
    queryKey: ["/api/spec", "hyvling"],
    queryFn: () => customFetch<{ id: number; kund: string | null; antalPaket: number; status: string }[]>("/api/spec?typ=hyvling"),
    enabled: laggTillIHyvlingDialog,
  });

  const { data: traslagList = [] } = useQuery<string[]>({
    queryKey: ["/api/lager/traslag"],
    queryFn: () => customFetch<string[]>("/api/lager/traslag"),
  });

  const sortedData = useMemo(() => {
    let rows = listResponse?.data ?? [];
    if (traslag && traslag !== "alla_traslag") {
      rows = rows.filter(r => r.traslag === traslag);
    }
    if (!sortKey) return rows;
    return [...rows].sort((a, b) => {
      const ra = a as Record<string, unknown>;
      const rb = b as Record<string, unknown>;
      // Primär sortering
      const primary = compareVal(ra[sortKey], rb[sortKey], sortDir);
      if (primary !== 0) return primary;
      // Om primär är Nom dim eller Act dim: jämför också bredd, sedan längd
      if (sortKey === "nomTjocklek") {
        const dimB = compareVal(ra["nomBredd"], rb["nomBredd"], sortDir);
        if (dimB !== 0) return dimB;
        if (!sortKey2 || sortKey2 === "langdDm") {
          const lng = compareVal(ra["langdDm"], rb["langdDm"], sortDir);
          if (lng !== 0) return lng;
        }
      }
      if (sortKey === "actTjocklek") {
        const dimB = compareVal(ra["actBredd"], rb["actBredd"], sortDir);
        if (dimB !== 0) return dimB;
        if (!sortKey2 || sortKey2 === "langdDm") {
          const lng = compareVal(ra["langdDm"], rb["langdDm"], sortDir);
          if (lng !== 0) return lng;
        }
      }
      // Sekundär sortering (alltid stigande)
      if (sortKey2) {
        const sec = compareVal(ra[sortKey2], rb[sortKey2], "asc");
        if (sec !== 0) return sec;
        if (sortKey2 === "nomTjocklek") {
          const secB = compareVal(ra["nomBredd"], rb["nomBredd"], "asc");
          if (secB !== 0) return secB;
        }
      }
      // Slutlig fallback: längd sedan datum
      if (sortKey !== "langdDm" && sortKey2 !== "langdDm") {
        const lng = compareVal(ra["langdDm"], rb["langdDm"], "asc");
        if (lng !== 0) return lng;
      }
      return compareVal(ra["datumIn"], rb["datumIn"], "asc");
    });
  }, [listResponse?.data, sortKey, sortDir, sortKey2, traslag]);

  function handleSort(col: SortKey) {
    if (sortKey === col) setSortDir(d => d === "asc" ? "desc" : "asc");
    else { setSortKey(col); setSortDir("asc"); }
  }

  function toggleSelect(id: number) {
    setSelectedIds(prev => {
      const next = new Set(prev);
      if (next.has(id)) next.delete(id);
      else next.add(id);
      return next;
    });
  }

  function toggleSelectAll() {
    if (selectedIds.size === sortedData.length) {
      setSelectedIds(new Set());
    } else {
      setSelectedIds(new Set(sortedData.map(p => p.id)));
    }
  }

  function clearSelection() {
    setSelectedIds(new Set());
  }

  function resetBatchPlatsFields() {
    setBatchPlatsValue("");
    setBatchPlatsMode("fritext");
    setBatchFackId("none");
  }

  function openBatchPlatsDialog() {
    resetBatchPlatsFields();
    setBatchPlatsDialog(true);
  }

  async function handleBatchPlats() {
    const isStructured = typeof batchPlatsMode === "number";
    if (!isStructured && !batchPlatsValue.trim()) return;

    const patch: Record<string, unknown> = isStructured
      ? { omradeId: batchPlatsMode, fackId: batchFackId === "none" ? null : Number(batchFackId) }
      : { plats: batchPlatsValue.trim() };

    const platsLabel = isStructured
      ? `${selectedOmrade?.namn ?? ""}${
          batchFackId === "none"
            ? " · Ej angivet fack"
            : ` · fack ${selectedOmradeDetail?.fack.find(f => String(f.id) === batchFackId)?.kod ?? ""}`
        }`
      : batchPlatsValue.trim();

    setBatchLoading(true);
    try {
      const data = await customFetch<{ uppdaterade: number }>("/api/lager/batch", {
        method: "PUT",
        headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
        body: JSON.stringify({ ids: Array.from(selectedIds), patch }),
      });
      // Träffar även /api/lager/omraden och /api/lager/omraden/:slug så
      // att områdes-/fackvyerna (t.ex. V01-kortet) uppdateras direkt.
      queryClient.invalidateQueries({
        predicate: (q) => typeof q.queryKey[0] === "string" && q.queryKey[0].startsWith("/api/lager"),
      });
      toast({ title: `${data.uppdaterade} poster uppdaterade till plats "${platsLabel}"` });
      setBatchPlatsDialog(false);
      resetBatchPlatsFields();
      clearSelection();
    } catch (err) {
      const message = err instanceof ApiError && err.data && typeof err.data === "object" && "error" in err.data
        ? String((err.data as { error: unknown }).error)
        : undefined;
      toast({ title: "Fel vid uppdatering", description: message, variant: "destructive" });
    } finally {
      setBatchLoading(false);
    }
  }

  async function handleBatchUtleverera() {
    setConfirmUtleverera(false);
    setBatchLoading(true);
    try {
      const today = new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10);
      await customFetch("/api/lager/batch", {
        method: "PUT",
        headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
        body: JSON.stringify({ ids: Array.from(selectedIds), patch: { datumUt: today } }),
      });
      queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["/api/lager"] });
      queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["/api/lager/statistik"] });
      toast({ title: `${selectedIds.size} poster markerade som utlevererade` });
      clearSelection();
    } catch {
      toast({ title: "Fel", variant: "destructive" });
    } finally {
      setBatchLoading(false);
    }
  }

  async function handleBatchDelete() {
    setConfirmDelete(false);
    setBatchLoading(true);
    try {
      await customFetch("/api/lager/batch", {
        method: "DELETE",
        headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
        body: JSON.stringify({ ids: Array.from(selectedIds) }),
      });
      queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["/api/lager"] });
      queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["/api/lager/statistik"] });
      toast({ title: `${selectedIds.size} poster borttagna` });
      clearSelection();
    } catch {
      toast({ title: "Fel vid borttagning", variant: "destructive" });
    } finally {
      setBatchLoading(false);
    }
  }

  async function handleSyncEtikett() {
    setSyncing(true);
    try {
      const data = await customFetch<{ nySkapade: number; uppdaterade: number }>("/api/sync/etikett", { method: "POST" });
      queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["/api/lager"] });
      queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["/api/lager/statistik"] });
      const delar = [];
      if (data.nySkapade > 0) delar.push(`${data.nySkapade} nya`);
      if (data.uppdaterade > 0) delar.push(`${data.uppdaterade} uppdaterade`);
      toast({
        title: "Synkroniserat från Paketlapp",
        description: delar.length > 0 ? delar.join(", ") : "Inga ändringar",
      });
    } catch {
      toast({ title: "Synkfel", description: "Kunde inte nå Paketlapp.", variant: "destructive" });
    } finally {
      setSyncing(false);
    }
  }

  function exportExcel() {
    const rows = sortedData.map(p => ({
      "Status": p.datumUt ? "Utlevererat" : "I lager",
      "Pkt Nr": p.pktNr ?? "",
      "Datum In": p.datumIn ?? "",
      "Datum Ut": p.datumUt ?? "",
      "Träslag": p.traslag ?? "",
      "Behandling": p.behandling ?? "",
      "Utförande": p.utforande ?? "",
      "Profil": p.profil ?? "",
      "Kvalitet": p.kvalitet ?? "",
      "Nom dim (mm)": p.nomTjocklek && p.nomBredd ? `${p.nomTjocklek}×${p.nomBredd}` : "",
      "Act dim (mm)": p.actTjocklek && p.actBredd ? `${p.actTjocklek}×${p.actBredd}` : "",
      "Längd (dm)": p.langdDm ?? "",
      "Antal": p.antal ?? "",
      "Volym (m³)": p.m3 ?? "",
      "Löpmeter": p.lpm ?? "",
      "Lagerplats": p.plats ?? "",
      "Anteckning": p.anteckning ?? "",
    }));
    const ws = XLSX.utils.json_to_sheet(rows);
    const wb = XLSX.utils.book_new();
    XLSX.utils.book_append_sheet(wb, ws, "Lagerlista");
    const filename = `lagerlista_${new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10)}.xlsx`;
    XLSX.writeFile(wb, filename);
  }

  const resetFilters = () => {
    setSearch(""); setStatus("i_lager"); setPlats(""); setTraslag("");
    setSortKey(null); setSortDir("asc"); setSortKey2(null);
    sessionStorage.removeItem(STORAGE_KEY);
  };
  const hasActiveFilters = search !== "" || status !== "i_lager" || plats !== "" || traslag !== "";

  function handlePrint() {
    const printDate = new Date().toLocaleDateString("sv-SE");
    const totalM3 = sortedData.reduce((s, p) => s + (parseFloat(String(p.m3 ?? "0")) || 0), 0);
    // Schaal och 21-3 visas separat i dimensionsstatistiken
    const SEPARAT_KVAL_PRINT = new Set(["schaal", "21-3", "2-1", "os/v", "os", "us+v", "c12"]);
    const SEPARAT_KVAL_PRINT_DIM = [{ nt: 47, traslagPrefix: "gran", kval: new Set(["1-6", "c24"]) }];
    function printKvalLabel(kval: unknown, nt?: unknown, traslag?: unknown): string {
      const k = String(kval ?? "").toLowerCase().trim();
      if (SEPARAT_KVAL_PRINT.has(k)) return String(kval ?? "").trim();
      const ntNum = nt != null ? parseFloat(String(nt)) : null;
      const ts = String(traslag ?? "").toLowerCase();
      for (const rule of SEPARAT_KVAL_PRINT_DIM) {
        if (ntNum === rule.nt && ts.startsWith(rule.traslagPrefix) && rule.kval.has(k)) return String(kval ?? "").trim();
      }
      return "";
    }

    // Beräkna statistik från den filtrerade listan
    const tradslagMap = new Map<string, number>();
    const dimMap = new Map<string, { label: string; m3: number; antal: number }>();
    for (const p of sortedData) {
      const m3 = parseFloat(String(p.m3 ?? "0")) || 0;
      const ts = p.traslag ?? "Övrigt";
      tradslagMap.set(ts, (tradslagMap.get(ts) ?? 0) + m3);
      if (p.nomTjocklek && p.nomBredd) {
        const kl = printKvalLabel((p as Record<string, unknown>).kvalitet, p.nomTjocklek, p.traslag);
        const dk = `${p.nomTjocklek}×${p.nomBredd}|${kl}`;
        const label = kl ? `${p.nomTjocklek}×${p.nomBredd} ${kl}` : `${p.nomTjocklek}×${p.nomBredd}`;
        const ex = dimMap.get(dk) ?? { label, m3: 0, antal: 0 };
        dimMap.set(dk, { label, m3: ex.m3 + m3, antal: ex.antal + 1 });
      }
    }
    const tradslagRows = [...tradslagMap.entries()]
      .sort((a, b) => b[1] - a[1])
      .map(([ts, m]) => `<tr><td>${ts}</td><td class="num">${m.toLocaleString("sv-SE",{minimumFractionDigits:3,maximumFractionDigits:3})} m³</td><td class="num">${totalM3 > 0 ? Math.round(m/totalM3*100) : 0} %</td></tr>`)
      .join("");
    const topDimRows = [...dimMap.values()]
      .sort((a, b) => b.m3 - a.m3)
      .slice(0, 8)
      .map(d => `<tr><td>${d.label} mm</td><td class="num">${d.antal} pkt</td><td class="num">${d.m3.toLocaleString("sv-SE",{minimumFractionDigits:3,maximumFractionDigits:3})} m³</td><td class="num">${totalM3 > 0 ? Math.round(d.m3/totalM3*100) : 0} %</td></tr>`)
      .join("");

    const PRINT_CSS = `
      @page { size: A4 landscape; margin: 12mm; }
      * { font-family: Arial, sans-serif; font-size: 8pt; margin: 0; padding: 0; box-sizing: border-box; }
      body { color: #1a1a1a; }
      .print-header { display: flex; justify-content: space-between; align-items: flex-end; border-bottom: 2px solid #2d5a27; padding-bottom: 6px; margin-bottom: 10px; }
      .print-header h1 { font-size: 13pt; color: #2d5a27; }
      .print-header .meta { font-size: 7.5pt; color: #555; text-align: right; line-height: 1.5; }
      .stats { display: flex; gap: 16px; margin-bottom: 10px; }
      .stat-box { border: 1px solid #d1d5db; border-radius: 4px; padding: 6px 10px; flex: 1; }
      .stat-box h3 { font-size: 7pt; text-transform: uppercase; color: #6b7280; letter-spacing: 0.5px; margin-bottom: 4px; font-weight: bold; }
      .stat-kpi { font-size: 16pt; font-weight: bold; color: #2d5a27; line-height: 1; }
      .stat-kpi-label { font-size: 7pt; color: #6b7280; margin-top: 1px; }
      .stat-table { width: 100%; border-collapse: collapse; font-size: 7.5pt; }
      .stat-table td { padding: 2px 4px; border-bottom: 1px solid #f3f4f6; }
      .stat-table .num { text-align: right; font-variant-numeric: tabular-nums; }
      @media print { .stats { page-break-inside: avoid; } }
      table { width: 100%; border-collapse: collapse; margin-top: 10px; }
      thead { background: #2d5a27; color: white; }
      th { padding: 4px 6px; text-align: left; font-weight: bold; font-size: 7.5pt; }
      th.num { text-align: right; }
      td { padding: 3px 6px; border-bottom: 1px solid #e5e7eb; vertical-align: top; }
      td.num { text-align: right; font-variant-numeric: tabular-nums; }
      tr:nth-child(even) { background: #f9fafb; }
      .foot { margin-top: 8px; font-size: 7.5pt; color: #555; border-top: 1px solid #ccc; padding-top: 4px; display: flex; justify-content: space-between; }
    `;
    const rows = sortedData.map(p => {
      const nomDim = p.nomTjocklek && p.nomBredd ? `${p.nomTjocklek}×${p.nomBredd}` : "—";
      const actDim = p.actTjocklek && p.actBredd ? `${p.actTjocklek}×${p.actBredd}` : "";
      const dim = actDim ? `${nomDim} → ${actDim}` : nomDim;
      return `<tr>
        <td>${p.pktNr ?? ""}</td>
        <td>${p.datumIn ? String(p.datumIn).slice(0,10) : ""}</td>
        <td>${p.traslag ?? ""}</td>
        <td>${p.behandling ?? ""}</td>
        <td>${p.utforande ?? ""}</td>
        <td>${p.profil ?? ""}</td>
        <td>${p.kvalitet ?? ""}</td>
        <td>${dim}</td>
        <td class="num">${p.langdDm ?? ""}</td>
        <td class="num">${p.antal ?? ""}</td>
        <td class="num">${p.m3 ? parseFloat(String(p.m3)).toLocaleString("sv-SE", { minimumFractionDigits: 3, maximumFractionDigits: 3 }) : ""}</td>
        <td>${p.plats ?? ""}</td>
      </tr>`;
    }).join("");
    const html = `<!DOCTYPE html><html><head><meta charset="utf-8"><title>Lagerlista ${printDate}</title><style>${PRINT_CSS}</style></head><body>
      <div class="print-header">
        <div><h1>Lagerlista — ${branding.companyName}</h1><div style="font-size:7.5pt;color:#555;margin-top:2px;">${search ? `Sökning: ${search}` : "Alla lagervaror"}${status !== "alla" ? ` · ${status === "i_lager" ? "I Lager" : "Utlevererat"}` : ""}${plats ? ` · ${plats}` : ""}</div></div>
        <div class="meta">Utskrivet ${printDate}<br>${sortedData.length} poster · ${totalM3.toLocaleString("sv-SE", { minimumFractionDigits: 3, maximumFractionDigits: 3 })} m³</div>
      </div>
      <div class="stats">
        <div class="stat-box">
          <h3>Totalt i lager</h3>
          <div class="stat-kpi">${totalM3.toLocaleString("sv-SE",{minimumFractionDigits:1,maximumFractionDigits:1})}</div>
          <div class="stat-kpi-label">m³ · ${sortedData.length} paket</div>
        </div>
        <div class="stat-box" style="flex:1.2">
          <h3>Träslag</h3>
          <table class="stat-table"><tbody>${tradslagRows}</tbody></table>
        </div>
        <div class="stat-box" style="flex:2">
          <h3>Topp dimensioner (volym)</h3>
          <table class="stat-table"><thead><tr><td>Dim</td><td class="num">Paket</td><td class="num">m³</td><td class="num">Andel</td></tr></thead><tbody>${topDimRows}</tbody></table>
        </div>
      </div>
      <table><thead><tr>
        <th>Pkt Nr</th><th>Datum In</th><th>Träslag</th><th>Behandling</th><th>Utförande</th><th>Profil</th><th>Kvalitet</th><th>Dim (mm)</th>
        <th class="num">Längd dm</th><th class="num">Antal</th><th class="num">m³</th><th>Plats</th>
      </tr></thead><tbody>${rows}</tbody></table>
      <div class="foot"><span>Lagersystem Virke · ${branding.companyName}</span><span>${branding.website}</span></div>
    </body></html>`;
    const w = window.open("", "_blank", "width=1100,height=750");
    if (!w) return;
    w.document.write(html);
    w.document.close();
    setTimeout(() => { w.print(); }, 400);
  }
  const handlePrintRef = useRef(handlePrint);
  useEffect(() => { handlePrintRef.current = handlePrint; });
  useEffect(() => {
    const h = () => handlePrintRef.current();
    window.addEventListener("shortcut:print", h);
    return () => window.removeEventListener("shortcut:print", h);
  }, []);

  const allSelected = sortedData.length > 0 && selectedIds.size === sortedData.length;
  const someSelected = selectedIds.size > 0 && !allSelected;

  return (
    <div className="space-y-4">
      <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row justify-between items-start sm:items-center gap-4">
        <div>
          <h1 className="text-2xl sm:text-3xl font-bold tracking-tight">Lagerlista</h1>
          <p className="text-muted-foreground text-sm">Hela virkeslagret. Klicka på kolumnrubrik för att sortera.</p>
        </div>
        <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2">
          <Button variant="outline" size="sm" onClick={handlePrint} title="Skriv ut / PDF">
            <Printer className="h-4 w-4 mr-1" />
            <span className="hidden sm:inline">Skriv ut</span>
          </Button>
          <Button variant="outline" size="sm" onClick={exportExcel} title="Exportera till Excel">
            <FileSpreadsheet className="h-4 w-4 mr-1" />
            <span className="hidden sm:inline">Excel</span>
          </Button>
          <Button variant="outline" size="sm" onClick={handleSyncEtikett} disabled={syncing}>
            <RefreshCw className={`h-4 w-4 ${syncing ? "animate-spin" : ""} sm:mr-2`} />
            <span className="hidden sm:inline">{syncing ? "Synkar..." : "Synka Paketlapp"}</span>
          </Button>
          <Button variant="outline" size="sm" onClick={() => setScanOpen(true)}>
            <ScanLine className="h-4 w-4 sm:mr-2" />
            <span className="hidden sm:inline">Scanna</span>
          </Button>
          <Link href="/lager/ny" className="inline-flex items-center justify-center gap-2 whitespace-nowrap rounded-md text-sm font-medium bg-primary text-primary-foreground hover:bg-primary/90 h-9 px-3">
            <Plus className="h-4 w-4" />
            <span className="hidden sm:inline">Ny post</span>
          </Link>
        </div>
      </div>
      <ScanDialog open={scanOpen} onOpenChange={setScanOpen} />

      {structuredOmradeId != null && (
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2 text-sm bg-primary/5 border border-primary/20 rounded-lg px-3 py-2">
          <MapPin className="h-4 w-4 text-primary shrink-0" />
          <span>
            Visar paket i <span className="font-medium">{omradeNamnParam ?? "område"}</span>
            {structuredFackId && structuredFackId !== "none" && (
              <> · fack <span className="font-medium">{fackKodParam ?? structuredFackId}</span></>
            )}
            {structuredFackId === "none" && <> · ej angivet fack</>}
          </span>
          <Link href="/lager" className="ml-auto text-xs text-primary hover:underline">Visa allt lager</Link>
        </div>
      )}

      <div className="bg-card border rounded-lg p-3 flex flex-col sm:flex-row gap-3 items-end sm:items-center">
        <div className="relative flex-1 w-full">
          <Search className="absolute left-2.5 top-2.5 h-4 w-4 text-muted-foreground" />
          <Input
            placeholder="Sök t.ex. 47x100, 28/29×100, gran regel, pknr..."
            className="pl-9"
            value={search}
            onChange={(e) => setSearch(e.target.value)}
          />
        </div>
        <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2 w-full sm:w-auto">
          <Select value={status} onValueChange={(val: "i_lager" | "utlevererat" | "alla") => setStatus(val)}>
            <SelectTrigger className="w-[120px]"><SelectValue /></SelectTrigger>
            <SelectContent>
              <SelectItem value="i_lager">I Lager</SelectItem>
              <SelectItem value="utlevererat">Utlevererat</SelectItem>
              <SelectItem value="alla">Alla</SelectItem>
            </SelectContent>
          </Select>

          <Select value={plats} onValueChange={setPlats}>
            <SelectTrigger className="w-[120px]"><SelectValue placeholder="Alla platser" /></SelectTrigger>
            <SelectContent>
              <SelectItem value="alla_platser">Alla platser</SelectItem>
              {platser.filter(Boolean).map(p => <SelectItem key={p} value={p}>{p}</SelectItem>)}
            </SelectContent>
          </Select>

          <Select value={traslag} onValueChange={setTraslag}>
            <SelectTrigger className="w-[120px]"><SelectValue placeholder="Alla träslag" /></SelectTrigger>
            <SelectContent>
              <SelectItem value="alla_traslag">Alla träslag</SelectItem>
              {traslagList.filter(Boolean).map(v => <SelectItem key={v} value={v}>{v}</SelectItem>)}
            </SelectContent>
          </Select>

          {hasActiveFilters && (
            <Button variant="ghost" size="icon" onClick={resetFilters} title="Återställ filter">
              <FilterX className="h-4 w-4" />
            </Button>
          )}
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Summerad stats för aktuell sökning */}
      {!isLoading && sortedData.length > 0 && (() => {
        const totalM3 = sortedData.reduce((s, p) => s + (parseFloat(String(p.m3 ?? "0")) || 0), 0);
        const totalAntal = sortedData.reduce((s, p) => s + (p.antal ?? 0), 0);
        return (
          <div className="flex items-center gap-4 text-sm text-muted-foreground px-1">
            <span className="flex items-center gap-1.5">
              <strong className="text-foreground">{sortedData.length}</strong> paket
            </span>
            <span className="flex items-center gap-1.5">
              <strong className="text-foreground">{totalAntal.toLocaleString("sv-SE")}</strong> st
            </span>
            <span className="flex items-center gap-1.5">
              <strong className="text-foreground">{totalM3.toLocaleString("sv-SE", { minimumFractionDigits: 3, maximumFractionDigits: 3 })}</strong> m³
            </span>
            {listResponse && listResponse.total > sortedData.length && (
              <span className="text-xs text-amber-600">(visar {sortedData.length} av {listResponse.total})</span>
            )}
          </div>
        );
      })()}

      {/* Sekundär sortering */}
      {sortKey && (
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2 text-sm text-muted-foreground px-1">
          <span className="shrink-0">Sedan på:</span>
          <Select value={sortKey2 ?? "ingen"} onValueChange={v => setSortKey2(v === "ingen" ? null : v as SortKey)}>
            <SelectTrigger className="w-[150px] h-8 text-sm">
              <SelectValue placeholder="Ingen" />
            </SelectTrigger>
            <SelectContent>
              <SelectItem value="ingen">Ingen</SelectItem>
              {([
                { value: "nomTjocklek", label: "Dimension" },
                { value: "langdDm", label: "Längd (dm)" },
                { value: "traslag", label: "Träslag" },
                { value: "behandling", label: "Behandling" },
                { value: "utforande", label: "Utförande" },
                { value: "kvalitet", label: "Kvalitet" },
                { value: "profil", label: "Profil" },
                { value: "plats", label: "Plats" },
                { value: "antal", label: "Antal" },
                { value: "m3", label: "Volym (m³)" },
              ] as { value: SortKey; label: string }[])
                .filter(o => o.value !== sortKey)
                .map(o => <SelectItem key={o.value} value={o.value}>{o.label}</SelectItem>)}
            </SelectContent>
          </Select>
          {sortKey2 && (
            <Button variant="ghost" size="icon" className="h-7 w-7" onClick={() => setSortKey2(null)} title="Ta bort sekundär sortering">
              <X className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
            </Button>
          )}
        </div>
      )}

      <div className="sm:hidden space-y-2">
        {isLoading ? (
          Array.from({ length: 6 }).map((_, i) => (
            <div key={i} className="border rounded-lg p-3 bg-card space-y-2">
              <Skeleton className="h-4 w-3/4" />
              <Skeleton className="h-4 w-1/2" />
            </div>
          ))
        ) : sortedData.length === 0 ? (
          <div className="border rounded-lg p-8 text-center text-muted-foreground bg-card">
            Inga lagerposter hittades.
          </div>
        ) : (
          sortedData.map((rawPost) => {
            const post = rawPost as typeof rawPost & { specs: SpecRef[] };
            const isActive = !post.datumUt;
            const isSelected = selectedIds.has(post.id);
            const utkastSpecsMob = (post.specs ?? []).filter(s => s.status === "utkast");
            const bekraftadeSpecsMob = (post.specs ?? []).filter(s => s.status === "bekraftad");
            return (
              <div
                key={post.id}
                className={`border rounded-lg p-3 bg-card transition-colors ${isSelected ? "border-primary/50 bg-primary/5" : ""}`}
              >
                <div className="flex items-start gap-2">
                  <Checkbox
                    checked={isSelected}
                    onCheckedChange={() => toggleSelect(post.id)}
                    className="mt-0.5 shrink-0"
                  />
                  <div className="flex-1 min-w-0 space-y-1">
                    <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-2">
                      <div className="flex items-center gap-2 flex-wrap">
                        {isActive
                          ? <Badge variant="default" className="bg-primary/20 text-primary shadow-none text-xs px-1.5">I lager</Badge>
                          : <Badge variant="secondary" className="shadow-none text-xs px-1.5">Utlev.</Badge>
                        }
                        {utkastSpecsMob.map(s => (
                          <Link key={s.specId} href={`/spec/${s.specId}`}>
                            <Badge variant="outline" className="text-xs cursor-pointer hover:bg-amber-50 border-amber-300 text-amber-700 gap-1 px-1.5 py-0">
                              <FileText className="h-2.5 w-2.5" />Spec #{s.specId}
                            </Badge>
                          </Link>
                        ))}
                        {bekraftadeSpecsMob.map(s => (
                          <Link key={s.specId} href={`/spec/${s.specId}`}>
                            <Badge variant="outline" className="text-xs cursor-pointer hover:bg-green-50 border-green-300 text-green-700 gap-1 px-1.5 py-0">
                              <FileText className="h-2.5 w-2.5" />Spec #{s.specId}
                            </Badge>
                          </Link>
                        ))}
                        <span className="font-medium text-sm">{post.traslag || "–"} · {post.profil || "–"}</span>
                        {kortBehandling(post.behandling) && (
                          <Badge variant="outline" className="text-xs border-sky-300 bg-sky-50 text-sky-800 px-1.5 py-0">
                            {kortBehandling(post.behandling)}
                          </Badge>
                        )}
                        {post.utforande && (
                          <Badge variant="outline" className="text-xs border-violet-300 bg-violet-50 text-violet-800 px-1.5 py-0">
                            {post.utforande}
                          </Badge>
                        )}
                      </div>
                      <div className="flex items-center gap-2 shrink-0">
                        <button
                          onClick={() => printPaketlappById(post.id)}
                          className="text-muted-foreground hover:text-foreground"
                          title="Skriv ut paketlapp"
                        >
                          <Printer className="h-4 w-4" />
                        </button>
                        <Link href={`/lager/${post.id}`} className="text-muted-foreground hover:text-foreground">
                          <Eye className="h-4 w-4" />
                        </Link>
                      </div>
                    </div>
                    <div className="text-sm text-muted-foreground">
                      {post.nomTjocklek && post.nomBredd ? `${post.nomTjocklek}×${post.nomBredd} mm` : ""}
                      {post.langdDm ? ` · ${post.langdDm} dm` : ""}
                      {post.antal ? ` · ${post.antal} st` : ""}
                      {post.m3 ? ` · ${post.m3.toFixed(3)} m³` : ""}
                    </div>
                    <div className="flex items-center justify-between text-xs text-muted-foreground">
                      <span className="font-mono">{post.pktNr || "–"}</span>
                      {(post.omradeNamn || post.plats) && (
                        <span className="flex items-center gap-1">
                          <MapPin className="h-3 w-3" />{formatStructuredLagerplats(post)}
                        </span>
                      )}
                    </div>
                  </div>
                </div>
              </div>
            );
          })
        )}
      </div>

      <div className="hidden sm:block border rounded-md bg-card overflow-x-auto">
        <Table className="text-sm">
          <TableHeader>
            <TableRow>
              <TableHead className="w-10">
                <Checkbox
                  checked={allSelected}
                  data-state={someSelected ? "indeterminate" : allSelected ? "checked" : "unchecked"}
                  onCheckedChange={toggleSelectAll}
                  aria-label="Välj alla"
                />
              </TableHead>
              <TableHead className="w-[120px]">Status</TableHead>
              <SortableHead label="Pkt Nr"     col="pktNr"       sortKey={sortKey} sortDir={sortDir} sortKey2={sortKey2} onSort={handleSort} />
              <SortableHead label="Datum In"   col="datumIn"     sortKey={sortKey} sortDir={sortDir} sortKey2={sortKey2} onSort={handleSort} />
              <SortableHead label="Träslag"    col="traslag"     sortKey={sortKey} sortDir={sortDir} sortKey2={sortKey2} onSort={handleSort} />
              <SortableHead label="Behandling" col="behandling"  sortKey={sortKey} sortDir={sortDir} sortKey2={sortKey2} onSort={handleSort} />
              <SortableHead label="Utförande"  col="utforande"   sortKey={sortKey} sortDir={sortDir} sortKey2={sortKey2} onSort={handleSort} />
              <SortableHead label="Profil"     col="profil"      sortKey={sortKey} sortDir={sortDir} sortKey2={sortKey2} onSort={handleSort} />
              <SortableHead label="Kvalitet"   col="kvalitet"    sortKey={sortKey} sortDir={sortDir} sortKey2={sortKey2} onSort={handleSort} />
              <SortableHead label="Dim (mm)"   col="nomTjocklek" sortKey={sortKey} sortDir={sortDir} sortKey2={sortKey2} onSort={handleSort} />
              <SortableHead label="Act dim (mm)" col="actTjocklek" sortKey={sortKey} sortDir={sortDir} sortKey2={sortKey2} onSort={handleSort} />
              <SortableHead label="Längd (dm)" col="langdDm"     sortKey={sortKey} sortDir={sortDir} sortKey2={sortKey2} onSort={handleSort} className="text-right" />
              <SortableHead label="Antal"      col="antal"       sortKey={sortKey} sortDir={sortDir} sortKey2={sortKey2} onSort={handleSort} className="text-right" />
              <SortableHead label="Volym (m³)" col="m3"          sortKey={sortKey} sortDir={sortDir} sortKey2={sortKey2} onSort={handleSort} className="text-right hidden sm:table-cell" />
              <SortableHead label="Plats"      col="plats"       sortKey={sortKey} sortDir={sortDir} sortKey2={sortKey2} onSort={handleSort} />
              <TableHead className="w-[40px]"></TableHead>
            </TableRow>
          </TableHeader>
          <TableBody>
            {isLoading ? (
              Array.from({ length: 10 }).map((_, i) => (
                <TableRow key={i}>
                  {Array.from({ length: 16 }).map((_, j) => (
                    <TableCell key={j}><Skeleton className="h-5 w-full" /></TableCell>
                  ))}
                </TableRow>
              ))
            ) : sortedData.length === 0 ? (
              <TableRow>
                <TableCell colSpan={16} className="h-32 text-center text-muted-foreground">
                  Inga lagerposter hittades.
                </TableCell>
              </TableRow>
            ) : (
              sortedData.map((rawPost) => {
                const post = rawPost as typeof rawPost & { specs: SpecRef[] };
                const isActive = !post.datumUt;
                const isSelected = selectedIds.has(post.id);
                const utkastSpecs = (post.specs ?? []).filter(s => s.status === "utkast");
                const bekraftadeSpecs = (post.specs ?? []).filter(s => s.status === "bekraftad");
                return (
                  <TableRow key={post.id} className={`hover:bg-muted/50 transition-colors ${isSelected ? "bg-primary/5" : ""}`}>
                    <TableCell>
                      <Checkbox
                        checked={isSelected}
                        onCheckedChange={() => toggleSelect(post.id)}
                        aria-label={`Välj rad ${post.pktNr}`}
                      />
                    </TableCell>
                    <TableCell>
                      <div className="flex flex-col gap-1">
                        {isActive
                          ? <Badge variant="default" className="bg-primary/20 text-primary hover:bg-primary/30 shadow-none text-xs w-fit">I lager</Badge>
                          : <Badge variant="secondary" className="bg-muted text-muted-foreground shadow-none text-xs w-fit">Utlev.</Badge>
                        }
                        {utkastSpecs.map(s => (
                          <Link key={s.specId} href={`/spec/${s.specId}`}>
                            <Badge variant="outline" className="text-xs max-w-[110px] cursor-pointer hover:bg-amber-50 border-amber-300 text-amber-700 gap-1 px-1.5 py-0 overflow-hidden">
                              <FileText className="h-2.5 w-2.5 shrink-0" />
                              <span className="truncate">Spec #{s.specId}{s.kund ? ` · ${s.kund}` : ""}</span>
                            </Badge>
                          </Link>
                        ))}
                        {bekraftadeSpecs.map(s => (
                          <Link key={s.specId} href={`/spec/${s.specId}`}>
                            <Badge variant="outline" className="text-xs max-w-[110px] cursor-pointer hover:bg-green-50 border-green-300 text-green-700 gap-1 px-1.5 py-0 overflow-hidden">
                              <FileText className="h-2.5 w-2.5 shrink-0" />
                              <span className="truncate">Spec #{s.specId}{s.kund ? ` · ${s.kund}` : ""}</span>
                            </Badge>
                          </Link>
                        ))}
                      </div>
                    </TableCell>
                    <TableCell className="font-medium font-mono text-xs">{post.pktNr || "-"}</TableCell>
                    <TableCell>{post.datumIn ? format(new Date(post.datumIn), 'yyyy-MM-dd') : "-"}</TableCell>
                    <TableCell>{post.traslag || "-"}</TableCell>
                    <TableCell>
                      {kortBehandling(post.behandling) ? (
                        <Badge variant="outline" className="border-sky-300 bg-sky-50 text-sky-800">
                          {kortBehandling(post.behandling)}
                        </Badge>
                      ) : "-"}
                    </TableCell>
                    <TableCell>
                      {post.utforande ? (
                        <Badge variant="outline" className="border-violet-300 bg-violet-50 text-violet-800">
                          {post.utforande}
                        </Badge>
                      ) : "-"}
                    </TableCell>
                    <TableCell>{post.profil || "-"}</TableCell>
                    <TableCell>{post.kvalitet || "-"}</TableCell>
                    <TableCell>{post.nomTjocklek && post.nomBredd ? `${post.nomTjocklek}×${post.nomBredd}` : "-"}</TableCell>
                    <TableCell className="text-muted-foreground">
                      {post.actTjocklek && post.actBredd ? `${post.actTjocklek}×${post.actBredd}` : "-"}
                    </TableCell>
                    <TableCell className="text-right">{post.langdDm ?? "-"}</TableCell>
                    <TableCell className="text-right">{post.antal ?? "-"}</TableCell>
                    <TableCell className="text-right font-medium hidden sm:table-cell">
                      {post.m3 ? post.m3.toFixed(3) : "-"}
                    </TableCell>
                    <TableCell>{formatStructuredLagerplats(post)}</TableCell>
                    <TableCell>
                      <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 justify-end">
                        <button
                          onClick={() => printPaketlappById(post.id)}
                          className="text-muted-foreground hover:text-foreground"
                          title="Skriv ut paketlapp"
                        >
                          <Printer className="h-4 w-4" />
                        </button>
                        <Link href={`/lager/${post.id}`} className="text-muted-foreground hover:text-foreground">
                          <Eye className="h-4 w-4" />
                        </Link>
                      </div>
                    </TableCell>
                  </TableRow>
                );
              })
            )}
          </TableBody>
        </Table>
      </div>

      {!isLoading && listResponse && (
        <p className="text-sm text-muted-foreground text-center">
          Visar {sortedData.length} av {listResponse.total} poster
          {selectedIds.size > 0 && ` · ${selectedIds.size} valda`}
        </p>
      )}

      {selectedIds.size > 0 && (
        <>
          {/* ── Mobilvy: full-width panel längst ned ── */}
          <div className="sm:hidden fixed bottom-0 left-0 right-0 z-50 bg-card border-t shadow-2xl px-3 pt-3 pb-safe pb-4">
            <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-3">
              <span className="text-sm font-semibold flex items-center gap-1.5">
                <CheckSquare className="h-4 w-4 text-primary" />
                {selectedIds.size} valda poster
              </span>
              <Button size="sm" variant="ghost" className="h-8 w-8 p-0" onClick={clearSelection}>
                <X className="h-4 w-4" />
              </Button>
            </div>
            <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-2">
              <Button size="sm" variant="default" className="gap-1.5 justify-start" onClick={createSpec} disabled={batchLoading}>
                <FileText className="h-3.5 w-3.5 shrink-0" />
                Skapa spec
              </Button>
              <Button size="sm" variant="outline" className="gap-1.5 justify-start" onClick={() => { setSelectedSpecId(""); setLaggTillISpecDialog(true); }} disabled={batchLoading}>
                <FilePlus2 className="h-3.5 w-3.5 shrink-0" />
                Lägg till i spec
              </Button>
              <Button size="sm" variant="outline" className="gap-1.5 justify-start text-orange-700 border-orange-300" onClick={createHyvling} disabled={batchLoading}>
                <Wrench className="h-3.5 w-3.5 shrink-0" />
                Skapa hyvlingsorder
              </Button>
              <Button size="sm" variant="outline" className="gap-1.5 justify-start text-orange-700 border-orange-300" onClick={() => { setSelectedHyvlingId(""); setLaggTillIHyvlingDialog(true); }} disabled={batchLoading}>
                <FilePlus2 className="h-3.5 w-3.5 shrink-0" />
                Lägg till i hyvling
              </Button>
              <Button size="sm" variant="outline" className="gap-1.5 justify-start" onClick={openBatchPlatsDialog} disabled={batchLoading}>
                <MapPin className="h-3.5 w-3.5 shrink-0" />
                Ändra plats
              </Button>
              <Button size="sm" variant="outline" className="gap-1.5 justify-start" onClick={() => setConfirmUtleverera(true)} disabled={batchLoading}>
                <ArrowDown className="h-3.5 w-3.5 shrink-0" />
                Utleverera
              </Button>
              <Button size="sm" variant="destructive" className="col-span-2 gap-1.5 justify-start" onClick={() => setConfirmDelete(true)} disabled={batchLoading}>
                <Trash2 className="h-3.5 w-3.5 shrink-0" />
                Ta bort {selectedIds.size} poster
              </Button>
            </div>
          </div>

          {/* ── Desktopvy: flytande pill ── */}
          <div className="hidden sm:flex fixed bottom-6 left-1/2 -translate-x-1/2 z-50 items-center gap-2 bg-card border shadow-xl rounded-full px-4 py-2">
            <CheckSquare className="h-4 w-4 text-primary shrink-0" />
            <span className="text-sm font-medium whitespace-nowrap">{selectedIds.size} valda</span>
            <div className="w-px h-5 bg-border mx-1" />
            <Button size="sm" variant="default" className="rounded-full h-8 gap-1" onClick={createSpec} disabled={batchLoading}>
              <FileText className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
              Skapa spec
            </Button>
            <Button size="sm" variant="outline" className="rounded-full h-8 gap-1" onClick={() => { setSelectedSpecId(""); setLaggTillISpecDialog(true); }} disabled={batchLoading}>
              <FilePlus2 className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
              Lägg till i spec
            </Button>
            <Button size="sm" variant="outline" className="rounded-full h-8 gap-1 text-orange-700 border-orange-300 hover:bg-orange-50" onClick={createHyvling} disabled={batchLoading}>
              <Wrench className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
              Skapa hyvlingsorder
            </Button>
            <Button size="sm" variant="outline" className="rounded-full h-8 gap-1 text-orange-700 border-orange-300 hover:bg-orange-50" onClick={() => { setSelectedHyvlingId(""); setLaggTillIHyvlingDialog(true); }} disabled={batchLoading}>
              <FilePlus2 className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
              Lägg till i hyvlingsorder
            </Button>
            <div className="w-px h-5 bg-border mx-1" />
            <Button size="sm" variant="outline" className="rounded-full h-8 gap-1" onClick={openBatchPlatsDialog} disabled={batchLoading}>
              <MapPin className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
              Ändra plats
            </Button>
            <Button size="sm" variant="outline" className="rounded-full h-8" onClick={() => setConfirmUtleverera(true)} disabled={batchLoading}>
              Utleverera
            </Button>
            <Button size="sm" variant="destructive" className="rounded-full h-8 gap-1" onClick={() => setConfirmDelete(true)} disabled={batchLoading}>
              <Trash2 className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
              Ta bort
            </Button>
            <Button size="sm" variant="ghost" className="rounded-full h-8 w-8 p-0" onClick={clearSelection}>
              <X className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
            </Button>
          </div>
        </>
      )}

      <AlertDialog open={confirmUtleverera} onOpenChange={setConfirmUtleverera}>
        <AlertDialogContent>
          <AlertDialogHeader>
            <AlertDialogTitle>Markera {selectedIds.size} poster som utlevererade?</AlertDialogTitle>
            <AlertDialogDescription>
              Datum ut sätts till idag för alla valda poster. De visas inte längre under "I lager".
            </AlertDialogDescription>
          </AlertDialogHeader>
          <AlertDialogFooter>
            <AlertDialogCancel>Avbryt</AlertDialogCancel>
            <AlertDialogAction onClick={handleBatchUtleverera}>Utleverera</AlertDialogAction>
          </AlertDialogFooter>
        </AlertDialogContent>
      </AlertDialog>

      <AlertDialog open={confirmDelete} onOpenChange={setConfirmDelete}>
        <AlertDialogContent>
          <AlertDialogHeader>
            <AlertDialogTitle>Flytta {selectedIds.size} poster till papperskorgen?</AlertDialogTitle>
            <AlertDialogDescription>
              Posterna flyttas till papperskorgen. En admin kan återställa dem därifrån om det behövs.
            </AlertDialogDescription>
          </AlertDialogHeader>
          <AlertDialogFooter>
            <AlertDialogCancel>Avbryt</AlertDialogCancel>
            <AlertDialogAction
              className="bg-destructive text-destructive-foreground hover:bg-destructive/90"
              onClick={handleBatchDelete}
            >
              Flytta till papperskorgen
            </AlertDialogAction>
          </AlertDialogFooter>
        </AlertDialogContent>
      </AlertDialog>

      <Dialog open={laggTillISpecDialog} onOpenChange={(v) => { setLaggTillISpecDialog(v); if (!v) setSelectedSpecId(""); }}>
        <DialogContent className="w-[min(calc(100vw-2rem),420px)]">
          <DialogHeader>
            <DialogTitle>Lägg till i befintlig spec</DialogTitle>
          </DialogHeader>
          <div className="space-y-3 py-2">
            <p className="text-sm text-muted-foreground">{selectedIds.size} markerade paket läggs till i vald spec.</p>
            <Select value={selectedSpecId} onValueChange={setSelectedSpecId}>
              <SelectTrigger className="w-full overflow-hidden">
                <SelectValue placeholder="Välj spec…" />
              </SelectTrigger>
              <SelectContent className="max-w-[min(calc(100vw-2rem),400px)] max-h-[40vh]">
                {specList.filter(s => s.status !== "bekraftad").map(s => {
                  const label = [
                    `#${s.id}`,
                    s.kund ? `— ${s.kund}` : "",
                    s.orderNr ? `/ Order ${s.orderNr}` : "",
                    s.trailerNr ? `/ ${s.trailerNr}` : "",
                    `(${s.antalPaket} paket)`,
                  ].filter(Boolean).join(" ");
                  return (
                    <SelectItem key={s.id} value={String(s.id)} className="max-w-full">
                      <span className="block truncate">{label}</span>
                    </SelectItem>
                  );
                })}
                {specList.filter(s => s.status !== "bekraftad").length === 0 && (
                  <div className="p-4 text-sm text-muted-foreground text-center">Inga öppna specs hittades</div>
                )}
              </SelectContent>
            </Select>
          </div>
          <DialogFooter>
            <Button variant="outline" onClick={() => setLaggTillISpecDialog(false)}>Avbryt</Button>
            <Button onClick={handleLaggTillISpec} disabled={batchLoading || !selectedSpecId}>
              {batchLoading ? "Lägger till…" : "Lägg till"}
            </Button>
          </DialogFooter>
        </DialogContent>
      </Dialog>

      <Dialog open={laggTillIHyvlingDialog} onOpenChange={(v) => { setLaggTillIHyvlingDialog(v); if (!v) setSelectedHyvlingId(""); }}>
        <DialogContent className="w-[min(calc(100vw-2rem),420px)] max-h-[min(90vh,520px)] flex flex-col">
          <DialogHeader className="flex-none">
            <DialogTitle>Lägg till i befintlig hyvlingsorder</DialogTitle>
          </DialogHeader>
          <div className="flex flex-col gap-3 py-2 min-h-0 flex-1">
            <p className="text-sm text-muted-foreground flex-none">{selectedIds.size} markerade paket läggs till i vald hyvlingsorder.</p>
            {hyvlingList.filter(h => h.status !== "bekraftad").length === 0 ? (
              <p className="text-sm text-muted-foreground text-center py-4">Inga öppna hyvlingsordrar hittades</p>
            ) : (
              <div className="overflow-y-auto border rounded-md divide-y flex-1">
                {hyvlingList.filter(h => h.status !== "bekraftad").map(h => {
                  const selected = selectedHyvlingId === String(h.id);
                  return (
                    <button
                      key={h.id}
                      type="button"
                      onClick={() => setSelectedHyvlingId(String(h.id))}
                      className={`w-full text-left px-3 py-2 text-sm transition-colors ${selected ? "bg-orange-50 text-orange-900 font-medium" : "hover:bg-muted"}`}
                    >
                      <span className="font-medium">#{h.id}</span>
                      {h.kund && <span className="text-muted-foreground"> — {h.kund}</span>}
                      <span className="text-muted-foreground"> ({h.antalPaket} paket)</span>
                    </button>
                  );
                })}
              </div>
            )}
          </div>
          <DialogFooter className="flex-none">
            <Button variant="outline" onClick={() => setLaggTillIHyvlingDialog(false)}>Avbryt</Button>
            <Button className="bg-orange-600 hover:bg-orange-700 text-white" onClick={handleLaggTillIHyvling} disabled={batchLoading || !selectedHyvlingId}>
              {batchLoading ? "Lägger till…" : "Lägg till"}
            </Button>
          </DialogFooter>
        </DialogContent>
      </Dialog>

      <Dialog open={batchPlatsDialog} onOpenChange={setBatchPlatsDialog}>
        <DialogContent className="sm:max-w-sm">
          <DialogHeader>
            <DialogTitle>Ändra lagerplats</DialogTitle>
          </DialogHeader>
          <div className="space-y-3 py-2">
            <p className="text-sm text-muted-foreground">Ny lagerplats för {selectedIds.size} valda poster:</p>

            <Select
              value={typeof batchPlatsMode === "number" ? String(batchPlatsMode) : "fritext"}
              onValueChange={(v) => {
                if (v === "fritext") {
                  setBatchPlatsMode("fritext");
                } else {
                  setBatchPlatsMode(Number(v));
                  setBatchFackId("none");
                }
              }}
            >
              <SelectTrigger className="w-full h-11">
                <SelectValue />
              </SelectTrigger>
              <SelectContent>
                <SelectItem value="fritext">Fri text</SelectItem>
                {omradenList.map(o => (
                  <SelectItem key={o.id} value={String(o.id)}>{o.namn}</SelectItem>
                ))}
              </SelectContent>
            </Select>

            {typeof batchPlatsMode === "number" ? (
              <Select value={batchFackId} onValueChange={setBatchFackId}>
                <SelectTrigger className="w-full h-11">
                  <SelectValue placeholder="Välj fack…" />
                </SelectTrigger>
                <SelectContent>
                  <SelectItem value="none">Ej angivet fack</SelectItem>
                  {(selectedOmradeDetail?.fack ?? []).map(f => (
                    <SelectItem key={f.id} value={String(f.id)}>{f.kod}</SelectItem>
                  ))}
                </SelectContent>
              </Select>
            ) : (
              <Input
                value={batchPlatsValue}
                onChange={(e) => setBatchPlatsValue(e.target.value)}
                placeholder="t.ex. B3"
                onKeyDown={(e) => e.key === "Enter" && handleBatchPlats()}
                autoFocus
              />
            )}
          </div>
          <DialogFooter>
            <Button variant="outline" onClick={() => setBatchPlatsDialog(false)}>Avbryt</Button>
            <Button
              onClick={handleBatchPlats}
              disabled={batchLoading || (typeof batchPlatsMode !== "number" && !batchPlatsValue.trim())}
            >
              {batchLoading ? "Uppdaterar..." : "Uppdatera"}
            </Button>
          </DialogFooter>
        </DialogContent>
      </Dialog>
    </div>
  );
}


================ FILE: artifacts/lager/src/pages/inventory/PaketlappPreview.tsx ================

import { useEffect, useState } from "react";
import { useParams } from "wouter";
import QRCode from "qrcode";
import PaketlappPrint from "./PaketlappPrint";
import type { LagerPost } from "@workspace/db";

export default function PaketlappPreview() {
  const { id } = useParams<{ id: string }>();
  const [post, setPost] = useState<LagerPost | null>(null);
  const [siblings, setSiblings] = useState<LagerPost[] | undefined>();
  const [qrDataUrl, setQrDataUrl] = useState<string | undefined>();

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!id) return;
    fetch(`/api/lager/${id}`)
      .then((r) => r.json())
      .then(async (p: LagerPost) => {
        setPost(p);
        const url = `${window.location.origin}/lager/${p.id}`;
        const qr = await QRCode.toDataURL(url, { width: 160, margin: 1, color: { dark: "#000", light: "#fff" } });
        setQrDataUrl(qr);

        if (p.pktNr) {
          try {
            const all = await fetch(`/api/lager/alla-pktnr/${encodeURIComponent(p.pktNr)}`).then(r => r.json()) as LagerPost[];
            if (all.length > 1) {
              setSiblings(all.sort((a, b) => (Number(a.langdDm) || 0) - (Number(b.langdDm) || 0)));
            }
          } catch { /* ignore */ }
        }
      });
  }, [id]);

  if (!post) return <div style={{ padding: 20 }}>Laddar...</div>;

  return (
    <div style={{ background: "#f0f0f0", minHeight: "100vh", display: "flex", justifyContent: "center", padding: "20px" }}>
      <div style={{ boxShadow: "0 4px 24px rgba(0,0,0,0.18)" }}>
        <PaketlappPrint post={post} qrDataUrl={qrDataUrl} siblings={siblings} />
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}


================ FILE: artifacts/lager/src/pages/inventory/PaketlappPrint.tsx ================

import type { LagerPost } from "@workspace/db";
import { useBranding } from "@/components/branding/BrandingContext";

interface PaketlappPrintProps {
  post: LagerPost;
  qrDataUrl?: string;
  siblings?: LagerPost[];
}

function computedNomM3(post: LagerPost): number | null {
  if (post.nomTjocklek && post.nomBredd && post.lpm)
    return (post.nomTjocklek / 1000) * (post.nomBredd / 1000) * post.lpm;
  return post.m3 ? Number(post.m3) : null;
}

function computedActM3(post: LagerPost): number | null {
  if (post.actM3) return Number(post.actM3);
  if (post.actTjocklek && post.actBredd && post.lpm)
    return (post.actTjocklek / 1000) * (post.actBredd / 1000) * post.lpm;
  return null;
}

function sumNomM3(posts: LagerPost[]): number | null {
  let total = 0;
  let any = false;
  for (const p of posts) {
    const v = computedNomM3(p);
    if (v != null) { total += v; any = true; }
  }
  return any ? total : null;
}

function sumActM3(posts: LagerPost[]): number | null {
  let total = 0;
  let any = false;
  for (const p of posts) {
    const v = computedActM3(p);
    if (v != null) { total += v; any = true; }
  }
  return any ? total : null;
}

function sumAntal(posts: LagerPost[]): number | null {
  let total = 0;
  let any = false;
  for (const p of posts) {
    if (p.antal != null) { total += Number(p.antal); any = true; }
  }
  return any ? total : null;
}

export default function PaketlappPrint({ post, qrDataUrl, siblings }: PaketlappPrintProps) {
  const branding = useBranding();
  const allPosts = siblings && siblings.length > 1 ? siblings : null;

  const nomM3 = allPosts ? sumNomM3(allPosts) : computedNomM3(post);
  const actM3 = allPosts ? sumActM3(allPosts) : computedActM3(post);
  const totalAntal = allPosts ? sumAntal(allPosts) : (post.antal != null ? Number(post.antal) : null);

  const nomDimShort = post.nomTjocklek && post.nomBredd
    ? `${post.nomTjocklek}×${post.nomBredd}`
    : null;
  const actDimShort = post.actTjocklek && post.actBredd
    ? `${post.actTjocklek}×${post.actBredd}`
    : null;

  const lengthMm = post.langdDm ? `${Number(post.langdDm) * 100} mm` : "—";
  const antal = post.antal != null ? `${post.antal} st` : "—";
  const today = new Date().toLocaleDateString("sv-SE");

  return (
    <div style={{
      width: "210mm",
      height: "297mm",
      margin: "0 auto",
      background: "#fff",
      fontFamily: "Arial, 'Helvetica Neue', sans-serif",
      color: "#000",
      boxSizing: "border-box",
      display: "flex",
      flexDirection: "column",
    }}>
      <FullLabel
        post={post}
        qrDataUrl={qrDataUrl}
        nomM3={nomM3}
        actM3={actM3}
        nomDimShort={nomDimShort}
        actDimShort={actDimShort}
        lengthMm={lengthMm}
        antal={antal}
        today={today}
        height="128mm"
        showFooter={false}
        allPosts={allPosts}
        totalAntal={totalAntal}
      />

      {/* ── Miniremsa ── */}
      <MiniStrip
        post={post}
        nomM3={nomM3}
        actM3={actM3}
        nomDimShort={nomDimShort}
        actDimShort={actDimShort}
        qrDataUrl={qrDataUrl}
        allPosts={allPosts}
        totalAntal={totalAntal}
      />

      <FullLabel
        post={post}
        qrDataUrl={qrDataUrl}
        nomM3={nomM3}
        actM3={actM3}
        nomDimShort={nomDimShort}
        actDimShort={actDimShort}
        lengthMm={lengthMm}
        antal={antal}
        today={today}
        height="130mm"
        allPosts={allPosts}
        totalAntal={totalAntal}
      />
    </div>
  );
}

interface FullLabelProps {
  post: LagerPost;
  qrDataUrl?: string;
  nomM3: number | null;
  actM3: number | null;
  nomDimShort: string | null;
  actDimShort: string | null;
  lengthMm: string;
  antal: string;
  today: string;
  height: string;
  showFooter?: boolean;
  allPosts: LagerPost[] | null;
  totalAntal: number | null;
}

function FullLabel({ post, qrDataUrl, nomM3, actM3, nomDimShort, actDimShort, lengthMm, antal, today, height, showFooter = true, allPosts, totalAntal }: FullLabelProps) {
  const branding = useBranding();
  const hasActDim = !!actDimShort;
  const isBlandad = !!allPosts;

  return (
    <div style={{
      height,
      flexShrink: 0,
      boxSizing: "border-box",
      padding: "4mm 7mm",
      display: "flex",
      flexDirection: "column",
      gap: "2mm",
      borderBottom: "3px solid #000",
    }}>

      {/* ── Header ── */}
      <div style={{
        display: "flex",
        alignItems: "center",
        justifyContent: "space-between",

        paddingBottom: "2mm",
        flexShrink: 0,
      }}>
        <div style={{ display: "flex", alignItems: "center", gap: "2mm" }}>
          {branding.logoUrl && <img src={branding.logoUrl} alt="" style={{ height: "9mm", width: "auto", objectFit: "contain" }} />}
          <div>
            <div style={{ fontSize: "9pt", fontWeight: 700 }}>{branding.companyName}</div>
            <div style={{ fontSize: "6pt", color: "#555" }}>{branding.website}</div>
          </div>
        </div>
        <div style={{ display: "flex", alignItems: "center", gap: "4mm" }}>
          <img src="/kd-logo.png" alt="KD" style={{ height: "10mm", width: "auto", objectFit: "contain" }} />
          {qrDataUrl && (
            <img src={qrDataUrl} alt="QR" style={{ width: "12mm", height: "12mm", display: "block" }} />
          )}
          <div style={{ textAlign: "right", fontSize: "6pt", color: "#555" }}>
            <div>Utskrivet</div>
            <div style={{ fontWeight: 700, color: "#000" }}>{today}</div>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* ── NOM DIM ── */}
      <div style={{ flexShrink: 0, lineHeight: 1 }}>
        <div style={{ fontSize: "6pt", textTransform: "uppercase", color: "#666", letterSpacing: "0.1em", marginBottom: "0.5mm" }}>
          Nom. mått
        </div>
        <div style={{ fontSize: "68pt", fontWeight: 900, letterSpacing: "0.02em", lineHeight: 1 }}>
          {nomDimShort ?? "—"}
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* ── ACT DIM + Profil/Träslag ── */}
      <div style={{
        display: "flex",
        alignItems: "baseline",
        gap: "6mm",
        flexShrink: 0,
        borderBottom: "1px solid #ccc",
        paddingBottom: "2mm",
      }}>
        {hasActDim && (

            <div style={{ fontSize: "6pt", textTransform: "uppercase", color: "#666", letterSpacing: "0.1em" }}>Akt. mått</div>
            <div style={{ fontSize: "36pt", fontWeight: 900, lineHeight: 1 }}>{actDimShort}</div>
          </div>
        )}
        <div style={{ fontSize: "14pt", fontWeight: 700, color: "#222" }}>
          {[post.profil, post.traslag, post.kvalitet].filter(Boolean).join(" · ")}
        </div>
        <div style={{ marginLeft: "auto", textAlign: "right", fontSize: "10pt", color: "#333" }}>
          {actM3 != null
            ? <span>{actM3.toFixed(3)} m³</span>
            : nomM3 != null
              ? <span>{nomM3.toFixed(3)} m³</span>
              : null}
          {post.plats && <div style={{ fontSize: "9pt", color: "#555" }}>{post.plats}</div>}
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* ── Längd + Antal — antingen tabell (blandade) eller stor text ── */}
      {isBlandad ? (
        <div style={{ flex: 1, minHeight: 0, overflow: "hidden" }}>
          <div style={{ fontSize: "6pt", textTransform: "uppercase", color: "#666", letterSpacing: "0.1em", marginBottom: "1mm" }}>

            {post.pktNr || "—"}

          <div style={{ fontSize: "10pt", color: "#333", fontWeight: 600, textAlign: "right" }}>{post.plats}</div>
        )}

    </div>
  );

function MiniStrip({ post, nomM3, actM3, nomDimShort, actDimShort, qrDataUrl, allPosts, totalAntal }: {
  post: LagerPost;
  nomM3: number | null;
  actM3: number | null;
  nomDimShort: string | null;
  actDimShort: string | null;
  qrDataUrl?: string;
  allPosts: LagerPost[] | null;
  totalAntal: number | null;
}) {
  const isBlandad = !!allPosts;

  return (
    <div style={{
      height: "39mm",
      flexShrink: 0,
      padding: "0 7mm",
      display: "flex",
      alignItems: "center",
      justifyContent: "space-between",
      gap: "4mm",
      background: "#f0f0f0",
      borderTop: "1px dashed #999",
      borderBottom: "1px dashed #999",
    }}>
      <div style={{ flexShrink: 0 }}>
        <div style={{ fontSize: "5.5pt", color: "#666", textTransform: "uppercase", letterSpacing: "0.1em" }}>Pkt-Nr</div>
        <div style={{ fontSize: "26pt", fontWeight: 900, lineHeight: 1 }}>{post.pktNr || "—"}</div>
      </div>

      <div style={{ flex: 1, fontSize: "8pt", lineHeight: 1.6 }}>
        <div>
          {nomDimShort && <span style={{ fontWeight: 900, fontSize: "14pt" }}>{nomDimShort}</span>}
          {actDimShort && <span style={{ color: "#333", fontSize: "11pt" }}> → {actDimShort}</span>}
          {!isBlandad && post.langdDm && <span style={{ color: "#333" }}> &nbsp;| {Number(post.langdDm) * 100} mm</span>}
          {!isBlandad && post.antal != null && <span style={{ color: "#333" }}> &nbsp;| {post.antal} st</span>}
          {isBlandad && totalAntal != null && <span style={{ color: "#333" }}> &nbsp;| Blandade längder | {totalAntal} st</span>}
        </div>
        <div>
          {[post.profil, post.traslag, post.kvalitet].filter(Boolean).join(" · ")}
          {(actM3 ?? nomM3) != null && <span style={{ color: "#555" }}> &nbsp;| {(actM3 ?? nomM3)!.toFixed(3)} m³</span>}
        </div>
        {isBlandad && (
          <div style={{ fontSize: "7pt", color: "#444", marginTop: "0.5mm" }}>
            {allPosts.map((p, i) => (
              <span key={i}>
                {i > 0 && " · "}
                {p.langdDm ? `${Number(p.langdDm) * 100} mm` : "—"} × {p.antal ?? "—"} st
              </span>
            ))}
          </div>
        )}
      </div>

      {qrDataUrl && (
        <img src={qrDataUrl} alt="QR" style={{ width: "20mm", height: "20mm", flexShrink: 0 }} />
      )}
    </div>
  );
}


================ FILE: artifacts/lager/src/pages/inventory/duplicatePrefill.test.ts ================

import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import {
  farSkapaLagerpost,
  foreslaDupliceratPaketnummer,
  skapaSakerDupliceringsPrefill,
} from "./duplicatePrefill";

describe("säker duplicering av lagerpost", () => {
  it("kopierar produktuppgifter och utförande men aldrig interna integrationsfält", () => {
    const result = skapaSakerDupliceringsPrefill({
      id: 159682,
      pktNr: "159682",
      utforande: "4RH",
      nomTjocklek: 50,
      anteckning: "Gebhardt",
      externSourceId: "hemligt-source-id",
      externRevision: 7,
      idempotencyKey: "ska-inte-med",
      skapadAt: "2026-09-15",
      raderadAt: null,
    });

    expect(result).toMatchObject({
      pktNr: "159682",
      utforande: "4RH",
      nomTjocklek: 50,
      anteckning: "Gebhardt",
    });
    expect(result).not.toHaveProperty("id");
    expect(result).not.toHaveProperty("externSourceId");
    expect(result).not.toHaveProperty("externRevision");
    expect(result).not.toHaveProperty("idempotencyKey");
    expect(result).not.toHaveProperty("skapadAt");
    expect(result).not.toHaveProperty("raderadAt");
  });

  it("kopierar inte Paketlapps integrationsursprung till en manuell kopia", () => {
    expect(
      skapaSakerDupliceringsPrefill({ produktion: "PAKETLAPP_HYVLERI" }),
    ).toEqual({ produktion: null });
  });

  it("blockerar skapande under kontroll, vid dubblett och vid kontrollfel", () => {
    expect(farSkapaLagerpost("checking", true)).toBe(false);
    expect(farSkapaLagerpost("duplicate", true)).toBe(false);
    expect(farSkapaLagerpost("error", true)).toBe(false);
    expect(farSkapaLagerpost("available", true)).toBe(true);
    expect(farSkapaLagerpost("idle", false)).toBe(true);
  });

  it("föreslår 159683 från 159682 men tillåter inte förslaget när det är upptaget", () => {
    expect(foreslaDupliceratPaketnummer("159682")).toBe("159683");
    expect(farSkapaLagerpost("duplicate", true)).toBe(false);
  });
});

================ FILE: artifacts/lager/src/pages/inventory/duplicatePrefill.ts ================

export type PaketnummerkontrollStatus =
  | "idle"
  | "checking"
  | "available"
  | "duplicate"
  | "error";

const DUPLICERBARA_FALT = [
  "vilmabasId",
  "mottaget",
  "plats",
  "omradeId",
  "fackId",
  "anmarkNing",
  "pktNr",
  "produktion",
  "profil",
  "varuslag",
  "traslag",
  "behandling",
  "utforande",
  "kvalitet",
  "nomTjocklek",
  "nomBredd",
  "actTjocklek",
  "actBredd",
  "antal",
  "langdDm",
  "m3",
  "m3Pris",
  "m3Sum",
  "lpm",
  "actM3",
  "anteckning",
  "leverantor",
] as const;

export function skapaSakerDupliceringsPrefill(
  post: Record<string, unknown>,
): Record<string, unknown> {
  const prefill: Record<string, unknown> = {};
  for (const falt of DUPLICERBARA_FALT) {
    if (Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(post, falt)) {
      prefill[falt] = post[falt];
    }
  }

  if (prefill.produktion === "PAKETLAPP_HYVLERI") {
    prefill.produktion = null;
  }

  return prefill;
}

export function foreslaDupliceratPaketnummer(pktNr: unknown): string {
  const original = String(pktNr ?? "").trim();
  const numeric = Number(original);
  return original && Number.isInteger(numeric) ? String(numeric + 1) : "";
}

export function farSkapaLagerpost(
  status: PaketnummerkontrollStatus,
  harPaketnummer: boolean,
): boolean {
  return !harPaketnummer || status === "available";
}
