import { useMemo } from "react";
import { Link, useSearch, useLocation } from "wouter";
import { useQuery } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import { useListLagerPoster, getListLagerPosterQueryKey, useListLagerOmraden } from "@workspace/api-client-react";
import { customFetch } from "@workspace/api-client-react";
import { Card, CardContent, CardHeader, CardTitle } from "@/components/ui/card";
import { Badge } from "@/components/ui/badge";
import { Skeleton } from "@/components/ui/skeleton";
import { Input } from "@/components/ui/input";
import { Select, SelectContent, SelectItem, SelectTrigger, SelectValue } from "@/components/ui/select";
import { MapPin, Package, BarChart2, Map as MapIcon, List, Search, X, Boxes, LayoutGrid } from "lucide-react";
import { LagerkartaSVG } from "@/components/LagerkartaSVG";
import type { OmgangDetalj } from "@/pages/inventering/types";
import { beraknaOmradesprogress, omradeskartaHref } from "./inventeringskarta";

type DimGroup = { dim: string; totalM3: number; antal: number; posterAntal: number };
type ProfilGroup = { profil: string; totalM3: number; dims: DimGroup[] };
type PlatsStats = {
  namn: string;
  totalM3: number;
  posterAntal: number;
  totalAntal: number;
  profilGroups: ProfilGroup[];
};

type SortOption = "m3_desc" | "poster_desc" | "namn_asc";

export default function LagerplatsPage() {
  const searchString = useSearch();
  const [, setLocation] = useLocation();

  const params = new URLSearchParams(searchString);
  const omgangId = Number(params.get("omgangId")) || null;
  const view = (params.get("view") as "karta" | "lista") || (omgangId ? "karta" : "lista");
  const search = params.get("sok") ?? "";
  const sortBy = (params.get("sortering") as SortOption) || "m3_desc";
  const filterProfil = params.get("profil") ?? "__all__";

  // Per-key defaults — a param is omitted from the URL when it equals its default.
  const PARAM_DEFAULTS: Record<string, string> = {
    view: "lista",
    sok: "",
    sortering: "m3_desc",
    profil: "__all__",
  };

  function updateParams(updates: Record<string, string>) {
    const next = new URLSearchParams(searchString);
    for (const [key, val] of Object.entries(updates)) {
      if (val === PARAM_DEFAULTS[key]) {
        next.delete(key);
      } else {
        next.set(key, val);
      }
    }
    const qs = next.toString();
    setLocation(qs ? `/lagerplatser?${qs}` : "/lagerplatser", { replace: true });
  }

  const setView = (v: "karta" | "lista") => updateParams({ view: v });
  const setSearch = (v: string) => updateParams({ sok: v });
  const setSortBy = (v: SortOption) => updateParams({ sortering: v });
  const setFilterProfil = (v: string) => updateParams({ profil: v });

  const { data: listResponse, isLoading } = useListLagerPoster(
    { status: "i_lager", limit: 3000 },
    { query: { queryKey: getListLagerPosterQueryKey({ status: "i_lager", limit: 3000 }) } }
  );
  const { data: omradenResponse } = useListLagerOmraden();
  const { data: omgang } = useQuery<OmgangDetalj>({
    queryKey: ["/api/inventeringsomgangar", omgangId],
    queryFn: () => customFetch<OmgangDetalj>(`/api/inventeringsomgangar/${omgangId}`),
    enabled: !!omgangId,
    refetchOnMount: "always",
  });

  const allPaket = listResponse?.data ?? [];
  const omradenList = omradenResponse ?? [];

  // Auktoritativ statistik för områden med strukturerade fack, nyckel =
  // slug. Används av kartan (för zoner med `detailSlug`) och av
  // "Områden"-listan nedan, så att siffrorna alltid matchar detaljvyn.
  const omradeStatsBySlug = useMemo(
    () =>
      Object.fromEntries(
        omradenList.map((o) => [o.slug, { namn: o.namn, m3: o.totalM3, count: o.posterAntal, pkt: o.totalAntal }]),
      ),
    [omradenList],
  );
  const inventeringsprogressBySlug = useMemo(() => {
    if (!omgang) return undefined;
    const perOmrade = beraknaOmradesprogress(omgang.fack);
    return Object.fromEntries(
      omradenList.flatMap((omrade) => {
        const progress = perOmrade.get(omrade.id);
        return progress ? [[omrade.slug, progress]] : [];
      }),
    );
  }, [omgang, omradenList]);

  const platsStats = useMemo<Map<string, PlatsStats>>(() => {
    const map = new Map<string, PlatsStats>();
    for (const post of allPaket) {
      const key = post.plats ?? "(Okänd plats)";
      if (!map.has(key)) map.set(key, { namn: key, totalM3: 0, posterAntal: 0, totalAntal: 0, profilGroups: [] });
      const ps = map.get(key)!;
      const m3 = post.m3 ? Number(post.m3) : 0;
      ps.totalM3 += m3;
      ps.posterAntal++;
      ps.totalAntal += post.antal ?? 0;

      const profilKey = post.profil ?? "Övrigt";
      let pg = ps.profilGroups.find(g => g.profil === profilKey);
      if (!pg) { pg = { profil: profilKey, totalM3: 0, dims: [] }; ps.profilGroups.push(pg); }
      pg.totalM3 += m3;

      const dim = post.nomTjocklek && post.nomBredd ? `${post.nomTjocklek}×${post.nomBredd}` : "Okänd dim";
      let dg = pg.dims.find(d => d.dim === dim);
      if (!dg) { dg = { dim, totalM3: 0, antal: 0, posterAntal: 0 }; pg.dims.push(dg); }
      dg.totalM3 += m3;
      dg.antal += post.antal ?? 0;
      dg.posterAntal++;
    }
    map.forEach(ps => {
      ps.totalM3 = Math.round(ps.totalM3 * 1000) / 1000;
      ps.profilGroups.sort((a, b) => b.totalM3 - a.totalM3);
      ps.profilGroups.forEach(pg => {
        pg.totalM3 = Math.round(pg.totalM3 * 1000) / 1000;
        pg.dims.sort((a, b) => b.totalM3 - a.totalM3);
        pg.dims.forEach(d => { d.totalM3 = Math.round(d.totalM3 * 1000) / 1000; });
      });
    });
    return map;
  }, [allPaket]);

  // Collect all unique profiler for the filter dropdown
  const allProfiler = useMemo(() => {
    const set = new Set<string>();
    platsStats.forEach(ps => ps.profilGroups.forEach(pg => set.add(pg.profil)));
    return Array.from(set).sort();
  }, [platsStats]);

  const platserList = useMemo(() => {
    let list = Array.from(platsStats.values());

    // Filter by profil
    if (filterProfil !== "__all__") {
      list = list.filter(ps => ps.profilGroups.some(pg => pg.profil === filterProfil));
    }

    // Filter by search
    const q = search.trim().toLowerCase();
    if (q) {
      list = list.filter(ps => ps.namn.toLowerCase().includes(q));
    }

    // Sort
    if (sortBy === "m3_desc") {
      list.sort((a, b) => b.totalM3 - a.totalM3);
    } else if (sortBy === "poster_desc") {
      list.sort((a, b) => b.posterAntal - a.posterAntal);
    } else if (sortBy === "namn_asc") {
      list.sort((a, b) => a.namn.localeCompare(b.namn, "sv"));
    }

    return list;
  }, [platsStats, search, sortBy, filterProfil]);

  // Summary totals always over ALL places (no filter)
  const allPlatserList = useMemo(() => Array.from(platsStats.values()), [platsStats]);
  const totalM3All = allPlatserList.reduce((s, p) => s + p.totalM3, 0);

  const totalM3 = platserList.reduce((s, p) => s + p.totalM3, 0);

  const hasFilters = search.trim() !== "" || filterProfil !== "__all__";

  return (
    <div className="space-y-4 h-full">
      <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row justify-between items-start sm:items-center gap-3">
        <div>
          <h1 className="text-2xl sm:text-3xl font-bold tracking-tight">{omgang ? `Inventera via karta · ${omgang.namn}` : "Lagerplatser"}</h1>
          <p className="text-muted-foreground text-sm">
            {omgang ? "Välj ett område för att börja eller fortsätta inventera." : "Klicka på en plats för att se vad som finns där."}
          </p>
        </div>
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
          <div className="flex border rounded-md overflow-hidden">
            <button
              onClick={() => setView("karta")}
              className={`px-3 py-1.5 text-sm flex items-center gap-1.5 transition-colors ${view === "karta" ? "bg-primary text-primary-foreground" : "hover:bg-muted"}`}
            >
              <MapIcon className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />Karta
            </button>
            <button
              onClick={() => setView("lista")}
              className={`px-3 py-1.5 text-sm flex items-center gap-1.5 border-l transition-colors ${view === "lista" ? "bg-primary text-primary-foreground" : "hover:bg-muted"}`}
            >
              <List className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />Lista
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>

      {view === "karta" && (
        <LagerkartaSVG
          platserStats={allPlatserList.map(p => ({
            namn: p.namn,
            m3: p.totalM3,
            count: p.posterAntal,
            pkt: p.totalAntal,
          }))}
          omradeStatsBySlug={omradeStatsBySlug}
          omgangId={omgangId ?? undefined}
          inventeringsprogressBySlug={inventeringsprogressBySlug}
        />
      )}

      {omradenList.length > 0 && (
        <div className="space-y-2">
          <h2 className="text-sm font-semibold text-muted-foreground flex items-center gap-1.5">
            <LayoutGrid className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />Områden ({omradenList.length})
          </h2>
          <div className="grid gap-2 grid-cols-2 sm:grid-cols-3 lg:grid-cols-4">
            {omradenList.map((o) => (
              <Link key={o.slug} href={omradeskartaHref(o.slug, omgangId)}>
                <Card className="hover:shadow-md transition-shadow cursor-pointer h-full">
                  <CardContent className="p-3 space-y-1">
                    <div className="font-medium text-sm truncate">{o.namn}</div>
                    <div className="text-xs text-muted-foreground flex items-center gap-2">
                      {inventeringsprogressBySlug?.[o.slug] ? (
                        <span className="flex items-center gap-1">
                          <Boxes className="h-3 w-3" />
                          {inventeringsprogressBySlug[o.slug].klara} / {inventeringsprogressBySlug[o.slug].totalt} klara
                        </span>
                      ) : (
                        <>
                          <span className="flex items-center gap-1"><Boxes className="h-3 w-3" />{o.fackAntal} fack</span>
                          <span>{o.totalM3.toFixed(1)} m³</span>
                        </>
                      )}
                    </div>
                  </CardContent>
                </Card>
              </Link>
            ))}
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

      {view === "lista" && (
        <>
          <div className="grid gap-3 grid-cols-2 sm:grid-cols-3">
            <Card>
              <CardContent className="p-4">
                <div className="text-sm text-muted-foreground">Platser</div>
                <div className="text-2xl font-bold">
                  {platserList.length}
                  {hasFilters && allPlatserList.length !== platserList.length && (
                    <span className="text-base font-normal text-muted-foreground"> / {allPlatserList.length}</span>
                  )}
                </div>
              </CardContent>
            </Card>
            <Card>
              <CardContent className="p-4">
                <div className="text-sm text-muted-foreground">Total volym</div>
                <div className="text-2xl font-bold">
                  {hasFilters ? totalM3.toFixed(1) : totalM3All.toFixed(1)} m³
                </div>
              </CardContent>
            </Card>
            <Card>
              <CardContent className="p-4">
                <div className="text-sm text-muted-foreground">Poster i lager</div>
                <div className="text-2xl font-bold">{platserList.reduce((s, p) => s + p.posterAntal, 0)}</div>
              </CardContent>
            </Card>
          </div>

          {/* Search, sort, filter controls */}
          <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row gap-2">
            <div className="relative flex-1">
              <Search className="absolute left-2.5 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 h-4 w-4 text-muted-foreground pointer-events-none" />
              <Input
                placeholder="Sök platsnamn…"
                value={search}
                onChange={e => setSearch(e.target.value)}
                className="pl-8 pr-8"
              />
              {search && (
                <button
                  onClick={() => setSearch("")}
                  className="absolute right-2.5 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 text-muted-foreground hover:text-foreground"
                  aria-label="Rensa sökning"
                >
                  <X className="h-4 w-4" />
                </button>
              )}
            </div>

            <Select value={sortBy} onValueChange={v => setSortBy(v as SortOption)}>
              <SelectTrigger className="w-full sm:w-48">
                <SelectValue placeholder="Sortering" />
              </SelectTrigger>
              <SelectContent>
                <SelectItem value="m3_desc">Volym (hög → låg)</SelectItem>
                <SelectItem value="poster_desc">Antal poster</SelectItem>
                <SelectItem value="namn_asc">Platsnamn A → Ö</SelectItem>
              </SelectContent>
            </Select>

            {allProfiler.length > 0 && (
              <Select value={filterProfil} onValueChange={setFilterProfil}>
                <SelectTrigger className="w-full sm:w-48">
                  <SelectValue placeholder="Varuslag" />
                </SelectTrigger>
                <SelectContent>
                  <SelectItem value="__all__">Alla varuslag</SelectItem>
                  {allProfiler.map(p => (
                    <SelectItem key={p} value={p}>{p}</SelectItem>
                  ))}
                </SelectContent>
              </Select>
            )}
          </div>

          {isLoading ? (
            <div className="grid gap-4 sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-3">
              {Array.from({ length: 6 }).map((_, i) => <Skeleton key={i} className="h-48 rounded-xl" />)}
            </div>
          ) : platserList.length === 0 ? (
            <div className="flex flex-col items-center justify-center py-16 text-center text-muted-foreground">
              <MapPin className="h-10 w-10 mb-3 opacity-30" />
              <p className="text-sm">Inga platser matchar sökningen.</p>
              {hasFilters && (
                <button
                  onClick={() => updateParams({ sok: "", profil: "__all__" })}
                  className="mt-2 text-xs text-primary hover:underline"
                >
                  Rensa filter
                </button>
              )}
            </div>
          ) : (
            <div className="grid gap-4 sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-3">
              {platserList.map((p) => {
                const pct = totalM3All > 0 ? (p.totalM3 / totalM3All) * 100 : 0;
                return (
                  <Card key={p.namn} className="hover:shadow-md transition-shadow">
                    <CardHeader className="pb-2">
                      <CardTitle className="flex items-center justify-between text-base">
                        <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                          <MapPin className="h-4 w-4 text-primary shrink-0" />
                          <span>{p.namn}</span>
                        </div>
                        <Badge variant="outline">{p.totalM3.toFixed(2)} m³</Badge>
                      </CardTitle>
                      <div className="h-1.5 bg-muted rounded-full overflow-hidden mt-1">
                        <div className="h-full bg-primary rounded-full" style={{ width: `${Math.min(pct, 100)}%` }} />
                      </div>
                      <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">{pct.toFixed(1)}% av total volym</p>
                    </CardHeader>
                    <CardContent className="space-y-3">
                      <div className="flex gap-4 text-sm">
                        <div><span className="text-muted-foreground">Poster: </span><span className="font-medium">{p.posterAntal} st</span></div>
                        <div><span className="text-muted-foreground">Styck: </span><span className="font-medium">{p.totalAntal} st</span></div>
                      </div>
                      {p.profilGroups.length > 0 && (
                        <div>
                          <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground mb-1.5 flex items-center gap-1">
                            <BarChart2 className="h-3 w-3" />Profiler (top 3)
                          </p>
                          <div className="space-y-1">
                            {p.profilGroups.slice(0, 3).map(pg => (
                              <div key={pg.profil} className="flex items-center justify-between text-xs">
                                <span className="font-medium">{pg.profil}</span>
                                <span className="text-muted-foreground">{pg.totalM3.toFixed(2)} m³</span>
                              </div>
                            ))}
                          </div>
                        </div>
                      )}
                      <Link href={`/lager?plats=${encodeURIComponent(p.namn)}`}
                        className="text-xs text-primary hover:underline flex items-center gap-1">
                        <Package className="h-3 w-3" />Visa alla poster
                      </Link>
                    </CardContent>
                  </Card>
                );
              })}
            </div>
          )}
        </>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}


================ FILE: artifacts/lager/src/pages/lagerplatser/OmradeDetaljPage.tsx ================

import { useState } from "react";
import { Link, useParams, useLocation, useSearch } from "wouter";
import { useMutation, useQuery } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import { useGetLagerOmrade, customFetch } from "@workspace/api-client-react";
import { Breadcrumb, BreadcrumbItem, BreadcrumbLink, BreadcrumbList, BreadcrumbPage, BreadcrumbSeparator } from "@/components/ui/breadcrumb";
import { Card, CardContent } from "@/components/ui/card";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { Sheet, SheetContent, SheetHeader, SheetTitle } from "@/components/ui/sheet";
import { Skeleton } from "@/components/ui/skeleton";
import { Package, Boxes, Ruler, ClipboardList, Loader2 } from "lucide-react";
import { OmradeDetaljkarta } from "@/components/omradeDetalj/OmradeDetaljkarta";
import type { FackMedStats } from "@/components/omradeDetalj/types";
import { useToast } from "@/hooks/use-toast";
import type { OmgangDetalj } from "@/pages/inventering/types";
import { fackAtgard, medInventeringsstatus, omradeskartaHref, sessionsHref } from "./inventeringskarta";

export default function OmradeDetaljPage() {
  const { slug } = useParams<{ slug: string }>();
  const { data: omrade, isLoading } = useGetLagerOmrade(slug ?? "");
  const searchString = useSearch();
  const omgangId = Number(new URLSearchParams(searchString).get("omgangId")) || null;
  const { data: omgang, isLoading: omgangLoading } = useQuery<OmgangDetalj>({
    queryKey: ["/api/inventeringsomgangar", omgangId],
    queryFn: () => customFetch<OmgangDetalj>(`/api/inventeringsomgangar/${omgangId}`),
    enabled: !!omgangId,
    refetchOnMount: "always",
  });
  const [selectedFack, setSelectedFack] = useState<FackMedStats | null>(null);
  const [, setLocation] = useLocation();
  const { toast } = useToast();

  const startaInventeringMutation = useMutation({
    mutationFn: (fackId: number) =>
      customFetch<{ atertagen: boolean; session: { id: number } }>("/api/inventering/start", {
        method: "POST",
        body: JSON.stringify({ omradeId: omrade?.id, fackId, ...(omgangId ? { omgangId } : {}) }),
      }),
    onSuccess: (data) => {
      if (data.atertagen) toast({ title: "Återupptar pågående inventering" });
      setLocation(sessionsHref("skanna", data.session.id, omgangId, slug));
    },
    onError: () => toast({ title: "Kunde inte starta inventering", variant: "destructive" }),
  });

  if (isLoading || (omgangId && omgangLoading)) {
    return (
      <div className="space-y-4">
        <Skeleton className="h-6 w-64" />
        <Skeleton className="h-80 rounded-xl" />
      </div>
    );
  }

  if (!omrade) {
    return (
      <div className="flex flex-col items-center justify-center py-16 text-center text-muted-foreground">
        <p className="text-sm">Området hittades inte.</p>
        <Link href="/lagerplatser" className="mt-2 text-xs text-primary hover:underline">Tillbaka till Lagerplatser</Link>
      </div>
    );
  }
  const fackMedStatus = medInventeringsstatus(omrade.fack, omrade.id, omgang);
  const inventeringslage = !!omgangId && !!omgang;

  const visaPaketHref = (fack?: FackMedStats | "none") => {
    const params = new URLSearchParams();
    params.set("omradeId", String(omrade.id));
    params.set("omradeNamn", omrade.namn);
    if (fack === "none") {
      params.set("fackId", "none");
    } else if (fack) {
      params.set("fackId", String(fack.id));
      params.set("fackKod", fack.kod);
    }
    return `/lager?${params.toString()}`;
  };

  return (
    <div className="space-y-4">
      <Breadcrumb>
        <BreadcrumbList>
          <BreadcrumbItem>
            <BreadcrumbLink asChild>
              <Link href={omgangId ? `/lagerplatser?omgangId=${omgangId}` : "/lagerplatser"}>
                {inventeringslage ? "Inventeringskarta" : "Lagerplatser"}
              </Link>
            </BreadcrumbLink>
          </BreadcrumbItem>
          <BreadcrumbSeparator />
          <BreadcrumbItem>
            <BreadcrumbPage>{omrade.namn}</BreadcrumbPage>
          </BreadcrumbItem>
        </BreadcrumbList>
      </Breadcrumb>

      <div>
        <h1 className="text-2xl sm:text-3xl font-bold tracking-tight">{omrade.namn}</h1>
        <p className="text-muted-foreground text-sm">
          {inventeringslage ? `Välj ett fack i ${omgang.namn}.` : "Klicka på ett fack för att se vad som finns där."}
        </p>
      </div>

      <div className="grid gap-3 grid-cols-2 sm:grid-cols-4">
        <Card>
          <CardContent className="p-4">
            <div className="text-sm text-muted-foreground">Poster i lager</div>
            <div className="text-2xl font-bold">{omrade.posterAntal}</div>
          </CardContent>
        </Card>
        <Card>
          <CardContent className="p-4">
            <div className="text-sm text-muted-foreground">Total volym</div>
            <div className="text-2xl font-bold">{omrade.totalM3.toFixed(1)} m³</div>
          </CardContent>
        </Card>
        <Card>
          <CardContent className="p-4">
            <div className="text-sm text-muted-foreground">Styck totalt</div>
            <div className="text-2xl font-bold">{omrade.totalAntal}</div>
          </CardContent>
        </Card>
        <Card>
          <CardContent className="p-4">
            <div className="text-sm text-muted-foreground">Antal fack</div>
            <div className="text-2xl font-bold">{omrade.fackAntal}</div>
          </CardContent>
        </Card>
      </div>

      <OmradeDetaljkarta slug={slug ?? ""} fack={fackMedStatus} onFackClick={setSelectedFack} selectedFackId={selectedFack?.id} />

      {omrade.ejAngivet.posterAntal > 0 && (
        <Card>
          <CardContent className="p-4 flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:items-center justify-between gap-2">
            <div>
              <div className="font-medium flex items-center gap-2">
                <ClipboardList className="h-4 w-4 text-muted-foreground" />
                Ej angivet fack
              </div>
              <div className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">
                {omrade.ejAngivet.posterAntal} poster · {omrade.ejAngivet.totalM3.toFixed(2)} m³
              </div>
            </div>
            <Link href={visaPaketHref("none")} className="text-xs text-primary hover:underline flex items-center gap-1">
              <Package className="h-3 w-3" />Visa paket
            </Link>
          </CardContent>
        </Card>
      )}

      <Sheet open={!!selectedFack} onOpenChange={(open) => !open && setSelectedFack(null)}>
        <SheetContent>
          {selectedFack && (
            <>
              <SheetHeader>
                <div className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">{omrade.namn}</div>
                <SheetTitle>Fack {selectedFack.kod}</SheetTitle>
              </SheetHeader>
              <div className="mt-6 space-y-4">
                <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3">
                  <div className="rounded-lg border p-3">
                    <div className="text-xs text-muted-foreground flex items-center gap-1"><Ruler className="h-3 w-3" />Volym</div>
                    <div className="text-xl font-bold">{selectedFack.totalM3.toFixed(2)} m³</div>
                  </div>
                  <div className="rounded-lg border p-3">
                    <div className="text-xs text-muted-foreground flex items-center gap-1"><Boxes className="h-3 w-3" />Styck</div>
                    <div className="text-xl font-bold">{selectedFack.totalAntal}</div>
                  </div>
                </div>
                <div className="text-sm text-muted-foreground">{selectedFack.posterAntal} poster i lager</div>
                {inventeringslage && (
                  <div className="rounded-md border bg-muted/30 px-3 py-2 text-sm font-medium">
                    {selectedFack.inventeringStatus === "ej_startad" ? "Ej påbörjad"
                      : selectedFack.inventeringStatus === "pagaende" ? "Pågår"
                      : selectedFack.inventeringStatus === "avslutad" ? "✓ Klar"
                      : selectedFack.inventeringStatus === "avbruten" ? "Avbruten"
                      : "Ingår ej"}
                  </div>
                )}

                <Link href={visaPaketHref(selectedFack)}>
                  <Button className="w-full" variant="default">
                    <Package className="h-4 w-4 mr-2" />Visa paket
                  </Button>
                </Link>
                {(!inventeringslage || (fackAtgard(selectedFack.inventeringStatus) === "starta" && omgang.status === "pagaende")) && (
                  <Button
                    className="w-full"
                    variant="outline"
                    disabled={startaInventeringMutation.isPending}
                    onClick={() => selectedFack && startaInventeringMutation.mutate(selectedFack.id)}
                  >
                    {startaInventeringMutation.isPending ? (
                      <Loader2 className="h-4 w-4 mr-2 animate-spin" />
                    ) : (
                      <ClipboardList className="h-4 w-4 mr-2" />
                    )}
                    {inventeringslage ? "Starta inventering" : "Inventera fack"}
                  </Button>
                )}
                {inventeringslage && fackAtgard(selectedFack.inventeringStatus) === "fortsatt" && selectedFack.inventeringSessionId && (
                  <Button
                    className="w-full"
                    variant="outline"
                    onClick={() => setLocation(sessionsHref("skanna", selectedFack.inventeringSessionId!, omgangId, slug))}
                  >
                    <ClipboardList className="h-4 w-4 mr-2" />Fortsätt inventera
                  </Button>
                )}
                {inventeringslage && fackAtgard(selectedFack.inventeringStatus) === "visa" && selectedFack.inventeringSessionId && (
                  <Button
                    className="w-full"
                    variant="outline"
                    onClick={() => setLocation(sessionsHref("resultat", selectedFack.inventeringSessionId!, omgangId, slug))}
                  >
                    Visa resultat
                  </Button>
                )}
              </div>
            </>
          )}
        </SheetContent>
      </Sheet>
    </div>
  );
}

import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import type { FackMedStats } from "@/components/omradeDetalj/types";
import type { OmgangDetalj, OmgangFackRad } from "@/pages/inventering/types";
import {
  beraknaOmradesprogress,
  fackAtgard,
  inventeringsstatusForFack,
  medInventeringsstatus,
  omradeskartaHref,
  sessionsHref,
} from "../inventeringskarta";

const scope: OmgangFackRad[] = [
  { fackId: 1, fackKod: "V01", fackAktivKod: "V01", omradeIdVidStart: 10, omradeNamnVidStart: "Röda", sessionId: null, sessionStatus: "ej_startad" },
  { fackId: 2, fackKod: "V02", fackAktivKod: "V02", omradeIdVidStart: 10, omradeNamnVidStart: "Röda", sessionId: 202, sessionStatus: "pagaende" },
  { fackId: 3, fackKod: "V03", fackAktivKod: "V03", omradeIdVidStart: 10, omradeNamnVidStart: "Röda", sessionId: 203, sessionStatus: "avslutad" },
  { fackId: 4, fackKod: "V04", fackAktivKod: "V04", omradeIdVidStart: 10, omradeNamnVidStart: "Röda", sessionId: 204, sessionStatus: "avbruten" },
  { fackId: 1, fackKod: "V01", fackAktivKod: "V01", omradeIdVidStart: 20, omradeNamnVidStart: "Lastplan", sessionId: 205, sessionStatus: "avslutad" },
];

const liveFack: FackMedStats[] = [
  { id: 1, kod: "V01", sortering: 1, totalM3: 1, posterAntal: 1, totalAntal: 1 },
  { id: 2, kod: "V02", sortering: 2, totalM3: 1, posterAntal: 1, totalAntal: 1 },
  { id: 3, kod: "V03", sortering: 3, totalM3: 1, posterAntal: 1, totalAntal: 1 },
  { id: 4, kod: "V04", sortering: 4, totalM3: 1, posterAntal: 1, totalAntal: 1 },
  { id: 99, kod: "V99", sortering: 99, totalM3: 0, posterAntal: 0, totalAntal: 0 },
];

const omgang = { id: 7, status: "pagaende", fack: scope } as OmgangDetalj;

describe("inventeringskarta", () => {
  it("beräknar områdesprogress enbart från fryst scope", () => {
    expect(beraknaOmradesprogress(scope).get(10)).toEqual({ klara: 1, totalt: 4 });
    expect(beraknaOmradesprogress(scope).get(20)).toEqual({ klara: 1, totalt: 1 });
  });

  it("matchar scope med både fack-ID och område", () => {
    expect(inventeringsstatusForFack(1, 10, scope)).toEqual({ status: "ej_startad", sessionId: null });
    expect(inventeringsstatusForFack(1, 20, scope)).toEqual({ status: "avslutad", sessionId: 205 });
    expect(inventeringsstatusForFack(99, 10, scope)).toEqual({ status: "ingar_ej", sessionId: null });
  });

  it("visar alla statusar separat och blockerar fack utanför scope", () => {
    const markerade = medInventeringsstatus(liveFack, 10, omgang);
    expect(markerade.map((f) => f.inventeringStatus)).toEqual([
      "ej_startad",
      "pagaende",
      "avslutad",
      "avbruten",
      "ingar_ej",
    ]);
    expect(fackAtgard(markerade[0].inventeringStatus)).toBe("starta");
    expect(fackAtgard(markerade[1].inventeringStatus)).toBe("fortsatt");
    expect(fackAtgard(markerade[2].inventeringStatus)).toBe("visa");
    expect(fackAtgard(markerade[3].inventeringStatus)).toBe("visa");
    expect(fackAtgard(markerade[4].inventeringStatus)).toBe("blockerad");
  });

  it("bevarar omgångs- och kartkontext i navigeringen", () => {
    expect(omradeskartaHref("roda-magasin", 7)).toBe("/lagerplatser/omrade/roda-magasin?omgangId=7");
    expect(sessionsHref("skanna", 202, 7, "roda-magasin")).toBe("/inventering/skanna/202?omgangId=7&kartOmrade=roda-magasin");
    expect(sessionsHref("resultat", 203, 7, "roda-magasin")).toBe("/inventering/203?omgangId=7&kartOmrade=roda-magasin");
  });

  it("behåller normala Lagerplatser-URL:er utan omgångskontext", () => {
    expect(medInventeringsstatus(liveFack, 10)).toBe(liveFack);
    expect(fackAtgard()).toBe("normal");
    expect(omradeskartaHref("roda-magasin")).toBe("/lagerplatser/omrade/roda-magasin");
    expect(sessionsHref("skanna", 202)).toBe("/inventering/skanna/202");
  });
});

================ FILE: artifacts/lager/src/pages/lagerplatser/inventeringskarta.ts ================

import type { FackMedStats, InventeringskartStatus } from "@/components/omradeDetalj/types";
import type { OmgangDetalj, OmgangFackRad } from "@/pages/inventering/types";

export type Omradesprogress = {
  klara: number;
  totalt: number;
};

export function inventeringsQuery(omgangId: number, kartOmrade?: string): string {
  const params = new URLSearchParams({ omgangId: String(omgangId) });
  if (kartOmrade) params.set("kartOmrade", kartOmrade);
  return params.toString();
}

export function omradeskartaHref(slug: string, omgangId?: number | null): string {
  const bas = `/lagerplatser/omrade/${slug}`;
  return omgangId ? `${bas}?${inventeringsQuery(omgangId)}` : bas;
}

export function sessionsHref(
  typ: "skanna" | "resultat",
  sessionId: number,
  omgangId?: number | null,
  kartOmrade?: string,
): string {
  const bas = typ === "skanna" ? `/inventering/skanna/${sessionId}` : `/inventering/${sessionId}`;
  return omgangId && kartOmrade ? `${bas}?${inventeringsQuery(omgangId, kartOmrade)}` : bas;
}

export function beraknaOmradesprogress(fack: OmgangFackRad[]): Map<number, Omradesprogress> {
  const progress = new Map<number, Omradesprogress>();
  for (const rad of fack) {
    const nu = progress.get(rad.omradeIdVidStart) ?? { klara: 0, totalt: 0 };
    nu.totalt += 1;
    if (rad.sessionStatus === "avslutad") nu.klara += 1;
    progress.set(rad.omradeIdVidStart, nu);
  }
  return progress;
}

export function inventeringsstatusForFack(
  fackId: number,
  omradeId: number,
  scope: OmgangFackRad[],
): { status: InventeringskartStatus; sessionId: number | null } {
  const scopeRad = scope.find((rad) => rad.fackId === fackId && rad.omradeIdVidStart === omradeId);
  if (!scopeRad) return { status: "ingar_ej", sessionId: null };
  return { status: scopeRad.sessionStatus, sessionId: scopeRad.sessionId };
}

export function medInventeringsstatus(
  fack: FackMedStats[],
  omradeId: number,
  omgang?: OmgangDetalj,
): FackMedStats[] {
  if (!omgang) return fack;
  return fack.map((rad) => {
    const inventering = inventeringsstatusForFack(rad.id, omradeId, omgang.fack);
    return {
      ...rad,
      inventeringStatus: inventering.status,
      inventeringSessionId: inventering.sessionId,
    };
  });
}

export function fackAtgard(status?: InventeringskartStatus): "normal" | "starta" | "fortsatt" | "visa" | "blockerad" {
  if (!status) return "normal";
  if (status === "ej_startad") return "starta";
  if (status === "pagaende") return "fortsatt";
  if (status === "avslutad" || status === "avbruten") return "visa";
  return "blockerad";
}

================ FILE: artifacts/lager/src/pages/larm/LarmPage.tsx ================

import { useState } from "react";
import { useQuery, useQueryClient } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import { customFetch } from "@workspace/api-client-react";
import { Card, CardContent, CardHeader, CardTitle } from "@/components/ui/card";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { Input } from "@/components/ui/input";
import { Label } from "@/components/ui/label";
import { Switch } from "@/components/ui/switch";
import { Badge } from "@/components/ui/badge";
import { useToast } from "@/hooks/use-toast";
import { BellRing, Plus, Trash2, Loader2, Bell } from "lucide-react";

interface Larm {
  id: number;
  namn: string;
  nomTjocklek: number | null;
  nomBredd: number | null;
  langdDm: number | null;
  varuslag: string | null;
  profil: string | null;
  minM3: number;
  aktiv: boolean;
}

interface TriggeredLarm {
  id: number;
  namn: string;
  currentM3: number;
  minM3: number;
  nomTjocklek: number | null;
  nomBredd: number | null;
  langdDm: number | null;
  varuslag: string | null;
  profil: string | null;
}

const EMPTY_FORM = {
  namn: "",
  nomTjocklek: "" as number | "",
  nomBredd: "" as number | "",
  langdM: "",
  varuslag: "",
  profil: "",
  minM3: "" as number | "",
};

const PROFIL_SUGGESTIONS = [
  "Klädsel", "C24", "Trall", "Sågat", "Regel", "Stolpe",
  "Panel", "Bräda", "Fjällpanel", "Tegelläkt", "Lockläkt", "Vindskivor",
];

function dmToM(dm: number): string {
  return (dm / 10).toFixed(1).replace(".", ",");
}

function formatLarmFilters(l: Larm): string {
  const parts: string[] = [];
  if (l.profil) parts.push(l.profil);
  if (l.varuslag) parts.push(l.varuslag);
  if (l.nomTjocklek || l.nomBredd) parts.push(`${l.nomTjocklek ?? "?"}×${l.nomBredd ?? "?"}`);
  if (l.langdDm) parts.push(`${dmToM(l.langdDm)} m`);
  return parts.join(" · ") || "Alla poster";
}

export default function LarmPage() {
  const { toast } = useToast();
  const queryClient = useQueryClient();
  const [form, setForm] = useState(EMPTY_FORM);
  const [saving, setSaving] = useState(false);

  const { data: larm = [], isLoading } = useQuery<Larm[]>({
    queryKey: ["/api/larm"],
    queryFn: () => customFetch<Larm[]>("/api/larm"),
  });

  const { data: triggered = [] } = useQuery<TriggeredLarm[]>({
    queryKey: ["/api/larm/status"],
    queryFn: () => customFetch<TriggeredLarm[]>("/api/larm/status"),
    refetchInterval: 60_000,
  });

  async function handleAdd() {
    if (!form.namn || form.minM3 === "") {
      toast({ title: "Fyll i namn och minsta volym", variant: "destructive" }); return;
    }
    const langdDm = form.langdM !== ""
      ? Math.round(parseFloat(form.langdM.replace(",", ".")) * 10)
      : null;
    if (form.langdM !== "" && isNaN(langdDm!)) {
      toast({ title: "Ogiltigt längdvärde", variant: "destructive" }); return;
    }
    setSaving(true);
    try {
      await customFetch("/api/larm", {
        method: "POST",
        headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
        body: JSON.stringify({
          namn: form.namn,
          nomTjocklek: form.nomTjocklek !== "" ? Number(form.nomTjocklek) : null,
          nomBredd: form.nomBredd !== "" ? Number(form.nomBredd) : null,
          langdDm,
          varuslag: form.varuslag || null,
          profil: form.profil || null,
          minM3: Number(form.minM3),
        }),
      });
      queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["/api/larm"] });
      queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["/api/larm/status"] });
      setForm(EMPTY_FORM);
      toast({ title: "Larm sparat" });
    } catch {
      toast({ title: "Kunde inte spara larm", variant: "destructive" });
    } finally {
      setSaving(false);
    }
  }

  async function toggleAktiv(l: Larm) {
    await customFetch(`/api/larm/${l.id}`, {
      method: "PUT",
      headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
      body: JSON.stringify({ aktiv: !l.aktiv }),
    });
    queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["/api/larm"] });
    queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["/api/larm/status"] });
  }

  async function handleDelete(id: number) {
    if (!confirm("Ta bort detta larm?")) return;
    await customFetch(`/api/larm/${id}`, { method: "DELETE" });
    queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["/api/larm"] });
    queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["/api/larm/status"] });
    toast({ title: "Larm borttaget" });
  }

  return (
    <div className="space-y-6">
      <div>
        <h1 className="text-2xl sm:text-3xl font-bold tracking-tight">Larmgränser</h1>
        <p className="text-muted-foreground text-sm">
          Skapa larm som triggas när volymen sjunker under ett tröskelvärde.
          Kombinera profil, träslag, dimension och längd fritt — alla filter är valfria.
        </p>
      </div>

      {triggered.length > 0 && (
        <div className="rounded-lg border-2 border-destructive/50 bg-destructive/5 p-4 space-y-2">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2 font-semibold text-destructive">
            <BellRing className="h-5 w-5" />
            {triggered.length} larm {triggered.length === 1 ? "aktivt" : "aktiva"}
          </div>
          {triggered.map(t => (
            <div key={t.id} className="text-sm">
              <span className="font-medium">{t.namn}</span>
              {" — "}
              <span className="text-destructive">
                {t.currentM3.toFixed(2)} m³ i lager (under gränsen {t.minM3} m³)
              </span>
            </div>
          ))}
        </div>
      )}

      <div className="grid gap-6 lg:grid-cols-2">
        <Card>
          <CardHeader>
            <CardTitle className="flex items-center gap-2">
              <Plus className="h-4 w-4" />
              Nytt larm
            </CardTitle>
          </CardHeader>
          <CardContent className="space-y-4">
            <div className="space-y-1">
              <Label>Namn på larmet</Label>
              <Input
                value={form.namn}
                onChange={e => setForm(f => ({ ...f, namn: e.target.value }))}
                placeholder="t.ex. Klädsel 22×170 3,6m"
              />
            </div>

            <div className="space-y-1">
              <Label>Profil — valfritt</Label>
              <Input
                value={form.profil}
                onChange={e => setForm(f => ({ ...f, profil: e.target.value }))}
                placeholder="t.ex. Klädsel"
                list="profil-larm"
              />
              <datalist id="profil-larm">
                {PROFIL_SUGGESTIONS.map(v => <option key={v} value={v} />)}
              </datalist>
            </div>

            <div className="space-y-1">
              <Label>Träslag — valfritt</Label>
              <Input
                value={form.varuslag}
                onChange={e => setForm(f => ({ ...f, varuslag: e.target.value }))}
                placeholder="t.ex. Gran"
                list="varuslag-larm"
              />
              <datalist id="varuslag-larm">
                {["Gran", "Furu", "Imp"].map(v => <option key={v} value={v} />)}
              </datalist>
            </div>

            <div className="space-y-1">
              <Label>Dimension (tjocklek × bredd i mm) — valfritt</Label>
              <div className="flex gap-2 items-center">
                <Input
                  type="number"
                  value={form.nomTjocklek}
                  onChange={e => setForm(f => ({ ...f, nomTjocklek: e.target.value === "" ? "" : Number(e.target.value) }))}
                  placeholder="Tjocklek"
                  className="flex-1"
                />
                <span className="text-muted-foreground">×</span>
                <Input
                  type="number"
                  value={form.nomBredd}
                  onChange={e => setForm(f => ({ ...f, nomBredd: e.target.value === "" ? "" : Number(e.target.value) }))}
                  placeholder="Bredd"
                  className="flex-1"
                />
              </div>
            </div>

            <div className="space-y-1">
              <Label>Längd (meter) — valfritt</Label>
              <Input
                value={form.langdM}
                onChange={e => setForm(f => ({ ...f, langdM: e.target.value }))}
                placeholder="t.ex. 3,6"
                inputMode="decimal"
              />
              <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">
                Ange i meter med komma, t.ex. 3,6 eller 4,2. Lämna tomt för alla längder.
              </p>
            </div>

            <div className="space-y-1">
              <Label>Larmgräns — under X m³ triggas larmet</Label>
              <Input
                type="number"
                step="0.1"
                value={form.minM3}
                onChange={e => setForm(f => ({ ...f, minM3: e.target.value === "" ? "" : Number(e.target.value) }))}
                placeholder="t.ex. 5"
              />
            </div>

            <Button className="w-full gap-2" onClick={handleAdd} disabled={saving}>
              {saving ? <Loader2 className="h-4 w-4 animate-spin" /> : <Plus className="h-4 w-4" />}
              Skapa larm
            </Button>
          </CardContent>
        </Card>

        <div className="space-y-3">
          <h2 className="font-semibold text-sm text-muted-foreground uppercase tracking-wide">Befintliga larm</h2>
          {isLoading ? (
            <div className="text-muted-foreground text-sm">Laddar...</div>
          ) : larm.length === 0 ? (
            <div className="text-center text-muted-foreground py-12 border rounded-lg">
              <Bell className="h-8 w-8 mx-auto mb-2 opacity-30" />
              <p className="text-sm">Inga larm skapade ännu.</p>
            </div>
          ) : (
            larm.map(l => {
              const isTriggered = triggered.some(t => t.id === l.id);
              return (
                <Card key={l.id} className={isTriggered ? "border-destructive/50" : ""}>
                  <CardContent className="p-4">
                    <div className="flex items-start justify-between gap-2">
                      <div className="flex-1 space-y-1">
                        <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                          <span className="font-medium text-sm">{l.namn}</span>
                          {isTriggered && (
                            <Badge variant="destructive" className="text-xs gap-1">
                              <BellRing className="h-3 w-3" /> Larm
                            </Badge>
                          )}
                        </div>
                        <div className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">
                          <span className="font-medium text-foreground">{formatLarmFilters(l)}</span>
                        </div>
                        <div className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">
                          Gräns: <span className="font-medium text-foreground">{l.minM3} m³</span>
                          {isTriggered && (() => {
                            const t = triggered.find(t => t.id === l.id);
                            return t ? <span className="text-destructive ml-2">({t.currentM3.toFixed(2)} m³ nu)</span> : null;
                          })()}
                        </div>
                      </div>
                      <div className="flex items-center gap-2 shrink-0">
                        <Switch
                          checked={l.aktiv}
                          onCheckedChange={() => toggleAktiv(l)}
                          title={l.aktiv ? "Inaktivera" : "Aktivera"}
                        />
                        <Button
                          variant="ghost"
                          size="icon"
                          className="h-8 w-8 text-muted-foreground hover:text-destructive"
                          onClick={() => handleDelete(l.id)}
                        >
                          <Trash2 className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
                        </Button>
                      </div>
                    </div>
                  </CardContent>
                </Card>
              );
            })
          )}
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}


================ FILE: artifacts/lager/src/pages/lego/LegoList.tsx ================

import { useEffect, useState, useMemo } from "react";
import { useLocation } from "wouter";
import { customFetch } from "@workspace/api-client-react";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { Badge } from "@/components/ui/badge";
import { Input } from "@/components/ui/input";
import { Plus, Wrench, Trash2, ChevronRight, Search, X, BarChart2, FileDown, Link2, ScanLine, Loader2 } from "lucide-react";
import { useToast } from "@/hooks/use-toast";
import { useUser } from "@clerk/react";
import LegoSkannaDialog, { type LegoPaketRow, type LegoSkanMeta } from "@/components/LegoSkannaDialog";
import {
  AlertDialog,
  AlertDialogAction,
  AlertDialogCancel,
  AlertDialogContent,
  AlertDialogDescription,
  AlertDialogFooter,
  AlertDialogHeader,
  AlertDialogTitle,
} from "@/components/ui/alert-dialog";
import {
  Select,
  SelectContent,
  SelectItem,
  SelectTrigger,
  SelectValue,
} from "@/components/ui/select";
import { Link } from "wouter";

type LegoJobbSummary = {
  id: number;
  jobbNr: number | null;
  kund: string;
  datumIn: string | null;
  datumUt: string | null;
  status: string;
  anteckning: string | null;
  kopplatJobbId: number | null;
  kopplatSpecId: number | null;
  skapadAt: string;
  antalPaket: number;
  totalM3: number;
  totalActM3: number;
  totalPris: number;
};

type TreeRow = {
  job: LegoJobbSummary;
  depth: number;
  isLastChild: boolean;
};

function statusBadge(status: string) {
  if (status === "levererat") return <Badge className="bg-green-100 text-green-800 border-green-200">Levererat</Badge>;
  if (status === "hyvlat") return <Badge className="bg-orange-100 text-orange-800 border-orange-200">Hyvlat</Badge>;
  return <Badge variant="outline">Mottaget</Badge>;
}

function buildTree(jobs: LegoJobbSummary[]): TreeRow[] {
  const byId = new Map(jobs.map(j => [j.id, j]));
  const childrenOf = new Map<number, LegoJobbSummary[]>();
  const childIds = new Set<number>();

  for (const job of jobs) {
    if (job.kopplatJobbId && byId.has(job.kopplatJobbId)) {
      const existing = childrenOf.get(job.kopplatJobbId) ?? [];
      existing.push(job);
      childrenOf.set(job.kopplatJobbId, existing);
      childIds.add(job.id);
    }
  }

  const roots = jobs.filter(j => !childIds.has(j.id));
  const result: TreeRow[] = [];

  function addJob(job: LegoJobbSummary, depth: number, isLastChild: boolean) {
    result.push({ job, depth, isLastChild });
    const children = childrenOf.get(job.id) ?? [];
    children.forEach((child, i) => addJob(child, depth + 1, i === children.length - 1));
  }

  roots.forEach(r => addJob(r, 0, true));
  return result;
}

export default function LegoList() {
  const [jobs, setJobs] = useState<LegoJobbSummary[]>([]);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [deleteTarget, setDeleteTarget] = useState<LegoJobbSummary | null>(null);
  const { toast } = useToast();
  const { user } = useUser();
  const [, navigate] = useLocation();

  const role = (user?.publicMetadata?.role as string) ?? null;
  const isAdmin = role !== "anvandare";

  const [searchKund, setSearchKund] = useState("");
  const [searchStatus, setSearchStatus] = useState("alla");
  const [scanOpen, setScanOpen] = useState(false);
  const [creating, setCreating] = useState(false);

  async function handleScanResult(rows: LegoPaketRow[], meta: LegoSkanMeta) {
    if (rows.length === 0) return;
    setCreating(true);
    try {
      const kund = meta.avsandare?.trim() || "Ny kund";
      const datumIn = meta.datum || null;
      const newJob = await customFetch<{ id: number }>("/api/lego", {
        method: "POST",
        headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
        body: JSON.stringify({ kund, datumIn }),
      });
      await customFetch(`/api/lego/${newJob.id}`, {
        method: "PUT",
        headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
        body: JSON.stringify({ kund, datumIn, paket: rows }),
      });
      toast({ title: `Legojobb skapat med ${rows.length} paket` });
      navigate(`/lego/${newJob.id}`);
    } catch (e: any) {
      toast({ title: "Kunde inte skapa legojobb", description: e.message, variant: "destructive" });
    } finally {
      setCreating(false);
    }
  }

  async function load() {
    setLoading(true);
    const data = await customFetch<LegoJobbSummary[]>("/api/lego");
    setJobs(data);
    setLoading(false);
  }

  useEffect(() => { load(); }, []);

  async function confirmDelete() {
    if (!deleteTarget) return;
    await customFetch(`/api/lego/${deleteTarget.id}`, { method: "DELETE" });
    toast({ title: `Legojobb #${deleteTarget.jobbNr ?? deleteTarget.id} raderat` });
    setDeleteTarget(null);
    load();
  }

  const filtered = useMemo(() => {
    return jobs.filter(j => {
      if (searchKund && !j.kund.toLowerCase().includes(searchKund.toLowerCase())) return false;
      if (searchStatus !== "alla" && j.status !== searchStatus) return false;
      return true;
    });
  }, [jobs, searchKund, searchStatus]);

  // When filter is active, show flat list. When no filter, show tree.
  const hasFilter = !!(searchKund || searchStatus !== "alla");

  const treeRows = useMemo(() => {
    if (hasFilter) return filtered.map(j => ({ job: j, depth: 0, isLastChild: true }));
    return buildTree(jobs);
  }, [jobs, filtered, hasFilter]);

  const hasPrisData = jobs.some(j => j.totalPris > 0);

  return (
    <div className="space-y-6">
      <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-2">
        <div>
          <h1 className="text-2xl sm:text-3xl font-bold tracking-tight">Legoarbeten</h1>
          <p className="text-muted-foreground text-sm hidden sm:block">
            Kunders virke som hyvlas och levereras tillbaka.
          </p>
        </div>
        <div className="flex gap-2 flex-wrap justify-end">
          <Button variant="outline" size="sm" className="gap-1.5" onClick={() => window.open("/api/lego/export", "_blank")}>
            <FileDown className="h-4 w-4" />
            <span className="hidden sm:inline">Excel</span>
          </Button>
          <Link href="/lego/statistik">
            <Button variant="outline" size="sm" className="gap-1.5">
              <BarChart2 className="h-4 w-4" />
              <span className="hidden sm:inline">Statistik</span>
            </Button>
          </Link>
          <Button variant="outline" size="sm" className="gap-1.5" onClick={() => setScanOpen(true)} disabled={creating}>
            {creating ? <Loader2 className="h-4 w-4 animate-spin" /> : <ScanLine className="h-4 w-4" />}
            <span className="hidden sm:inline">Skanna</span>
          </Button>
          <Link href="/lego/ny">
            <Button size="sm" className="gap-1.5">
              <Plus className="h-4 w-4" />
              <span className="hidden sm:inline">Nytt jobb</span>
            </Button>
          </Link>
        </div>
      </div>

      <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2 items-center">
        <div className="relative flex-1 min-w-[180px]">
          <Search className="absolute left-2.5 top-2.5 h-4 w-4 text-muted-foreground" />
          <Input
            className="pl-9"
            placeholder="Sök kund..."
            value={searchKund}
            onChange={e => setSearchKund(e.target.value)}
          />
        </div>
        <Select value={searchStatus} onValueChange={setSearchStatus}>
          <SelectTrigger className="w-[140px]">
            <SelectValue />
          </SelectTrigger>
          <SelectContent>
            <SelectItem value="alla">Alla status</SelectItem>
            <SelectItem value="mottaget">Mottaget</SelectItem>
            <SelectItem value="hyvlat">Hyvlat</SelectItem>
            <SelectItem value="levererat">Levererat</SelectItem>
          </SelectContent>
        </Select>
        {hasFilter && (
          <Button variant="ghost" size="sm" onClick={() => { setSearchKund(""); setSearchStatus("alla"); }} className="gap-1 text-muted-foreground">
            <X className="h-4 w-4" /> Rensa
          </Button>
        )}
        {hasFilter && (
          <span className="text-sm text-muted-foreground">{filtered.length} av {jobs.length}</span>
        )}
      </div>

      {loading ? (
        <div className="text-muted-foreground text-sm">Laddar...</div>
      ) : treeRows.length === 0 ? (
        <div className="text-center py-20 text-muted-foreground">
          <Wrench className="h-12 w-12 mx-auto mb-3 opacity-30" />
          <p className="font-medium">{jobs.length === 0 ? "Inga legojobb ännu" : "Inga poster matchar filtret"}</p>
          {jobs.length === 0 && <p className="text-sm mt-1">Klicka "Nytt jobb" för att registrera ett legoarbete.</p>}
        </div>
      ) : (
        <>
          {/* ── Mobilkortvy ── */}
          <div className="sm:hidden space-y-2">
            {treeRows.map(({ job: j, depth }) => (
              <div
                key={j.id}
                className={`border rounded-lg p-3 bg-card cursor-pointer active:bg-muted/30 ${depth > 0 ? "ml-4 border-l-2 border-l-muted-foreground/20" : ""}`}
                onClick={() => navigate(`/lego/${j.id}`)}
              >
                <div className="flex items-start justify-between gap-2">
                  <div className="flex-1 min-w-0">
                    <div className="flex items-center gap-2 flex-wrap mb-1">
                      {statusBadge(j.status)}
                      <span className={`font-medium text-sm truncate ${depth > 0 ? "text-muted-foreground" : ""}`}>
                        {j.kund}
                        {j.kopplatSpecId && <Link2 className="inline h-3 w-3 ml-1 text-muted-foreground/50" />}
                      </span>
                    </div>
                    <div className="text-xs text-muted-foreground space-y-0.5">
                      <div className="flex gap-3 flex-wrap">
                        {j.datumIn && <span>In: {j.datumIn}</span>}
                        {j.datumUt && <span>Ut: {j.datumUt}</span>}
                      </div>
                      <div className="flex gap-3 flex-wrap font-medium text-foreground">
                        {j.antalPaket > 0 && <span>{j.antalPaket} paket</span>}
                        {j.totalActM3 > 0 && <span className="text-blue-700">{Number(j.totalActM3).toFixed(3)} m³</span>}
                        {j.totalPris > 0 && <span className="text-green-700">{j.totalPris.toLocaleString("sv-SE", { maximumFractionDigits: 0 })} kr</span>}
                      </div>
                    </div>
                  </div>
                  <div className="flex items-center gap-1 shrink-0">
                    <span className="text-xs text-muted-foreground font-mono">#{j.jobbNr ?? j.id}</span>
                    {isAdmin && (
                      <button
                        onClick={(e) => { e.stopPropagation(); setDeleteTarget(j); }}
                        className="p-1.5 rounded hover:bg-destructive/10 text-muted-foreground hover:text-destructive"
                      >
                        <Trash2 className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
                      </button>
                    )}
                    <ChevronRight className="h-4 w-4 text-muted-foreground" />
                  </div>
                </div>
              </div>
            ))}
          </div>

          {/* ── Desktoptabell ── */}
          <div className="hidden sm:block rounded-lg border overflow-hidden">
            <table className="w-full text-sm">
              <thead className="bg-muted/50 border-b">
                <tr>
                  <th className="text-left px-4 py-3 font-medium text-muted-foreground">#</th>
                  <th className="text-left px-4 py-3 font-medium text-muted-foreground">Kund</th>
                  <th className="text-left px-4 py-3 font-medium text-muted-foreground">Mottaget</th>
                  <th className="text-left px-4 py-3 font-medium text-muted-foreground">Levererat</th>
                  <th className="text-right px-4 py-3 font-medium text-muted-foreground">Paket</th>
                  <th className="text-right px-4 py-3 font-medium text-muted-foreground">Nom m³</th>
                  <th className="text-right px-4 py-3 font-medium text-muted-foreground">Act m³</th>
                  {hasPrisData && <th className="text-right px-4 py-3 font-medium text-muted-foreground">Totalpris</th>}
                  <th className="text-left px-4 py-3 font-medium text-muted-foreground">Status</th>
                  <th className="px-4 py-3"></th>
                </tr>
              </thead>
              <tbody>
                {treeRows.map(({ job: j, depth }) => (
                  <tr
                    key={j.id}
                    className={`border-b last:border-0 hover:bg-muted/30 cursor-pointer ${depth > 0 ? "bg-muted/10" : ""}`}
                    onClick={() => navigate(`/lego/${j.id}`)}
                  >
                    <td className="px-4 py-3 text-muted-foreground font-mono">
                      <div className="flex items-center gap-1" style={{ paddingLeft: depth * 20 }}>
                        {depth > 0 && (
                          <span className="text-muted-foreground/40 shrink-0 select-none">↳</span>
                        )}
                        {j.jobbNr ?? j.id}
                      </div>
                    </td>
                    <td className="px-4 py-3">
                      <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5">
                        <span className={depth > 0 ? "font-normal text-muted-foreground" : "font-medium"}>
                          {j.kund}
                        </span>
                        {j.kopplatSpecId && (
                          <Link2 className="h-3 w-3 text-muted-foreground/50 shrink-0" title="Kopplad till spec" />
                        )}
                      </div>
                    </td>
                    <td className="px-4 py-3 text-muted-foreground">{j.datumIn ?? "–"}</td>
                    <td className="px-4 py-3 text-muted-foreground">{j.datumUt ?? "–"}</td>
                    <td className="px-4 py-3 text-right tabular-nums">{j.antalPaket}</td>
                    <td className="px-4 py-3 text-right tabular-nums">{j.totalM3 > 0 ? Number(j.totalM3).toFixed(3) : "–"}</td>
                    <td className="px-4 py-3 text-right tabular-nums text-blue-700">
                      {j.totalActM3 > 0 ? Number(j.totalActM3).toFixed(3) : <span className="text-muted-foreground">–</span>}
                    </td>
                    {hasPrisData && (
                      <td className="px-4 py-3 text-right tabular-nums font-medium">
                        {j.totalPris > 0
                          ? j.totalPris.toLocaleString("sv-SE", { minimumFractionDigits: 0, maximumFractionDigits: 0 }) + " kr"
                          : "–"}
                      </td>
                    )}
                    <td className="px-4 py-3">{statusBadge(j.status)}</td>
                    <td className="px-4 py-3">
                      <div className="flex items-center gap-1 justify-end">
                        {isAdmin && (
                          <button
                            onClick={(e) => { e.stopPropagation(); setDeleteTarget(j); }}
                            className="p-1.5 rounded hover:bg-destructive/10 text-muted-foreground hover:text-destructive"
                            title="Radera"
                          >
                            <Trash2 className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
                          </button>
                        )}
                        <ChevronRight className="h-4 w-4 text-muted-foreground" />
                      </div>
                    </td>
                  </tr>
                ))}
              </tbody>
            </table>
          </div>
        </>
      )}

      <AlertDialog open={!!deleteTarget} onOpenChange={(open) => { if (!open) setDeleteTarget(null); }}>
        <AlertDialogContent>
          <AlertDialogHeader>
            <AlertDialogTitle>Radera legojobb #{deleteTarget?.jobbNr ?? deleteTarget?.id}?</AlertDialogTitle>
            <AlertDialogDescription>Jobbet och alla tillhörande paket raderas permanent.</AlertDialogDescription>
          </AlertDialogHeader>
          <AlertDialogFooter>
            <AlertDialogCancel>Avbryt</AlertDialogCancel>
            <AlertDialogAction className="bg-destructive text-destructive-foreground hover:bg-destructive/90" onClick={confirmDelete}>
              Radera
            </AlertDialogAction>
          </AlertDialogFooter>
        </AlertDialogContent>
      </AlertDialog>

      <LegoSkannaDialog
        open={scanOpen}
        onOpenChange={setScanOpen}
        onPackagesFound={handleScanResult}
      />
    </div>
  );
}

import { useState, useEffect, useMemo, useRef } from "react";
import { flushSync } from "react-dom";
import { useParams, useLocation } from "wouter";

import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { Input } from "@/components/ui/input";
import { Badge } from "@/components/ui/badge";
import { Card, CardContent, CardHeader, CardTitle } from "@/components/ui/card";
import {
  ArrowLeft, Save, CheckCircle, Loader2, Trash2, Plus, Printer, RotateCcw, ScanLine,
  ArrowUpDown, ArrowUp, ArrowDown, FileDown, X, Link2,
} from "lucide-react";
import LegoSkannaDialog, { type LegoPaketRow } from "@/components/LegoSkannaDialog";
import { useToast } from "@/hooks/use-toast";
import {
  AlertDialog, AlertDialogAction, AlertDialogCancel, AlertDialogContent,
  AlertDialogDescription, AlertDialogFooter, AlertDialogHeader, AlertDialogTitle, AlertDialogTrigger,
} from "@/components/ui/alert-dialog";
import { Select, SelectContent, SelectItem, SelectTrigger, SelectValue } from "@/components/ui/select";

  printDocument,
} from "@/lib/documentOutput";
import { DocumentCompanyFooter, DocumentCertificationBlock, DocumentInvoiceDetailsBlock } from "@/lib/specDocumentFields";

type Paket = {
  id?: number;
  pktNr: string;
  traslag: string;
  kvalitet: string;
  rawTjocklek: string;
  rawBredd: string;
  hyvlatTill: string;
  hyvlTjocklek: string;
  hyvlBredd: string;
  langdDm: string;
  antal: string;
  m3: string;
  lpm: string;
  prisM3: string;
  anteckning: string;
};

type KopplatSpec = {
  id: number;
  typ: string;
  hyvlingNr: number | null;
  kund: string | null;
  status: string;
};

type KopplatJobb = {
  id: number;
  jobbNr: number | null;
  kund: string;
  status: string;
};

// Unified picker item: source = "spec" | "lego"
type PickerItem =
  | { source: "spec"; id: number; typ: string; hyvlingNr: number | null; kund: string | null; status: string; antalPaket: number }
  | { source: "lego"; id: number; jobbNr: number | null; kund: string; status: string; antalPaket: number };

type LegoJobb = {
  id: number;
  jobbNr: number | null;
  kund: string;
  datumIn: string | null;
  datumUt: string | null;
  status: string;
  anteckning: string | null;
  kopplatSpecId: number | null;
  kopplatSpec: KopplatSpec | null;
  kopplatJobbId: number | null;
  kopplatJobb: KopplatJobb | null;
  skapadAt: string;
  paket: Paket[];
  documentSenderSnapshot?: Branding | null;
  showCertificationOnDocument?: boolean;
  useAlternateDocumentSender?: boolean;
};

const emptyPaket = (): Paket => ({
  pktNr: "", traslag: "", kvalitet: "", rawTjocklek: "", rawBredd: "",
  hyvlatTill: "", hyvlTjocklek: "", hyvlBredd: "",
  langdDm: "", antal: "", m3: "", lpm: "", prisM3: "", anteckning: "",
});

function toN(s: string): number | null {
  const n = parseFloat(s.replace(",", "."));
  return isNaN(n) ? null : n;
}

function calcM3(p: Paket): number | null {
  const tk = toN(p.rawTjocklek), br = toN(p.rawBredd), ld = toN(p.langdDm), an = toN(p.antal);
  if (tk && br && ld && an) return parseFloat(((tk / 1000) * (br / 1000) * (ld / 10) * an).toFixed(4));
  return null;
}

function calcLpm(p: Paket): number | null {
  const ld = toN(p.langdDm), an = toN(p.antal);
  if (ld && an) return parseFloat(((ld / 10) * an).toFixed(2));
  return null;
}

function statusBadge(status: string) {
  if (status === "levererat") return <Badge className="bg-green-100 text-green-800 border-green-200">Levererat</Badge>;
  if (status === "hyvlat") return <Badge className="bg-orange-100 text-orange-800 border-orange-200">Hyvlat</Badge>;
  return <Badge variant="outline">Mottaget</Badge>;
}
function escapeHtml(value: string | null | undefined): string {
  return (value ?? "").replace(/[&<>"']/g, (character) => ({ "&": "&amp;", "<": "&lt;", ">": "&gt;", '"': "&quot;", "'": "&#039;" }[character]!));
}

export default function LegoPage() {
  const params = useParams<{ id?: string }>();
  const jobbId = params.id && params.id !== "ny" ? parseInt(params.id) : null;
  const [, navigate] = useLocation();
  const { toast } = useToast();
  const branding = useBranding();

  const [kund, setKund] = useState("");
  const [kunder, setKunder] = useState<{ id: number; namn: string }[]>([]);
  const [datumIn, setDatumIn] = useState(new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10));
  const [anteckning, setAnteckning] = useState("");
  const [status, setStatus] = useState("mottaget");
  const [paket, setPaket] = useState<Paket[]>([emptyPaket()]);
  const [jobbNr, setJobbNr] = useState<number | null>(null);
  const [useAlternateDocumentSender, setUseAlternateDocumentSender] = useState(false);
  const [documentSenderSnapshot, setDocumentSenderSnapshot] = useState<Branding | null>(null);
  const [alternateSender, setAlternateSender] = useState<Branding>({ ...branding });
  const [alternateLogoFile, setAlternateLogoFile] = useState<File | null>(null);
  const [alternateLogoPreview, setAlternateLogoPreview] = useState<string | null>(branding.logoUrl);
  const [showCertificationOnDocument, setShowCertificationOnDocument] = useState(false);

  const [kopplatSpecId, setKopplatSpecId] = useState<number | null>(null);
  const [kopplatSpec, setKopplatSpec] = useState<KopplatSpec | null>(null);
  const [kopplatJobbId, setKopplatJobbId] = useState<number | null>(null);
  const [kopplatJobb, setKopplatJobb] = useState<KopplatJobb | null>(null);
  const [allePickerItems, setAllePickerItems] = useState<PickerItem[]>([]);
  const [specSokQuery, setSpecSokQuery] = useState("");
  const [specPickerOpen, setSpecPickerOpen] = useState(false);

  const [saving, setSaving] = useState(false);
  const [levering, setLevering] = useState(false);
  const [pdfLoading, setPdfLoading] = useState(false);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);
  const [scanOpen, setScanOpen] = useState(false);
  const [sortCol, setSortCol] = useState<keyof Paket | null>(null);
  const [sortDir, setSortDir] = useState<"asc" | "desc">("asc");
  const [printDialogOpen, setPrintDialogOpen] = useState(false);
  const [printSelected, setPrintSelected] = useState<Set<number>>(new Set());
  const [printFilter, setPrintFilter] = useState<Set<number> | null>(null);
  const documentSender = documentSenderSnapshot ?? (useAlternateDocumentSender ? alternateSender : branding);
  const senderLocation = [documentSender.postalCode, documentSender.city].filter(Boolean).join(" ");
  const senderLine = [documentSender.companyName, documentSender.streetAddress, senderLocation, documentSender.country, documentSender.phone ? `Tel: ${documentSender.phone}` : "", documentSender.email].filter(Boolean).join(" · ");

  // Load all specs + lego jobs for combined picker
  // Remove any lingering print portals when navigating away
  useEffect(() => {
    return () => {
      document.getElementById("lego-spec-print-portal")?.remove();
      document.getElementById("lego-spec-print-style")?.remove();
      cleanupDocumentPrint();
    };
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => {
    Promise.all([
      customFetch<{ id: number; typ: string; hyvlingNr: number | null; kund: string | null; status: string; antalPaket: number }[]>("/api/spec").catch(() => []),
      customFetch<{ id: number; jobbNr: number | null; kund: string; status: string; antalPaket: number }[]>("/api/lego").catch(() => []),
    ]).then(([specs, jobbs]) => {
      const specItems: PickerItem[] = specs.map(s => ({ source: "spec", ...s }));
      const jobbItems: PickerItem[] = jobbs.map(j => ({ source: "lego", ...j }));
      setAllePickerItems([...specItems, ...jobbItems]);
    });
  }, []);

  // Load existing job
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!jobbId) return;
    setLoading(true);
    customFetch<LegoJobb>(`/api/lego/${jobbId}`).then(j => {
      setKund(j.kund);
      setDatumIn(j.datumIn ?? "");
      setAnteckning(j.anteckning ?? "");
      setStatus(j.status);
      setShowCertificationOnDocument(j.showCertificationOnDocument === true);
      setJobbNr(j.jobbNr);
      setUseAlternateDocumentSender(j.useAlternateDocumentSender ?? false);
      setDocumentSenderSnapshot(j.documentSenderSnapshot ? normalizeBranding(j.documentSenderSnapshot) : null);
      if (j.documentSenderSnapshot) {
        const savedSender = normalizeBranding(j.documentSenderSnapshot);
        setAlternateSender(savedSender);
        setAlternateLogoPreview(savedSender.logoUrl);
      }
      setKopplatSpecId(j.kopplatSpecId ?? null);
      setKopplatSpec(j.kopplatSpec ?? null);
      setKopplatJobbId(j.kopplatJobbId ?? null);
      setKopplatJobb(j.kopplatJobb ?? null);
      setPaket(j.paket.length > 0
        ? j.paket.map(p => ({
            id: p.id,
            pktNr: p.pktNr ?? "",
            traslag: p.traslag ?? "",
            kvalitet: p.kvalitet ?? "",
            rawTjocklek: p.rawTjocklek?.toString() ?? "",
            rawBredd: p.rawBredd?.toString() ?? "",
            hyvlatTill: p.hyvlatTill ?? "",
            hyvlTjocklek: p.hyvlTjocklek?.toString() ?? "",
            hyvlBredd: p.hyvlBredd?.toString() ?? "",
            langdDm: p.langdDm?.toString() ?? "",
            antal: p.antal?.toString() ?? "",
            m3: p.m3?.toString() ?? "",
            lpm: p.lpm?.toString() ?? "",
            prisM3: p.prisM3?.toString() ?? "",
            anteckning: p.anteckning ?? "",
          }))
        : [emptyPaket()]);
    }).finally(() => setLoading(false));
  }, [jobbId]);

  // Load customers for autocomplete
  useEffect(() => {
    customFetch<{ id: number; namn: string }[]>("/api/kunder").then(setKunder).catch(() => {});
  }, []);

  function updatePaket(i: number, field: keyof Paket, value: string) {
    setPaket(prev => {
      const next = [...prev];
      const updated = { ...next[i], [field]: value };
      // Auto-calc m3 and lpm
      if (["rawTjocklek", "rawBredd", "langdDm", "antal"].includes(field)) {
        const m3 = calcM3({ ...updated });
        const lpm = calcLpm({ ...updated });
        if (m3 !== null) updated.m3 = m3.toString();
        if (lpm !== null) updated.lpm = lpm.toString();
      }
      next[i] = updated;
      return next;
    });
  }

  function pickerLabel(item: PickerItem): string {
    if (item.source === "lego") {
      return `Lego #${item.jobbNr ?? item.id} — ${item.kund}`;
    }
    const nr = item.typ === "hyvling" && item.hyvlingNr ? `Hyvl #${item.hyvlingNr}` : `Spec #${(item as any).specNr ?? item.id}`;
    const kundStr = item.kund ? ` — ${item.kund}` : "";
    const typStr = item.typ === "hyvling" ? " (hyvling)" : " (leverans)";
    return `${nr}${kundStr}${typStr}`;
  }

  function kopplingSummary(): string | null {
    if (kopplatSpec) {
      const nr = kopplatSpec.typ === "hyvling" && kopplatSpec.hyvlingNr ? `Hyvl #${kopplatSpec.hyvlingNr}` : `Spec #${(kopplatSpec as any).specNr ?? kopplatSpec.id}`;
      const kundStr = kopplatSpec.kund ? ` — ${kopplatSpec.kund}` : "";
      const typStr = kopplatSpec.typ === "hyvling" ? " (hyvling)" : " (leverans)";
      return `${nr}${kundStr}${typStr}`;
    }
    if (kopplatJobb) {
      return `Lego #${kopplatJobb.jobbNr ?? kopplatJobb.id} — ${kopplatJobb.kund}`;
    }
    return null;
  }

  function kopplingsNavigate() {
    if (kopplatSpec) {
      navigate(kopplatSpec.typ === "hyvling" ? `/hyvling/${kopplatSpec.id}` : `/spec/${kopplatSpec.id}`);
    } else if (kopplatJobb) {
      navigate(`/lego/${kopplatJobb.id}`);
    }
  }

  function clearKoppling() {
    setKopplatSpecId(null);
    setKopplatSpec(null);
    setKopplatJobbId(null);
    setKopplatJobb(null);
  }

  function addRow() {
    setPaket(prev => [...prev, emptyPaket()]);
  }

  function addScannedRows(rows: LegoPaketRow[], _meta?: unknown) {
    setPaket(prev => {
      const existing = prev.filter(p => p.pktNr || p.rawTjocklek || p.langdDm || p.antal);
      return [...existing, ...rows];
    });
  }

  function removeRow(i: number) {
    setPaket(prev => prev.length > 1 ? prev.filter((_, idx) => idx !== i) : [emptyPaket()]);
  }

  async function save(): Promise<number | null> {
    if (!kund.trim()) { toast({ title: "Kund krävs", variant: "destructive" }); return null; }
    setSaving(true);
    try {
      const filledPaket = paket.filter(p => p.pktNr || p.rawTjocklek || p.langdDm || p.antal);
      let alternateSenderPayload = alternateSender;
      if (useAlternateDocumentSender && alternateLogoFile) {
        const upload = await customFetch<{ uploadURL: string; objectPath: string }>("/api/storage/uploads/request-url", {
          method: "POST",
          headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
          body: JSON.stringify({
            name: alternateLogoFile.name,
            size: alternateLogoFile.size,
            contentType: alternateLogoFile.type,
          }),
        });
        const uploadResponse = await fetch(upload.uploadURL, {
          method: "PUT",
          headers: { "Content-Type": alternateLogoFile.type },
          body: alternateLogoFile,
        });
        if (!uploadResponse.ok) throw new Error("Kunde inte ladda upp den alternativa logotypen.");
        alternateSenderPayload = { ...alternateSender, logoPath: upload.objectPath, logoUrl: null };
        setAlternateSender(alternateSenderPayload);
        setAlternateLogoFile(null);
      }
      const body = {
        kund: kund.trim(),
        datumIn: datumIn || null,
        anteckning: anteckning || null,
        status,
        kopplatSpecId: kopplatSpecId ?? null,
        kopplatJobbId: kopplatJobbId ?? null,
        showCertificationOnDocument,
        alternateSender: useAlternateDocumentSender ? alternateSenderPayload : null,
        paket: filledPaket.map(p => ({
          ...p,
          rawTjocklek: toN(p.rawTjocklek),
          rawBredd: toN(p.rawBredd),
          hyvlTjocklek: toN(p.hyvlTjocklek),
          hyvlBredd: toN(p.hyvlBredd),
          langdDm: toN(p.langdDm),
          antal: toN(p.antal),
          m3: toN(p.m3),
          lpm: toN(p.lpm),
          prisM3: toN(p.prisM3),
        })),
      };
      if (jobbId) {
        await customFetch(`/api/lego/${jobbId}`, {
          method: "PUT",
          headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
          body: JSON.stringify(body),
        });
        toast({ title: "Legojobb sparat" });
        return jobbId;
      } else {
        const data = await customFetch<{ id: number }>("/api/lego", {
          method: "POST",
          headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
          body: JSON.stringify(body),
        });
        // Save paket for new job
        if (filledPaket.length > 0) {
          await customFetch(`/api/lego/${data.id}`, {
            method: "PUT",
            headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
            body: JSON.stringify({ ...body, paket: filledPaket.map(p => ({
              ...p,
              rawTjocklek: toN(p.rawTjocklek),
              rawBredd: toN(p.rawBredd),
              hyvlTjocklek: toN(p.hyvlTjocklek),
              hyvlBredd: toN(p.hyvlBredd),
              langdDm: toN(p.langdDm),
              antal: toN(p.antal),
              m3: toN(p.m3),
              lpm: toN(p.lpm),
              prisM3: toN(p.prisM3),
            })) }),
          });
        }
        toast({ title: "Legojobb skapat" });
        navigate(`/lego/${data.id}`);
        return data.id;
      }
    } catch {
      toast({ title: "Fel vid sparning", variant: "destructive" });
      return null;
    } finally {
      setSaving(false);
    }
  }

  async function leverera() {
    let id = jobbId;
    if (!id) { id = await save(); if (!id) return; }
    else await save();
    setLevering(true);
    try {
      await customFetch(`/api/lego/${id}/leverera`, { method: "POST" });
      setStatus("levererat");
      toast({ title: `Legojobb #${jobbNr ?? id} markerat som levererat` });
      await printLegoSpec();
    } catch (error) {
      toast({
        title: "Legoordern kunde inte levereras",
        description: error instanceof Error ? error.message : "Kontrollera dokumentavsändaren och försök igen.",
        variant: "destructive",
      });
    } finally {
      setLevering(false);
    }
  }

  async function ateroppen() {
    if (!jobbId) return;
    await customFetch(`/api/lego/${jobbId}/ateroppen`, { method: "POST" });
    setStatus("hyvlat");
    toast({ title: "Legojobb återöppnat" });
  }

  async function markeraHyvlat() {
    let id = jobbId;
    if (!id) { id = await save(); if (!id) return; }
    else await save();
    await customFetch(`/api/lego/${id}`, {
      method: "PUT",
      headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
      body: JSON.stringify({ kund, datumIn, anteckning, status: "hyvlat" }),
