    });
    setStatus("hyvlat");
    toast({ title: "Markerat som hyvlat" });
  }

  function printFollesedel(id?: number, existingWin?: Window | null) {
    const printId = id ?? jobbId;
    if (!printId) return;
    const win = existingWin ?? window.open("", "_blank");
    if (!win) return;
    const filledPaket = paket.filter(p => p.pktNr || p.rawTjocklek || p.langdDm || p.antal);
    const totalNomM3 = filledPaket.reduce((s, p) => s + (calcM3(p) ?? 0), 0);
    const totalLpm = filledPaket.reduce((s, p) => s + (toN(p.lpm) ?? 0), 0);
    const totalAntal = filledPaket.reduce((s, p) => s + (toN(p.antal) ?? 0), 0);
    const totalPris = filledPaket.reduce((s, p) => {
      const m3v = calcM3(p), prv = toN(p.prisM3);
      return s + (m3v != null && prv != null ? m3v * prv : 0);
    }, 0);
    const hasPris = filledPaket.some(p => p.prisM3 && toN(p.prisM3) != null);
    const hasAnt = filledPaket.some(p => p.anteckning);
    const calcActM3F = (p: Paket): number | null => {
      const tk = toN(p.hyvlTjocklek), br = toN(p.hyvlBredd), ld = toN(p.langdDm), an = toN(p.antal);
      return tk && br && ld && an ? parseFloat(((tk / 1000) * (br / 1000) * (ld / 10) * an).toFixed(4)) : null;
    };
    const totalActM3F = filledPaket.reduce((s, p) => s + (calcActM3F(p) ?? 0), 0);
    const hasActM3F = filledPaket.some(p => calcActM3F(p) != null);
    win.document.write(`<!DOCTYPE html><html><head><meta charset="utf-8"><title>Följesedel Lego #${jobbNr ?? printId}</title>
<style>
* { box-sizing: border-box; margin: 0; padding: 0; font-family: Arial, sans-serif; }
body { padding: 20px; font-size: 11px; }
h1 { font-size: 18px; margin-bottom: 4px; }
.header { margin-bottom: 16px; }
.meta { display: flex; gap: 40px; margin-bottom: 12px; }
.meta div { }
.meta strong { display: block; font-size: 10px; text-transform: uppercase; color: #666; margin-bottom: 2px; }
table { width: 100%; border-collapse: collapse; margin-top: 8px; }
th { background: #f0f0f0; text-align: left; padding: 5px 6px; font-size: 10px; border: 1px solid #ccc; }
td { padding: 4px 6px; border: 1px solid #ddd; vertical-align: top; }
tfoot td { background: #f9f9f9; font-weight: bold; }
.footer { margin-top: 20px; font-size: 10px; color: #555; }
.tr { text-align: right; }
.act { text-align: right; color: #1d4ed8; }
@media print { body { padding: 10px; } thead { display: table-header-group; } }
</style></head><body>
<div class="header">
<h1>Följesedel – Legoarbete</h1>
<p style="color:#555;font-size:11px">${escapeHtml(documentSender.companyName)}</p>
</div>
<div class="meta">
<div><strong>Jobb #</strong>${jobbNr ?? printId}</div>
<div><strong>Kund</strong>${kund}</div>
<div><strong>Mottaget</strong>${datumIn || "–"}</div>
<div><strong>Datum</strong>${new Date().toLocaleDateString("sv-SE")}</div>
<div><strong>Antal paket</strong>${filledPaket.length}</div>
${anteckning ? `<div><strong>Anteckning</strong>${anteckning}</div>` : ""}
</div>
<table>
<thead><tr>
<th>Pkt nr</th><th>Träslag</th><th>Kval</th>
<th>Råvara dim (mm)</th><th>Hyvlat till</th><th>Dim ut (mm)</th>
<th>Längd (dm)</th><th>Antal</th><th>Lpm</th><th class="tr">Nom m³</th>
${hasActM3F ? "<th class='act'>Act m³</th>" : ""}
${hasPris ? "<th class='tr'>Pris/m³</th><th class='tr'>Totalpris</th>" : ""}
${hasAnt ? "<th>Ant.</th>" : ""}
</tr></thead>
<tbody>
${filledPaket.map(p => {
  const nomM3v = calcM3(p);
  const prv = toN(p.prisM3);
  const rowPris = nomM3v != null && prv != null ? (nomM3v * prv).toLocaleString("sv-SE", { minimumFractionDigits: 0, maximumFractionDigits: 0 }) : "–";
  const actM3v = calcActM3F(p);
  return `<tr>
<td>${p.pktNr || "–"}</td>
<td>${p.traslag || "–"}</td>
<td>${p.kvalitet || "–"}</td>
<td>${p.rawTjocklek && p.rawBredd ? `${p.rawTjocklek}×${p.rawBredd}` : "–"}</td>
<td>${p.hyvlatTill || "–"}</td>
<td>${p.hyvlTjocklek && p.hyvlBredd ? `${p.hyvlTjocklek}×${p.hyvlBredd}` : p.hyvlTjocklek || p.hyvlBredd || "–"}</td>
<td class="tr">${p.langdDm || "–"}</td>
<td class="tr">${p.antal || "–"}</td>
<td class="tr">${p.lpm ? parseFloat(p.lpm).toFixed(1) : "–"}</td>
<td class="tr">${nomM3v != null ? nomM3v.toFixed(3) : "–"}</td>
${hasActM3F ? `<td class="act">${actM3v != null ? actM3v.toFixed(3) : "–"}</td>` : ""}
${hasPris ? `<td class="tr">${p.prisM3 ? p.prisM3 + " kr" : "–"}</td><td class="tr">${rowPris} kr</td>` : ""}
${hasAnt ? `<td>${p.anteckning || ""}</td>` : ""}
</tr>`;
}).join("")}
</tbody>
<tfoot><tr>
<td colspan="${6 + (hasAnt ? 1 : 0)}" style="text-align:right">Totalt:</td>
<td class="tr">${totalAntal.toFixed(0)}</td>
<td class="tr">${totalLpm.toFixed(1)}</td>
<td class="tr">${totalNomM3.toFixed(3)}</td>
${hasActM3F ? `<td class="act">${totalActM3F.toFixed(3)}</td>` : ""}
${hasPris ? `<td></td><td class="tr"><strong>${totalPris.toLocaleString("sv-SE", { minimumFractionDigits: 0, maximumFractionDigits: 0 })} kr</strong></td>` : ""}
</tr></tfoot>
</table>
<div class="footer">
<p>Mottagarens underskrift: ______________________________ Datum: ____________</p>
</div>
</body></html>`);
    win.document.close();
    setTimeout(() => win.print(), 300);
  }

  async function printLegoSpec() {
    try {
      await printDocument({ sourceId: "lego-spec-print", title: `Legojobb #${jobbNr ?? jobbId ?? ""}` });
    } catch (error) {
      toast({
        title: "Kunde inte förbereda utskriften",
        description: error instanceof Error ? error.message : undefined,
        variant: "destructive",
      });
    }
  }

  function previewLegoSpec() {
    previewDocument({
      sourceId: "lego-spec-print",
      title: `Legojobb #${jobbNr ?? jobbId ?? ""} – förhandsgranskning`,
    });
  }

  async function downloadLegoPdf() {
    setPdfLoading(true);
    try {
      await downloadDocumentPdf({
        sourceId: "lego-spec-print",
        title: `Legojobb #${jobbNr ?? jobbId ?? ""}`,
        fileName: `Legojobb-${jobbNr ?? jobbId ?? "utkast"}${kund ? `-${kund}` : ""}.pdf`,
      });
    } catch (error) {
      toast({
        title: "PDF-export misslyckades",
        description: error instanceof Error ? error.message : "Okänt fel",
        variant: "destructive",
      });
    } finally {
      setPdfLoading(false);
    }
  }

  const printLegoSpecRef = useRef(printLegoSpec);
  useEffect(() => { printLegoSpecRef.current = printLegoSpec; });
  useEffect(() => {
    const h = () => printLegoSpecRef.current();
    window.addEventListener("shortcut:print", h);
    return () => window.removeEventListener("shortcut:print", h);
  }, []);

  function buildFollesedelHtml(
    printId: number,
    nr: number | null,
    kundNamn: string,
    datum: string,
    anteckn: string | null,
    rader: Paket[],
  ): string {
    const filled = rader.filter(p => p.pktNr || p.rawTjocklek || p.langdDm || p.antal);
    const calcActM3B = (p: Paket): number | null => {
      const tk = toN(p.hyvlTjocklek), br = toN(p.hyvlBredd), ld = toN(p.langdDm), an = toN(p.antal);
      return tk && br && ld && an ? parseFloat(((tk / 1000) * (br / 1000) * (ld / 10) * an).toFixed(4)) : null;
    };
    const totalNomM3 = filled.reduce((s, p) => s + (calcM3(p) ?? 0), 0);
    const totalActM3B = filled.reduce((s, p) => s + (calcActM3B(p) ?? 0), 0);
    const hasActM3B = filled.some(p => calcActM3B(p) != null);
    const totalLpm = filled.reduce((s, p) => s + (toN(p.lpm) ?? 0), 0);
    const totalAntal = filled.reduce((s, p) => s + (toN(p.antal) ?? 0), 0);
    const totalPris = filled.reduce((s, p) => {
      const m3v = calcM3(p), prv = toN(p.prisM3);
      return s + (m3v != null && prv != null ? m3v * prv : 0);
    }, 0);
    const hasPris = filled.some(p => p.prisM3 && toN(p.prisM3) != null);
    const hasAnt = filled.some(p => p.anteckning);
    return `
<div class="jobb">
<div class="meta">
<div><strong>Jobb #</strong>${nr ?? printId}</div>
<div><strong>Kund</strong>${kundNamn}</div>
<div><strong>Mottaget</strong>${datum || "–"}</div>
<div><strong>Datum</strong>${new Date().toLocaleDateString("sv-SE")}</div>
<div><strong>Antal paket</strong>${filled.length}</div>
${anteckn ? `<div><strong>Anteckning</strong>${anteckn}</div>` : ""}
</div>
<table>
<thead><tr>
<th>Pkt nr</th><th>Träslag</th><th>Kval</th>
<th>Råvara dim (mm)</th><th>Hyvlat till</th><th>Dim ut (mm)</th>
<th>Längd (dm)</th><th>Antal</th><th>Lpm</th><th class="tr">Nom m³</th>
${hasActM3B ? "<th class='act'>Act m³</th>" : ""}
${hasPris ? "<th class='tr'>Pris/m³</th><th class='tr'>Totalpris</th>" : ""}
${hasAnt ? "<th>Ant.</th>" : ""}
</tr></thead>
<tbody>
${filled.map(p => {
  const nomM3v = calcM3(p), prv = toN(p.prisM3);
  const rowPris = nomM3v != null && prv != null ? (nomM3v * prv).toLocaleString("sv-SE", { minimumFractionDigits: 0, maximumFractionDigits: 0 }) : "–";
  const actM3v = calcActM3B(p);
  return `<tr>
<td>${p.pktNr || "–"}</td><td>${p.traslag || "–"}</td><td>${p.kvalitet || "–"}</td>
<td>${p.rawTjocklek && p.rawBredd ? `${p.rawTjocklek}×${p.rawBredd}` : "–"}</td>
<td>${p.hyvlatTill || "–"}</td>
<td>${p.hyvlTjocklek && p.hyvlBredd ? `${p.hyvlTjocklek}×${p.hyvlBredd}` : p.hyvlTjocklek || p.hyvlBredd || "–"}</td>
<td class="tr">${p.langdDm || "–"}</td><td class="tr">${p.antal || "–"}</td>
<td class="tr">${p.lpm ? parseFloat(p.lpm).toFixed(1) : "–"}</td>
<td class="tr">${nomM3v != null ? nomM3v.toFixed(3) : "–"}</td>
${hasActM3B ? `<td class="act">${actM3v != null ? actM3v.toFixed(3) : "–"}</td>` : ""}
${hasPris ? `<td class="tr">${p.prisM3 ? p.prisM3 + " kr" : "–"}</td><td class="tr">${rowPris} kr</td>` : ""}
${hasAnt ? `<td>${p.anteckning || ""}</td>` : ""}
</tr>`;
}).join("")}
</tbody>
<tfoot><tr>
<td colspan="${6 + (hasAnt ? 1 : 0)}" style="text-align:right">Totalt:</td>
<td class="tr">${totalAntal.toFixed(0)}</td>
<td class="tr">${totalLpm.toFixed(1)}</td>
<td class="tr">${totalNomM3.toFixed(3)}</td>
${hasActM3B ? `<td class="act">${totalActM3B.toFixed(3)}</td>` : ""}
${hasPris ? `<td></td><td class="tr"><strong>${totalPris.toLocaleString("sv-SE", { minimumFractionDigits: 0, maximumFractionDigits: 0 })} kr</strong></td>` : ""}
</tr></tfoot>
</table>
</div>`;
  }

  async function printBada() {
    if (!jobbId || !kopplatJobb) return;
    const win = window.open("", "_blank");
    if (!win) return;
    // Fetch the linked job's packets
    const linked = await customFetch<LegoJobb>(`/api/lego/${kopplatJobb.id}`);
    const linkedPaket: Paket[] = linked.paket.map(p => ({
      id: p.id,
      pktNr: (p as any).pktNr ?? "",
      traslag: (p as any).traslag ?? "",
      kvalitet: (p as any).kvalitet ?? "",
      rawTjocklek: (p as any).rawTjocklek?.toString() ?? "",
      rawBredd: (p as any).rawBredd?.toString() ?? "",
      hyvlatTill: (p as any).hyvlatTill ?? "",
      hyvlTjocklek: (p as any).hyvlTjocklek?.toString() ?? "",
      hyvlBredd: (p as any).hyvlBredd?.toString() ?? "",
      langdDm: (p as any).langdDm?.toString() ?? "",
      antal: (p as any).antal?.toString() ?? "",
      m3: (p as any).m3?.toString() ?? "",
      lpm: (p as any).lpm?.toString() ?? "",
      prisM3: (p as any).prisM3?.toString() ?? "",
      anteckning: (p as any).anteckning ?? "",
    }));

    const html1 = buildFollesedelHtml(jobbId, jobbNr, kund, datumIn, anteckning, paket);
    const html2 = buildFollesedelHtml(kopplatJobb.id, kopplatJobb.jobbNr, kopplatJobb.kund, linked.datumIn ?? "", linked.anteckning, linkedPaket);

    win.document.write(`<!DOCTYPE html><html><head><meta charset="utf-8">
<title>Följesedlar Lego #${jobbNr ?? jobbId} + #${kopplatJobb.jobbNr ?? kopplatJobb.id}</title>
<style>
* { box-sizing: border-box; margin: 0; padding: 0; font-family: Arial, sans-serif; }
body { padding: 20px; font-size: 11px; }
h1 { font-size: 16px; margin-bottom: 4px; }
.jobb { margin-bottom: 30px; page-break-inside: avoid; }
.jobb-separator { border-top: 2px dashed #aaa; margin: 24px 0; page-break-before: always; }
.meta { display: flex; gap: 32px; margin-bottom: 10px; flex-wrap: wrap; }
.meta div { }
.meta strong { display: block; font-size: 10px; text-transform: uppercase; color: #666; margin-bottom: 2px; }
table { width: 100%; border-collapse: collapse; margin-top: 6px; }
th { background: #f0f0f0; text-align: left; padding: 4px 5px; font-size: 10px; border: 1px solid #ccc; }
td { padding: 3px 5px; border: 1px solid #ddd; vertical-align: top; }
tfoot td { background: #f9f9f9; font-weight: bold; }
.tr { text-align: right; }
.sig { margin-top: 16px; font-size: 10px; color: #555; }
@media print { body { padding: 8px; } thead { display: table-header-group; } }
</style></head><body>
<h1>Följesedlar – Legoarbeten</h1>
<p style="color:#555;font-size:11px;margin-bottom:16px">${escapeHtml(documentSender.companyName)} — Utskrivet ${new Date().toLocaleDateString("sv-SE")}</p>
${html1}
<div class="jobb-separator"></div>
${html2}
<div class="sig"><p>Mottagarens underskrift: ______________________________ Datum: ____________</p></div>
</body></html>`);
    win.document.close();
    setTimeout(() => win.print(), 300);
  }

  const totals = useMemo(() => {
    const filled = paket.filter(p => p.pktNr || p.rawTjocklek || p.langdDm || p.antal);
    return {
      antal: filled.reduce((s, p) => s + (toN(p.antal) ?? 0), 0),
      lpm: filled.reduce((s, p) => s + (toN(p.lpm) ?? 0), 0),
      m3: filled.reduce((s, p) => s + (toN(p.m3) ?? 0), 0),
      pris: filled.reduce((s, p) => {
        const m3 = toN(p.m3), pr = toN(p.prisM3);
        return s + (m3 != null && pr != null ? m3 * pr : 0);
      }, 0),
    };
  }, [paket]);

  // Paket med originalindex, grupperade per längd, för print-dialog
  const printPaketGroups = useMemo(() => {
    const withIdx = paket
      .map((p, i) => ({ p, i }))
      .filter(({ p }) => p.pktNr || p.rawTjocklek || p.langdDm || p.antal);
    const byLength = new Map<string, { p: Paket; i: number }[]>();
    withIdx.forEach(({ p, i }) => {
      const key = p.langdDm || "—";
      if (!byLength.has(key)) byLength.set(key, []);
      byLength.get(key)!.push({ p, i });
    });
    return Array.from(byLength.entries())
      .sort(([a], [b]) => (parseFloat(a) || 0) - (parseFloat(b) || 0));
  }, [paket]);

  // Sorted indices: we sort a list of original indices so editing still maps correctly
  const numericCols: (keyof Paket)[] = ["rawTjocklek", "rawBredd", "hyvlTjocklek", "hyvlBredd", "langdDm", "antal", "m3", "lpm", "prisM3"];
  const sortedIndices = useMemo(() => {
    const indices = paket.map((_, i) => i);
    if (!sortCol) return indices;
    return [...indices].sort((a, b) => {
      const va = paket[a][sortCol] as string;
      const vb = paket[b][sortCol] as string;
      let cmp: number;
      if (numericCols.includes(sortCol)) {
        cmp = (toN(va) ?? -Infinity) - (toN(vb) ?? -Infinity);
      } else {
        cmp = (va ?? "").localeCompare(vb ?? "", "sv");
      }
      return sortDir === "asc" ? cmp : -cmp;
    });
  }, [paket, sortCol, sortDir]);

  function handleSortClick(col: keyof Paket) {
    if (sortCol === col) {
      if (sortDir === "asc") setSortDir("desc");
      else { setSortCol(null); setSortDir("asc"); }
    } else {
      setSortCol(col);
      setSortDir("asc");
    }
  }

  function SortIcon({ col }: { col: keyof Paket }) {
    if (sortCol !== col) return <ArrowUpDown className="h-3 w-3 ml-1 opacity-30" />;
    return sortDir === "asc"
      ? <ArrowUp className="h-3 w-3 ml-1 text-primary" />
      : <ArrowDown className="h-3 w-3 ml-1 text-primary" />;
  }

  const isLevererat = status === "levererat";
  const displayNr = jobbNr ?? jobbId;

  if (loading) return <div className="flex items-center gap-2 text-muted-foreground py-10"><Loader2 className="h-4 w-4 animate-spin" /> Laddar...</div>;

  return (
    <div className="space-y-6 max-w-6xl">
      <LegoSkannaDialog
        open={scanOpen}
        onOpenChange={setScanOpen}
        onPackagesFound={addScannedRows}
      />

      {/* ── Paketväljare för spec-utskrift ── */}
      <AlertDialog open={printDialogOpen} onOpenChange={setPrintDialogOpen}>
        <AlertDialogContent className="max-w-lg">
          <AlertDialogHeader>
            <AlertDialogTitle>Välj paket att skriva ut</AlertDialogTitle>
            <AlertDialogDescription>
              Välj vilka paket som ska vara med i spec-utskriften. Grupperade per längd.
            </AlertDialogDescription>
          </AlertDialogHeader>
          <div className="py-1 max-h-[60vh] overflow-y-auto">
            <div className="flex gap-3 mb-3 px-1">
              <button
                className="text-xs text-primary underline"
                onClick={() => setPrintSelected(new Set(printPaketGroups.flatMap(([, items]) => items.map(x => x.i))))}
              >
                Välj alla
              </button>
              <button
                className="text-xs text-muted-foreground underline"
                onClick={() => setPrintSelected(new Set())}
              >
                Välj ingen
              </button>
            </div>
            {printPaketGroups.map(([langd, items]) => {
              const allInGroup = items.every(({ i }) => printSelected.has(i));
              const someInGroup = items.some(({ i }) => printSelected.has(i));
              return (
                <div key={langd} className="mb-3">
                  {/* Längd-rubrik med "välj alla i gruppen" */}
                  <label className="flex items-center gap-2 px-2 py-1.5 bg-muted/60 rounded cursor-pointer font-semibold text-sm">
                    <input
                      type="checkbox"
                      className="h-4 w-4 accent-primary"
                      checked={allInGroup}
                      ref={el => { if (el) el.indeterminate = someInGroup && !allInGroup; }}
                      onChange={e => {
                        const next = new Set(printSelected);
                        items.forEach(({ i }) => e.target.checked ? next.add(i) : next.delete(i));
                        setPrintSelected(next);
                      }}
                    />
                    {langd} dm
                    <span className="text-muted-foreground font-normal text-xs ml-auto">
                      {items.filter(({ i }) => printSelected.has(i)).length} / {items.length} paket
                    </span>
                  </label>
                  {/* Individuella paket */}
                  <div className="pl-4">
                    {items.map(({ p, i }) => (
                      <label key={i} className="flex items-center gap-2 px-2 py-1 rounded cursor-pointer hover:bg-muted/40 text-sm">
                        <input
                          type="checkbox"
                          className="h-3.5 w-3.5 accent-primary"
                          checked={printSelected.has(i)}
                          onChange={e => {
                            const next = new Set(printSelected);
                            if (e.target.checked) next.add(i); else next.delete(i);
                            setPrintSelected(next);
                          }}
                        />
                        <span className="font-mono font-medium">{p.pktNr || `#${i + 1}`}</span>
                        {p.rawTjocklek && p.rawBredd && (
                          <span className="text-muted-foreground text-xs">{p.rawTjocklek}×{p.rawBredd}</span>
                        )}
                        {p.antal && <span className="text-muted-foreground text-xs ml-auto">{p.antal} st</span>}
                      </label>
                    ))}
                  </div>
                </div>
              );
            })}
          </div>
          <AlertDialogFooter>
            <AlertDialogCancel>Avbryt</AlertDialogCancel>
            <AlertDialogAction
              disabled={printSelected.size === 0}
              onClick={() => {
                setPrintDialogOpen(false);
                const allIdx = printPaketGroups.flatMap(([, items]) => items.map(x => x.i));
                const isAll = printSelected.size === allIdx.length;
                flushSync(() => setPrintFilter(isAll ? null : new Set(printSelected)));
                void printLegoSpec();
                window.addEventListener("afterprint", () => setPrintFilter(null), { once: true });
              }}
            >
              <Printer className="h-4 w-4 mr-2" /> Skriv ut ({printSelected.size} paket)
            </AlertDialogAction>
          </AlertDialogFooter>
        </AlertDialogContent>
      </AlertDialog>
      {/* Header */}
      <div className="flex items-start justify-between gap-4 flex-wrap">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
          <Button variant="ghost" size="icon" onClick={() => navigate("/lego")} className="shrink-0">
            <ArrowLeft className="h-4 w-4" />
          </Button>
          <div>
            <h1 className="text-2xl font-bold">
              {jobbId ? `Legojobb #${displayNr}` : "Nytt legojobb"}
            </h1>
            {jobbId && <div className="mt-1">{statusBadge(status)}</div>}
          </div>
        </div>
        <div className="flex gap-2 flex-wrap">
          {!isLevererat && (
            <Button variant="outline" className="gap-2" onClick={() => setScanOpen(true)}>
              <ScanLine className="h-4 w-4" /> Skanna följesedel
            </Button>
          )}
          {jobbId && (
            <Button variant="outline" className="gap-2" onClick={() => {
              const url = `/api/lego/${jobbId}/export`;
              window.open(url, "_blank");
            }}>
              <FileDown className="h-4 w-4" /> Excel
            </Button>
          )}
          {jobbId && (
            <Button variant="outline" className="gap-2" onClick={previewLegoSpec}>
              <Printer className="h-4 w-4" /> Förhandsvisa
            </Button>
          )}
          {jobbId && (
            <Button variant="outline" className="gap-2" onClick={downloadLegoPdf} disabled={pdfLoading}>
              {pdfLoading ? <Loader2 className="h-4 w-4 animate-spin" /> : <FileDown className="h-4 w-4" />}
              {pdfLoading ? "Genererar PDF…" : "Ladda ner PDF"}
            </Button>
          )}
          {jobbId && (
            <Button variant="outline" className="gap-2" onClick={() => {
              setPrintSelected(new Set(printPaketGroups.flatMap(([, items]) => items.map(x => x.i))));
              setPrintDialogOpen(true);
            }}>
              <Printer className="h-4 w-4" /> Skriv ut
            </Button>
          )}
          {jobbId && kopplatJobb && (
            <Button variant="outline" className="gap-2 text-[#2d5a27] border-[#2d5a27]/40 hover:bg-[#2d5a27]/5" onClick={printBada}>
              <Printer className="h-4 w-4" /> Skriv ut båda
            </Button>
          )}
          <Button variant="outline" onClick={() => save()} disabled={saving} className="gap-2">
            {saving ? <Loader2 className="h-4 w-4 animate-spin" /> : <Save className="h-4 w-4" />}
            Spara
          </Button>
          {!isLevererat && status !== "mottaget" && (
            <AlertDialog>
              <AlertDialogTrigger asChild>
                <Button variant="outline" className="gap-2 text-orange-600 border-orange-200 hover:bg-orange-50">
                  <RotateCcw className="h-4 w-4" /> Återöppna
                </Button>
              </AlertDialogTrigger>
              <AlertDialogContent>
                <AlertDialogHeader>
                  <AlertDialogTitle>Återöppna legojobb?</AlertDialogTitle>
                  <AlertDialogDescription>Statusen ändras tillbaka till "Hyvlat".</AlertDialogDescription>
                </AlertDialogHeader>
                <AlertDialogFooter>
                  <AlertDialogCancel>Avbryt</AlertDialogCancel>
                  <AlertDialogAction onClick={ateroppen}>Återöppna</AlertDialogAction>
                </AlertDialogFooter>
              </AlertDialogContent>
            </AlertDialog>
          )}
          {isLevererat && (
            <AlertDialog>
              <AlertDialogTrigger asChild>
                <Button variant="outline" className="gap-2">
                  <RotateCcw className="h-4 w-4" /> Återöppna
                </Button>
              </AlertDialogTrigger>
              <AlertDialogContent>
                <AlertDialogHeader>
                  <AlertDialogTitle>Återöppna levererat jobb?</AlertDialogTitle>
                  <AlertDialogDescription>Statusen ändras tillbaka till "Hyvlat".</AlertDialogDescription>
                </AlertDialogHeader>
                <AlertDialogFooter>
                  <AlertDialogCancel>Avbryt</AlertDialogCancel>
                  <AlertDialogAction onClick={ateroppen}>Återöppna</AlertDialogAction>
                </AlertDialogFooter>
              </AlertDialogContent>
            </AlertDialog>
          )}
          {!isLevererat && status === "mottaget" && jobbId && (
            <Button variant="outline" className="gap-2 text-orange-600" onClick={markeraHyvlat}>
              <CheckCircle className="h-4 w-4" /> Markera hyvlat
            </Button>
          )}
          {!isLevererat && (
            <AlertDialog>
              <AlertDialogTrigger asChild>
                <Button className="gap-2 bg-green-700 hover:bg-green-800" disabled={levering}>
                  {levering ? <Loader2 className="h-4 w-4 animate-spin" /> : <CheckCircle className="h-4 w-4" />}
                  Leverera & skriv ut
                </Button>
              </AlertDialogTrigger>
              <AlertDialogContent>
                <AlertDialogHeader>
                  <AlertDialogTitle>Bekräfta leverans?</AlertDialogTitle>
                  <AlertDialogDescription>
                    Jobbet sparas, markeras som levererat och en följesedel öppnas för utskrift.
                  </AlertDialogDescription>
                </AlertDialogHeader>
                <AlertDialogFooter>
                  <AlertDialogCancel>Avbryt</AlertDialogCancel>
                  <AlertDialogAction className="bg-green-700 hover:bg-green-800" onClick={leverera}>
                    Leverera
                  </AlertDialogAction>
                </AlertDialogFooter>
              </AlertDialogContent>
            </AlertDialog>
          )}
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Job details */}
      <Card>
        <CardHeader><CardTitle className="text-base">Jobböversikt</CardTitle></CardHeader>
        <CardContent>
          <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-4 gap-4">
            <div className="space-y-1.5">
              <label className="text-sm font-medium">Kund *</label>
              <Input
                placeholder="Kundens namn"
                value={kund}
                onChange={e => setKund(e.target.value)}
                list="kund-list"
                disabled={isLevererat}
              />
              <datalist id="kund-list">
                {kunder.map(k => <option key={k.id} value={k.namn} />)}
              </datalist>
            </div>
            <div className="space-y-1.5">
              <label className="text-sm font-medium">Mottaget datum</label>
              <Input type="date" value={datumIn} onChange={e => setDatumIn(e.target.value)} disabled={isLevererat} />
            </div>
            <div className="space-y-1.5">
              <label className="text-sm font-medium">Status</label>
              <Select value={status} onValueChange={setStatus} disabled={isLevererat}>
                <SelectTrigger><SelectValue /></SelectTrigger>
                <SelectContent>
                  <SelectItem value="mottaget">Mottaget</SelectItem>
                  <SelectItem value="hyvlat">Hyvlat</SelectItem>
                  <SelectItem value="levererat">Levererat</SelectItem>
                </SelectContent>
              </Select>
            </div>
            <div className="space-y-1.5">
              <label className="text-sm font-medium">Anteckning</label>
              <Input placeholder="Fritext..." value={anteckning} onChange={e => setAnteckning(e.target.value)} disabled={isLevererat} />
            </div>
          </div>
          <label className="mt-3 flex items-center gap-2 text-sm font-medium">
            <Checkbox checked={showCertificationOnDocument} onCheckedChange={checked => setShowCertificationOnDocument(checked === true)} disabled={isLevererat} />
            Visa certifieringsuppgifter på dokumentet
          </label>
          <div className="mt-4 rounded-md border p-3">
            <label className="flex items-center gap-2 text-sm font-medium">
              <Checkbox checked={useAlternateDocumentSender} onCheckedChange={(checked) => setUseAlternateDocumentSender(checked === true)} disabled={isLevererat} />
              Legoarbete – använd annan dokumentavsändare
            </label>
            {useAlternateDocumentSender && (
              <div className="mt-3 grid grid-cols-1 gap-3 sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-3">
                {([
                  ["companyName", "Företagsnamn"], ["streetAddress", "Adress"], ["postalCode", "Postnummer"],
                  ["city", "Ort"], ["country", "Land"], ["phone", "Telefon"], ["email", "E-post"],
                  ["organizationNumber", "Organisationsnummer"], ["vatNumber", "VAT-nummer"], ["website", "Webbplats"],
                  ["certificationText", "Certifieringstext"], ["certificationNumber", "Certifieringsnummer"],
                  ["invoiceDetails", "Fakturauppgifter"],
                ] as const).map(([field, label]) => (
                  <div className="space-y-1" key={field}>
                    <label className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">{label}</label>
                    <Input value={alternateSender[field] ?? ""} disabled={isLevererat} onChange={(event) => setAlternateSender(current => ({ ...current, [field]: event.target.value || null } as Branding))} />
                  </div>
                ))}
                <label className="flex items-center gap-2 text-sm font-medium">
                  <Checkbox checked={alternateSender.approvedForFTax} onCheckedChange={checked => setAlternateSender(current => ({ ...current, approvedForFTax: checked === true }))} disabled={isLevererat} />
                  Godkänd för F-skatt
                </label>
                <div className="space-y-1 sm:col-span-2 lg:col-span-3">
                  <label className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">Logotyp (valfritt)</label>
                  <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-3">
                    {alternateLogoPreview && (
                      <img src={alternateLogoPreview} alt="Alternativ dokumentlogotyp" className="h-12 w-auto rounded border bg-white object-contain p-1" />
                    )}
                    <Input
                      type="file"
                      accept="image/png,image/jpeg,image/webp,image/svg+xml"
                      disabled={isLevererat}
                      className="max-w-sm"
                      onChange={(event) => {
                        const next = event.target.files?.[0];
                        if (!next) return;
                        if (!["image/png", "image/jpeg", "image/webp", "image/svg+xml"].includes(next.type) || next.size > 5 * 1024 * 1024) {
                          toast({ title: "Ogiltig logotyp", description: "Välj PNG, JPG, WebP eller SVG, högst 5 MB.", variant: "destructive" });
                          event.target.value = "";
                          return;
                        }
                        setAlternateLogoFile(next);
                        setAlternateLogoPreview(URL.createObjectURL(next));
                      }}
                    />
                    {(alternateSender.logoPath || alternateLogoPreview) && !isLevererat && (
                      <Button
                        type="button"
                        size="sm"
                        variant="ghost"
                        onClick={() => {
                          setAlternateLogoFile(null);
                          setAlternateLogoPreview(null);
                          setAlternateSender(current => ({ ...current, logoPath: null, logoUrl: null }));
                        }}
                      >
                        Ta bort logotyp
                      </Button>
                    )}
                  </div>
                </div>
              </div>
            )}
          </div>

          {/* Kopplad spec/jobb */}
          <div className="mt-4 pt-4 border-t">
            <div className="flex items-center gap-2 mb-2">
              <Link2 className="h-4 w-4 text-muted-foreground" />
              <label className="text-sm font-medium">Kopplad (spec eller legojobb)</label>
            </div>
            {kopplingSummary() ? (
              <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                <button
                  className="text-sm font-medium text-primary hover:underline flex-1 text-left"
                  onClick={kopplingsNavigate}
                >
                  {kopplingSummary()}
                </button>
                {!isLevererat && (
                  <Button variant="ghost" size="sm" className="h-7 px-2 text-muted-foreground hover:text-destructive" onClick={clearKoppling}>
                    <X className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
                  </Button>
                )}
              </div>
            ) : !isLevererat ? (
              <div className="space-y-2">
                {!specPickerOpen ? (
                  <Button variant="outline" size="sm" className="gap-2 text-muted-foreground border-dashed" onClick={() => setSpecPickerOpen(true)}>
                    <Plus className="h-3.5 w-3.5" /> Koppla till spec eller legojobb...
                  </Button>
                ) : (
                  <div className="border rounded-lg p-2 space-y-2 max-w-md">
                    <div className="flex gap-2">
                      <Input
                        className="h-8 text-sm flex-1"
                        placeholder="Sök kund, nummer (Lego/Spec/Hyvl)..."
                        value={specSokQuery}
                        onChange={e => setSpecSokQuery(e.target.value)}
                        autoFocus
                      />
                      <Button variant="ghost" size="sm" className="h-8 px-2" onClick={() => { setSpecPickerOpen(false); setSpecSokQuery(""); }}>
                        <X className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
                      </Button>
                    </div>
                    <div className="max-h-56 overflow-y-auto space-y-1">
                      {allePickerItems
                        .filter(item => {
                          if (item.source === "lego" && item.id === jobbId) return false;
                          const label = pickerLabel(item).toLowerCase();
                          const q = specSokQuery.toLowerCase();
                          return !q || label.includes(q) || String(item.id).includes(q);
                        })
                        .slice(0, 25)
                        .map(item => {
                          const key = `${item.source}-${item.id}`;
                          const badge = item.source === "lego"
                            ? <span className="text-xs bg-orange-100 text-orange-700 rounded px-1.5 py-0.5 shrink-0">Lego</span>
                            : item.source === "spec" && item.typ === "hyvling"
                              ? <span className="text-xs bg-blue-100 text-blue-700 rounded px-1.5 py-0.5 shrink-0">Hyvling</span>
                              : <span className="text-xs bg-gray-100 text-gray-600 rounded px-1.5 py-0.5 shrink-0">Spec</span>;
                          return (
                            <button
                              key={key}
                              className="w-full text-left text-sm px-2 py-1.5 rounded hover:bg-muted flex items-center justify-between gap-2"
                              onClick={() => {
                                if (item.source === "lego") {
                                  setKopplatJobbId(item.id);
                                  setKopplatJobb({ id: item.id, jobbNr: item.jobbNr, kund: item.kund, status: item.status });
                                  setKopplatSpecId(null);
                                  setKopplatSpec(null);
                                } else {
                                  setKopplatSpecId(item.id);
                                  setKopplatSpec({ id: item.id, typ: item.typ, hyvlingNr: item.hyvlingNr, specNr: (item as any).specNr ?? null, kund: item.kund, status: item.status });
                                  setKopplatJobbId(null);
                                  setKopplatJobb(null);
                                }
                                setSpecPickerOpen(false);
                                setSpecSokQuery("");
                              }}
                            >
                              <span className="flex-1 truncate">{pickerLabel(item)}</span>
                              <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 shrink-0">
                                <span className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">{item.antalPaket} pkt</span>
                                {badge}
                              </div>
                            </button>
                          );
                        })}
                      {allePickerItems.length === 0 && (
                        <p className="text-sm text-muted-foreground text-center py-2">Laddar...</p>
                      )}
                    </div>
                  </div>
                )}
              </div>
            ) : (
              <p className="text-sm text-muted-foreground">—</p>
            )}
          </div>
        </CardContent>
      </Card>

      {/* Packages */}
      <Card>
        <CardHeader>
          <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
            <CardTitle className="text-base">Paket</CardTitle>
            {totals.m3 > 0 && (
              <div className="flex gap-4 text-sm text-muted-foreground flex-wrap">
                <span>Antal: <strong className="text-foreground">{totals.antal}</strong></span>
                <span>Lpm: <strong className="text-foreground">{totals.lpm.toFixed(1)}</strong></span>
                <span>m³: <strong className="text-foreground">{totals.m3.toFixed(3)}</strong></span>
                {totals.pris > 0 && (
                  <span className="text-green-700 font-semibold">
                    Totalpris: <strong>{totals.pris.toLocaleString("sv-SE", { minimumFractionDigits: 0, maximumFractionDigits: 0 })} kr</strong>
                  </span>
                )}
              </div>
            )}
          </div>
        </CardHeader>
        <CardContent className="p-0">
          <div className="overflow-x-auto">
            <table className="w-full text-sm">
              <thead className="bg-muted/50 border-y">
                <tr>
                  {([
                    ["pktNr", "Pkt nr"],
                    ["traslag", "Träslag"],
                    ["kvalitet", "Kval"],
                    ["rawTjocklek", "Råv. tjocklek"],
                    ["rawBredd", "Råv. bredd"],
                    ["hyvlatTill", "Hyvlat till"],
                    ["hyvlTjocklek", "Utdim T"],
                    ["hyvlBredd", "Utdim B"],
                    ["langdDm", "Längd (dm)"],
                    ["antal", "Antal"],
                  ] as [keyof Paket, string][]).map(([col, label]) => (
                    <th
                      key={col}
                      className="text-left px-3 py-2 font-medium whitespace-nowrap cursor-pointer select-none hover:bg-muted/80 transition-colors"
                      onClick={() => handleSortClick(col)}
                    >
                      <span className="flex items-center">
                        {label}
                        <SortIcon col={col} />
                      </span>
                    </th>
                  ))}
                  <th
                    className="text-right px-3 py-2 font-medium whitespace-nowrap cursor-pointer select-none hover:bg-muted/80 transition-colors"
                    onClick={() => handleSortClick("lpm")}
                  >
                    <span className="flex items-center justify-end">Lpm<SortIcon col="lpm" /></span>
                  </th>
                  <th
                    className="text-right px-3 py-2 font-medium whitespace-nowrap cursor-pointer select-none hover:bg-muted/80 transition-colors"
                    onClick={() => handleSortClick("m3")}
                  >
                    <span className="flex items-center justify-end">m³<SortIcon col="m3" /></span>
                  </th>
                  <th
                    className="text-right px-3 py-2 font-medium whitespace-nowrap cursor-pointer select-none hover:bg-muted/80 transition-colors"
                    onClick={() => handleSortClick("prisM3")}
                  >
                    <span className="flex items-center justify-end">Pris/m³<SortIcon col="prisM3" /></span>
                  </th>
                  <th className="text-right px-3 py-2 font-medium whitespace-nowrap">Totalpris (kr)</th>
                  <th className="text-left px-3 py-2 font-medium whitespace-nowrap">Ant.</th>
                  <th className="px-3 py-2"></th>
                </tr>
              </thead>
              <tbody>
                {sortedIndices.map((origIdx) => {
                  const p = paket[origIdx];
                  return (
                  <tr key={origIdx} className="border-b last:border-0 hover:bg-muted/20">
                    {(["pktNr", "traslag", "kvalitet", "rawTjocklek", "rawBredd", "hyvlatTill", "hyvlTjocklek", "hyvlBredd", "langdDm", "antal"] as (keyof Paket)[]).map(field => (
                      <td key={field} className="px-2 py-1">
                        <Input
                          className="h-8 min-w-[60px] w-full text-xs"
                          value={p[field] as string}
                          onChange={e => updatePaket(origIdx, field, e.target.value)}
                          disabled={isLevererat}
                          placeholder={field === "hyvlatTill" ? "T.ex. Hyvel" : ""}
                        />
                      </td>
                    ))}
                    <td className="px-2 py-1 text-muted-foreground text-xs text-right">
                      {p.lpm ? parseFloat(p.lpm).toFixed(1) : "–"}
                    </td>
                    <td className="px-2 py-1 text-muted-foreground text-xs text-right">
                      {p.m3 ? parseFloat(p.m3).toFixed(3) : "–"}
                    </td>
                    <td className="px-2 py-1">
                      <Input
                        className="h-8 w-[80px] text-xs text-right"
                        value={p.prisM3}
                        onChange={e => updatePaket(origIdx, "prisM3", e.target.value)}
                        disabled={isLevererat}
                        placeholder="kr/m³"
                      />
                    </td>
                    <td className="px-2 py-1 text-xs text-right font-medium">
                      {(() => {
                        const m3v = toN(p.m3), prv = toN(p.prisM3);
                        return m3v != null && prv != null
                          ? (m3v * prv).toLocaleString("sv-SE", { minimumFractionDigits: 0, maximumFractionDigits: 0 })
                          : "–";
                      })()}
                    </td>
                    <td className="px-2 py-1">
                      <Input
                        className="h-8 min-w-[80px] w-full text-xs"
                        value={p.anteckning}
                        onChange={e => updatePaket(origIdx, "anteckning", e.target.value)}
                        disabled={isLevererat}
                        placeholder="..."
                      />
                    </td>
                    <td className="px-2 py-1">
                      {!isLevererat && (
                        <button
                          onClick={() => removeRow(origIdx)}
                          className="p-1 rounded hover:bg-destructive/10 text-muted-foreground hover:text-destructive"
                        >
                          <Trash2 className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
                        </button>
                      )}
                    </td>
                  </tr>
                  );
                })}
              </tbody>
            </table>
          </div>
          {!isLevererat && (
            <div className="p-3 border-t">
              <Button variant="outline" size="sm" className="gap-2" onClick={addRow}>
                <Plus className="h-3.5 w-3.5" /> Lägg till rad
              </Button>
            </div>
          )}
        </CardContent>
      </Card>

      {/* Dolt spec-print-block — samma format som vanlig SPECIFIKATION */}
      {(() => {
        const filled = paket
          .map((p, i) => ({ p, i }))
          .filter(({ p }) => p.pktNr || p.rawTjocklek || p.langdDm || p.antal)
          .filter(({ i }) => !printFilter || printFilter.has(i))
          .map(({ p }) => p);
        const calcRowActM3 = (p: Paket): number | null => {
          const tk = toN(p.hyvlTjocklek), br = toN(p.hyvlBredd), ld = toN(p.langdDm), an = toN(p.antal);
          return tk && br && ld && an ? parseFloat(((tk / 1000) * (br / 1000) * (ld / 10) * an).toFixed(4)) : null;
        };
        const totalNomM3D = filled.reduce((s, p) => s + (calcM3(p) ?? 0), 0);
        const totalActM3 = filled.reduce((s, p) => s + (calcRowActM3(p) ?? 0), 0);
        const hasActM3 = filled.some(p => calcRowActM3(p) != null);
        const totalLpm = filled.reduce((s, p) => s + (toN(p.lpm) ?? 0), 0);
        const totalAntal = filled.reduce((s, p) => s + (toN(p.antal) ?? 0), 0);
        const totalPris = filled.reduce((s, p) => {
          const m3v = calcM3(p), prv = toN(p.prisM3);
          return s + (m3v != null && prv != null ? m3v * prv : 0);
        }, 0);
        const hasPris = filled.some(p => toN(p.prisM3) != null);
        const today = new Date().toLocaleDateString("sv-SE", { day: "numeric", month: "long", year: "numeric" });
        const thS: React.CSSProperties = { padding: "4px 6px", fontWeight: "bold", fontSize: "9px", textAlign: "center", borderRight: "1px solid rgba(0,0,0,0.15)", whiteSpace: "nowrap", verticalAlign: "middle" };
        const tdS: React.CSSProperties = { padding: "3px 5px", fontSize: "9px", color: "#000", borderRight: "1px solid #ddd", whiteSpace: "nowrap" };
        // Unika längder sorterade numeriskt — precis som vanliga specar
        const lengths = [...new Set(filled.map(p => p.langdDm).filter(Boolean))].sort((a, b) => parseFloat(a) - parseFloat(b));
        const perLength: Record<string, number> = {};
        for (const p of filled) {
          if (p.langdDm) perLength[p.langdDm] = (perLength[p.langdDm] ?? 0) + (toN(p.antal) ?? 0);
        }
        return (
          <div id="lego-spec-print" style={{ display: "none", visibility: "hidden", position: "absolute", left: "-9999px", top: 0, width: 0, height: 0, overflow: "hidden", pointerEvents: "none", fontFamily: "'Arial', sans-serif", fontSize: "10px", color: "#000" }}>
            <div className="document-print-page">

            {/* Header */}
            <div style={{ display: "flex", alignItems: "stretch", justifyContent: "space-between", marginBottom: "3mm", borderBottom: "2px solid #000", paddingBottom: "2mm", gap: "6mm" }}>
              <div style={{ display: "flex", alignItems: "center", gap: "8px", minWidth: "160px" }}>
                {documentSender.logoUrl && <img className="document-header-logo" data-document-logo="true" src={documentSender.logoUrl} alt={documentSender.companyName || "Logotyp"} />}
                <div style={{ lineHeight: "1.5" }}>
                  {documentSender.companyName && <div style={{ fontWeight: "bold", fontSize: "11px", letterSpacing: "0.3px" }}>{documentSender.companyName}</div>}
                  {documentSender.streetAddress && <div style={{ fontSize: "8.5px" }}>{documentSender.streetAddress}</div>}
                  {senderLocation && <div style={{ fontSize: "8.5px" }}>{senderLocation}</div>}
                  {documentSender.phone && <div style={{ fontSize: "8.5px" }}>Tel: {documentSender.phone}</div>}
                  {documentSender.email && <div style={{ fontSize: "8.5px" }}>{documentSender.email}</div>}
                </div>
              </div>
              <div style={{ flex: 1, display: "flex", flexDirection: "column", alignItems: "center", justifyContent: "center" }}>
                <div style={{ fontSize: "22px", fontWeight: "bold", letterSpacing: "4px", color: "#000" }}>LEGOJOBB</div>
                {displayNr && <div style={{ fontSize: "10px", fontWeight: "bold", marginTop: "2px" }}>Jobb #{displayNr}</div>}
              </div>
              <div style={{ minWidth: "140px", display: "flex", flexDirection: "column", justifyContent: "center", gap: "3px" }}>
                {[
                  { label: "Datum", value: today },
                  { label: "Mottaget", value: datumIn || "—" },
                  { label: "Antal paket", value: String(filled.length) },
                ].map(({ label, value }) => (
                  <div key={label} style={{ display: "flex", gap: "4px", fontSize: "10px", borderBottom: "1px solid #ccc", paddingBottom: "2px" }}>
                    <span style={{ fontWeight: "bold", minWidth: "72px" }}>{label}:</span>
                    <span>{value}</span>
                  </div>
                ))}
              </div>
            </div>

            {/* Kund / Anteckning */}
            <div style={{ marginBottom: "3mm", fontSize: "12px", display: "flex", gap: "8px", alignItems: "baseline" }}>
              <span style={{ fontWeight: "bold", minWidth: "60px" }}>Kund:</span>
              <span style={{ borderBottom: "1.5px solid #000", flex: 1, minWidth: "200px", paddingBottom: "1px", fontWeight: "bold", fontSize: "13px" }}>{kund || ""}</span>
              {anteckning && <>
                <span style={{ fontWeight: "bold", minWidth: "70px", marginLeft: "10mm" }}>Anteckning:</span>
                <span style={{ borderBottom: "1px solid #aaa", flex: 1, paddingBottom: "1px", fontSize: "10px" }}>{anteckning}</span>
              </>}
            </div>

            {/* Tabell med per-längd-kolumner precis som vanliga specar */}
            <table style={{ width: "100%", borderCollapse: "collapse", border: "1.5px solid #000" }}>
              <thead>
                <tr style={{ background: "#ddd", color: "#000", WebkitPrintColorAdjust: "exact", printColorAdjust: "exact" }}>
                  <th style={thS}>Pkt Nr</th>
                  <th style={thS}>Träslag</th>
                  <th style={thS}>Kval</th>
                  <th style={{ ...thS, borderRight: "1.5px solid #000" }} colSpan={2}>Råvara (mm)</th>
                  <th style={thS}>Hyvlat till</th>
                  <th style={{ ...thS, borderRight: "1.5px solid #000" }} colSpan={2}>Dim ut (mm)</th>
                  {lengths.map((l, i) => (
                    <th key={l} style={{ ...thS, fontWeight: "bold", borderRight: i === lengths.length - 1 ? "1.5px solid #000" : "1px solid #aaa" }}>{l}</th>
                  ))}
                  <th style={thS}>Styck</th>
                  <th style={thS}>Lpm</th>
                  <th style={{ ...thS, borderRight: (hasActM3 || hasPris) ? "1px solid #999" : "none" }}>Nom m³</th>
                  {hasActM3 && <th style={{ ...thS, borderRight: hasPris ? "1px solid #999" : "none", color: "#1d4ed8" }}>Act m³</th>}
                  {hasPris && <th style={thS}>Pris/m³</th>}
                  {hasPris && <th style={{ ...thS, borderRight: "none" }}>Summa</th>}
                </tr>
              </thead>
              <tbody>
                {filled.map((p, idx) => {
                  const nomM3v = calcM3(p), prv = toN(p.prisM3);
                  const rowPris = nomM3v != null && prv != null ? (nomM3v * prv).toFixed(0) : "";
                  return (
                    <tr key={idx} style={{ borderBottom: "1px solid #ccc", background: idx % 2 === 0 ? "#fff" : "#f4f4f4" }}>
                      <td style={{ ...tdS, fontWeight: "bold" }}>{p.pktNr || "–"}</td>
                      <td style={tdS}>{p.traslag || "–"}</td>
                      <td style={tdS}>{p.kvalitet || "–"}</td>
                      <td style={{ ...tdS, textAlign: "right" }}>{p.rawTjocklek || ""}</td>
                      <td style={{ ...tdS, textAlign: "right", borderRight: "1.5px solid #aaa" }}>{p.rawBredd || ""}</td>
                      <td style={tdS}>{p.hyvlatTill || "–"}</td>
                      <td style={{ ...tdS, textAlign: "right" }}>{p.hyvlTjocklek || ""}</td>
                      <td style={{ ...tdS, textAlign: "right", borderRight: "1.5px solid #aaa" }}>{p.hyvlBredd || ""}</td>
                      {lengths.map((l, i) => (
                        <td key={l} style={{ ...tdS, textAlign: "center", fontWeight: "bold", borderRight: i === lengths.length - 1 ? "1.5px solid #aaa" : "1px solid #ddd" }}>
                          {p.langdDm === l ? (p.antal || "") : ""}
                        </td>
                      ))}
                      <td style={{ ...tdS, textAlign: "right", fontWeight: "bold" }}>{p.antal || "–"}</td>
                      <td style={{ ...tdS, textAlign: "right" }}>{p.lpm ? parseFloat(p.lpm).toFixed(1) : ""}</td>
                      <td style={{ ...tdS, textAlign: "right", borderRight: (hasActM3 || hasPris) ? "1px solid #ddd" : "none" }}>{(() => { const v = calcM3(p); return v != null ? v.toFixed(3) : ""; })()}</td>
                      {hasActM3 && <td style={{ ...tdS, textAlign: "right", color: "#1d4ed8", borderRight: hasPris ? "1px solid #ddd" : "none" }}>{(() => { const a = calcRowActM3(p); return a != null ? a.toFixed(3) : ""; })()}</td>}
                      {hasPris && <td style={{ ...tdS, textAlign: "right" }}>{p.prisM3 ? `${p.prisM3} kr` : ""}</td>}
                      {hasPris && <td style={{ ...tdS, textAlign: "right", fontWeight: "bold", borderRight: "none" }}>{rowPris ? `${rowPris} kr` : ""}</td>}
                    </tr>
                  );
                })}
                {Array.from({ length: Math.max(0, 4 - filled.length) }).map((_, i) => (
                  <tr key={`empty-${i}`} style={{ borderBottom: "1px solid #ccc", height: "16px" }}>
                    <td style={tdS}></td><td style={tdS}></td><td style={tdS}></td>
                    <td style={tdS}></td><td style={{ ...tdS, borderRight: "1.5px solid #aaa" }}></td>
                    <td style={tdS}></td>
                    <td style={tdS}></td><td style={{ ...tdS, borderRight: "1.5px solid #aaa" }}></td>
                    {lengths.map((l, li) => <td key={l} style={{ ...tdS, borderRight: li === lengths.length - 1 ? "1.5px solid #aaa" : "1px solid #ddd" }}></td>)}
                    <td style={tdS}></td><td style={tdS}></td>
                    <td style={{ ...tdS, borderRight: (hasActM3 || hasPris) ? "1px solid #ddd" : "none" }}></td>
                    {hasActM3 && <td style={{ ...tdS, borderRight: hasPris ? "1px solid #ddd" : "none" }}></td>}
                    {hasPris && <td style={tdS}></td>}
                    {hasPris && <td style={{ ...tdS, borderRight: "none" }}></td>}
                  </tr>
                ))}
              </tbody>
              <tfoot>
                <tr style={{ borderTop: "2px solid #000", fontWeight: "bold", background: "#e8e8e8", fontSize: "10px" }}>
                  <td style={{ ...tdS, whiteSpace: "nowrap" }} colSpan={8}>Summa: {filled.length} paket</td>
                  {lengths.map((l, i) => (
                    <td key={l} style={{ ...tdS, textAlign: "center", fontWeight: "bold", borderRight: i === lengths.length - 1 ? "1.5px solid #aaa" : "1px solid #ddd" }}>
                      {perLength[l] ?? ""}
                    </td>
                  ))}
                  <td style={{ ...tdS, textAlign: "right" }}>{totalAntal.toFixed(0)}</td>
                  <td style={{ ...tdS, textAlign: "right" }}>{totalLpm.toFixed(1)}</td>
                  <td style={{ ...tdS, textAlign: "right", borderRight: (hasActM3 || hasPris) ? "1px solid #ddd" : "none" }}>{totalNomM3D.toFixed(3)}</td>
                  {hasActM3 && <td style={{ ...tdS, textAlign: "right", color: "#1d4ed8", borderRight: hasPris ? "1px solid #ddd" : "none" }}>{totalActM3.toFixed(3)}</td>}
                  {hasPris && <td style={{ ...tdS, textAlign: "right" }}></td>}
                  {hasPris && <td style={{ ...tdS, textAlign: "right", borderRight: "none" }}>{totalPris.toFixed(0)} kr</td>}
                </tr>
              </tfoot>
            </table>

            {/* Prissummering */}
            {hasPris && (
              <div style={{ marginTop: "5mm", display: "flex", justifyContent: "flex-end" }}>
                <table style={{ borderCollapse: "collapse", border: "1px solid #000", minWidth: "200px" }}>
                  <tbody>
                    <tr style={{ borderBottom: "1px solid #ccc" }}>
                      <td style={{ padding: "3px 8px", fontSize: "10px", textAlign: "right" }}>Total volym:</td>
                      <td style={{ padding: "3px 8px", fontWeight: "bold", fontSize: "10px", textAlign: "right" }}>{totalNomM3D.toFixed(3)} m³</td>
                    </tr>
                    <tr style={{ borderTop: "2px solid #000", background: "#e8e8e8" }}>
                      <td style={{ padding: "4px 8px", fontWeight: "bold", fontSize: "11px", textAlign: "right" }}>TOTALT:</td>
                      <td style={{ padding: "4px 8px", fontWeight: "bold", fontSize: "13px", textAlign: "right" }}>{totalPris.toFixed(0)} kr</td>
                    </tr>
                  </tbody>
                </table>
              </div>
            )}
            <DocumentCertificationBlock
              show={showCertificationOnDocument}
              certificationText={documentSender.certificationText}
              certificationNumber={documentSender.certificationNumber}
            />

            {/* Underskrift */}
            <div style={{ marginTop: "10mm", display: "flex", gap: "20mm" }}>
              {["Avsändare", "Mottagare"].map(label => (
                <div key={label} style={{ flex: 1 }}>
                  <div style={{ borderBottom: "1px solid #000", marginBottom: "2px", height: "10mm" }}></div>
                  <div style={{ fontSize: "8.5px" }}>{label}</div>
                </div>
              ))}
            </div>

            <DocumentInvoiceDetailsBlock invoiceDetails={documentSender.invoiceDetails} />
            <DocumentCompanyFooter profile={documentSender} />
            </div>
          </div>
        );
      })()}
    </div>
  );
}


================ FILE: artifacts/lager/src/pages/lego/LegoStatistik.tsx ================

import { useEffect, useState, useMemo } from "react";
import { customFetch } from "@workspace/api-client-react";
import { Card, CardContent, CardHeader, CardTitle } from "@/components/ui/card";
import { ArrowLeft, BarChart2, FileDown } from "lucide-react";
import { Link } from "wouter";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import {
  BarChart, Bar, XAxis, YAxis, CartesianGrid, Tooltip, Legend,
  ResponsiveContainer, PieChart, Pie, Cell,
} from "recharts";

type StatRow = {
  manad: string;
  kund: string;
  antalJobb: number;
  antalPaket: number;
  totalM3: number;
  totalPris: number;
};

type VsEgetData = {
  legoM3: number;
  egetM3: number;
  totalM3: number;
  legoAndel: number;
  egetAndel: number;
  perKund: { kund: string; antalJobb: number; actM3: number; andel: number; legoAndel: number }[];
};

const COLORS = ["#2d5a27", "#4f9a43", "#7dc36f", "#a8d9a0", "#3b82f6", "#6366f1", "#f59e0b", "#ef4444", "#8b5cf6", "#10b981"];
const LEGO_COLOR = "#3b82f6";
const EGET_COLOR = "#2d5a27";

function swedishMonth(yyyyMM: string): string {
  const [y, m] = yyyyMM.split("-");
  const months = ["Jan", "Feb", "Mar", "Apr", "Maj", "Jun", "Jul", "Aug", "Sep", "Okt", "Nov", "Dec"];
  return `${months[parseInt(m) - 1]} ${y}`;
}

function fmtKr(n: number) {
  return n.toLocaleString("sv-SE", { minimumFractionDigits: 0, maximumFractionDigits: 0 }) + " kr";
}

function fmtM3(n: number) {
  return n.toLocaleString("sv-SE", { minimumFractionDigits: 3, maximumFractionDigits: 3 }) + " m³";
}

export default function LegoStatistik() {
  const [rows, setRows] = useState<StatRow[]>([]);
  const [vsEget, setVsEget] = useState<VsEgetData | null>(null);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);

  useEffect(() => {
    Promise.all([
      customFetch<StatRow[]>("/api/lego/statistik"),
      customFetch<VsEgetData>("/api/lego/vs-eget"),
    ]).then(([statData, vsData]) => {
      setRows(statData);
      setVsEget(vsData);
      setLoading(false);
    });
  }, []);

  const manader = useMemo(() => [...new Set(rows.map(r => r.manad))].sort().reverse(), [rows]);
  const kunder = useMemo(() => [...new Set(rows.map(r => r.kund))].sort(), [rows]);
  const hasPris = rows.some(r => r.totalPris > 0);

  const barData = useMemo(() => manader.slice(0, 12).reverse().map(m => {
    const monthRows = rows.filter(r => r.manad === m);
    const entry: Record<string, string | number> = { manad: swedishMonth(m) };
    for (const k of kunder) {
      const r = monthRows.find(r => r.kund === k);
      entry[k] = r ? parseFloat(r.totalM3.toFixed(3)) : 0;
    }
    return entry;
  }), [manader, kunder, rows]);

  const kundTotals = useMemo(() => kunder.map(k => {
    const kundRows = rows.filter(r => r.kund === k);
    return {
      kund: k,
      totalJobb: kundRows.reduce((s, r) => s + r.antalJobb, 0),
      totalPaket: kundRows.reduce((s, r) => s + r.antalPaket, 0),
      totalM3: kundRows.reduce((s, r) => s + r.totalM3, 0),
      totalPris: kundRows.reduce((s, r) => s + r.totalPris, 0),
    };
  }).sort((a, b) => b.totalM3 - a.totalM3), [kunder, rows]);

  if (loading) return <div className="text-muted-foreground py-10 text-sm">Laddar statistik...</div>;

  const pieData = vsEget && vsEget.totalM3 > 0
    ? [
        { name: "Lego", value: vsEget.legoM3 },
        { name: "Eget", value: vsEget.egetM3 },
      ]
    : null;

  return (
    <div className="space-y-6">
      <div className="flex items-center gap-3 justify-between">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
          <Link href="/lego">
            <Button variant="ghost" size="icon"><ArrowLeft className="h-4 w-4" /></Button>
          </Link>
          <div>
            <h1 className="text-2xl font-bold">Lego-statistik</h1>
            <p className="text-muted-foreground text-sm">m³ hyvlat per kund och månad</p>
          </div>
        </div>
        <Button variant="outline" className="gap-2" onClick={() => window.open("/api/lego/statistik/export", "_blank")}>
          <FileDown className="h-4 w-4" /> Excel
        </Button>
      </div>

      {/* ── Lego vs Eget comparison ── */}
      {vsEget && vsEget.totalM3 > 0 && (
        <Card className="border-2">
          <CardHeader>
            <CardTitle className="text-base">Lego vs Eget arbete — total volym</CardTitle>
          </CardHeader>
          <CardContent>
            <div className="flex flex-col md:flex-row gap-6 items-center">
              {/* Pie chart */}
              <div className="flex-shrink-0">
                <ResponsiveContainer width={180} height={180}>
                  <PieChart>
                    <Pie
                      data={pieData!}
                      cx={85}
                      cy={85}
                      innerRadius={50}
                      outerRadius={80}
                      paddingAngle={3}
                      dataKey="value"
                    >
                      <Cell fill={LEGO_COLOR} />
                      <Cell fill={EGET_COLOR} />
                    </Pie>
                    <Tooltip formatter={(v: number) => [fmtM3(v)]} />
                  </PieChart>
                </ResponsiveContainer>
              </div>

              {/* Stats */}
              <div className="flex-1 space-y-4 w-full">
                {/* Lego bar */}
                <div>
                  <div className="flex justify-between items-end mb-1">
                    <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                      <span className="w-3 h-3 rounded-full inline-block" style={{ background: LEGO_COLOR }} />
                      <span className="font-semibold text-sm">Lego</span>
                    </div>
                    <div className="text-right">
                      <span className="font-bold text-lg tabular-nums">{vsEget.legoAndel}%</span>
                      <span className="text-muted-foreground text-xs ml-2">{fmtM3(vsEget.legoM3)}</span>
                    </div>
                  </div>
                  <div className="w-full h-4 bg-muted rounded-full overflow-hidden">
                    <div
                      className="h-full rounded-full transition-all"
                      style={{ width: `${vsEget.legoAndel}%`, background: LEGO_COLOR }}
                    />
                  </div>
                </div>

                {/* Eget bar */}
                <div>
                  <div className="flex justify-between items-end mb-1">
                    <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                      <span className="w-3 h-3 rounded-full inline-block" style={{ background: EGET_COLOR }} />
                      <span className="font-semibold text-sm">Eget arbete</span>
                    </div>
                    <div className="text-right">
                      <span className="font-bold text-lg tabular-nums">{vsEget.egetAndel}%</span>
                      <span className="text-muted-foreground text-xs ml-2">{fmtM3(vsEget.egetM3)}</span>
                    </div>
                  </div>
                  <div className="w-full h-4 bg-muted rounded-full overflow-hidden">
                    <div
                      className="h-full rounded-full transition-all"
                      style={{ width: `${vsEget.egetAndel}%`, background: EGET_COLOR }}
                    />
                  </div>
                </div>

                {/* Total */}
                <div className="pt-1 border-t flex justify-between items-center text-sm">
                  <span className="text-muted-foreground">Totalt hyvlat</span>
                  <span className="font-bold tabular-nums">{fmtM3(vsEget.totalM3)}</span>
                </div>
              </div>
            </div>

            {/* Per lego customer */}
            {vsEget.perKund.length > 0 && (
              <div className="mt-6">
                <p className="text-sm font-medium text-muted-foreground mb-3">m³ per legokund (av total volym)</p>
                <div className="space-y-2">
                  {vsEget.perKund.map((k, i) => (
                    <div key={k.kund}>
                      <div className="flex justify-between items-end mb-1">
                        <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                          <span
                            className="w-2.5 h-2.5 rounded-full inline-block"
                            style={{ background: COLORS[i % COLORS.length] }}
                          />
                          <span className="text-sm font-medium">{k.kund}</span>
                          <span className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">({k.antalJobb} jobb)</span>
                        </div>
                        <div className="text-right">
                          <span className="font-semibold text-sm tabular-nums">{k.andel}%</span>
                          <span className="text-muted-foreground text-xs ml-2">{fmtM3(k.actM3)}</span>
                          <span className="text-muted-foreground text-xs ml-1">· {k.legoAndel}% av lego</span>
                        </div>
                      </div>
                      <div className="w-full h-2.5 bg-muted rounded-full overflow-hidden">
                        <div
                          className="h-full rounded-full"
                          style={{ width: `${k.andel}%`, background: COLORS[i % COLORS.length] }}
                        />
                      </div>
                    </div>
                  ))}
                </div>
              </div>
            )}
          </CardContent>
        </Card>
      )}

      {rows.length === 0 ? (
        <div className="text-center py-20 text-muted-foreground">
          <BarChart2 className="h-12 w-12 mx-auto mb-3 opacity-30" />
          <p>Ingen data ännu. Registrera legojobb för att se statistik.</p>
        </div>
      ) : (
        <>
          {/* Bar chart - m3 per month per customer */}
          <Card>
            <CardHeader><CardTitle className="text-base">m³ per månad (12 månader)</CardTitle></CardHeader>
            <CardContent>
              <ResponsiveContainer width="100%" height={280}>
                <BarChart data={barData} margin={{ top: 4, right: 16, left: 0, bottom: 4 }}>
                  <CartesianGrid strokeDasharray="3 3" stroke="#f0f0f0" />
                  <XAxis dataKey="manad" tick={{ fontSize: 11 }} />
                  <YAxis tick={{ fontSize: 11 }} unit=" m³" width={60} />
                  <Tooltip formatter={(v: number) => [`${v.toFixed(3)} m³`]} />
                  <Legend />
                  {kunder.map((k, i) => (
                    <Bar key={k} dataKey={k} stackId="a" fill={COLORS[i % COLORS.length]} radius={i === kunder.length - 1 ? [3, 3, 0, 0] : undefined} />
                  ))}
                </BarChart>
              </ResponsiveContainer>
            </CardContent>
          </Card>

          {/* Per customer totals */}
          <Card>
            <CardHeader><CardTitle className="text-base">Summering per kund (allt)</CardTitle></CardHeader>
            <CardContent className="p-0">
              <table className="w-full text-sm">
                <thead className="bg-muted/50 border-b">
                  <tr>
                    <th className="text-left px-4 py-3 font-medium">Kund</th>
                    <th className="text-right px-4 py-3 font-medium">Jobb</th>
                    <th className="text-right px-4 py-3 font-medium">Paket</th>
                    <th className="text-right px-4 py-3 font-medium">m³</th>
                    {vsEget && vsEget.legoM3 > 0 && (
                      <th className="text-right px-4 py-3 font-medium">Andel av lego</th>
                    )}
                    {hasPris && <th className="text-right px-4 py-3 font-medium">Totalpris</th>}
                  </tr>
                </thead>
                <tbody>
                  {kundTotals.map(k => {
                    const vsKund = vsEget?.perKund.find(p => p.kund === k.kund);
                    return (
                      <tr key={k.kund} className="border-b last:border-0">
                        <td className="px-4 py-2 font-medium">{k.kund}</td>
                        <td className="px-4 py-2 text-right tabular-nums">{k.totalJobb}</td>
                        <td className="px-4 py-2 text-right tabular-nums">{k.totalPaket}</td>
                        <td className="px-4 py-2 text-right tabular-nums font-medium">{k.totalM3.toFixed(3)}</td>
                        {vsEget && vsEget.legoM3 > 0 && (
                          <td className="px-4 py-2 text-right tabular-nums text-blue-700 font-medium">
                            {vsKund ? `${vsKund.legoAndel}%` : "–"}
                          </td>
                        )}
                        {hasPris && <td className="px-4 py-2 text-right tabular-nums font-medium text-green-700">{k.totalPris > 0 ? fmtKr(k.totalPris) : "–"}</td>}
                      </tr>
                    );
                  })}
                  <tr className="bg-muted/30 font-semibold">
                    <td className="px-4 py-2">Totalt</td>
                    <td className="px-4 py-2 text-right">{kundTotals.reduce((s, k) => s + k.totalJobb, 0)}</td>
                    <td className="px-4 py-2 text-right">{kundTotals.reduce((s, k) => s + k.totalPaket, 0)}</td>
                    <td className="px-4 py-2 text-right">{kundTotals.reduce((s, k) => s + k.totalM3, 0).toFixed(3)}</td>
                    {vsEget && vsEget.legoM3 > 0 && <td className="px-4 py-2 text-right text-blue-700">100%</td>}
                    {hasPris && <td className="px-4 py-2 text-right text-green-700">{fmtKr(kundTotals.reduce((s, k) => s + k.totalPris, 0))}</td>}
                  </tr>
                </tbody>
              </table>
            </CardContent>
          </Card>

          {/* Monthly breakdown table */}
          <Card>
            <CardHeader><CardTitle className="text-base">Månadsöversikt</CardTitle></CardHeader>
            <CardContent className="p-0">
              <div className="overflow-x-auto">
                <table className="w-full text-sm">
                  <thead className="bg-muted/50 border-b">
                    <tr>
                      <th className="text-left px-4 py-3 font-medium">Månad</th>
                      <th className="text-left px-4 py-3 font-medium">Kund</th>
                      <th className="text-right px-4 py-3 font-medium">Jobb</th>
                      <th className="text-right px-4 py-3 font-medium">Paket</th>
                      <th className="text-right px-4 py-3 font-medium">m³</th>
                      {hasPris && <th className="text-right px-4 py-3 font-medium">Totalpris</th>}
                    </tr>
                  </thead>
                  <tbody>
                    {manader.map(m => {
                      const mRows = rows.filter(r => r.manad === m);
                      const mTotal = mRows.reduce((s, r) => s + r.totalM3, 0);
                      const mPris = mRows.reduce((s, r) => s + r.totalPris, 0);
                      return [
                        ...mRows.map((r, i) => (
                          <tr key={`${m}-${r.kund}`} className="border-b">
                            <td className="px-4 py-2 text-muted-foreground">{i === 0 ? swedishMonth(m) : ""}</td>
                            <td className="px-4 py-2">{r.kund}</td>
                            <td className="px-4 py-2 text-right tabular-nums">{r.antalJobb}</td>
                            <td className="px-4 py-2 text-right tabular-nums">{r.antalPaket}</td>
                            <td className="px-4 py-2 text-right tabular-nums">{r.totalM3.toFixed(3)}</td>
                            {hasPris && <td className="px-4 py-2 text-right tabular-nums">{r.totalPris > 0 ? fmtKr(r.totalPris) : "–"}</td>}
                          </tr>
                        )),
                        mRows.length > 1 && (
                          <tr key={`${m}-total`} className="bg-muted/20 border-b-2 border-muted">
                            <td className="px-4 py-1.5 text-xs text-muted-foreground"></td>
                            <td className="px-4 py-1.5 text-xs font-medium text-muted-foreground">Summa {swedishMonth(m)}</td>
                            <td className="px-4 py-1.5 text-right text-xs font-medium tabular-nums">{mRows.reduce((s, r) => s + r.antalJobb, 0)}</td>
                            <td className="px-4 py-1.5 text-right text-xs font-medium tabular-nums">{mRows.reduce((s, r) => s + r.antalPaket, 0)}</td>
                            <td className="px-4 py-1.5 text-right text-xs font-medium tabular-nums">{mTotal.toFixed(3)}</td>
                            {hasPris && <td className="px-4 py-1.5 text-right text-xs font-medium tabular-nums">{mPris > 0 ? fmtKr(mPris) : "–"}</td>}
                          </tr>
                        ),
                      ];
                    })}
                  </tbody>
                </table>
              </div>
            </CardContent>
          </Card>
        </>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}
