================ FILE: artifacts/lager/src/pages/papperskorg/PapperskorgPage.tsx ================

import { useState } from "react";
import { customFetch } from "@workspace/api-client-react";
import { useQuery, useMutation, useQueryClient } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import { useToast } from "@/hooks/use-toast";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { Badge } from "@/components/ui/badge";
import {
  AlertDialog,
  AlertDialogAction,
  AlertDialogCancel,
  AlertDialogContent,
  AlertDialogDescription,
  AlertDialogFooter,
  AlertDialogHeader,
  AlertDialogTitle,
} from "@/components/ui/alert-dialog";
import { Trash2, RotateCcw, AlertTriangle, PackageOpen } from "lucide-react";
import { format } from "date-fns";
import { sv } from "date-fns/locale";

interface LagerPost {
  id: number;
  pktNr: string | null;
  traslag: string | null;
  profil: string | null;
  kvalitet: string | null;
  nomTjocklek: number | null;
  nomBredd: number | null;
  antal: number | null;
  plats: string | null;
  raderadAt: string | null;
  uppdateradAt: string;
}

function formatDim(p: LagerPost) {
  if (p.nomTjocklek && p.nomBredd) return `${p.nomTjocklek}×${p.nomBredd}`;
  return "—";
}

export default function PapperskorgPage() {
  const { toast } = useToast();
  const qc = useQueryClient();
  const [permanentId, setPermanentId] = useState<number | null>(null);

  const { data: poster = [], isLoading } = useQuery<LagerPost[]>({
    queryKey: ["/api/lager/papperskorg"],
    queryFn: () => customFetch<LagerPost[]>("/api/lager/papperskorg"),
  });

  const aterstallMutation = useMutation({
    mutationFn: (id: number) =>
      customFetch(`/api/lager/${id}/aterstall`, { method: "POST" }),
    onSuccess: () => {
      qc.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["/api/lager/papperskorg"] });
      qc.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["/api/lager"] });
      toast({ title: "Paket återställt", description: "Paketet är tillbaka i lagerlistan." });
    },
    onError: () => {
      toast({ title: "Fel vid återställning", variant: "destructive" });
    },
  });

  const permanentDeleteMutation = useMutation({
    mutationFn: (id: number) =>
      customFetch(`/api/lager/${id}/permanent`, { method: "DELETE" }),
    onSuccess: () => {
      qc.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["/api/lager/papperskorg"] });
      toast({ title: "Paketet är permanent raderat" });
    },
    onError: () => {
      toast({ title: "Fel vid radering", variant: "destructive" });
    },
  });

  return (
    <div className="flex flex-col h-full">
      <div className="border-b px-6 py-4 flex items-center gap-3 bg-background">
        <Trash2 className="h-6 w-6 text-destructive" />
        <div>
          <h1 className="text-xl font-semibold">Papperskorg</h1>
          <p className="text-sm text-muted-foreground">Raderade paket — kan återställas av admin</p>
        </div>
        {poster.length > 0 && (
          <Badge variant="destructive" className="ml-auto">{poster.length} paket</Badge>
        )}
      </div>

      <div className="p-6 flex-1 overflow-auto">
        {isLoading && (
          <div className="text-center text-muted-foreground py-16">Laddar…</div>
        )}

        {!isLoading && poster.length === 0 && (
          <div className="flex flex-col items-center justify-center py-24 gap-3 text-muted-foreground">
            <PackageOpen className="h-12 w-12 opacity-20" />
            <p className="font-medium">Papperskorgen är tom</p>
            <p className="text-sm">Raderade paket hamnar här och kan återställas</p>
          </div>
        )}

        {poster.length > 0 && (
          <>
            <div className="flex items-center gap-2 mb-4 rounded-lg border border-amber-200 bg-amber-50 px-4 py-3 text-sm text-amber-800">
              <AlertTriangle className="h-4 w-4 shrink-0" />
              Paketen nedan kan återställas. "Radera permanent" tar bort dem för alltid.
            </div>

            <div className="rounded-lg border overflow-hidden">
              <table className="w-full text-sm">
                <thead className="bg-muted/50">
                  <tr>
                    <th className="text-left px-4 py-2.5 font-medium text-muted-foreground">Paket-ID</th>
                    <th className="text-left px-4 py-2.5 font-medium text-muted-foreground">Träslag</th>
                    <th className="text-left px-4 py-2.5 font-medium text-muted-foreground">Profil</th>
                    <th className="text-left px-4 py-2.5 font-medium text-muted-foreground">Dim</th>
                    <th className="text-left px-4 py-2.5 font-medium text-muted-foreground">Antal</th>
                    <th className="text-left px-4 py-2.5 font-medium text-muted-foreground">Plats</th>
                    <th className="text-left px-4 py-2.5 font-medium text-muted-foreground">Raderades</th>
                    <th className="px-4 py-2.5" />
                  </tr>
                </thead>
                <tbody>
                  {poster.map((p) => (
                    <tr key={p.id} className="border-t hover:bg-muted/20 transition-colors">
                      <td className="px-4 py-3 font-mono font-medium">{p.pktNr ?? "—"}</td>
                      <td className="px-4 py-3">{p.traslag ?? "—"}</td>
                      <td className="px-4 py-3">
                        <Badge variant="outline" className="font-normal">{p.profil ?? "—"}</Badge>
                      </td>
                      <td className="px-4 py-3 font-mono text-xs">{formatDim(p)}</td>
                      <td className="px-4 py-3">{p.antal ?? "—"}</td>
                      <td className="px-4 py-3 text-muted-foreground text-xs">{p.plats ?? "—"}</td>
                      <td className="px-4 py-3 text-muted-foreground text-xs">
                        {p.raderadAt
                          ? format(new Date(p.raderadAt), "d MMM yyyy HH:mm", { locale: sv })
                          : "—"}
                      </td>
                      <td className="px-4 py-3">
                        <div className="flex items-center gap-2 justify-end">
                          <Button
                            size="sm"
                            variant="outline"
                            className="gap-1.5 h-7 text-xs"
                            onClick={() => aterstallMutation.mutate(p.id)}
                            disabled={aterstallMutation.isPending}
                          >
                            <RotateCcw className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
                            Återställ
                          </Button>
                          <Button
                            size="sm"
                            variant="destructive"
                            className="gap-1.5 h-7 text-xs"
                            onClick={() => setPermanentId(p.id)}
                          >
                            <Trash2 className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
                            Radera permanent
                          </Button>
                        </div>
                      </td>
                    </tr>
                  ))}
                </tbody>
              </table>
            </div>
          </>
        )}
      </div>

      <AlertDialog open={permanentId !== null} onOpenChange={(o) => { if (!o) setPermanentId(null); }}>
        <AlertDialogContent>
          <AlertDialogHeader>
            <AlertDialogTitle>Radera permanent?</AlertDialogTitle>
            <AlertDialogDescription>
              Detta raderar paketet för alltid. Det går inte att ångra.
            </AlertDialogDescription>
          </AlertDialogHeader>
          <AlertDialogFooter>
            <AlertDialogCancel>Avbryt</AlertDialogCancel>
            <AlertDialogAction
              className="bg-destructive text-destructive-foreground hover:bg-destructive/90"
              onClick={() => {
                if (permanentId !== null) {
                  permanentDeleteMutation.mutate(permanentId);
                  setPermanentId(null);
                }
              }}
            >
              Radera permanent
            </AlertDialogAction>
          </AlertDialogFooter>
        </AlertDialogContent>
      </AlertDialog>
    </div>
  );
}


================ FILE: artifacts/lager/src/pages/prislistor/PrislistorPage.tsx ================

import { useRef, useState, useEffect } from "react";
import { useQuery, useMutation, useQueryClient } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import { customFetch } from "@workspace/api-client-react";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { Input } from "@/components/ui/input";
import { Card, CardContent, CardHeader, CardTitle, CardDescription } from "@/components/ui/card";
import { Badge } from "@/components/ui/badge";
import { useToast } from "@/hooks/use-toast";
import { Upload, Trash2, ArrowLeft, Printer, FileSpreadsheet, Loader2, RefreshCw, Pencil, Check, X, Eraser, ChevronUp, ChevronDown } from "lucide-react";
import { Link } from "wouter";
import { format } from "date-fns";
import { sv } from "date-fns/locale";
import { useBranding } from "@/components/branding/BrandingContext";

interface Prislista {
  id: number;
  namn: string;
  kund: string | null;
  datum: string | null;
  skapadAt: string;
}

interface PrislistaRad {
  id: number;
  nomMatt: string | null;
  actMatt: string | null;
  traslag: string | null;
  beskrivning: string | null;
  lpmPerM3: string | null;
  prisPerM3: string | null;
  prisPerLpm: string | null;
  grundmalatPrisPerLpm: string | null;
  arRubrik: boolean;
  sortering: number;
}

interface PrislistaDetail extends Prislista {
  rader: PrislistaRad[];
}

export default function PrislistorPage() {
  const branding = useBranding();
  const [vald, setVald] = useState<number | null>(null);
  const [editingId, setEditingId] = useState<number | null>(null);
  const [editNamn, setEditNamn] = useState("");
  const fileRef = useRef<HTMLInputElement>(null);
  const { toast } = useToast();
  const queryClient = useQueryClient();

  const { data: listor = [], isLoading } = useQuery<Prislista[]>({
    queryKey: ["/api/prislistor"],
    queryFn: () => customFetch<Prislista[]>("/api/prislistor"),
  });

  const { data: detalj, isLoading: detaljLaddar } = useQuery<PrislistaDetail>({
    queryKey: ["/api/prislistor", vald],
    queryFn: () => customFetch<PrislistaDetail>(`/api/prislistor/${vald}`),
    enabled: vald !== null,
  });

  const importMutation = useMutation({
    mutationFn: async (file: File) => {
      const form = new FormData();
      form.append("file", file);
      return customFetch<{ namn: string; antalRader: number; id: number }>("/api/prislistor/import", { method: "POST", body: form });
    },
    onSuccess: (data) => {
      queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["/api/prislistor"] });
      toast({ title: "Prislista importerad", description: `${data.namn} — ${data.antalRader} rader` });
      setVald(data.id);
    },
    onError: (e: Error) => toast({ title: "Importfel", description: e.message, variant: "destructive" }),
  });

  const raderaMutation = useMutation({
    mutationFn: async (id: number) => {
      await customFetch(`/api/prislistor/${id}`, { method: "DELETE" });
    },
    onSuccess: () => {
      queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["/api/prislistor"] });
      setVald(null);
      toast({ title: "Prislista raderad" });
    },
    onError: (e: Error) => toast({ title: "Fel", description: e.message, variant: "destructive" }),
  });

  const synkaMutation = useMutation({
    mutationFn: async (id: number) => {
      return customFetch<{ uppdaterade: number; raderProcessade: number; misslyckade: string[] }>(`/api/prislistor/${id}/synka-lager`, { method: "POST" });
    },
    onSuccess: (data) => {
      toast({
        title: "Priser synkroniserade",
        description: `${data.uppdaterade} lagerposter uppdaterade från ${data.raderProcessade} prislistarader.`,
      });
    },
    onError: (e: Error) => toast({ title: "Fel vid synkronisering", description: e.message, variant: "destructive" }),
  });

  const rensaMutation = useMutation({
    mutationFn: async () => {
      return customFetch<{ rensade: number }>("/api/prislistor/rensa-lager-priser", { method: "POST" });
    },
    onSuccess: (data) => {
      toast({ title: "Priser rensade", description: `${data.rensade} paket fick m³-pris nollställt. Synka prislistorna på nytt.` });
    },
    onError: (e: Error) => toast({ title: "Fel", description: e.message, variant: "destructive" }),
  });

  const reorderMutation = useMutation({
    mutationFn: async (items: { id: number; sortering: number }[]) => {
      return customFetch("/api/prislistor/reorder", {
        method: "PATCH",
        headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
        body: JSON.stringify(items),
      });
    },
    onSuccess: () => queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["/api/prislistor"] }),
  });

  function flytta(lista: Prislista, riktning: -1 | 1) {
    const idx = listor.findIndex(l => l.id === lista.id);
    if (idx < 0) return;
    const nyIdx = idx + riktning;
    if (nyIdx < 0 || nyIdx >= listor.length) return;
    const ny = [...listor];
    [ny[idx], ny[nyIdx]] = [ny[nyIdx], ny[idx]];
    reorderMutation.mutate(ny.map((l, i) => ({ id: l.id, sortering: i })));
  }

  const byttNamnMutation = useMutation({
    mutationFn: async ({ id, namn }: { id: number; namn: string }) => {
      return customFetch(`/api/prislistor/${id}`, {
        method: "PUT",
        headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
        body: JSON.stringify({ namn }),
      });
    },
    onSuccess: () => {
      queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["/api/prislistor"] });
      setEditingId(null);
      toast({ title: "Namn sparat" });
    },
    onError: (e: Error) => toast({ title: "Fel", description: e.message, variant: "destructive" }),
  });

  function startEdit(lista: Prislista, e: React.MouseEvent) {
    e.stopPropagation();
    setEditingId(lista.id);
    setEditNamn(lista.namn);
  }

  function cancelEdit(e?: React.MouseEvent) {
    e?.stopPropagation();
    setEditingId(null);
    setEditNamn("");
  }

  function saveEdit(id: number, e?: React.MouseEvent) {
    e?.stopPropagation();
    if (editNamn.trim()) byttNamnMutation.mutate({ id, namn: editNamn.trim() });
  }

  function handleFilVal(e: React.ChangeEvent<HTMLInputElement>) {
    const file = e.target.files?.[0];
    if (file) importMutation.mutate(file);
    e.target.value = "";
  }

  function handlePrint() {
    if (!detalj) return;
    const win = window.open("", "_blank");
    if (!win) return;

    const rubrik = detalj.kund || detalj.namn;
    const datumText = detalj.datum ? `Prislista ${detalj.datum} tillsvidare` : "";

    const raderHtml = detalj.rader.map((rad) => {
      if (rad.arRubrik) {
        return `<tr class="rubrik-rad"><td colspan="8">${rad.beskrivning ?? ""}</td></tr>`;
      }
      const lpm = rad.lpmPerM3 ? Number(rad.lpmPerM3).toFixed(1) : "—";
      const pm3 = rad.prisPerM3 ? Number(rad.prisPerM3).toLocaleString("sv-SE") + " kr" : "—";
      const plpm = rad.prisPerLpm ? Number(rad.prisPerLpm).toFixed(2) + " kr" : "—";
      const grund = rad.grundmalatPrisPerLpm ? Number(rad.grundmalatPrisPerLpm).toFixed(2) + " kr" : "";
      return `<tr>
        <td class="mono">${rad.nomMatt ?? "—"}</td>
        <td class="mono dim">${rad.actMatt ?? "—"}</td>
        <td>${rad.traslag ?? ""}</td>
        <td>${rad.beskrivning ?? ""}</td>
        <td class="num">${lpm}</td>
        <td class="num bold">${pm3}</td>
        <td class="num">${plpm}</td>
        <td class="num">${grund}</td>
      </tr>`;
    }).join("\n");

    win.document.write(`<!DOCTYPE html>
<html lang="sv">
<head>
  <meta charset="utf-8" />
  <title>Prislista – ${rubrik}</title>
  <style>
    *, *::before, *::after { box-sizing: border-box; margin: 0; padding: 0; }

    header img { height: 22mm; width: auto; }
    header .meta { text-align: right; }

    header .meta p { font-size: 8pt; color: #000; }

    table { width: 100%; border-collapse: collapse; }
    thead tr { border-bottom: 1.5pt solid #000; }
    thead th { padding: 1.5mm 2mm 1.5mm 0; font-size: 7.5pt; font-weight: 700; text-align: left; white-space: nowrap; }
    thead th.num { text-align: right; }

    tbody tr { border-bottom: 0.5pt solid #aaa; }
    tbody tr.rubrik-rad td {
      padding: 3mm 0 0.5mm 0;
      font-size: 7pt;
      font-weight: 700;
      text-transform: uppercase;
      letter-spacing: 0.05em;
      color: #000;
      border-bottom: none;
    }
    tbody td { padding: 1mm 2mm 1mm 0; vertical-align: middle; }
    td.mono { font-family: "Courier New", monospace; font-size: 7.5pt; white-space: nowrap; }
    td.dim { color: #000; }
    td.num { text-align: right; white-space: nowrap; }
    td.bold { font-weight: 600; }

    col.nom { width: 18mm; }
    col.akt { width: 16mm; }
    col.trasl { width: 14mm; }
    col.beskr { }
    col.lpm { width: 15mm; }
    col.pm3 { width: 21mm; }
    col.plpm { width: 19mm; }
    col.grund { width: 21mm; }

    footer { margin-top: 6mm; font-size: 7pt; color: #000; border-top: 0.5pt solid #aaa; padding-top: 2mm; }
  </style>
</head>
<body>
  <header>
    ${branding.logoUrl ? `<img src="${branding.logoUrl}" alt="${branding.companyName}" />` : ""}
    <div class="meta">
      <h1>${rubrik}</h1>
      ${datumText ? `<p>${datumText}</p>` : ""}

      </tr>

    win.document.close();
    win.focus();
    setTimeout(() => win.print(), 400);
  }

  const handlePrintRef = useRef(handlePrint);
  useEffect(() => { handlePrintRef.current = handlePrint; });
  useEffect(() => {
    const h = () => handlePrintRef.current();
    window.addEventListener("shortcut:print", h);
    return () => window.removeEventListener("shortcut:print", h);
  }, []);

  if (vald !== null && detalj) {
    return (
      <div className="p-4 md:p-6 max-w-5xl mx-auto space-y-4">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-3 print:hidden flex-wrap">
          <Button variant="ghost" size="sm" onClick={() => setVald(null)}>
            <ArrowLeft className="h-4 w-4 mr-1" /> Tillbaka

                  className="h-8 text-sm font-semibold w-72"

                  {byttNamnMutation.isPending ? <Loader2 className="h-4 w-4 animate-spin" /> : <Check className="h-4 w-4" />}
                </Button>
                <Button size="icon" variant="ghost" className="h-8 w-8" onClick={() => cancelEdit()}>
                  <X className="h-4 w-4" />
                </Button>
              </div>
            ) : (
              <div className="flex items-center gap-1 group print:hidden">
                <h1 className="text-xl font-bold">{detalj.namn}</h1>
                <Button
                  size="icon"
                  variant="ghost"
                  className="h-7 w-7 opacity-0 group-hover:opacity-100 transition-opacity text-muted-foreground hover:text-foreground"
                  onClick={e => startEdit(detalj, e)}
                  title="Redigera namn"
                >
                  <Pencil className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
                </Button>
              </div>
            )}
            <h1 className="text-xl font-bold hidden print:block">{detalj.namn}</h1>
          </div>
          {detalj.datum && (
            <p className="text-sm text-muted-foreground">Prislista {detalj.datum} tillsvidare</p>
          )}
        </div>

        {detaljLaddar ? (
          <div className="flex justify-center p-10"><Loader2 className="h-6 w-6 animate-spin" /></div>
        ) : (
          <div className="overflow-x-auto">
            <table className="w-full text-sm border-collapse">

                          {rad.beskrivning}
                        </td>

          <Button
            variant="outline"
            onClick={() => {

              : <><Eraser className="mr-2 h-4 w-4" /> Rensa alla priser</>}

          <Button onClick={() => fileRef.current?.click()} disabled={importMutation.isPending}>
            {importMutation.isPending

              : <><Upload className="mr-2 h-4 w-4" /> Importera Excel</>}
          </Button>
          <input ref={fileRef} type="file" accept=".xlsx,.xls" className="hidden" onChange={handleFilVal} />
        </div>
      </div>

      {isLoading ? (
        <div className="flex justify-center p-10"><Loader2 className="h-6 w-6 animate-spin" /></div>

            <p className="font-medium">Inga prislistor ännu</p>
            <p className="text-sm text-muted-foreground mt-1">
              Importera en Excel-fil (.xlsx) med er prislista.
            </p>
          </div>
          <Button variant="outline" onClick={() => fileRef.current?.click()}>
            <Upload className="mr-2 h-4 w-4" /> Välj Excel-fil
          </Button>
        </div>
      ) : (
        <div className="grid gap-3">
          {listor.map((lista) => {
            const isEditing = editingId === lista.id;
            return (
              <Card
                key={lista.id}
                className={`transition-shadow ${isEditing ? "shadow-md" : "cursor-pointer hover:shadow-md"}`}
                onClick={() => !isEditing && setVald(lista.id)}
              >
                <CardHeader className="pb-2">
                  <div className="flex items-start justify-between gap-3">
                    <div className="min-w-0 flex-1">
                      {isEditing ? (
                        <div className="flex items-center gap-2" onClick={e => e.stopPropagation()}>
                          <Input
                            autoFocus
                            value={editNamn}
                            onChange={e => setEditNamn(e.target.value)}
                            onKeyDown={e => {
                              if (e.key === "Enter") saveEdit(lista.id);
                              if (e.key === "Escape") cancelEdit();
                            }}
                            className="h-8 text-sm font-medium"
                          />
                          <Button
                            size="icon"
                            variant="ghost"
                            className="h-8 w-8 text-primary shrink-0"
                            disabled={byttNamnMutation.isPending}
                            onClick={e => saveEdit(lista.id, e)}
                          >
                            {byttNamnMutation.isPending
                              ? <Loader2 className="h-4 w-4 animate-spin" />
                              : <Check className="h-4 w-4" />}
                          </Button>
                          <Button
                            size="icon"
                            variant="ghost"
                            className="h-8 w-8 shrink-0"
                            onClick={cancelEdit}
                          >
                            <X className="h-4 w-4" />
                          </Button>
                        </div>
                      ) : (
                        <div className="flex items-center gap-1 group">
                          <CardTitle className="text-base">{lista.namn}</CardTitle>
                          <Button
                            size="icon"
                            variant="ghost"
                            className="h-6 w-6 opacity-0 group-hover:opacity-100 transition-opacity text-muted-foreground hover:text-foreground shrink-0"
                            onClick={e => startEdit(lista, e)}
                            title="Redigera namn"
                          >
                            <Pencil className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
                          </Button>
                        </div>
                      )}
                      <CardDescription className="mt-0.5">
                        {lista.datum && <span>Prislista {lista.datum} • </span>}
                        Importerad {format(new Date(lista.skapadAt), "d MMM yyyy", { locale: sv })}
                      </CardDescription>
                    </div>
                    {!isEditing && (
                      <div className="flex items-center gap-1 shrink-0">
                        <Button
                          size="icon"
                          variant="ghost"
                          className="h-7 w-7 text-muted-foreground"
                          disabled={listor.indexOf(lista) === 0 || reorderMutation.isPending}
                          onClick={(e) => { e.stopPropagation(); flytta(lista, -1); }}
                          title="Flytta upp"
                        >
                          <ChevronUp className="h-4 w-4" />
                        </Button>
                        <Button
                          size="icon"
                          variant="ghost"
                          className="h-7 w-7 text-muted-foreground"
                          disabled={listor.indexOf(lista) === listor.length - 1 || reorderMutation.isPending}
                          onClick={(e) => { e.stopPropagation(); flytta(lista, 1); }}
                          title="Flytta ned"
                        >
                          <ChevronDown className="h-4 w-4" />
                        </Button>
                        <Button
                          size="sm"
                          variant="ghost"
                          className="text-muted-foreground hover:text-destructive"
                          onClick={(e) => {
                            e.stopPropagation();
                            if (confirm("Radera prislistan?")) raderaMutation.mutate(lista.id);
                          }}
                        >
                          <Trash2 className="h-4 w-4" />
                        </Button>
                      </div>
                    )}
                  </div>
                </CardHeader>
              </Card>
            );
          })}
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

import { useState, useMemo, useEffect, useRef } from "react";
import { useQuery } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import { customFetch } from "@workspace/api-client-react";
import { Input } from "@/components/ui/input";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { Badge } from "@/components/ui/badge";
import { Search, Printer, Package, Layers, FileSpreadsheet, ArrowUpDown, Scissors } from "lucide-react";
import { useDebounce } from "@/hooks/useDebounce";
import { useBranding } from "@/components/branding/BrandingContext";

interface LangdRad {
  langdDm: number;
  antalPaket: number;
  totalAntal: number;
  m3: number;
}

interface LagerGrupp {
  varuslag: string;
  traslag: string | null;
  kvalitet: string | null;
  profil: string | null;
  nomTjocklek: number | null;
  nomBredd: number | null;
  actTjocklek: number | null;
  actBredd: number | null;
  antalPaket: number;
  totalAntal: number;
  totalM3: number;
  langder: LangdRad[];
}

interface LagerStatusData {
  groups: LagerGrupp[];
  totalPaket: number;
  totalM3: number;
}

type SortMode = "traslag_dim" | "dim_traslag" | "m3_desc" | "paket_desc";

const SORT_OPTIONS: { value: SortMode; label: string }[] = [
  { value: "traslag_dim", label: "Träslag → Dimension" },
  { value: "dim_traslag", label: "Dimension → Träslag" },
  { value: "m3_desc",     label: "m³ (störst först)" },
  { value: "paket_desc",  label: "Antal paket (störst)" },
];

// Anpassad träslagsordning — Gran först, Furu näst, övriga alfabetiskt
const TRASLAG_PRIO: Record<string, number> = {
  "gran":       1,
  "gran/furu":  2,
  "furu/gran":  2,
  "furu":       3,
  "tall":       4,
  "lärk":       5,
  "imp":        6,
};

function traslagPrio(t: string | null): number {
  return TRASLAG_PRIO[(t ?? "").toLowerCase()] ?? 99;
}

function dimKey(g: LagerGrupp): [number, number] {
  return [g.nomTjocklek ?? 9999, g.nomBredd ?? 9999];
}

function sortGroups(groups: LagerGrupp[], mode: SortMode): LagerGrupp[] {
  return [...groups].sort((a, b) => {
    if (mode === "traslag_dim") {
      const tp = traslagPrio(a.traslag) - traslagPrio(b.traslag);
      if (tp !== 0) return tp;
      // Inom samma träslag: alfabetisk om lika prio
      const ta = (a.traslag ?? "").localeCompare(b.traslag ?? "", "sv");
      if (ta !== 0) return ta;
      const [at, ab] = dimKey(a);
      const [bt, bb] = dimKey(b);
      return at !== bt ? at - bt : ab - bb;
    }
    if (mode === "dim_traslag") {
      const [at, ab] = dimKey(a);
      const [bt, bb] = dimKey(b);
      if (at !== bt) return at - bt;
      if (ab !== bb) return ab - bb;
      const tp = traslagPrio(a.traslag) - traslagPrio(b.traslag);
      if (tp !== 0) return tp;
      return (a.traslag ?? "").localeCompare(b.traslag ?? "", "sv");
    }
    if (mode === "m3_desc")    return b.totalM3    - a.totalM3;
    if (mode === "paket_desc") return b.antalPaket - a.antalPaket;
    return 0;
  });
}

function fmtM3(v: number) {
  return v.toLocaleString("sv-SE", { minimumFractionDigits: 3, maximumFractionDigits: 3 });
}

function fmtLangd(dm: number) {
  if (!dm) return "—";
  return `${dm} dm`;
}

function nomDimStr(g: LagerGrupp) {
  if (g.nomTjocklek && g.nomBredd) return `${g.nomTjocklek}×${g.nomBredd}`;
  return null;
}

function actDimStr(g: LagerGrupp) {
  if (g.actTjocklek && g.actBredd) return `${g.actTjocklek}×${g.actBredd}`;
  return null;
}

export default function LagerStatusPage() {
  const branding = useBranding();
  const [sok, setSok] = useState("");
  const [sortMode, setSortMode] = useState<SortMode>("traslag_dim");
  const [baraHyvlat, setBaraHyvlat] = useState(false);
  const debouncedSok = useDebounce(sok, 300);

  const { data, isLoading } = useQuery<LagerStatusData>({
    queryKey: ["/api/rapport/lagerstatus", debouncedSok],
    queryFn: () =>
      customFetch<LagerStatusData>(
        `/api/rapport/lagerstatus${debouncedSok ? `?q=${encodeURIComponent(debouncedSok)}` : ""}`
      ),
  });

  const rawGroups = data?.groups ?? [];
  const groups = useMemo(() => {
    const sorted = sortGroups(rawGroups, sortMode);
    if (baraHyvlat) return sorted.filter(g =>
      g.actTjocklek != null && g.actBredd != null &&
      (g.actTjocklek !== g.nomTjocklek || g.actBredd !== g.nomBredd)
    );
    return sorted;
  }, [rawGroups, sortMode, baraHyvlat]);

  const visadM3 = useMemo(() => groups.reduce((s, g) => s + g.totalM3, 0), [groups]);
  const visadPaket = useMemo(() => groups.reduce((s, g) => s + g.antalPaket, 0), [groups]);
  const printDate = new Date().toLocaleDateString("sv-SE");

  function handlePrint() {
    // ── Statistik-sammanställning ──────────────────────────────────────────
    const totalM3 = data?.totalM3 ?? 0;
    const totalPaket = data?.totalPaket ?? 0;

    const tradslagMap = new Map<string, number>();
    for (const g of groups) {
      const ts = g.traslag ?? "Övrigt";
      tradslagMap.set(ts, (tradslagMap.get(ts) ?? 0) + g.totalM3);
    }
    const tradslagRows = [...tradslagMap.entries()]
      .sort((a, b) => b[1] - a[1])
      .map(([ts, m]) =>
        `<tr><td>${ts}</td><td class="snum">${fmtM3(m)} m³</td><td class="snum">${totalM3 > 0 ? Math.round(m / totalM3 * 100) : 0} %</td></tr>`)
      .join("");

    const topDimRows = [...groups]
      .sort((a, b) => b.totalM3 - a.totalM3)
      .slice(0, 8)
      .map(g => {
        const nom = nomDimStr(g);
        const act = actDimStr(g);
        const dimLabel = [nom, act && act !== nom ? `→ ${act}` : null, g.kvalitet].filter(Boolean).join(" ");
        const tsLabel = g.traslag ?? "";
        return `<tr><td>${dimLabel} mm</td><td>${tsLabel}</td><td class="snum">${g.antalPaket} pkt</td><td class="snum">${fmtM3(g.totalM3)} m³</td><td class="snum">${totalM3 > 0 ? Math.round(g.totalM3 / totalM3 * 100) : 0} %</td></tr>`;
      })
      .join("");
    // ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    const PRINT_CSS = `
      @page { size: A4 portrait; margin: 15mm 12mm 15mm 12mm; }
      * { box-sizing: border-box; }
      body { font-family: Arial, Helvetica, sans-serif; font-size: 9pt; color: #111; margin: 0; }
      .print-header { display: flex; justify-content: space-between; align-items: flex-end; border-bottom: 2px solid #2d5a27; padding-bottom: 6px; margin-bottom: 10px; }
      .print-header h1 { margin: 0; font-size: 14pt; color: #2d5a27; }
      .print-header .meta { font-size: 8pt; color: #555; text-align: right; line-height: 1.5; }
      .stats { display: flex; gap: 12px; margin-bottom: 12px; page-break-inside: avoid; }
      .stat-box { border: 1px solid #d1d5db; border-radius: 4px; padding: 6px 10px; flex: 1; }
      .stat-box h3 { font-size: 7pt; text-transform: uppercase; color: #6b7280; letter-spacing: 0.5px; margin-bottom: 4px; font-weight: bold; }
      .stat-kpi { font-size: 16pt; font-weight: bold; color: #2d5a27; line-height: 1; }
      .stat-kpi-label { font-size: 7pt; color: #6b7280; margin-top: 1px; }
      .stat-table { width: 100%; border-collapse: collapse; font-size: 7.5pt; }
      .stat-table td { padding: 2px 4px; border-bottom: 1px solid #f3f4f6; }
      .snum { text-align: right; font-variant-numeric: tabular-nums; }
      .group { page-break-inside: avoid; margin-bottom: 10px; border: 1px solid #bbb; border-radius: 3px; overflow: hidden; }
      .group-header { background: #e8f0e6; padding: 4px 8px; display: flex; justify-content: space-between; align-items: center; }
      .group-title { font-weight: bold; font-size: 10pt; }
      .group-sub { font-size: 8pt; color: #444; }
      table { width: 100%; border-collapse: collapse; }
      thead th { background: #f4f4f4; font-size: 8pt; text-align: left; padding: 2px 8px; border-bottom: 1px solid #ccc; font-weight: 600; }
      thead th.num { text-align: right; }
      tbody td { padding: 2px 8px; border-bottom: 1px solid #eee; font-size: 9pt; }
      tbody td.num { text-align: right; font-variant-numeric: tabular-nums; }
      tbody tr:last-child td { border-bottom: none; }
      tbody tr.totals td { font-weight: bold; background: #f4f4f4; font-size: 8pt; color: #333; }
      .grand-total { margin-top: 12px; font-size: 8pt; color: #555; border-top: 1px solid #ccc; padding-top: 6px; }
    `;

    const sortLabel = SORT_OPTIONS.find(o => o.value === sortMode)?.label ?? "";

    const groupsHtml = groups.map(g => {
      const nom = nomDimStr(g);
      const act = actDimStr(g);
      const rowsHtml = g.langder.map(l => `
        <tr>
          <td>${fmtLangd(l.langdDm)}</td>
          <td class="num">${l.antalPaket}</td>
          <td class="num">${l.totalAntal}</td>
          <td class="num">${fmtM3(l.m3)}</td>
        </tr>`).join("");
      const totalRow = g.langder.length > 1 ? `
        <tr class="totals">
          <td>Totalt</td>
          <td class="num">${g.antalPaket}</td>
          <td class="num">${g.totalAntal}</td>
          <td class="num">${fmtM3(g.totalM3)}</td>
        </tr>` : "";
      return `
        <div class="group">
          <div class="group-header">
            <span class="group-title">${g.varuslag || "—"}${g.traslag ? ` · ${g.traslag}` : ""}${nom ? ` · ${nom}` : ""}${act && act !== nom ? ` → ${act}` : ""}${g.profil ? ` · ${g.profil}` : ""}${g.kvalitet ? ` · ${g.kvalitet}` : ""}</span>
            <span class="group-sub">${g.antalPaket} paket · ${fmtM3(g.totalM3)} m³</span>
          </div>
          <table>
            <thead><tr>
              <th>Längd</th>
              <th class="num">Ant. paket</th>
              <th class="num">Ant. st</th>
              <th class="num">m³</th>
            </tr></thead>
            <tbody>${rowsHtml}${totalRow}</tbody>
          </table>
        </div>`;
    }).join("");

    const html = `<!DOCTYPE html><html lang="sv"><head>
      <meta charset="utf-8">
      <title>Lagerstatus ${printDate}</title>
      <style>${PRINT_CSS}</style>
    </head><body>
      <div class="print-header">
        <h1>Lagerstatus — ${branding.companyName}</h1>
        <div class="meta">
          Utskrivet ${printDate}${sok ? `<br>Sökning: ${sok}` : ""}<br>
          Sortering: ${sortLabel}<br>
          ${totalPaket} paket &nbsp;·&nbsp; ${fmtM3(totalM3)} m³ &nbsp;·&nbsp; ${groups.length} dimensioner
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="stats">
        <div class="stat-box">
          <h3>Totalt i lager</h3>
          <div class="stat-kpi">${totalM3.toLocaleString("sv-SE", { minimumFractionDigits: 1, maximumFractionDigits: 1 })}</div>
          <div class="stat-kpi-label">m³ · ${totalPaket} paket</div>
        </div>
        <div class="stat-box" style="flex:1.2">
          <h3>Träslag</h3>
          <table class="stat-table"><tbody>${tradslagRows}</tbody></table>
        </div>
        <div class="stat-box" style="flex:2.4">
          <h3>Topp dimensioner (volym)</h3>
          <table class="stat-table">
            <thead><tr><td>Dim</td><td>Träslag</td><td class="snum">Paket</td><td class="snum">m³</td><td class="snum">Andel</td></tr></thead>
            <tbody>${topDimRows}</tbody>
          </table>
        </div>
      </div>
      ${groupsHtml}
      <div class="grand-total">
        Totalt i lager: <strong>${totalPaket} paket</strong> · <strong>${fmtM3(totalM3)} m³</strong>
        &nbsp;·&nbsp; Lagersystem Virke${branding.website ? ` &nbsp;·&nbsp; ${branding.website}` : ""}
      </div>
    </body></html>`;

    const w = window.open("", "_blank", "width=900,height=700");
    if (!w) return;
    w.document.write(html);
    w.document.close();
    w.focus();
    setTimeout(() => { w.print(); }, 400);
  }

  const handlePrintRef = useRef(handlePrint);
  useEffect(() => { handlePrintRef.current = handlePrint; });
  useEffect(() => {
    const h = () => handlePrintRef.current();
    window.addEventListener("shortcut:print", h);
    return () => window.removeEventListener("shortcut:print", h);
  }, []);

  function handleExcel() {
    const base = import.meta.env.BASE_URL.replace(/\/$/, "");
    const qs = debouncedSok ? `?q=${encodeURIComponent(debouncedSok)}` : "";
    window.open(`${base}/api/rapport/lagerstatus/excel${qs}`, "_blank");
  }

  return (
    <div className="max-w-5xl mx-auto space-y-6">
      <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-3 flex-wrap">
        <div>
          <h1 className="text-2xl font-bold tracking-tight">Lagerstatus</h1>
          <p className="text-muted-foreground text-sm mt-0.5">
            Färdighyvlat virke i lager — grupperat per dimension och längd
          </p>
        </div>
        <div className="flex gap-2">
          <Button variant="outline" className="gap-2" onClick={handlePrint} disabled={groups.length === 0}>
            <Printer className="h-4 w-4" /> Skriv ut / PDF
          </Button>
          <Button variant="outline" className="gap-2 text-green-800 border-green-700/40 hover:bg-green-50" onClick={handleExcel} disabled={groups.length === 0}>
            <FileSpreadsheet className="h-4 w-4" /> Excel
          </Button>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Sök + sortering + filter */}
      <div className="flex gap-2 items-center flex-wrap">
        <div className="relative flex-1 min-w-48">
          <Search className="absolute left-3 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 h-4 w-4 text-muted-foreground" />
          <Input
            className="pl-9 text-base"
            placeholder="Sök t.ex. c24 45 eller gran 45x145 eller röda magasinet…"
            value={sok}
            onChange={e => setSok(e.target.value)}
            autoFocus
          />
        </div>
        <button
          onClick={() => setBaraHyvlat(v => !v)}
          className={`flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-2 rounded-md border text-sm font-medium transition-colors shrink-0 ${
            baraHyvlat
              ? "bg-sky-600 text-white border-sky-600 hover:bg-sky-700"
              : "bg-background text-foreground border-input hover:bg-muted"
          }`}
        >
          <Scissors className="h-4 w-4" />
          Bara hyvlat
        </button>
        <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 shrink-0">
          <ArrowUpDown className="h-4 w-4 text-muted-foreground" />
          <select
            className="text-sm border rounded-md px-2 py-2 bg-background focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-ring"
            value={sortMode}
            onChange={e => setSortMode(e.target.value as SortMode)}
          >
            {SORT_OPTIONS.map(o => (
              <option key={o.value} value={o.value}>{o.label}</option>
            ))}
          </select>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Totalrad */}
      {data && (
        <div className="flex gap-4 text-sm text-muted-foreground">
          <span className="flex items-center gap-1.5">
            <Package className="h-4 w-4" />
            <strong className="text-foreground">{visadPaket}</strong> paket
          </span>
          <span className="flex items-center gap-1.5">
            <Layers className="h-4 w-4" />
            <strong className="text-foreground">{fmtM3(visadM3)}</strong> m³
          </span>
          {groups.length > 0 && (
            <span className="ml-auto text-xs">{groups.length} dimensioner</span>
          )}
        </div>
      )}

      {isLoading && (
        <div className="text-sm text-muted-foreground py-8 text-center">Laddar…</div>
      )}

      {!isLoading && groups.length === 0 && (
        <div className="text-center py-16 text-muted-foreground text-sm">
          {sok ? `Inga resultat för "${sok}"` : "Inga paket i lager"}
        </div>
      )}

      {/* Grupp-kort */}
      <div className="space-y-4">
        {groups.map((g, gi) => {
          const nom = nomDimStr(g);
          const act = actDimStr(g);
          return (
            <div key={gi} className="rounded-lg border bg-card shadow-sm overflow-hidden">
              <div className="flex items-center gap-3 px-4 py-3 bg-muted/40 border-b flex-wrap">
                <div className="flex items-center gap-2 flex-wrap flex-1 min-w-0">
                  <span className="font-semibold text-base">{g.varuslag || "—"}</span>
                  {g.traslag && <span className="text-muted-foreground text-sm">· {g.traslag}</span>}
                  {nom && (
                    <Badge variant="outline" className="font-mono text-xs px-1.5">{nom}</Badge>
                  )}
                  {act && act !== nom && (
                    <span className="text-xs text-muted-foreground font-mono">→ {act}</span>
                  )}
                  {g.profil && g.profil.toLowerCase() !== "sågat" && (
                    <Badge variant="outline" className="text-xs px-1.5 text-sky-700 border-sky-300 bg-sky-50">
                      {g.profil}
                    </Badge>
                  )}
                  {g.kvalitet && (
                    <Badge className="text-xs px-1.5 bg-[#2d5a27]/10 text-[#2d5a27] border-[#2d5a27]/20 hover:bg-[#2d5a27]/10">
                      {g.kvalitet}
                    </Badge>
                  )}
                </div>
                <div className="flex items-center gap-4 text-sm shrink-0">
                  <span className="text-muted-foreground">
                    <strong className="text-foreground">{g.antalPaket}</strong> paket
                  </span>
                  <span className="text-muted-foreground">
                    <strong className="text-foreground">{fmtM3(g.totalM3)}</strong> m³
                  </span>
                </div>
              </div>

              <div className="overflow-x-auto">
                <table className="w-full text-sm">
                  <thead>
                    <tr className="text-xs text-muted-foreground border-b">
                      <th className="text-left font-medium px-4 py-2">Längd</th>
                      <th className="text-right font-medium px-4 py-2">Ant. paket</th>
                      <th className="text-right font-medium px-4 py-2">Ant. st</th>
                      <th className="text-right font-medium px-4 py-2">m³</th>
                    </tr>
                  </thead>
                  <tbody>
                    {g.langder.map((l, li) => (
                      <tr key={li} className="border-b last:border-0 hover:bg-muted/30 transition-colors">
                        <td className="px-4 py-2 font-mono font-medium">{fmtLangd(l.langdDm)}</td>
                        <td className="px-4 py-2 text-right">{l.antalPaket}</td>
                        <td className="px-4 py-2 text-right text-muted-foreground">{l.totalAntal}</td>
                        <td className="px-4 py-2 text-right font-mono">{fmtM3(l.m3)}</td>
                      </tr>
                    ))}
                    {g.langder.length > 1 && (
                      <tr className="bg-muted/20 font-semibold text-xs text-muted-foreground">
                        <td className="px-4 py-1.5">Totalt</td>
                        <td className="px-4 py-1.5 text-right text-foreground">{g.antalPaket}</td>
                        <td className="px-4 py-1.5 text-right text-foreground">{g.totalAntal}</td>
                        <td className="px-4 py-1.5 text-right text-foreground font-mono">{fmtM3(g.totalM3)}</td>
                      </tr>
                    )}
                  </tbody>
                </table>
              </div>
            </div>
          );
        })}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}


================ FILE: artifacts/lager/src/pages/rapport/RapportPage.tsx ================

import { useState } from "react";
import { useQuery } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import { useGetLagerStatistik, getGetLagerStatistikQueryKey, customFetch } from "@workspace/api-client-react";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { Card, CardContent, CardHeader, CardTitle } from "@/components/ui/card";
import { Skeleton } from "@/components/ui/skeleton";
import { Input } from "@/components/ui/input";
import { Label } from "@/components/ui/label";
import { Tabs, TabsContent, TabsList, TabsTrigger } from "@/components/ui/tabs";
import { Printer, Download, TrendingUp, Package, BarChart3, Wallet, Users, RefreshCw, Truck, CalendarSearch, Search, Eye, EyeOff } from "lucide-react";
import { format, subMonths } from "date-fns";
import { sv } from "date-fns/locale";
import { BarChart, Bar, XAxis, YAxis, CartesianGrid, Tooltip, ResponsiveContainer, Cell, LabelList } from "recharts";

type TrendPoint = { manad: string; label: string; m3In: number; m3Ut: number; antalIn: number; antalUt: number };
type LagertrendPoint = { datum: string; label: string; m3: number };

interface OmsattningData {
  totalt: { m3: number; lpm: number; antal: number; intakt: number; posterCount: number };
  perDimension: { dimension: string; varuslag: string; antal: number; m3: number; lpm: number; intakt: number }[];
  perLeverantor: { leverantor: string; antal: number; m3: number; intakt: number }[];
  perManad: { manad: string; label: string; antal: number; m3: number; intakt: number }[];
}

interface VardeData {
  totalVarde: number;
  totalM3: number;
  matchade: number;
  omatchade: number;
  andelMatchad: number;
  medM3Sum: number;
  medM3Pris: number;
  medPrislista: number;
}

interface KopareData {
  perKund: { kund: string; m3: number; antal: number; andel: number }[];
  totalM3: number;
}

interface SnapshotData {
  datum: string;
  antalPaket: number;
  totalM3: number;
  snittpris: number;
  totalVarde: number;
  andelMedPris: number;
  perDimension: { dim: string; traslag: string; profil: string; m3: number; antal: number; varde: number }[];
}

interface LeveranserData {
  total: { antalSpecs: number; antalPaket: number; m3: number; varde: number };
  perKund: { kund: string; antalSpecs: number; antalPaket: number; m3: number; varde: number }[];
  perManad: { manad: string; label: string; antalSpecs: number; m3: number; varde: number }[];
}

export default function RapportPage() {
  const today = format(new Date(), "d MMMM yyyy", { locale: sv });
  const defaultFrom = format(subMonths(new Date(), 12), "yyyy-MM-dd");
  const defaultTo = format(new Date(), "yyyy-MM-dd");

  const [from, setFrom] = useState(defaultFrom);
  const [to, setTo] = useState(defaultTo);
  const [filterLev, setFilterLev] = useState("");
  const [filterDim, setFilterDim] = useState("");
  const [kopareFrom, setKopareFrom] = useState("");
  const [kopareTo, setKopareTo] = useState("");
  const [levFrom, setLevFrom] = useState(defaultFrom);
  const [levTo, setLevTo] = useState(defaultTo);
  const [snapshotDatum, setSnapshotDatum] = useState(format(new Date(), "yyyy-MM-dd"));
  const [snapshotQuery, setSnapshotQuery] = useState("");
  const [visPris, setVisPris] = useState(false);
  const [manuellPrisStr, setManuellPrisStr] = useState("");
  const manuellPris = parseFloat(manuellPrisStr.replace(",", ".")) || 0;

  const { data: statistik, isLoading } = useGetLagerStatistik({
    query: { queryKey: getGetLagerStatistikQueryKey() }
  });

  const { data: leveranstrend = [] } = useQuery<TrendPoint[]>({
    queryKey: ["/api/lager/leveranstrend"],
    queryFn: () => customFetch<TrendPoint[]>("/api/lager/leveranstrend"),
  });

  const { data: lagertrend = [] } = useQuery<LagertrendPoint[]>({
    queryKey: ["/api/lager/lagertrend"],
    queryFn: () => customFetch<LagertrendPoint[]>("/api/lager/lagertrend"),
  });

  const omsattningParams = new URLSearchParams({ from, to });
  if (filterLev) omsattningParams.set("leverantor", filterLev);
  if (filterDim) omsattningParams.set("dimension", filterDim);

  const { data: omsattning, isLoading: omsLoading } = useQuery<OmsattningData>({
    queryKey: ["/api/rapport/omsattning", from, to, filterLev, filterDim],
    queryFn: () => customFetch<OmsattningData>(`/api/rapport/omsattning?${omsattningParams}`),
  });

  const { data: varde, isLoading: vardeLoading, refetch: refetchVarde, isFetching: vardeFetching } = useQuery<VardeData>({
    queryKey: ["/api/rapport/varde"],
    queryFn: () => customFetch<VardeData>("/api/rapport/varde"),
    refetchOnMount: "always",
    staleTime: 0,
  });

  const kopareParams = new URLSearchParams();
  if (kopareFrom) kopareParams.set("from", kopareFrom);
  if (kopareTo) kopareParams.set("to", kopareTo);

  const { data: kopare, isLoading: kopareLoading } = useQuery<KopareData>({
    queryKey: ["/api/rapport/kopare", kopareFrom, kopareTo],
    queryFn: () => customFetch<KopareData>(`/api/rapport/kopare?${kopareParams}`),
  });

  const levParams = new URLSearchParams({ from: levFrom, to: levTo });
  const { data: leveranser, isLoading: leveranserLoading } = useQuery<LeveranserData>({
    queryKey: ["/api/rapport/leveranser", levFrom, levTo],
    queryFn: () => customFetch<LeveranserData>(`/api/rapport/leveranser?${levParams}`),
  });

  const { data: snapshot, isLoading: snapshotLoading } = useQuery<SnapshotData>({
    queryKey: ["/api/lager/snapshot", snapshotQuery],
    queryFn: () => customFetch<SnapshotData>(`/api/lager/snapshot?datum=${snapshotQuery}`),
    enabled: !!snapshotQuery,
  });

  const COLORS = ["#2d5a27", "#4a8a42", "#6ab05e", "#3b7bbf", "#5499d5", "#e07b39", "#d4513a", "#8b5cf6", "#ec4899", "#14b8a6"];

  const svh = statistik ? (statistik as any).sagadVsHyvlat : null;
  const leverantorer = statistik ? (statistik as any).leverantorFordelning ?? [] : [];
  const topDim = statistik ? (statistik as any).topSagadeDimensioner ?? [] : [];
  const tradslagFordelning: { traslag: string; totalM3: number; antal: number; andel: number }[] =
    statistik ? (statistik as any).tradslagFordelning ?? [] : [];
  const trendDelta = lagertrend.length > 1 ? lagertrend[lagertrend.length - 1].m3 - lagertrend[0].m3 : 0;

  function printSnapshot() {
    if (!snapshot) return;
    const w = window.open("", "_blank", "width=900,height=700");
    if (!w) return;
    const hasPris = snapshot.andelMedPris > 0;
    const manuellVarde = manuellPris > 0 ? manuellPris * snapshot.totalM3 : 0;
    const rows = snapshot.perDimension.map((r, i) => `
      <tr style="background:${i % 2 === 0 ? "#f5f5f5" : "#fff"}">
        <td style="padding:4px 10px 4px 6px;border-bottom:1px solid #e0e0e0">${r.dim}</td>
        <td style="padding:4px 10px 4px 6px;border-bottom:1px solid #e0e0e0">${r.traslag || "–"}</td>
        <td style="padding:4px 10px 4px 6px;border-bottom:1px solid #e0e0e0">${r.profil || "–"}</td>
        <td style="padding:4px 6px;border-bottom:1px solid #e0e0e0;text-align:right">${r.antal}</td>
        <td style="padding:4px 6px;border-bottom:1px solid #e0e0e0;text-align:right">${r.m3.toFixed(1)}</td>
        ${visPris && hasPris ? `<td style="padding:4px 6px;border-bottom:1px solid #e0e0e0;text-align:right;color:#555">${r.varde > 0 ? r.varde.toLocaleString("sv-SE", { maximumFractionDigits: 0 }) + " kr" : "–"}</td>` : ""}
      </tr>`).join("");
    const kpiStyle = "display:inline-block;margin:0 12px 8px 0;padding:8px 14px;border:1px solid #ddd;border-radius:6px;text-align:center;min-width:100px";
    w.document.write(`<!DOCTYPE html><html lang="sv"><head><meta charset="utf-8"/>
      <title>Lagerstatus ${snapshot.datum}</title>
      <style>
        body { font-family: Arial, sans-serif; font-size: 11pt; margin: 0; padding: 20px; color: #111; }
        h1 { font-size: 15pt; margin: 0 0 2px; color: #2d5a27; }
        .meta { font-size: 9pt; color: #666; margin-bottom: 14px; }
        table { border-collapse: collapse; width: 100%; font-size: 10pt; }
        th { background: #2d5a27; color: #fff; padding: 5px 6px; text-align: left; }
        th.num { text-align: right; }
        .obs { font-size: 9pt; color: #b45309; background: #fffbeb; border: 1px solid #fcd34d; padding: 6px 10px; border-radius: 4px; margin-bottom: 10px; }
        .manuell { font-size: 9pt; color: #1d4ed8; background: #eff6ff; border: 1px solid #bfdbfe; padding: 6px 10px; border-radius: 4px; margin-bottom: 10px; }
        @media print { @page { margin: 1.5cm; size: A4; } }
      </style>
    </head><body>
      <h1>Lagerstatus — ${snapshot.datum}</h1>
      <div class="meta">Utskrivet ${new Date().toLocaleDateString("sv-SE")} &nbsp;|&nbsp; ${snapshot.antalPaket} paket &nbsp;|&nbsp; ${snapshot.totalM3.toFixed(1)} m³</div>
      <div style="margin-bottom:14px">
        <div style="${kpiStyle}"><div style="font-size:18pt;font-weight:bold">${snapshot.totalM3.toFixed(1)}</div><div style="font-size:8pt;color:#666">m³ i lager</div></div>
        <div style="${kpiStyle}"><div style="font-size:18pt;font-weight:bold">${snapshot.antalPaket}</div><div style="font-size:8pt;color:#666">antal paket</div></div>
        ${visPris && hasPris ? `<div style="${kpiStyle}"><div style="font-size:18pt;font-weight:bold">${snapshot.snittpris.toLocaleString("sv-SE")}</div><div style="font-size:8pt;color:#666">snittpris kr/m³ (auto)</div></div>` : ""}
        ${visPris && hasPris ? `<div style="${kpiStyle}"><div style="font-size:18pt;font-weight:bold">${snapshot.totalVarde.toLocaleString("sv-SE", { maximumFractionDigits: 0 })}</div><div style="font-size:8pt;color:#666">totalt värde kr (auto)</div></div>` : ""}
        ${visPris && manuellPris > 0 ? `<div style="${kpiStyle};border-color:#bfdbfe;background:#eff6ff"><div style="font-size:18pt;font-weight:bold;color:#1d4ed8">${manuellPris.toLocaleString("sv-SE")}</div><div style="font-size:8pt;color:#1d4ed8">manuellt pris kr/m³</div></div>` : ""}
        ${visPris && manuellPris > 0 ? `<div style="${kpiStyle};border-color:#bfdbfe;background:#eff6ff"><div style="font-size:18pt;font-weight:bold;color:#1d4ed8">${manuellVarde.toLocaleString("sv-SE", { maximumFractionDigits: 0 })}</div><div style="font-size:8pt;color:#1d4ed8">beräknat värde kr</div></div>` : ""}
      </div>
      ${manuellPris > 0 ? `<div class="manuell">Beräknat värde baseras på manuellt angivet snittpris ${manuellPris.toLocaleString("sv-SE")} kr/m³ × ${snapshot.totalM3.toFixed(1)} m³ = ${manuellVarde.toLocaleString("sv-SE", { maximumFractionDigits: 0 })} kr</div>` : ""}
      ${!manuellPris && snapshot.andelMedPris < 100 && snapshot.andelMedPris > 0 ? `<div class="obs">Snittpris baseras på ${snapshot.andelMedPris}% av lagret — resterande paket saknade pris vid detta datum.</div>` : ""}
      <table>
        <thead><tr>
          <th>Dimension</th><th>Träslag</th><th>Profil</th>
          <th class="num">Paket</th><th class="num">m³</th>
          ${visPris && hasPris ? `<th class="num">Värde</th>` : ""}
        </tr></thead>
        <tbody>${rows}</tbody>
      </table>
    </body></html>`);
    w.document.close();
    setTimeout(() => w.print(), 400);
  }

  function downloadExcel() {
    const params = new URLSearchParams({ from, to });
    if (filterLev) params.set("leverantor", filterLev);
    if (filterDim) params.set("dimension", filterDim);
    window.open(`/api/rapport/omsattning/excel?${params}`, "_blank");
  }

  function formatKr(n: number) {
    return n.toLocaleString("sv-SE", { minimumFractionDigits: 0, maximumFractionDigits: 0 }) + " kr";
  }

  if (isLoading) {
    return (
      <div className="space-y-4 p-6">
        <Skeleton className="h-8 w-64" />
        <Skeleton className="h-48 w-full" />
        <Skeleton className="h-48 w-full" />
      </div>
    );
  }

  if (!statistik) return null;

  return (
    <div className="max-w-5xl mx-auto space-y-6 p-4 md:p-6">
      <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
        <div>
          <h1 className="text-2xl font-bold">Rapport</h1>
          <p className="text-muted-foreground text-sm">Lagerstatus, omsättning och lagervärde</p>
        </div>
        <Button variant="outline" onClick={() => window.print()} className="gap-2">
          <Printer className="h-4 w-4" />
          Skriv ut
        </Button>
      </div>

      <Tabs defaultValue="lager">
        <div className="overflow-x-auto w-full">
          <TabsList className="flex w-max min-w-full sm:grid sm:grid-cols-6 sm:w-full sm:max-w-3xl">
            <TabsTrigger value="lager" className="gap-1.5 shrink-0"><Package className="h-3.5 w-3.5 hidden sm:block" />Lager</TabsTrigger>
            <TabsTrigger value="omsattning" className="gap-1.5 shrink-0"><TrendingUp className="h-3.5 w-3.5 hidden sm:block" />Omsättning</TabsTrigger>
            <TabsTrigger value="varde" className="gap-1.5 shrink-0"><Wallet className="h-3.5 w-3.5 hidden sm:block" />Lagervärde</TabsTrigger>
            <TabsTrigger value="kopare" className="gap-1.5 shrink-0"><Users className="h-3.5 w-3.5 hidden sm:block" />Köpare</TabsTrigger>
            <TabsTrigger value="leveranser" className="gap-1.5 shrink-0"><Truck className="h-3.5 w-3.5 hidden sm:block" />Leveranser</TabsTrigger>
            <TabsTrigger value="tidpunkt" className="gap-1.5 shrink-0"><CalendarSearch className="h-3.5 w-3.5 hidden sm:block" />Tidpunkt</TabsTrigger>
          </TabsList>
        </div>

        {/* ─── TAB: LAGER ─────────────────────────────────────── */}
        <TabsContent value="lager" className="space-y-6 mt-4">
          <div className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">Genererad {today}</div>

          {/* KPI */}
          <div className="grid grid-cols-2 md:grid-cols-4 gap-4">
            {[
              { label: "m³ i lager", value: statistik.totalM3.toFixed(1) },
              { label: "aktiva poster", value: statistik.posterILager },
              { label: "m³ netto (30 dagar)", value: (trendDelta >= 0 ? "+" : "") + trendDelta.toFixed(1), color: trendDelta >= 0 ? "text-green-600" : "text-orange-600" },
              { label: "löpmeter", value: statistik.totalLpm.toFixed(0) },
            ].map(({ label, value, color }) => (
              <Card key={label}>
                <CardContent className="pt-4 text-center">
                  <div className={`text-2xl font-bold ${color ?? ""}`}>{value}</div>
                  <div className="text-xs text-muted-foreground mt-1">{label}</div>
                </CardContent>
              </Card>
            ))}
          </div>

          {/* Sågat vs Hyvlat + Träslag */}
          <div className="grid md:grid-cols-2 gap-4">
            {svh && (
              <Card>
                <CardHeader className="pb-3"><CardTitle className="text-base">Sammansättning</CardTitle></CardHeader>
                <CardContent className="space-y-3">
                  {[
                    { label: "Sågat (nom. m³)", color: "bg-blue-500", ...svh.sagat },
                    { label: "Hyvlat / Annat (act. m³)", color: "bg-purple-500", ...svh.hyvlat },
                  ].map(({ label, color, andel, m3 }) => (
                    <div key={label}>
                      <div className="flex justify-between text-sm mb-1">
                        <span>{label}</span>
                        <span className="font-medium tabular-nums">{m3.toFixed(1)} m³ · {andel} %</span>
                      </div>
                      <div className="w-full h-2 bg-muted rounded-full overflow-hidden">
                        <div className={`h-full rounded-full ${color}`} style={{ width: `${andel}%` }} />
                      </div>
                    </div>
                  ))}
                </CardContent>
              </Card>
            )}

            {tradslagFordelning.length > 0 && (
              <Card>
                <CardHeader className="pb-3"><CardTitle className="text-base">Träslag</CardTitle></CardHeader>
                <CardContent>
                  <table className="w-full text-sm">
                    <thead>
                      <tr className="border-b text-muted-foreground">
                        <th className="text-left py-1 pr-4 font-medium">Träslag</th>
                        <th className="text-right py-1 pr-4 font-medium">m³</th>
                        <th className="text-right py-1 pr-4 font-medium">Poster</th>
                        <th className="text-right py-1 font-medium">Andel</th>
                      </tr>
                    </thead>
                    <tbody>
                      {tradslagFordelning.map((ts) => (
                        <tr key={ts.traslag} className="border-b border-muted/50 hover:bg-muted/30">
                          <td className="py-1 pr-4 font-medium">{ts.traslag}</td>
                          <td className="py-1 pr-4 text-right tabular-nums">{ts.totalM3.toFixed(1)}</td>
                          <td className="py-1 pr-4 text-right text-muted-foreground">{ts.antal}</td>
                          <td className="py-1 text-right text-muted-foreground">{ts.andel} %</td>
                        </tr>
                      ))}
                    </tbody>
                  </table>
                </CardContent>
              </Card>
            )}
          </div>

          {/* Volym per månad */}
          {leveranstrend.length > 0 && (
            <Card>
              <CardHeader className="pb-3"><CardTitle className="text-base">Volym in/ut per månad (12 mån)</CardTitle></CardHeader>
              <CardContent>
                <table className="w-full text-sm">
                  <thead>
                    <tr className="border-b text-muted-foreground">
                      <th className="text-left py-1 pr-4 font-medium">Månad</th>
                      <th className="text-right py-1 pr-4 font-medium">m³ In</th>
                      <th className="text-right py-1 pr-4 font-medium">m³ Ut</th>
                      <th className="text-right py-1 pr-4 font-medium">Ant. In</th>
                      <th className="text-right py-1 font-medium">Ant. Ut</th>
                    </tr>
                  </thead>
                  <tbody>
                    {leveranstrend.map((row) => (
                      <tr key={row.manad} className="border-b border-muted/50 hover:bg-muted/30">
                        <td className="py-1 pr-4">{row.label}</td>
                        <td className="py-1 pr-4 text-right text-blue-600 font-medium">{row.m3In.toFixed(1)}</td>
                        <td className="py-1 pr-4 text-right text-green-600 font-medium">{row.m3Ut.toFixed(1)}</td>
                        <td className="py-1 pr-4 text-right text-muted-foreground">{row.antalIn}</td>
                        <td className="py-1 text-right text-muted-foreground">{row.antalUt}</td>
                      </tr>
                    ))}
                    <tr className="font-semibold bg-muted/40">
                      <td className="py-1.5 pr-4">Totalt</td>
                      <td className="py-1.5 pr-4 text-right text-blue-700">{leveranstrend.reduce((s, r) => s + r.m3In, 0).toFixed(1)}</td>
                      <td className="py-1.5 pr-4 text-right text-green-700">{leveranstrend.reduce((s, r) => s + r.m3Ut, 0).toFixed(1)}</td>
                      <td className="py-1.5 pr-4 text-right">{leveranstrend.reduce((s, r) => s + r.antalIn, 0)}</td>
                      <td className="py-1.5 text-right">{leveranstrend.reduce((s, r) => s + r.antalUt, 0)}</td>
                    </tr>
                  </tbody>
                </table>
              </CardContent>
            </Card>
          )}

          {/* Top leverantörer + dimensioner */}
          <div className="grid md:grid-cols-2 gap-4">
            {leverantorer.length > 0 && (
              <Card>
                <CardHeader className="pb-3"><CardTitle className="text-base">Topp leverantörer</CardTitle></CardHeader>
                <CardContent>
                  <table className="w-full text-sm">
                    <thead><tr className="border-b text-muted-foreground">
                      <th className="text-left py-1 pr-4 font-medium">Leverantör</th>
                      <th className="text-right py-1 pr-4 font-medium">m³</th>
                      <th className="text-right py-1 font-medium">Poster</th>
                    </tr></thead>
                    <tbody>
                      {leverantorer.map((lev: any) => (
                        <tr key={lev.leverantor} className="border-b border-muted/50 hover:bg-muted/30">
                          <td className="py-1 pr-4 font-medium">{lev.leverantor}</td>
                          <td className="py-1 pr-4 text-right">{lev.totalM3.toFixed(1)}</td>
                          <td className="py-1 text-right text-muted-foreground">{lev.antal}</td>
                        </tr>
                      ))}
                    </tbody>
                  </table>
                </CardContent>
              </Card>
            )}
            {topDim.length > 0 && (
              <Card>
                <CardHeader className="pb-3"><CardTitle className="text-base">Topp dimensioner</CardTitle></CardHeader>
                <CardContent>
                  <table className="w-full text-sm">
                    <thead><tr className="border-b text-muted-foreground">
                      <th className="text-left py-1 pr-4 font-medium">Dimension</th>
                      <th className="text-right py-1 pr-4 font-medium">m³</th>
                      <th className="text-right py-1 font-medium">Andel</th>
                    </tr></thead>
                    <tbody>
                      {topDim.slice(0, 8).map((dim: any) => (
                        <tr key={dim.dimension} className="border-b border-muted/50 hover:bg-muted/30">
                          <td className="py-1 pr-4 font-medium">{dim.dimension} mm</td>
                          <td className="py-1 pr-4 text-right">{dim.totalM3.toFixed(1)}</td>
                          <td className="py-1 text-right text-muted-foreground">{dim.andel} %</td>
                        </tr>
                      ))}
                    </tbody>
                  </table>
                </CardContent>
              </Card>
            )}
          </div>
        </TabsContent>

        {/* ─── TAB: OMSÄTTNING ────────────────────────────────── */}
        <TabsContent value="omsattning" className="space-y-4 mt-4">
          {/* Filter-rad */}
          <Card>
            <CardContent className="pt-4">
              <div className="grid grid-cols-2 md:grid-cols-4 gap-3">
                <div className="space-y-1">
                  <Label className="text-xs">Från</Label>
                  <Input type="date" value={from} onChange={e => setFrom(e.target.value)} />
                </div>
                <div className="space-y-1">
                  <Label className="text-xs">Till</Label>
                  <Input type="date" value={to} onChange={e => setTo(e.target.value)} />
                </div>
                <div className="space-y-1">
                  <Label className="text-xs">Leverantör (sök)</Label>
                  <Input placeholder="Alla" value={filterLev} onChange={e => setFilterLev(e.target.value)} />
                </div>
                <div className="space-y-1">
                  <Label className="text-xs">Dimension (sök)</Label>
                  <Input placeholder="t.ex. 50x150" value={filterDim} onChange={e => setFilterDim(e.target.value)} />
                </div>
              </div>
            </CardContent>
          </Card>

          {omsLoading ? (
            <Skeleton className="h-48 w-full" />
          ) : omsattning ? (
            <>
              {/* Totalt KPI */}
              <div className="grid grid-cols-2 md:grid-cols-4 gap-4">
                {[
                  { label: "levererade paket", value: omsattning.totalt.posterCount },
                  { label: "m³ levererat", value: omsattning.totalt.m3.toFixed(1) },
                  { label: "löpmeter levererat", value: omsattning.totalt.lpm.toFixed(0) },
                  { label: "intäkt (kr)", value: formatKr(omsattning.totalt.intakt), highlight: true },
                ].map(({ label, value, highlight }) => (
                  <Card key={label}>
                    <CardContent className="pt-4 text-center">
                      <div className={`text-2xl font-bold ${highlight ? "text-green-700" : ""}`}>{value}</div>
                      <div className="text-xs text-muted-foreground mt-1">{label}</div>
                    </CardContent>
                  </Card>
                ))}
              </div>

              <div className="flex justify-end">
                <Button variant="outline" className="gap-2" onClick={downloadExcel}>
                  <Download className="h-4 w-4" />
                  Exportera Excel
                </Button>
              </div>

              {/* Per månad */}
              {omsattning.perManad.length > 0 && (
                <Card>
                  <CardHeader className="pb-3"><CardTitle className="text-base flex items-center gap-2"><BarChart3 className="h-4 w-4" />Per månad</CardTitle></CardHeader>
                  <CardContent>
                    <table className="w-full text-sm">
                      <thead><tr className="border-b text-muted-foreground">
                        <th className="text-left py-1 pr-4 font-medium">Månad</th>
                        <th className="text-right py-1 pr-4 font-medium">Paket</th>
                        <th className="text-right py-1 pr-4 font-medium">m³</th>
                        <th className="text-right py-1 font-medium">Intäkt</th>
                      </tr></thead>
                      <tbody>
                        {omsattning.perManad.map((m) => (
                          <tr key={m.manad} className="border-b border-muted/50 hover:bg-muted/30">
                            <td className="py-1.5 pr-4">{m.label}</td>
                            <td className="py-1.5 pr-4 text-right text-muted-foreground">{m.antal}</td>
                            <td className="py-1.5 pr-4 text-right font-medium">{m.m3.toFixed(1)}</td>
                            <td className="py-1.5 text-right font-medium">{m.intakt > 0 ? formatKr(m.intakt) : "—"}</td>
                          </tr>
                        ))}
                      </tbody>
                    </table>
                  </CardContent>
                </Card>
              )}

              {/* Per dimension */}
              {omsattning.perDimension.length > 0 && (
                <Card>
                  <CardHeader className="pb-3"><CardTitle className="text-base">Per dimension</CardTitle></CardHeader>
                  <CardContent>
                    <table className="w-full text-sm">
                      <thead><tr className="border-b text-muted-foreground">
                        <th className="text-left py-1 pr-4 font-medium">Dimension</th>
                        <th className="text-right py-1 pr-4 font-medium">Paket</th>
                        <th className="text-right py-1 pr-4 font-medium">m³</th>
                        <th className="text-right py-1 font-medium">Intäkt</th>
                      </tr></thead>
                      <tbody>
                        {omsattning.perDimension.map((d) => (
                          <tr key={d.dimension} className="border-b border-muted/50 hover:bg-muted/30">
                            <td className="py-1.5 pr-4 font-medium font-mono text-sm">{d.dimension} mm</td>
                            <td className="py-1.5 pr-4 text-right text-muted-foreground">{d.antal}</td>
                            <td className="py-1.5 pr-4 text-right">{d.m3.toFixed(1)}</td>
                            <td className="py-1.5 text-right">{d.intakt > 0 ? formatKr(d.intakt) : "—"}</td>
                          </tr>
                        ))}
                      </tbody>
                    </table>
                  </CardContent>
                </Card>
              )}

              {/* Per leverantör */}
              {omsattning.perLeverantor.length > 0 && (
                <Card>
                  <CardHeader className="pb-3"><CardTitle className="text-base">Per leverantör</CardTitle></CardHeader>
                  <CardContent>
                    <table className="w-full text-sm">
                      <thead><tr className="border-b text-muted-foreground">
                        <th className="text-left py-1 pr-4 font-medium">Leverantör</th>
                        <th className="text-right py-1 pr-4 font-medium">Paket</th>
                        <th className="text-right py-1 pr-4 font-medium">m³</th>
                        <th className="text-right py-1 font-medium">Intäkt</th>
                      </tr></thead>
                      <tbody>
                        {omsattning.perLeverantor.map((l) => (
                          <tr key={l.leverantor} className="border-b border-muted/50 hover:bg-muted/30">
                            <td className="py-1.5 pr-4 font-medium">{l.leverantor}</td>
                            <td className="py-1.5 pr-4 text-right text-muted-foreground">{l.antal}</td>
                            <td className="py-1.5 pr-4 text-right">{l.m3.toFixed(1)}</td>
                            <td className="py-1.5 text-right">{l.intakt > 0 ? formatKr(l.intakt) : "—"}</td>
                          </tr>
                        ))}
                      </tbody>
                    </table>
                  </CardContent>
                </Card>
              )}
            </>
          ) : null}
        </TabsContent>

        {/* ─── TAB: LAGERVÄRDE ────────────────────────────────── */}
        <TabsContent value="varde" className="space-y-4 mt-4">
          <div className="flex justify-end">
            <Button variant="outline" size="sm" onClick={() => refetchVarde()} disabled={vardeFetching}>
              <RefreshCw className={`h-4 w-4 mr-1 ${vardeFetching ? "animate-spin" : ""}`} />
              Uppdatera
            </Button>
          </div>
          {vardeLoading ? (
            <Skeleton className="h-48 w-full" />
          ) : varde ? (
            <>
              <div className="grid grid-cols-2 md:grid-cols-4 gap-4">
                {[
                  { label: "Beräknat lagervärde", value: formatKr(varde.totalVarde), highlight: true },
                  { label: "m³ i lager", value: varde.totalM3.toFixed(1) },
                  { label: "från leveransnota", value: `${varde.medM3Sum} st` },
                  { label: "från m³-pris", value: `${varde.medM3Pris} st` },
                ].map(({ label, value, highlight }) => (
                  <Card key={label}>
                    <CardContent className="pt-4 text-center">
                      <div className={`text-2xl font-bold ${highlight ? "text-green-700" : ""}`}>{value}</div>
                      <div className="text-xs text-muted-foreground mt-1">{label}</div>
                    </CardContent>
                  </Card>
                ))}
              </div>

              {varde.omatchade > 0 && (
                <p className="text-sm text-muted-foreground bg-muted/50 rounded-lg px-4 py-2">
                  <strong>{varde.omatchade} poster</strong> saknar prisuppgift och ingår inte i värdeberäkningen.
                  Fyll i pris/m³ direkt på lagerposten eller kör Synka priser för bättre täckning.
                </p>
              )}

              <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">
                Lagervärdet beräknas med nominell m³. Prioritetsordning: (1) leveransnotvärde (m³-summa från följesedeln), (2) nom-m³ × eget pris/m³ på lagerposten, (3) nom-m³ × bästa matchning i prislistorna. Exkl. moms.
              </p>
            </>
          ) : null}
        </TabsContent>
        {/* ─── TAB: KÖPARE ────────────────────────────────────── */}
        <TabsContent value="kopare" className="space-y-4 mt-4">
          <Card>
            <CardContent className="pt-4">
              <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3 max-w-sm">
                <div className="space-y-1">
                  <Label className="text-xs">Från</Label>
                  <Input type="date" value={kopareFrom} onChange={e => setKopareFrom(e.target.value)} />
                </div>
                <div className="space-y-1">
                  <Label className="text-xs">Till</Label>
                  <Input type="date" value={kopareTo} onChange={e => setKopareTo(e.target.value)} />
                </div>
              </div>
            </CardContent>
          </Card>

          {kopareLoading ? (
            <Skeleton className="h-64 w-full" />
          ) : !kopare || kopare.perKund.length === 0 ? (
            <Card>
              <CardContent className="py-16 text-center text-muted-foreground">
                Inga bekräftade specifikationer med köpare hittades.
              </CardContent>
            </Card>
          ) : (
            <Card>
              <CardHeader className="pb-2">
                <CardTitle className="text-base">m³ levererat per köpare</CardTitle>
                <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">Totalt {kopare.totalM3.toFixed(1)} m³ — {kopare.perKund.length} köpare</p>
              </CardHeader>
              <CardContent>
                <ResponsiveContainer width="100%" height={Math.max(220, kopare.perKund.length * 42)}>
                  <BarChart
                    data={kopare.perKund}
                    layout="vertical"
                    margin={{ top: 4, right: 80, left: 8, bottom: 4 }}
                  >
                    <CartesianGrid strokeDasharray="3 3" horizontal={false} />
                    <XAxis type="number" tickFormatter={(v) => `${v.toFixed(1)}`} tick={{ fontSize: 11 }} />
                    <YAxis type="category" dataKey="kund" width={160} tick={{ fontSize: 11 }} />
                    <Tooltip
                      formatter={(value: number, _: string, props: any) => [
                        `${value.toFixed(2)} m³ (${props.payload.andel} %)`,
                        "Volym"
                      ]}
                    />
                    <Bar dataKey="m3" radius={[0, 4, 4, 0]}>
                      {kopare.perKund.map((_, i) => (
                        <Cell key={i} fill={COLORS[i % COLORS.length]} />
                      ))}
                      <LabelList
                        dataKey="andel"
                        position="right"
                        formatter={(v: number) => `${v} %`}
                        style={{ fontSize: 11, fill: "#555" }}
                      />
                    </Bar>
                  </BarChart>
