                </ResponsiveContainer>
              </CardContent>
            </Card>
          )}
        </TabsContent>

        {/* ─── TAB: LEVERANSER ─────────────────────────────────── */}
        <TabsContent value="leveranser" className="space-y-6 mt-4">

          {/* Datumfilter */}
          <Card>
            <CardContent className="pt-4">
              <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-4 items-end">
                <div className="space-y-1">
                  <Label className="text-xs">Från</Label>
                  <Input type="date" value={levFrom} onChange={e => setLevFrom(e.target.value)} className="h-8 text-sm w-40" />
                </div>
                <div className="space-y-1">
                  <Label className="text-xs">Till</Label>
                  <Input type="date" value={levTo} onChange={e => setLevTo(e.target.value)} className="h-8 text-sm w-40" />
                </div>
              </div>
            </CardContent>
          </Card>

          {leveranserLoading ? (
            <Skeleton className="h-32 w-full" />
          ) : !leveranser ? null : (
            <>
              {/* KPI-kort */}
              <div className="grid grid-cols-2 md:grid-cols-4 gap-4">
                {[
                  { label: "bekräftade specs", value: leveranser.total.antalSpecs },
                  { label: "paket levererade", value: leveranser.total.antalPaket },
                  { label: "m³ levererat", value: `${leveranser.total.m3.toFixed(1)} m³` },
                  { label: "uppskattat värde", value: formatKr(leveranser.total.varde) },
                ].map(({ label, value }) => (
                  <Card key={label}>
                    <CardContent className="pt-4 text-center">
                      <div className="text-2xl font-bold">{value}</div>
                      <div className="text-xs text-muted-foreground mt-1">{label}</div>
                    </CardContent>
                  </Card>
                ))}
              </div>

              {/* Månadsgraf */}
              {leveranser.perManad.length > 0 ? (
                <Card>
                  <CardHeader className="pb-3"><CardTitle className="text-base">m³ levererat per månad</CardTitle></CardHeader>
                  <CardContent>
                    <div className="h-[220px]">
                      <ResponsiveContainer width="100%" height="100%">
                        <BarChart data={leveranser.perManad} barCategoryGap="30%" margin={{ top: 4, right: 4, left: -20, bottom: 0 }}>
                          <XAxis dataKey="label" stroke="#888" fontSize={11} tickLine={false} axisLine={false} />
                          <YAxis stroke="#888" fontSize={11} tickLine={false} axisLine={false} />
                          <Tooltip
                            content={({ active, payload, label }) =>
                              active && payload?.length ? (
                                <div className="rounded-lg border bg-background p-3 shadow-sm text-sm">
                                  <div className="font-semibold mb-1">{label}</div>
                                  <div className="text-muted-foreground">m³: <span className="text-foreground font-medium">{payload[0]?.value}</span></div>
                                  <div className="text-muted-foreground">Specs: <span className="text-foreground font-medium">{(payload[0]?.payload as { antalSpecs: number }).antalSpecs}</span></div>
                                </div>
                              ) : null
                            }
                          />
                          <Bar dataKey="m3" name="m³ levererat" fill="hsl(142, 71%, 45%)" radius={[3, 3, 0, 0]} />
                        </BarChart>
                      </ResponsiveContainer>
                    </div>
                  </CardContent>
                </Card>
              ) : (
                <Card>
                  <CardContent className="pt-6 text-center text-muted-foreground text-sm py-12">
                    Inga bekräftade specifikationer i valt datumintervall.
                    <br />
                    <span className="text-xs mt-1 block">Bekräfta specifikationer under Specifikationer-sidan för att se leveransstatistik.</span>
                  </CardContent>
                </Card>
              )}

              {/* Kundtabell */}
              {leveranser.perKund.length > 0 && (
                <Card>
                  <CardHeader className="pb-3"><CardTitle className="text-base">Per kund</CardTitle></CardHeader>
                  <CardContent>
                    <table className="w-full text-sm">
                      <thead>
                        <tr className="border-b text-muted-foreground">
                          <th className="text-left py-2 pr-4 font-medium">Kund</th>
                          <th className="text-right py-2 pr-4 font-medium">Specs</th>
                          <th className="text-right py-2 pr-4 font-medium">Paket</th>
                          <th className="text-right py-2 pr-4 font-medium">m³</th>
                          <th className="text-right py-2 font-medium">Värde</th>
                        </tr>
                      </thead>
                      <tbody>
                        {leveranser.perKund.map((k, i) => (
                          <tr key={k.kund} className={i % 2 === 0 ? "bg-muted/30" : ""}>
                            <td className="py-1.5 pr-4 font-medium">{k.kund}</td>
                            <td className="py-1.5 pr-4 text-right tabular-nums">{k.antalSpecs}</td>
                            <td className="py-1.5 pr-4 text-right tabular-nums">{k.antalPaket}</td>
                            <td className="py-1.5 pr-4 text-right tabular-nums">{k.m3.toFixed(1)}</td>
                            <td className="py-1.5 text-right tabular-nums text-muted-foreground">{formatKr(k.varde)}</td>
                          </tr>
                        ))}
                        <tr className="border-t font-semibold">
                          <td className="py-2 pr-4">Totalt</td>
                          <td className="py-2 pr-4 text-right tabular-nums">{leveranser.total.antalSpecs}</td>
                          <td className="py-2 pr-4 text-right tabular-nums">{leveranser.total.antalPaket}</td>
                          <td className="py-2 pr-4 text-right tabular-nums">{leveranser.total.m3.toFixed(1)}</td>
                          <td className="py-2 text-right tabular-nums">{formatKr(leveranser.total.varde)}</td>
                        </tr>
                      </tbody>
                    </table>
                  </CardContent>
                </Card>
              )}
            </>
          )}
        </TabsContent>

        {/* ─── TAB: TIDPUNKT ─────────────────────────────────────── */}
        <TabsContent value="tidpunkt" className="space-y-6 mt-4">
          <Card>
            <CardHeader className="pb-3">
              <CardTitle className="text-base flex items-center gap-2">
                <CalendarSearch className="h-4 w-4" />
                Lagerstatus vid ett visst datum
              </CardTitle>
              <p className="text-sm text-muted-foreground">Välj ett datum för att se hur stort lagret var och vilket snittpris som gällde.</p>
            </CardHeader>
            <CardContent>
              <div className="flex flex-wrap items-end gap-3">
                <div className="space-y-1">
                  <Label className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">Datum</Label>
                  <Input
                    type="date"
                    value={snapshotDatum}
                    onChange={(e) => setSnapshotDatum(e.target.value)}
                    className="w-44"
                  />
                </div>
                <Button
                  onClick={() => setSnapshotQuery(snapshotDatum)}
                  disabled={!snapshotDatum}
                  className="gap-2"
                >
                  <Search className="h-4 w-4" />
                  Visa
                </Button>
                <div className="space-y-1">
                  <Label className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">Manuellt snittpris (kr/m³)</Label>
                  <Input
                    type="text"
                    inputMode="decimal"
                    placeholder="t.ex. 2 500"
                    value={manuellPrisStr}
                    onChange={(e) => setManuellPrisStr(e.target.value)}
                    className="w-36"
                  />
                </div>
                <Button
                  variant="outline"
                  onClick={() => setVisPris(v => !v)}
                  className="gap-2"
                  title={visPris ? "Dölj pris" : "Visa pris"}
                >
                  {visPris ? <Eye className="h-4 w-4" /> : <EyeOff className="h-4 w-4" />}
                  {visPris ? "Pris visas" : "Visa pris"}
                </Button>
                {snapshot && !snapshotLoading && (
                  <Button variant="outline" onClick={printSnapshot} className="gap-2">
                    <Printer className="h-4 w-4" />
                    Skriv ut
                  </Button>
                )}
              </div>
            </CardContent>
          </Card>

          {snapshotLoading && (
            <div className="space-y-3">
              <Skeleton className="h-28 w-full" />
              <Skeleton className="h-48 w-full" />
            </div>
          )}

          {snapshot && !snapshotLoading && (
            <>
              <div className="grid grid-cols-2 md:grid-cols-4 gap-4">
                {[
                  { label: "m³ i lager", value: snapshot.totalM3.toFixed(1) },
                  { label: "antal paket", value: snapshot.antalPaket },
                  ...(visPris && snapshot.andelMedPris > 0 ? [
                    { label: "snittpris kr/m³ (auto)", value: snapshot.snittpris > 0 ? snapshot.snittpris.toLocaleString("sv-SE") : "–" },
                    { label: "lagervärde (auto)", value: snapshot.totalVarde > 0 ? formatKr(snapshot.totalVarde) : "–" },
                  ] : []),
                ].map(({ label, value }) => (
                  <Card key={label}>
                    <CardContent className="pt-4 text-center">
                      <div className="text-2xl font-bold">{value}</div>
                      <div className="text-xs text-muted-foreground mt-1">{label}</div>
                    </CardContent>
                  </Card>
                ))}
                {visPris && manuellPris > 0 && (
                  <>
                    <Card className="border-blue-200 bg-blue-50">
                      <CardContent className="pt-4 text-center">
                        <div className="text-2xl font-bold text-blue-700">{manuellPris.toLocaleString("sv-SE")}</div>
                        <div className="text-xs text-blue-600 mt-1">manuellt pris kr/m³</div>
                      </CardContent>
                    </Card>
                    <Card className="border-blue-200 bg-blue-50">
                      <CardContent className="pt-4 text-center">
                        <div className="text-2xl font-bold text-blue-700">{formatKr(manuellPris * snapshot.totalM3)}</div>
                        <div className="text-xs text-blue-600 mt-1">beräknat värde</div>
                      </CardContent>
                    </Card>
                  </>
                )}
              </div>

              {visPris && manuellPris > 0 && (
                <div className="text-xs text-blue-700 bg-blue-50 border border-blue-200 rounded-md px-3 py-2">
                  Beräknat värde: {manuellPris.toLocaleString("sv-SE")} kr/m³ × {snapshot.totalM3.toFixed(1)} m³ = {formatKr(manuellPris * snapshot.totalM3)}
                </div>
              )}
              {visPris && !manuellPris && snapshot.andelMedPris < 100 && snapshot.andelMedPris > 0 && (
                <div className="text-xs text-amber-600 bg-amber-50 border border-amber-200 rounded-md px-3 py-2">
                  Obs: Snittpris och lagervärde baseras på {snapshot.andelMedPris}% av lagret — resterande paket saknade pris vid detta datum.
                </div>
              )}
              {visPris && !manuellPris && snapshot.andelMedPris === 0 && (
                <div className="text-xs text-muted-foreground bg-muted/50 border rounded-md px-3 py-2">
                  Inga paket hade registrerat pris vid detta datum — ange ett manuellt snittpris ovan för att beräkna lagervärde.
                </div>
              )}

              {snapshot.perDimension.length > 0 && (
                <Card>
                  <CardHeader className="pb-3"><CardTitle className="text-base">Per dimension</CardTitle></CardHeader>
                  <CardContent className="overflow-x-auto">
                    <table className="w-full text-sm">
                      <thead>
                        <tr className="border-b text-muted-foreground">
                          <th className="text-left py-2 pr-4 font-medium">Dimension</th>
                          <th className="text-left py-2 pr-4 font-medium">Träslag</th>
                          <th className="text-left py-2 pr-4 font-medium">Profil</th>
                          <th className="text-right py-2 pr-4 font-medium">Paket</th>
                          <th className="text-right py-2 pr-4 font-medium">m³</th>
                          {visPris && <th className="text-right py-2 font-medium">Värde</th>}
                        </tr>
                      </thead>
                      <tbody>
                        {snapshot.perDimension.map((r, i) => (
                          <tr key={`${r.dim}-${r.traslag}-${r.profil}`} className={i % 2 === 0 ? "bg-muted/30" : ""}>
                            <td className="py-1.5 pr-4 font-medium">{r.dim}</td>
                            <td className="py-1.5 pr-4 text-muted-foreground">{r.traslag || "–"}</td>
                            <td className="py-1.5 pr-4 text-muted-foreground">{r.profil || "–"}</td>
                            <td className="py-1.5 pr-4 text-right tabular-nums">{r.antal}</td>
                            <td className="py-1.5 pr-4 text-right tabular-nums">{r.m3.toFixed(1)}</td>
                            {visPris && <td className="py-1.5 text-right tabular-nums text-muted-foreground">{r.varde > 0 ? formatKr(r.varde) : "–"}</td>}
                          </tr>
                        ))}
                      </tbody>
                    </table>
                  </CardContent>
                </Card>
              )}
            </>
          )}
        </TabsContent>

      </Tabs>

      <style>{`
        @media print {
          body { font-size: 11px; color: #000 !important; }
          nav, aside, header, footer, .print\\:hidden, [role="tablist"] { display: none !important; }
          @page { margin: 1.5cm; }
          * { color: #000 !important; border-color: #aaa !important; background: #fff !important; }
          .bg-muted\\/30, .bg-muted\\/40, .bg-muted\\/50 { background: transparent !important; }
        }
      `}</style>
    </div>
  );
}


================ FILE: artifacts/lager/src/pages/skanna/SkannaUtPage.tsx ================

import { useEffect, useRef, useState, useCallback } from "react";
import { customFetch } from "@workspace/api-client-react";
import { useToast } from "@/hooks/use-toast";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { Badge } from "@/components/ui/badge";
import { ScanBarcode, Trash2, CheckCircle2, AlertCircle, X, Loader2 } from "lucide-react";
import { cn } from "@/lib/utils";

interface PaketRad {
  id: number;
  pktNr: string;
  traslag: string | null;
  profil: string | null;
  kvalitet: string | null;
  nomTjocklek: number | null;
  nomBredd: number | null;
  langdDm: number | null;
  antal: number | null;
  plats: string | null;
  datumUt: string | null;
}

// Svaret från GET /api/lager/paket/:pktNr — ett fysiskt paket (pktNr) kan
// bestå av flera lager_poster-rader (en per längd). Se lib/lagerFilters.ts
// (iLagerConditions) för vilka rader som räknas som "aktuella".
interface PaketData {
  pktNr: string;
  rader: PaketRad[];
  antalRader: number;
  totalAntal: number;
  totalM3: number;
}

interface PaketItem extends PaketData {
  scanAt: Date;
}

function formatDim(r: PaketRad): string {
  const t = r.nomTjocklek;
  const b = r.nomBredd;
  if (t && b) return `${t}×${b}`;
  return "—";
}

function formatLangd(r: PaketRad): string {
  if (!r.langdDm) return "—";
  return `${(r.langdDm / 10).toFixed(1)} m`;
}

function formatLangder(rader: PaketRad[]): string {
  return rader.map(formatLangd).join(", ");
}

function formatM3(m3: number): string {
  return `${m3.toFixed(2)} m³`;
}

export default function SkannaUtPage() {
  const inputRef = useRef<HTMLInputElement>(null);
  const [inputValue, setInputValue] = useState("");
  const [scanning, setScanning] = useState(false);
  const [items, setItems] = useState<PaketItem[]>([]);
  const [lastError, setLastError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [lastAdded, setLastAdded] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [saving, setSaving] = useState(false);
  const [savedCount, setSavedCount] = useState<number | null>(null);
  const { toast } = useToast();

  useEffect(() => {
    inputRef.current?.focus();
  }, []);

  const focusInput = useCallback(() => {
    inputRef.current?.focus();
  }, []);

  function addPaket(paket: PaketData) {
    setItems(prev => [{ ...paket, scanAt: new Date() }, ...prev]);
    setLastAdded(paket.pktNr);
    setSavedCount(null);
  }

  async function handleScan(raw: string) {
    const pktNr = raw.trim();
    if (!pktNr) return;

    if (items.some(i => i.pktNr === pktNr)) {
      setLastError(`${pktNr} finns redan i listan`);
      setLastAdded(null);
      setInputValue("");
      return;
    }

    setScanning(true);
    setLastError(null);
    setLastAdded(null);

    try {
      // Hämtar SAMTLIGA aktuella lager_poster-rader för paketet i ett svar
      // (inte bara en godtycklig rad) — ett pktNr är ett fysiskt paket även
      // om det ligger som flera längdrader.
      const paket = await customFetch<PaketData>(`/api/lager/paket/${encodeURIComponent(pktNr)}`);
      addPaket(paket);
    } catch {
      const digits = pktNr.replace(/^[A-Za-z]+/, "");
      if (digits && digits !== pktNr) {
        try {
          const paket = await customFetch<PaketData>(`/api/lager/paket/${encodeURIComponent(digits)}`);
          if (items.some(i => i.pktNr === paket.pktNr)) {
            setLastError(`${paket.pktNr} finns redan i listan`);
          } else {
            addPaket(paket);
          }
        } catch {
          setLastError(`Hittade inte paket "${pktNr}" i lagret`);
        }
      } else {
        setLastError(`Hittade inte paket "${pktNr}" i lagret`);
      }
    } finally {
      setScanning(false);
      setInputValue("");
    }
  }

  function handleKeyDown(e: React.KeyboardEvent<HTMLInputElement>) {
    if (e.key === "Enter") {
      handleScan(inputValue);
    }
  }

  function removeItem(pktNr: string) {
    setItems(prev => prev.filter(i => i.pktNr !== pktNr));
  }

  async function avregistreraAlla() {
    if (items.length === 0) return;
    setSaving(true);
    const today = new Date().toISOString().split("T")[0];
    // Samtliga rader för samtliga skannade paket avregistreras i ett enda
    // atomiskt anrop — servern (PUT /api/lager/batch) expanderar dessutom
    // till hela paketet per pktNr som ett extra skyddsnät.
    const ids = items.flatMap(i => i.rader.map(r => r.id));
    try {
      const result = await customFetch<{ uppdaterade: number }>("/api/lager/batch", {
        method: "PUT",
        body: JSON.stringify({ ids, patch: { datumUt: today } }),
      });
      setSavedCount(items.length);
      setItems([]);
      setLastError(null);
      setLastAdded(null);
      toast({
        title: `${items.length} paket avregistrerade (${result.uppdaterade} lagerrader)`,
        description: `Datum ut satt till ${today}`,
      });
    } catch {
      toast({
        title: "Fel vid avregistrering",
        description: "Försök igen eller kontakta support.",
        variant: "destructive",
      });
    } finally {
      setSaving(false);
      focusInput();
    }
  }

  return (
    <div className="flex flex-col h-full" onClick={focusInput}>
      <div className="border-b px-6 py-4 flex items-center gap-3 bg-background">
        <ScanBarcode className="h-6 w-6 text-[#2d5a27]" />
        <div>
          <h1 className="text-xl font-semibold">Skanna ut paket</h1>
          <p className="text-sm text-muted-foreground">Skanna streckkoder för att avregistrera sågade paket</p>
        </div>
      </div>

      <div className="flex flex-col gap-4 p-6 flex-1 overflow-auto">
        <div
          className={cn(
            "rounded-xl border-2 p-6 flex flex-col items-center gap-4 transition-colors cursor-text",
            lastError ? "border-destructive/50 bg-destructive/5" :
            lastAdded ? "border-green-500/50 bg-green-50/50" :
            "border-dashed border-muted-foreground/30 hover:border-muted-foreground/50"
          )}
          onClick={(e) => { e.stopPropagation(); inputRef.current?.focus(); }}
        >
          <div className="flex items-center gap-3 w-full max-w-md">
            <ScanBarcode className={cn("h-5 w-5 shrink-0", scanning ? "animate-pulse text-[#2d5a27]" : "text-muted-foreground")} />
            <input
              ref={inputRef}
              type="text"
              value={inputValue}
              onChange={e => { setInputValue(e.target.value); setLastError(null); }}
              onKeyDown={handleKeyDown}
              placeholder="Skanna eller skriv paket-ID…"
              className="flex-1 bg-transparent text-lg outline-none placeholder:text-muted-foreground/50 font-mono"
              autoComplete="off"
              autoCorrect="off"
              spellCheck={false}
            />
            {scanning && <Loader2 className="h-4 w-4 animate-spin text-muted-foreground" />}
          </div>

          {lastError && (
            <div className="flex items-center gap-2 text-sm text-destructive">
              <AlertCircle className="h-4 w-4 shrink-0" />
              {lastError}
            </div>
          )}
          {lastAdded && !lastError && (
            <div className="flex items-center gap-2 text-sm text-green-700">
              <CheckCircle2 className="h-4 w-4 shrink-0" />
              {lastAdded} tillagd i listan
            </div>
          )}
          {savedCount !== null && (
            <div className="flex items-center gap-2 text-sm text-green-700 font-medium">
              <CheckCircle2 className="h-4 w-4 shrink-0" />
              {savedCount} paket avregistrerade — redo för ny scanning
            </div>
          )}
        </div>

        {items.length > 0 && (
          <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
            <span className="text-sm text-muted-foreground">
              {items.length} paket skannade
            </span>
            <Button
              onClick={(e) => { e.stopPropagation(); avregistreraAlla(); }}
              disabled={saving}
              className="bg-[#2d5a27] hover:bg-[#224420] text-white"
            >
              {saving ? (
                <><Loader2 className="h-4 w-4 mr-2 animate-spin" />Avregistrerar…</>
              ) : (
                <>Avregistrera alla ({items.length})</>
              )}
            </Button>
          </div>
        )}

        {items.length > 0 && (
          <div className="rounded-lg border overflow-hidden">
            <table className="w-full text-sm">
              <thead className="bg-muted/50">
                <tr>
                  <th className="text-left px-4 py-2.5 font-medium text-muted-foreground">Paket-ID</th>
                  <th className="text-left px-4 py-2.5 font-medium text-muted-foreground">Träslag</th>
                  <th className="text-left px-4 py-2.5 font-medium text-muted-foreground">Profil</th>
                  <th className="text-left px-4 py-2.5 font-medium text-muted-foreground">Dim</th>
                  <th className="text-left px-4 py-2.5 font-medium text-muted-foreground">Längder</th>
                  <th className="text-left px-4 py-2.5 font-medium text-muted-foreground">Antal</th>
                  <th className="text-left px-4 py-2.5 font-medium text-muted-foreground">m³</th>
                  <th className="text-left px-4 py-2.5 font-medium text-muted-foreground">Plats</th>
                  <th className="px-4 py-2.5" />
                </tr>
              </thead>
              <tbody>
                {items.map((item, idx) => {
                  const forsta = item.rader[0];
                  return (
                    <tr
                      key={item.pktNr}
                      className={cn(
                        "border-t transition-colors",
                        idx === 0 ? "bg-green-50/60" : "bg-background hover:bg-muted/30"
                      )}
                    >
                      <td className="px-4 py-2.5 font-mono font-medium">
                        {item.pktNr}
                        {item.antalRader > 1 && (
                          <Badge variant="secondary" className="ml-2 font-normal">{item.antalRader} rader</Badge>
                        )}
                      </td>
                      <td className="px-4 py-2.5">{forsta?.traslag ?? "—"}</td>
                      <td className="px-4 py-2.5">
                        <Badge variant="outline" className="font-normal">{forsta?.profil ?? "—"}</Badge>
                      </td>
                      <td className="px-4 py-2.5 font-mono text-xs">{forsta ? formatDim(forsta) : "—"}</td>
                      <td className="px-4 py-2.5 text-xs">{formatLangder(item.rader)}</td>
                      <td className="px-4 py-2.5">{item.totalAntal}</td>
                      <td className="px-4 py-2.5">{formatM3(item.totalM3)}</td>
                      <td className="px-4 py-2.5 text-muted-foreground text-xs">{forsta?.plats ?? "—"}</td>
                      <td className="px-4 py-2.5">
                        <button
                          onClick={(e) => { e.stopPropagation(); removeItem(item.pktNr); }}
                          className="text-muted-foreground hover:text-destructive transition-colors"
                          title="Ta bort från lista"
                        >
                          <X className="h-4 w-4" />
                        </button>
                      </td>
                    </tr>
                  );
                })}
              </tbody>
            </table>
          </div>
        )}

        {items.length === 0 && savedCount === null && (
          <div className="flex-1 flex flex-col items-center justify-center text-center text-muted-foreground py-16 gap-3">
            <ScanBarcode className="h-12 w-12 opacity-20" />
            <div>
              <p className="font-medium">Redo att skanna</p>
              <p className="text-sm">Klicka i sökfältet ovan och börja skanna streckkoder</p>
            </div>
          </div>
        )}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}
